// ==UserScript==
// @name         Sniffies Soft Filter (Bottom / Vers Bottom)
// @namespace    https://sniffies.com/
// @version      0.11.2
// @last-change  2026-08-24 00:00:00 EDT
// @description  Soft-hide selected attitudes, profile text filters, chat-age badges, 24h/2h chat filters, a not-online-in-2h filter, auto-hide of repeat message-deleters and of profiles you've sent 4+ messages with no reply (until they reply), auto-unhide of manually-hidden profiles on reply, Global-Chat filtering of blocked profiles' messages (middle-click a Global Chat message to hide/block its author), top-bar attitude + Global-Chat quick-toggle buttons, profile reminders, and memory cleanup.
// @match        https://sniffies.com/*
// @grant        GM_openInTab
// @grant        GM_getValue
// @grant        GM_setValue
// @grant        GM_deleteValue
// ==/UserScript==

/*
===============================================================================
README / HOW TO USE
===============================================================================
Purpose:
  This userscript adds advanced filtering, bookmarking, reminders, quick phrases,
  and chat-age badges to the Sniffies map/profile UI.

Quick start:
  1) Install the userscript in Tampermonkey.
  2) Open https://sniffies.com and click "Show Filter" (top-right launcher).
  3) Enable/disable position filters in "Soft Filter".
  4) Use "Hide Now" to force a full hide pass on currently loaded markers.

Primary controls:
  - Enabled: master on/off switch.
  - Time flag on markers: show/hide chat-age badge on visible markers.
  - Hide chatted in last 24h: hide profiles with recent interaction, but
    auto-unhide one as soon as they reply (their last message newer than yours).
  - Hide not online in last <window>: hide profiles whose last-online time (from
    the partials feed) is older than the selected window. The window is chosen
    next to the toggle: 10 minutes, 2 hours, or a Custom number of minutes (1 to
    1440 -- last-online data is only kept for 24h, so longer windows can't work).
    Profiles online within the window stay shown, and profiles with an unanswered
    reply in the last 24h are never hidden by it.
  - Hide chatted ever: hide profiles with any known chat history.
  - Show only chatted profiles: hide profiles with no known chat history.
  - Exclude profile text: one keyword/phrase per line; any match hides.
  - Include profile text: one keyword/phrase per line; any match highlights.
  - Global Chat: any message whose author is currently hidden by the filters
    above is also hidden in the Global Chat. The author is the message element's
    profile id; attitude/position is read from the message header when the author
    is not on the map. Always on while Enabled (no separate toggle).

Map/profile shortcuts:
  - Shift + left-click marker: hide/unhide profile (block toggle).
  - Middle-click map marker: hide profile.
  - Middle-click a Global Chat message: hide/block that message's author (they
    and their messages stop appearing in Global Chat, same as the map).
  - Middle-click chat window: send random intro phrase.
  - Shift + right-click marker/chat: send random intro phrase.
  - Alt + Shift + right-click in chat: save highlighted/copied text as phrase.

Keyboard hotkeys:
  - g (outside a chat): open Global Chat.
  - c (outside a chat): toggle the Chats list panel.
  - n (inside a chat, text field not focused): jump to next unread chat.
  - b (inside a chat, text field not focused): jump to previous unread chat.
  All hotkeys ignore modifier-key combos and never fire while typing in an
  input, textarea, or contenteditable field, so they won't clobber typing.

Panels:
  - Include Matches: shows include keyword matches.
  - Bookmarks: save profiles, alias them, tag/category filter, broadcast message.
  - Appointments: reminder list with approaching/at-time alerts.

Google Drive sync:
  - Disabled by default in public builds.
  - Set GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID and OAuth redirect values in this file to enable.
  - OAuth uses PKCE code flow with state verification.
  - Access tokens are stored via Tampermonkey GM storage (not localStorage).
  - This build avoids page-context injection so it remains compatible with
    Sniffies' newer SES/lockdown startup.

Data storage:
  - Local-only (localStorage) unless Drive sync is manually enabled.
  - Stores filters, blocked IDs, notes, bookmarks, phrase usage history, and
    chat-age cache metadata used for UI behavior.
  - Export/Import supports optional passphrase encryption (AES-GCM + PBKDF2).

Donation links:
  - Cash App: https://cash.app/$mitchphudson
  - Venmo: https://account.venmo.com/u/Mitchphudson
  - Chime: $Mitchell-Hudson-1
  - Zelle: mitchphudson@gmail.com

===============================================================================
ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW
===============================================================================
1) Marker discovery:
   scanMarkers() indexes map markers and maintains idToMarker.

2) Profile enrichment:
   updatePartials() + fetchPartials() hydrate attitude/profile snippets.
   refreshFullProfileText() hydrates text for include/exclude matching.

3) Decision engine:
   applyHiding() executes hide/show/highlight logic in deterministic order:
   blocked -> text exclude -> recent-chat-24h hide -> recent-chat-2h hide ->
   any-chat hide -> missing-chat-history hide -> not-online hide ->
   repeated-delete hide -> unanswered-out hide -> attitude rules -> highlights.

4) Interaction capture:
   installChatCapture() patches fetch/XHR/WebSocket payload paths to detect
   chat activity timestamps, recent message snippets, and per-profile
   last-online (connect/disconnect) times from the partials feed.

5) UI rendering:
   buildPanel(), renderMatchPanel(), renderBookmarkPanel(), renderAppointmentPanel()
   manage floating UI with draggable persistence.

6) Maintenance:
   runMemoryGc() prunes stale caches/timers/preview data to control growth.
===============================================================================
*/

var __defProp = Object.defineProperty;
var __defProps = Object.defineProperties;
var __getOwnPropDescs = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors;
var __getOwnPropSymbols = Object.getOwnPropertySymbols;
var __hasOwnProp = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty;
var __propIsEnum = Object.prototype.propertyIsEnumerable;
var __defNormalProp = (obj, key, value) => key in obj ? __defProp(obj, key, { enumerable: true, configurable: true, writable: true, value }) : obj[key] = value;
var __spreadValues = (a, b) => {
  for (var prop in b || (b = {}))
    if (__hasOwnProp.call(b, prop))
      __defNormalProp(a, prop, b[prop]);
  if (__getOwnPropSymbols)
    for (var prop of __getOwnPropSymbols(b)) {
      if (__propIsEnum.call(b, prop))
        __defNormalProp(a, prop, b[prop]);
    }
  return a;
};
var __spreadProps = (a, b) => __defProps(a, __getOwnPropDescs(b));
(() => {
  "use strict";
  // ---- Boot prelude: detect the runtime (plain userscript vs. packaged Chrome extension)
  // and enforce a single active instance. The first instance stamps a data-attribute on <html>;
  // a second load sees it and returns early, so double-injection is a harmless no-op. ----
  const LOG_PREFIX = "[SniffiesFilter]";
  const SOFT_FILTER_SINGLETON_ATTR = "data-sniffies-soft-filter-active";
  const SOFT_FILTER_SINGLETON_STARTED_ATTR = "data-sniffies-soft-filter-started-at";
  const runtimeSource = typeof chrome !== "undefined" && chrome && chrome.runtime && chrome.runtime.id ? `chrome-extension:${chrome.runtime.id}` : "userscript";
  const extensionControlsHostedInPopup = runtimeSource.indexOf("chrome-extension:") === 0;
  const singletonRoot = document.documentElement;
  const activeRuntime = singletonRoot ? singletonRoot.getAttribute(SOFT_FILTER_SINGLETON_ATTR) : "";
  if (activeRuntime) {
    console.warn(LOG_PREFIX, `Duplicate runtime detected; skipping boot because ${activeRuntime} is already active.`);
    return;
  }
  if (singletonRoot) {
    singletonRoot.setAttribute(SOFT_FILTER_SINGLETON_ATTR, runtimeSource);
    singletonRoot.setAttribute(SOFT_FILTER_SINGLETON_STARTED_ATTR, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString());
  }
  const LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS = { quiet: 0, normal: 1, verbose: 2 };

  const PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_RETRY_MS = 10 * 60 * 1e3;
  const MAP_MARKER_RETRY_MS = 10 * 1e3;
  const CHAT_ACTIVITY_TTL_MS = 120 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3;

  const CHAT_STORAGE_SCAN_INTERVAL_MS = 60 * 1e3;

  // of message timestamps when live fetch/XHR/WS interception misses something). Skips values > 8MB.
  const CHAT_STORAGE_KEY_RE = /(chat|message|inbox|conversation|thread|dm|socket)/i;

  // Auto-hide a profile once you've sent this many messages in a row with no reply

  // Read state.logVerbosity, lowercased; falls back to 'quiet' if unset or not a known level.
  // The try/catch keeps logging safe even before `state` is initialized: the call-trace logs fired
  // from the load*() helpers run during `let state = loadState()`, when `state` is still in the
  // temporal dead zone and any reference to it would throw. Treat that window as 'quiet'.
  function currentLogVerbosity() {
    let raw = "quiet";
    try {
      raw = String((state == null ? void 0 : state.logVerbosity) || "quiet").toLowerCase();
    } catch (e) {
      return "quiet";
    }
    return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS, raw) ? raw : "quiet";
  }
  // True when the configured verbosity meets/exceeds the message's level (quiet<normal<verbose).
  // Unknown level arg defaults to 'normal' (target 1).
  function shouldLog(level = "normal") {
    var _a, _b;
    const current = (_a = LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS[currentLogVerbosity()]) != null ? _a : 0;
    const target = (_b = LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS[String(level || "normal").toLowerCase()]) != null ? _b : 1;
    return current >= target;
  }
  // console.debug with prefix; only emits at 'verbose' verbosity.
  // ---- Call-trace helpers (verbose-gated). traceArgs/tracePreview render call arguments in a
  // leak-resistant way: long strings are masked, secret-looking object keys are redacted, depth and
  // key counts are capped, and a throwing getter or circular ref can never crash the logger. Both are
  // excluded from tracing themselves (would otherwise recurse). See the log("\u2192 fn", ...) call sites. ----
  function tracePreview(v, depth) {
    if (v == null) return v;
    const t = typeof v;
    if (t === "string") return v.length > 40 ? "[str:" + v.length + "]" : v;
    if (t === "number" || t === "boolean") return v;
    if (t === "bigint" || t === "symbol" || t === "function") return "[" + t + "]";
    try {
      if (typeof Element !== "undefined" && v instanceof Element) return "<" + (v.tagName || "el").toLowerCase() + ">";
    } catch (e) {}
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return depth >= 2 ? "[array:" + v.length + "]" : v.slice(0, 6).map(function (x) { return tracePreview(x, depth + 1); });
    if (t === "object") {
      if (depth >= 2) return "{\u2026}";
      const out = {};
      let n = 0;
      for (const k in v) {
        if (n++ >= 12) { out["\u2026"] = "(more)"; break; }
        if (/token|pass|secret|code|auth|key|cred|cookie|bearer/i.test(k)) { out[k] = "[redacted]"; continue; }
        try { out[k] = tracePreview(v[k], depth + 1); } catch (e) { out[k] = "[unreadable]"; }
      }
      return out;
    }
    return "[" + t + "]";
  }
  function traceArgs(args) {
    try {
      const out = [];
      const n = Math.min(args.length, 8);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(tracePreview(args[i], 0));
      if (args.length > 8) out.push("\u2026(" + args.length + " args)");
      return out;
    } catch (e) { return "[args]"; }
  }
  function log(...args) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) console.debug(LOG_PREFIX, ...args);
  }
  // console.info with prefix; gated at 'normal' verbosity or higher.
  function logInfo(...args) {
    if (shouldLog("normal")) console.info(LOG_PREFIX, ...args);
  }
  // console.warn with prefix + ' WARN'; gated at 'normal' verbosity or higher.
  function logWarn(...args) {
    if (shouldLog("normal")) console.warn(`${LOG_PREFIX} WARN`, ...args);
  }
  // console.error with prefix; the only logger that emits unconditionally (ignores verbosity).
  function logError(...args) {
    console.error(LOG_PREFIX, ...args);
  }
  // Set the status element's text, if the element exists.
  function setStatus(text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setStatus", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = text;
  }
  // Current epoch millis (Date.now wrapper).
  function now() {
    return Date.now();
  }
  // True when the tab is hidden; used to pause background UI work.
  function isUiWorkSuspended() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isUiWorkSuspended", traceArgs(arguments));
    return !!document.hidden;
  }
  // Normalize each profile id, drop empties/dupes, then sort by localeCompare.
  // Returns a clean, deduped, sorted array.
  function normalizeIdList(ids) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeIdList", traceArgs(arguments));
    const out = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const raw of ids || []) {
      const id = normalizeProfileId(raw);
      if (!id || seen.has(id)) continue;
      seen.add(id);
      out.push(id);
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
    return out;
  }
  // Feature-detect WebCrypto: requires crypto.subtle plus TextEncoder/TextDecoder.
  function hasSubtleCrypto() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hasSubtleCrypto", traceArgs(arguments));
    return !!(globalThis.crypto && crypto.subtle && typeof TextEncoder !== "undefined" && typeof TextDecoder !== "undefined");
  }
  // Encode bytes to base64 via btoa, processing in 32768-byte chunks.
  // Chunking avoids a call-stack overflow from spreading a huge array into String.fromCharCode.
  function bytesToBase64(bytes) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ bytesToBase64", traceArgs(arguments));
    const arr = bytes instanceof Uint8Array ? bytes : new Uint8Array(bytes || []);
    let binary = "";
    // 32KB chunking caps the spread size into String.fromCharCode, preventing a max-call-stack error on large inputs.
    const chunkSize = 32768;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += chunkSize) {
      const chunk = arr.subarray(i, i + chunkSize);
      // Spreads a bounded chunk (<=32768 bytes) into char codes; the bound is why chunking exists.
      binary += String.fromCharCode(...chunk);
    }
    return btoa(binary);
  }
  // Decode base64 (whitespace stripped) to a Uint8Array via atob.
  function base64ToBytes(base64) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ base64ToBytes", traceArgs(arguments));
    const clean = String(base64 || "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
    const binary = atob(clean);
    const out = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) out[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    return out;
  }
  // Convert standard base64 to URL-safe base64 (+/ -> -_) and strip '=' padding.
  function toBase64Url(base64) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toBase64Url", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Base64 -> base64url: + becomes -, / becomes _, trailing = padding removed.
    return String(base64 || "").replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
  }
  // Convert URL-safe base64 back to standard and re-add '=' padding to a multiple of 4.
  function fromBase64Url(base64url) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ fromBase64Url", traceArgs(arguments));
    const value = String(base64url || "").replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
    // Re-pad base64url to a multiple of 4 chars before atob can decode it.
    const pad = value.length % 4 === 0 ? "" : "=".repeat(4 - value.length % 4);
    return value + pad;
  }
  // Return `size` cryptographically-random bytes via crypto.getRandomValues (default 16).
  function randomBytes(size = 16) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ randomBytes", traceArgs(arguments));
    const out = new Uint8Array(size);
    crypto.getRandomValues(out);
    return out;
  }
  // URL-safe base64 token from `size` random bytes (default 24).
  function randomToken(size = 24) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ randomToken", traceArgs(arguments));
    return toBase64Url(bytesToBase64(randomBytes(size)));
  }
  // SHA-256 the input string and return the digest as URL-safe base64.
  async function sha256Base64Url(input) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sha256Base64Url", traceArgs(arguments));
    const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(input || ""));
    const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
    return toBase64Url(bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array(digest)));
  }
  // Safe GM_getValue wrapper: returns fallback if GM API absent or it throws.
  // Awaits the result when GM_getValue returns a thenable (handles async GM impls).
  async function gmGetValueSafe(key, fallback = null) {
    log("→ gmGetValueSafe");
    if (typeof GM_getValue !== "function") return fallback;
    try {
      const value = GM_getValue(key, fallback);
      // Thenable detection: awaits async GM_getValue impls, passes through sync ones.
      return value && typeof value.then === "function" ? await value : value;
    } catch (e) {
      return fallback;
    }
  }
  // Safe GM_setValue wrapper returning true/false; awaits a thenable result, swallows errors.
  async function gmSetValueSafe(key, value) {
    log("→ gmSetValueSafe");
    if (typeof GM_setValue !== "function") return false;
    try {
      const result = GM_setValue(key, value);
      if (result && typeof result.then === "function") await result;
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  // Safe GM_deleteValue wrapper returning true/false; awaits a thenable result, swallows errors.
  async function gmDeleteValueSafe(key) {
    log("→ gmDeleteValueSafe");
    if (typeof GM_deleteValue !== "function") return false;
    try {
      const result = GM_deleteValue(key);
      if (result && typeof result.then === "function") await result;
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  // Derive a non-extractable AES-GCM-256 key from a passphrase via PBKDF2-SHA256.
  // Uses 210000 iterations (EXPORT_ENC_ITERATIONS) with the caller-supplied salt.
  async function derivePassphraseKey(passphrase, saltBytes, usage = ["encrypt", "decrypt"]) {
    log("→ derivePassphraseKey");
    const passBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(String(passphrase || ""));
    const keyMaterial = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", passBytes, "PBKDF2", false, ["deriveKey"]);
    return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
      // PBKDF2-SHA256 at 210k iterations stretches the passphrase into the AES key.
      { name: "PBKDF2", salt: saltBytes, iterations: EXPORT_ENC_ITERATIONS, hash: "SHA-256" },
      keyMaterial,
      { name: EXPORT_ENC_ALGO, length: 256 },
      false,
      usage
    );
  }
  // AES-GCM encrypt a string under a passphrase; throws if WebCrypto is unavailable.
  // Generates fresh 16-byte salt + 12-byte IV; returns a self-describing envelope (alg/kdf/iters/salt/iv/ciphertext, all base64).
  async function encryptStringWithPassphrase(plainText, passphrase) {
    log("→ encryptStringWithPassphrase");
    if (!hasSubtleCrypto()) throw new Error("WebCrypto unavailable");
    // Fresh 16-byte PBKDF2 salt per encryption (stored in the envelope).
    const salt = randomBytes(16);
    // Fresh 12-byte (96-bit) AES-GCM IV/nonce per encryption; must never be reused with the same key.
    const iv = randomBytes(12);
    const key = await derivePassphraseKey(passphrase, salt, ["encrypt"]);
    const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(plainText || ""));
    const cipherBuffer = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: EXPORT_ENC_ALGO, iv }, key, data);
    return {
      encrypted: true,
      version: EXPORT_ENC_VERSION,
      alg: EXPORT_ENC_ALGO,
      kdf: "PBKDF2-SHA256",
      iterations: EXPORT_ENC_ITERATIONS,
      salt: bytesToBase64(salt),
      iv: bytesToBase64(iv),
      ciphertext: bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer))
    };
  }
  // Reverse of encryptStringWithPassphrase: re-derive key from passphrase+envelope salt, AES-GCM decrypt.
  // Throws if WebCrypto unavailable or auth tag/passphrase is wrong.
  async function decryptStringWithPassphrase(envelope, passphrase) {
    log("→ decryptStringWithPassphrase");
    if (!hasSubtleCrypto()) throw new Error("WebCrypto unavailable");
    const salt = base64ToBytes((envelope == null ? void 0 : envelope.salt) || "");
    const iv = base64ToBytes((envelope == null ? void 0 : envelope.iv) || "");
    const ciphertext = base64ToBytes((envelope == null ? void 0 : envelope.ciphertext) || "");
    const key = await derivePassphraseKey(passphrase, salt, ["decrypt"]);
    const plainBuffer = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name: EXPORT_ENC_ALGO, iv }, key, ciphertext);
    return new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(plainBuffer));
  }
  // Try navigator.clipboard.writeText; on failure or absence, show a prompt() for manual copy.
  // Returns true only if programmatically copied; false when it fell back to the prompt.
  async function copyTextWithFallback(text, promptTitle) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ copyTextWithFallback", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const value = String(text || "");
    if ((_a = navigator.clipboard) == null ? void 0 : _a.writeText) {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    prompt(promptTitle, value);
    return false;
  }
  // Assign value onto window and globalThis (the userscript sandbox realm), and — unless
  // opts.sandboxOnly is set — also onto unsafeWindow (Tampermonkey PAGE realm) so read-only debug
  // globals are reachable from the page's own console without switching DevTools context.
  // sandboxOnly:true is required for anything that mutates state, triggers network calls, or exposes
  // private data (bookmarks/appointments/etc.) — the README/CLAUDE.md documents "avoids page-context
  // injection... keep work in the userscript sandbox", so a global reachable from the page's own JS
  // realm (a future sniffies.com XSS, or another extension/userscript sharing that page) must not be
  // able to read private data or invoke privileged actions with zero origin check.
  // Each target/assignment is try-wrapped and deduped.
  function exposeGlobal(name, value, opts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ exposeGlobal", traceArgs(arguments));
    const sandboxOnly = !!(opts && opts.sandboxOnly);
    const targets = [];
    try {
      targets.push(window);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    try {
      targets.push(globalThis);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    if (!sandboxOnly) {
      try {
        // Also expose onto unsafeWindow so read-only debug globals are reachable from the page's own console realm.
        if (typeof unsafeWindow !== "undefined" && unsafeWindow) targets.push(unsafeWindow);
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const t of targets) {
      if (!t || seen.has(t)) continue;
      seen.add(t);
      try {
        t[name] = value;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
  }
  // Split on newlines/commas, trim, lowercase, drop empties -> array of search terms.
  function parseTerms(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parseTerms", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Splits on a single newline OR comma (empty fields kept then dropped by filter(Boolean)).
    return (raw || "").split(/[\n,]/).map((v) => v.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);
  }
  // Like parseTerms but splits on runs of newlines/commas (collapses consecutive separators).
  function parseTagFilter(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parseTagFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    // The '+' collapses runs of newlines/commas into one split (vs parseTerms' single-char split).
    return String(raw || "").split(/[\n,]+/).map((v) => v.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);
  }
  // Split text into trimmed non-empty lines, deduped case-insensitively (first occurrence wins).
  // Preserves original casing/order of the kept lines.
  function parsePhraseLines(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parsePhraseLines", traceArgs(arguments));
    const source = String(raw || "");
    const lines = source.split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const deduped = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const line of lines) {
      const key = line.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      deduped.push(line);
    }
    return deduped;
  }
  // Coerce a category to 'intro' or 'other' (anything not 'intro' becomes 'other').
  function normalizeQuickPhraseCategory(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeQuickPhraseCategory", traceArgs(arguments));
    const v = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    return v === "intro" ? "intro" : "other";
  }
  // Parsed intro phrases from state; falls back to DEFAULT_STATE intros when state has none.
  function getIntroPhrases() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getIntroPhrases", traceArgs(arguments));
    const configured = parsePhraseLines(state.introMessages);
    if (configured.length) return configured;
    return parsePhraseLines(DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages);
  }
  // Parsed non-intro ('other') quick phrases from state.
  function getOtherPhrases() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getOtherPhrases", traceArgs(arguments));
    return parsePhraseLines(state.otherMessages);
  }
  // Merge intro + other phrases into one deduped (case-insensitive) list, intros first.
  function getCannedPhrases() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getCannedPhrases", traceArgs(arguments));
    const intro = getIntroPhrases();
    const other = getOtherPhrases();
    const merged = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const phrase of [...intro, ...other]) {
      const key = String(phrase || "").toLowerCase();
      if (!key || seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      merged.push(phrase);
    }
    return merged;
  }
  // Replace intro/other phrase state; any 'other' phrase also present in intro is dropped (intro wins).
  // Rewrites state.introMessages/otherMessages/cannedMessages and saves unless options.save === false.
  function setQuickPhraseLists(introPhrases, otherPhrases, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setQuickPhraseLists", traceArgs(arguments));
    const intro = parsePhraseLines((introPhrases || []).join("\n"));
    const introKeys = new Set(intro.map((p) => String(p || "").toLowerCase()));
    const otherRaw = parsePhraseLines((otherPhrases || []).join("\n"));
    const other = [];
    for (const phrase of otherRaw) {
      const key = String(phrase || "").toLowerCase();
      if (!key || introKeys.has(key)) continue;
      other.push(phrase);
    }
    state.introMessages = intro.join("\n");
    state.otherMessages = other.join("\n");
    state.cannedMessages = [...intro, ...other].join("\n");
    if (options.save !== false) saveState();
  }
  // Push state.introMessages/otherMessages back into the panel's #sfIntroMessages/#sfOtherMessages textareas, if present.
  function syncQuickPhraseTextareas() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ syncQuickPhraseTextareas", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const introInput = (_a = softPanelEl == null ? void 0 : softPanelEl.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(softPanelEl, "#sfIntroMessages");
    if (introInput) introInput.value = state.introMessages || "";
    const otherInput = (_b = softPanelEl == null ? void 0 : softPanelEl.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(softPanelEl, "#sfOtherMessages");
    if (otherInput) otherInput.value = state.otherMessages || "";
  }
  // Sanitize a phrase, reject if empty/<2 chars or duplicate, else append to intro/other and persist.
  // Side effects: setQuickPhraseLists (saves), recordPhraseAdded, sync textareas, re-render chat phrase panel. Returns {ok,...}.
  function addPhraseToQuickList(rawPhrase, category = "other") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ addPhraseToQuickList", traceArgs(arguments));
    const clean = sanitizeMessageText(rawPhrase);
    if (!clean || clean.length < 2) return { ok: false, reason: "empty" };
    const phrases = getCannedPhrases();
    const key = clean.toLowerCase();
    if (phrases.some((p) => String(p || "").toLowerCase() === key)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: "duplicate", phrase: clean, count: phrases.length };
    }
    const group = normalizeQuickPhraseCategory(category);
    const intro = getIntroPhrases();
    const other = getOtherPhrases();
    if (group === "intro") {
      intro.push(clean);
    } else {
      other.push(clean);
    }
    setQuickPhraseLists(intro, other);
    recordPhraseAdded(clean);
    syncQuickPhraseTextareas();
    renderChatPhrasePanel();
    return { ok: true, phrase: clean, count: getCannedPhrases().length, category: group };
  }
  // Wrap addPhraseToQuickList, publish the outcome to __sniffiesLastQuickPhraseSave, and set a status message.
  // Distinguishes added / duplicate / not-added in the status text.
  function saveQuickPhraseFromText(rawPhrase, category = "other") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveQuickPhraseFromText", traceArgs(arguments));
    const result = addPhraseToQuickList(rawPhrase, category);
    exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastQuickPhraseSave", {
      ok: !!(result == null ? void 0 : result.ok),
      reason: (result == null ? void 0 : result.reason) || null,
      phrase: (result == null ? void 0 : result.phrase) || sanitizeMessageText(rawPhrase || ""),
      count: Number((result == null ? void 0 : result.count) || 0),
      category: (result == null ? void 0 : result.category) || normalizeQuickPhraseCategory(category),
      ts: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
    });
    if (result.ok) {
      setStatus(`Quick phrase added (${result.count})`);
      return result;
    }
    if (result.reason === "duplicate") {
      setStatus("Quick phrase already exists");
      return result;
    }
    setStatus("Quick phrase not added");
    return result;
  }
  // Canonicalize a sort mode to 'alpha' | 'used' | 'recent' (maps aliases like 'alphabetical','most_used').
  // Defaults to 'recent' for unrecognized values.
  function normalizedQuickPhraseSort(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizedQuickPhraseSort", traceArgs(arguments));
    const mode = String(value || "").toLowerCase();
    if (mode === "alpha" || mode === "recent" || mode === "used") return mode;
    if (mode === "alphabetical") return "alpha";
    if (mode === "most_used" || mode === "mostused") return "used";
    if (mode === "most_recent" || mode === "recently_used" || mode === "recentused") return "recent";
    return "recent";
  }
  // Read a phrase's stats row by lowercased key; returns zeroed {count,lastUsedAt,addedAt} when absent.
  // Coerces count to a non-negative integer and parses the timestamps.
  function getPhraseStatsEntry(phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getPhraseStatsEntry", traceArgs(arguments));
    const key = String(phrase || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!key) return { count: 0, lastUsedAt: 0, addedAt: 0 };
    const row = phraseStats[key];
    return {
      count: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(row == null ? void 0 : row.count) || 0)),
      lastUsedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastUsedAt) || 0,
      addedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.addedAt) || 0
    };
  }
  // Stamp a quick-phrase's addedAt (first time only) while preserving its count; keyed by lowercased text.
  // Persists phraseStats to localStorage via savePhraseStats().
  function recordPhraseAdded(phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordPhraseAdded", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(phrase || "").trim();
    const key = text.toLowerCase();
    if (!key) return;
    const row = phraseStats[key] && typeof phraseStats[key] === "object" ? phraseStats[key] : {};
    const updated = {
      count: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(row.count) || 0)),
      lastUsedAt: parseTimestamp(row.lastUsedAt) || 0,
      // addedAt is set to now() only when previously unset, so re-adding an existing phrase keeps its original add time.
      addedAt: parseTimestamp(row.addedAt) || now()
    };
    phraseStats[key] = updated;
    savePhraseStats();
  }
  // Bump a quick-phrase's usage count and set lastUsedAt=now; backfills addedAt if missing.
  // Persists phraseStats to localStorage.
  function recordPhraseUsage(phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordPhraseUsage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(phrase || "").trim();
    const key = text.toLowerCase();
    if (!key) return;
    const row = phraseStats[key] && typeof phraseStats[key] === "object" ? phraseStats[key] : {};
    const nextCount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(row.count) || 0)) + 1;
    phraseStats[key] = {
      count: nextCount,
      lastUsedAt: now(),
      addedAt: parseTimestamp(row.addedAt) || now()
    };
    savePhraseStats();
  }
  // Return phrase rows (with count/lastUsedAt/addedAt stats) ordered per state.quickPhraseSort: used, recent, or alpha.
  // lastUsedAt falls back to addedAt for ordering; alpha compare only applies in 'alpha' mode, else ties keep original input index (stable).
  function sortPhrasesForQuickPanel(phrases) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sortPhrasesForQuickPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const mode = normalizedQuickPhraseSort(state.quickPhraseSort);
    const rows = (phrases || []).map((phrase, index) => {
      const text = String(phrase || "").trim();
      const key = text.toLowerCase();
      const stats = getPhraseStatsEntry(text);
      return {
        phrase: text,
        key,
        index,
        count: stats.count,
        lastUsedAt: stats.lastUsedAt,
        addedAt: stats.addedAt
      };
    });
    rows.sort((a, b) => {
      if (mode === "used") {
        if (b.count !== a.count) return b.count - a.count;
        const bUsed = b.lastUsedAt || b.addedAt;
        const aUsed = a.lastUsedAt || a.addedAt;
        if (bUsed !== aUsed) return bUsed - aUsed;
      } else if (mode === "recent") {
        const bRecent = b.lastUsedAt || b.addedAt;
        const aRecent = a.lastUsedAt || a.addedAt;
        if (bRecent !== aRecent) return bRecent - aRecent;
        if (b.count !== a.count) return b.count - a.count;
      }
      // Separate (non-else) if: alphabetical tie-break runs only in 'alpha' mode; used/recent ties instead fall through to the original index for stable order.
      if (mode === "alpha") {
        const byText = a.phrase.localeCompare(b.phrase, void 0, { sensitivity: "base" });
        if (byText !== 0) return byText;
      }
      return a.index - b.index;
    });
    return rows;
  }
  // Reparse state.excludeProfileTerms / includeProfileTerms into the profileExcludeTerms / profileIncludeTerms module vars.
  // Called eagerly right after definition to prime those vars.
  function refreshProfileTerms() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ refreshProfileTerms", traceArgs(arguments));
    profileExcludeTerms = parseTerms(state.excludeProfileTerms);
    profileIncludeTerms = parseTerms(state.includeProfileTerms);
  }
  refreshProfileTerms();
  // Read the saved match-panel {left,top} from localStorage; returns null if absent, unparseable, or coords aren't numbers.
  function loadMatchPanelPos() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadMatchPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(MATCH_PANEL_POS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    try {
      const pos = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (typeof pos.left === "number" && typeof pos.top === "number") return pos;
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Persist match-panel position {left,top} to localStorage.
  function saveMatchPanelPos(left, top) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveMatchPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(MATCH_PANEL_POS_KEY, JSON.stringify({ left, top }));
  }

  // Apply the saved match-panel position to the element, clamping left/top to >=0 and clearing bottom so top-anchoring wins.
  // No-op if no saved pos or no element.
  function applyMatchPanelPos(panel2) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applyMatchPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    const pos = loadMatchPanelPos();
    if (!pos || !panel2) return;
    panel2.style.left = `${Math.max(0, pos.left)}px`;
    panel2.style.top = `${Math.max(0, pos.top)}px`;
    panel2.style.bottom = "auto";
  }
  // Apply saved soft-panel position to the element (left/top clamped >=0), clearing right so left-anchoring wins.
  function applySoftPanelPos(panel2) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applySoftPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    const pos = loadSoftPanelPos();
    if (!pos || !panel2) return;
    panel2.style.left = `${Math.max(0, pos.left)}px`;
    panel2.style.top = `${Math.max(0, pos.top)}px`;
    panel2.style.right = "auto";
  }
  // Set the soft panel's maxHeight to the space from its top to the viewport bottom minus 12px, floored at 240px.
  // Defaults to softPanelEl; reads layout via getBoundingClientRect.
  function updateSoftPanelViewportBounds(panel2 = softPanelEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateSoftPanelViewportBounds", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!panel2) return;
    const rect = panel2.getBoundingClientRect();
    const top = Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.top));
    const available = Math.max(240, window.innerHeight - top - 12);
    panel2.style.maxHeight = `${available}px`;
  }
  // Apply saved appointment-panel position (left/top clamped >=0), clearing both right and bottom.
  function applyApptPanelPos(panel2) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applyApptPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    const pos = loadApptPanelPos();
    if (!pos || !panel2) return;
    panel2.style.left = `${Math.max(0, pos.left)}px`;
    panel2.style.top = `${Math.max(0, pos.top)}px`;
    panel2.style.right = "auto";
    panel2.style.bottom = "auto";
  }
  // Apply saved bookmark-panel position (left/top clamped >=0), clearing right.
  function applyBookmarkPanelPos(panel2) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applyBookmarkPanelPos", traceArgs(arguments));
    const pos = loadBookmarkPanelPos();
    if (!pos || !panel2) return;
    panel2.style.left = `${Math.max(0, pos.left)}px`;
    panel2.style.top = `${Math.max(0, pos.top)}px`;
    panel2.style.right = "auto";
  }
  // Wire pointer-drag on handle to move panel2; persists final position via saveFn (default saveMatchPanelPos) on release.
  // Ignores non-left-button presses and pointerdowns on interactive children (button/a/input/textarea/select/label).
  function makePanelDraggable(panel2, handle, saveFn = saveMatchPanelPos) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ makePanelDraggable", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!panel2 || !handle) return;
    let dragging = false;
    let offsetX = 0;
    let offsetY = 0;
    // Pointermove handler (active only while dragging): position panel at cursor minus grab offset, clamped >=0; clears bottom/right.
    const onMove = (e) => {
      log("→ onMove");
      if (!dragging) return;
      const left = Math.max(0, e.clientX - offsetX);
      const top = Math.max(0, e.clientY - offsetY);
      panel2.style.left = `${left}px`;
      panel2.style.top = `${top}px`;
      panel2.style.bottom = "auto";
      panel2.style.right = "auto";
    };
    // Pointerup handler: end drag, remove the capture-phase pointermove/pointerup listeners, and persist the current left/top via saveFn.
    const onUp = () => {
      log("→ onUp");
      if (!dragging) return;
      dragging = false;
      document.removeEventListener("pointermove", onMove, true);
      document.removeEventListener("pointerup", onUp, true);
      const left = parseInt(panel2.style.left || "0", 10) || 0;
      const top = parseInt(panel2.style.top || "0", 10) || 0;
      saveFn(left, top);
    };
    handle.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
      if (e.button !== 0) return;
      // Drag is suppressed when the pointerdown lands on an interactive control so clicks/inputs still work.
      if (e.target && e.target.closest("button,a,input,textarea,select,label")) return;
      const rect = panel2.getBoundingClientRect();
      dragging = true;
      offsetX = e.clientX - rect.left;
      offsetY = e.clientY - rect.top;
      document.addEventListener("pointermove", onMove, true);
      document.addEventListener("pointerup", onUp, true);
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  }
  // Load persisted settings: return the first parseable value across STATE_KEYS (newest-first), else normalized DEFAULT_STATE.
  // Runs each candidate through normalizeLoadedState.
  function loadState() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadState", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Merge a raw object over DEFAULT_STATE and reconcile quick-phrase fields: split intro vs other messages, migrate legacy cannedMessages into intro, dedupe other against intro.
    // Coerces hide/show booleans and forces showOnlyChats off when hideAnyChats is also set (mutually exclusive).
    const normalizeLoadedState = (rawObj) => {
      log("→ normalizeLoadedState");
      const source = rawObj && typeof rawObj === "object" ? rawObj : {};
      const merged = __spreadValues(__spreadValues({}, DEFAULT_STATE), source);
      const hasIntro = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(source, "introMessages");
      const hasOther = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(source, "otherMessages");
      const legacy = parsePhraseLines(source.cannedMessages);
      let intro = hasIntro ? parsePhraseLines(source.introMessages) : [];
      let other = hasOther ? parsePhraseLines(source.otherMessages) : [];
      if (!intro.length && legacy.length) intro = legacy;
      if (!intro.length) intro = parsePhraseLines(DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages);
      const introKeys = new Set(intro.map((p) => String(p || "").toLowerCase()));
      other = other.filter((p) => !introKeys.has(String(p || "").toLowerCase()));
      merged.introMessages = intro.join("\n");
      merged.otherMessages = other.join("\n");
      merged.cannedMessages = [...intro, ...other].join("\n");
      merged.hideAnyChats = !!merged.hideAnyChats;
      merged.showOnlyChats = !!merged.showOnlyChats;
      // hideAnyChats and showOnlyChats are mutually exclusive; if both are set, showOnly is forced off.
      if (merged.hideAnyChats && merged.showOnlyChats) merged.showOnlyChats = false;
      merged.hideNotOnline2h = !!merged.hideNotOnline2h;
      // Coerce the configurable "not online" window to a positive integer of minutes, clamped to 1..1440
      // (24h — the presence-data retention). Invalid/missing values fall back to 120 (2h).
      const notOnlineWin = Math.round(Number(merged.notOnlineWindowMinutes));
      merged.notOnlineWindowMinutes = Number.isFinite(notOnlineWin) && notOnlineWin > 0 ? Math.min(Math.max(notOnlineWin, 1), 24 * 60) : 120;
      return merged;
    };
    for (const k of STATE_KEYS) {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (raw) {
        try {
          return normalizeLoadedState(JSON.parse(raw));
        } catch (e) {
        }
      }
    }
    return normalizeLoadedState(DEFAULT_STATE);
  }
  // Serialize the current state to the newest STATE_KEYS entry in localStorage.

    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveState", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(STATE_KEYS[0], JSON.stringify(state));
  }
  // Load the blocked-profile id Set from the first valid array across BLOCKED_KEYS (newest-first), normalizing ids; empty Set if none.
  function loadBlockedSet() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadBlockedSet", traceArgs(arguments));
    for (const k of BLOCKED_KEYS) {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (raw) {
        try {
          const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (Array.isArray(arr)) return new Set(normalizeIdList(arr));
        } catch (e) {
        }
      }
    }
    return /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  }
  // Debounce a Google Drive notes sync ~1.5s after the last call (clears any pending timer); no-op without a Drive token.
  // Updates sync status to syncing/error and logs success/failure; reason is a label for logs.
  function scheduleDriveSync(reason = "auto") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleDriveSync", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!getGDriveToken()) return;
    if (gdriveSyncTimer) clearTimeout(gdriveSyncTimer);
    gdriveSyncTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      gdriveSyncTimer = 0;
      updateSyncStatus("syncing");
      try {
        await syncNotesToGDrive();
        logInfo(`Google Drive auto-sync complete (${reason})`);
      } catch (e) {
        logWarn(`Google Drive auto-sync failed (${reason})`, (e == null ? void 0 : e.message) || e);
        updateSyncStatus("error");
      }
    // 1500ms debounce window: rapid mutations coalesce into a single Drive sync.
    }, 1500);
  }
  // Re-normalize the blocked id Set, persist it to the newest BLOCKED_KEYS entry, and schedule a Drive sync.
  function saveBlockedSet() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveBlockedSet", traceArgs(arguments));
    blocked = new Set(normalizeIdList([...blocked]));
    localStorage.setItem(BLOCKED_KEYS[0], JSON.stringify([...blocked]));
    scheduleDriveSync("blocked");
  }
  // Load the blocked-at-ms map (when each currently-blocked id was hidden), keyed by BLOCKED_AT_KEYS[0].
  // Any id present in `blocked` but missing from the stored map (e.g. hidden before this feature existed,
  // or the map failed to persist) is backfilled to `now()` so a stale reply from before this page load
  // can't immediately auto-unhide them -- only a reply that arrives AFTER this moment will.
  function loadBlockedAtMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadBlockedAtMap", traceArgs(arguments));
    const map = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(BLOCKED_AT_KEYS[0]);
    if (raw) {
      try {
        const obj = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (obj && typeof obj === "object") {
          for (const [id, ts] of Object.entries(obj)) {
            const normId = normalizeProfileId(id);
            const at = parseTimestamp(ts);
            if (normId && at) map.set(normId, at);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    for (const id of blocked) {
      if (!map.has(id)) map.set(id, now());
    }
    return map;
  }
  // Persist the blocked-at-ms map, pruned to only ids currently in `blocked` so it can't grow unbounded.
  function saveBlockedAtMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveBlockedAtMap", traceArgs(arguments));

    for (const [id, ts] of blockedAt) {

    }

  }
  // Normalize categories/tags: accept an array or a comma/newline-separated string; trim, case-insensitively dedupe, cap each to 40 chars and the list to 24 items.
  function normalizeTagValues(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeTagValues", traceArgs(arguments));
    const values = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || "").split(/[\n,]+/);
    const out = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const raw of values) {
      const item = String(raw || "").trim();
      if (!item) continue;
      const key = item.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(item.slice(0, 40));
      // Hard cap of 24 tags/categories per bookmark (each already truncated to 40 chars above).
      if (out.length >= 24) break;
    }
    return out;
  }
  // Parse stored bookmarks (array or object map) into a Map keyed by normalized profileId, filling defaults for url/label/alias/tags and timestamps.
  // Rows without a valid id are skipped; returns empty Map on missing/invalid data.
  function loadBookmarks() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadBookmarks", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(BOOKMARKS_KEY);
    const map = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    if (!raw) return map;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      const rows = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : Object.values(parsed || {});
      for (const row of rows) {
        const id = normalizeProfileId((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileId) || (row == null ? void 0 : row.id));
        if (!id) continue;
        map.set(id, {
          profileId: id,
          profileUrl: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileUrl) || profileUrlForId(id)),
          profileLabel: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileLabel) || id.slice(0, 10)),
          alias: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.alias) || "").trim().slice(0, 80),
          categories: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.categories),
          tags: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.tags),
          lastSeenAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastSeenAt) || 0,
          createdAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.createdAt) || now(),
          updatedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt) || now()
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return map;
  }
  // Serialize the bookmarks Map (normalized fields, alias<=80 chars, capped at 2000 rows, id-less rows dropped) to localStorage.
  // Schedules a Drive sync unless sync=false.
  function saveBookmarks(sync = true) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveBookmarks", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rows = [...bookmarks.values()].map((row) => ({
      profileId: normalizeProfileId(row == null ? void 0 : row.profileId),
      profileUrl: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileUrl) || ""),
      profileLabel: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileLabel) || ""),
      alias: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.alias) || "").trim().slice(0, 80),
      categories: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.categories),
      tags: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.tags),
      lastSeenAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastSeenAt) || 0,
      createdAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.createdAt) || now(),
      updatedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt) || now()
    // Bookmarks are capped at 2000 (2e3) rows on save; rows lacking a profileId are dropped first.
    })).filter((row) => !!row.profileId).slice(0, 2e3);
    localStorage.setItem(BOOKMARKS_KEY, JSON.stringify(rows));
    if (sync) scheduleDriveSync("bookmarks");
  }
  // Return the icon-rules array from the first parseable value across ICON_RULES_KEYS (newest-first); empty array if none.
  // First successfully-parsed JSON value across a newest-first key array (skips absent keys and parse errors),
  // or undefined if none parse. Shared by the simple multi-key-fallback loaders below (loadBlockedSet keeps its
  // own loop because it must skip keys that parse to the wrong shape, not just unparseable ones).
  function readFirstParsedFromKeys(keys) {
    for (const k of keys || []) {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (!raw) continue;
      try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) {}
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  // Coerce a value to an integer star rating clamped to 0-5 (0 on non-finite).
  function normalizeProfileRating(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeProfileRating", traceArgs(arguments));
    const parsed = Math.round(Number(value) || 0);
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed)) return 0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(5, parsed));
  }
  // Load profile ratings, normalizing each key via normalizeProfileId and value via normalizeProfileRating.
  // Drops entries with no valid id or a zero rating; empty object on missing/invalid data.
  function loadProfileRatings() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadProfileRatings", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(RATINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) || {};
      const next = {};
      for (const id of Object.keys(parsed)) {
        const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
        const rating = normalizeProfileRating(parsed[id]);
        if (!normalizedId || !rating) continue;
        next[normalizedId] = rating;
      }
      return next;
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return {};
  }

  // Load per-profile chat-deletion counters, re-keying by normalized id and dropping zero-count/idless rows.
  // Accepts both {count,...} objects and a bare numeric count for each id.
  function loadChatDeletionStats() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadChatDeletionStats", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CHAT_DELETE_STATS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      const out = {};
      for (const [rawId, value] of Object.entries(parsed || {})) {
        const id = normalizeProfileId(rawId);
        const count = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number((value == null ? void 0 : value.count) || value || 0)));
        if (!id || !count) continue;
        out[id] = {
          count,
          lastDeletedTs: parseTimestamp(value == null ? void 0 : value.lastDeletedTs) || 0,
          updatedAt: parseTimestamp(value == null ? void 0 : value.updatedAt) || 0
        };
      }
      return out;
    } catch (e) {
      return {};
    }
  }
  // Rebuild and persist chat-deletion stats, dropping idless/zero-count rows.

  // Load the dedupe set of seen deletion-event fingerprints, dropping any older than CHAT_DELETE_RETENTION_MS (180 days).
  // Returns {} on missing/invalid JSON.
  function loadSeenChatDeletionEvents() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadSeenChatDeletionEvents", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CHAT_DELETE_EVENTS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      const out = {};
      const cutoff = now() - CHAT_DELETE_RETENTION_MS;
      for (const [fingerprint, tsValue] of Object.entries(parsed || {})) {
        const ts = parseTimestamp(tsValue);
        if (!fingerprint || !ts || ts < cutoff) continue;
        out[fingerprint] = ts;
      }
      return out;
    } catch (e) {
      return {};
    }
  }
