  // Debounced flush of deletion stats + seen-events: coalesces bursts into one save after 250ms.
  // No-op if a save timer is already pending.
  function scheduleChatDeletionSave() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleChatDeletionSave", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Coalescing guard: an in-flight timer short-circuits, so a burst of changes produces a single debounced save.
    if (chatDeletionSaveTimer) return;
    chatDeletionSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      chatDeletionSaveTimer = 0;
      saveChatDeletionStats();
      saveSeenChatDeletionEvents();
    }, 250);
  }
  // Coerce a raw pending-auto-message into {id, phrase, ts}, accepting id/profileId and phrase/text aliases.
  // Returns null if id, phrase, or ts is missing/invalid.
  function normalizePendingAutoMessageEntry(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizePendingAutoMessageEntry", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId((value == null ? void 0 : value.id) || (value == null ? void 0 : value.profileId));
    const phrase = String((value == null ? void 0 : value.phrase) || (value == null ? void 0 : value.text) || "").trim();
    const ts = parseTimestamp(value == null ? void 0 : value.ts);
    if (!id || !phrase || !ts) return null;
    return { id, phrase, ts };
  }
  // Load queued auto-messages into an id->entry map, accepting array, single-object, or id-keyed-object shapes.
  // Last write for a given id wins; returns {} on parse error.
  function loadPendingAutoMessages() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadPendingAutoMessages", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_MESSAGE_PENDING_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const map = {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      const rows = [];
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        rows.push(...parsed);
      } else if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsed, "id") || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsed, "profileId")) {
          rows.push(parsed);
        } else {
          rows.push(...Object.values(parsed));
        }
      }
      for (const row of rows) {
        const normalized = normalizePendingAutoMessageEntry(row);
        if (!normalized) continue;
        map[normalized.id] = normalized;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      return {};
    }
    return map;
  }
  // Normalize, sort by ts, and keep the newest 400 pending auto-messages (slice(-400)); removes the key entirely when empty.
  // Side effect: reassigns module-level pendingAutoMessages to the rewritten id-keyed payload.
  function savePendingAutoMessages() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ savePendingAutoMessages", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rows = Object.values(pendingAutoMessages || {}).map(normalizePendingAutoMessageEntry).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => (a.ts || 0) - (b.ts || 0)).slice(-400);
    if (!rows.length) {
      localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_MESSAGE_PENDING_KEY);
      return;
    }
    const payload = {};
    for (const row of rows) payload[row.id] = row;
    pendingAutoMessages = payload;
    localStorage.setItem(AUTO_MESSAGE_PENDING_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
  }
  // Queue an auto-message for a profile (overwriting any prior one for that id) and persist immediately.
  // Returns the stored row, or null if id/phrase is empty.
  function queuePendingAutoMessage(profileId, phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ queuePendingAutoMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const text = String(phrase || "").trim();
    if (!id || !text) return null;
    const row = { id, phrase: text, ts: now() };
    pendingAutoMessages[id] = row;
    savePendingAutoMessages();
    return row;
  }
  // Return the valid pending auto-message for a profile, or null.
  // Side effect: if the stored entry is invalid it is deleted and the map re-saved.
  function readPendingAutoMessage(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ readPendingAutoMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const row = normalizePendingAutoMessageEntry(pendingAutoMessages[id]);
    if (!row) {
      delete pendingAutoMessages[id];
      savePendingAutoMessages();
      return null;
    }
    return row;
  }
  // Atomically read-and-remove a profile's pending auto-message, persisting the deletion.
  // Returns the consumed row, or null if none valid.
  function consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ consumePendingAutoMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const row = readPendingAutoMessage(id);
    if (!row) return null;
    delete pendingAutoMessages[id];
    savePendingAutoMessages();
    return row;
  }
  // Debounced bookmark save (default 1200ms, floored at 300ms); also re-renders the bookmark panel if open.
  // No-op if a save timer is already pending.
  function scheduleSaveBookmarks(delay = 1200) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleSaveBookmarks", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (bookmarkSaveTimer) return;
    bookmarkSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      bookmarkSaveTimer = 0;
      saveBookmarks(false);
      if (state.showBookmarkPanel) renderBookmarkPanel();
    // delay | 0 truncates to int; floored at 300ms so callers can pass a smaller value but never save faster than that.
    }, Math.max(300, delay | 0));
  }
  // Load appointments into a Map keyed by appointment id, deriving atTs from atTs/when/time and filling defaults (profileUrl/label).
  // Drops rows lacking id, normalizable profileId, or a parseable atTs.
  function loadAppointments() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadAppointments", traceArgs(arguments));
    const map = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(APPOINTMENTS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return map;
    try {
      const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return map;
      for (const row of arr) {
        if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
        const id = String(row.id || "");
        const profileId = normalizeProfileId(row.profileId);
        const atTs = parseTimestamp(row.atTs || row.when || row.time);
        if (!id || !profileId || !atTs) continue;
        map.set(id, {
          id,
          profileId,
          profileUrl: row.profileUrl || `https://sniffies.com/profile/${profileId}`,
          profileLabel: row.profileLabel || profileId,
          note: String(row.note || ""),
          atTs,
          createdAt: parseTimestamp(row.createdAt) || now(),
          notifiedApproach: !!row.notifiedApproach,
          notifiedDue: !!row.notifiedDue
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return map;
  }
  // Persist appointments sorted ascending by atTs, keeping the soonest 800 (slice(0,800)).
  function saveAppointments() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveAppointments", traceArgs(arguments));
    const arr = [...appointments.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.atTs || 0) - (b.atTs || 0)).slice(0, 800);
    localStorage.setItem(APPOINTMENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(arr));
  }
  // Extract the first run of 6+ hex chars from any value and lowercase it; returns null if none.
  // Extraction (not validation): pulls the id out of URLs/labels rather than requiring an exact match.
  function normalizeProfileId(v) {
    if (v == null) return null;
    // Matches the first run of 6+ hex chars anywhere in the string (extraction, not full-string validation).
    const m = String(v).match(/[0-9a-f]{6,}/i);
    return m ? m[0].toLowerCase() : null;
  }
  // Convert relative phrases ('now', 'today', 'yesterday', '5m ago', '3 days ago') to an absolute ms timestamp.
  // Months approximated as 30 days and years as 365 days; returns 0 if unrecognized.
  function parseRelativeTimeString(raw) {
    const s = String(raw || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!s) return 0;
    const nowTs = now();
    if (/^(now|just now|moments? ago|a moment ago)$/.test(s)) return nowTs;
    if (/^today$/.test(s)) return nowTs;
    if (/^yesterday$/.test(s)) return nowTs - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3;
    const compact = s.match(/^(\d+)\s*(s|sec|secs|second|seconds|m|min|mins|minute|minutes|h|hr|hrs|hour|hours|d|day|days|w|week|weeks|mo|mos|month|months|y|yr|yrs|year|years)(?:\s*ago)?$/i);
    if (compact) {
      const count = Math.max(0, Number(compact[1]) || 0);
      const unit = String(compact[2] || "").toLowerCase();
      // Unit->seconds multiplier; month approximated as 30 days and year as 365 days. Order matters: 'mo' is tested before bare 'm'/'min'.
      const secMult = unit.startsWith("s") ? 1 : unit === "m" || unit.startsWith("min") || unit.startsWith("minute") ? 60 : unit.startsWith("h") ? 3600 : unit.startsWith("d") ? 86400 : unit.startsWith("w") ? 7 * 86400 : unit.startsWith("mo") ? 30 * 86400 : unit.startsWith("y") || unit.startsWith("yr") ? 365 * 86400 : 0;
      if (secMult > 0) return nowTs - count * secMult * 1e3;
    }
    const verboseAgo = s.match(/^(\d+)\s+(seconds?|minutes?|hours?|days?|weeks?|months?|years?)\s+ago$/i);
    if (verboseAgo) {
      return parseRelativeTimeString(`${verboseAgo[1]} ${verboseAgo[2]}`);
    }
    return 0;
  }
  // Coerce many shapes (Date, ms/sec/microsec number, numeric/ISO/relative string, Firestore {seconds,nanos}, Mongo {$date}) to a ms timestamp.
  // Recurses into common ts/time/date-style keys for objects; returns 0 when unparseable.
  function parseTimestamp(v) {
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e, _f, _g, _h, _i, _j, _k;
    if (v == null) return 0;
    if (v instanceof Date) return Number.isFinite(v.getTime()) ? v.getTime() : 0;
    if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) {
      // Magnitude heuristic: > 1e15 is treated as microseconds, divided by 1000 to milliseconds.
      if (v > 1e15) return Math.floor(v / 1e3);
      // > 1e12 is treated as already-milliseconds (taken as-is).
      if (v > 1e12) return Math.floor(v);
      // > 1e9 is treated as Unix seconds, multiplied by 1000 to milliseconds (anything <= 1e9 returns 0).
      if (v > 1e9) return Math.floor(v * 1e3);
      return 0;
    }
    if (typeof v === "string") {
      const s = v.trim();
      if (!s) return 0;
      // Any all-digit string is an epoch: route it through the numeric magnitude ladder (seconds / ms / µs) so
      // 11- and 12-digit values are interpreted consistently instead of being misread by Date.parse (which treats
      // a bare 12-digit number as a year). This subsumes the old 13- and 10-digit fast paths.
      if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) return parseTimestamp(Number(s));
      const rel = parseRelativeTimeString(s);
      if (rel) return rel;
      const t = Date.parse(s);
      return Number.isFinite(t) ? t : 0;
    }
    if (typeof v === "object") {
      const nested = parseTimestamp(
        (_g = (_f = (_e = (_d = (_c = (_b = (_a = v.ts) != null ? _a : v.time) != null ? _b : v.timestamp) != null ? _c : v.date) != null ? _d : v.createdAt) != null ? _e : v.updatedAt) != null ? _f : v.lastMessageAt) != null ? _g : v.value
      );
      if (nested) return nested;
      const sec = Number((_h = v.seconds) != null ? _h : v._seconds);
      if (Number.isFinite(sec) && sec > 0) {
        // Firestore-style {seconds, nanos} timestamp: combines seconds*1000 + nanos/1e6.
        const nanos = Number((_k = (_j = (_i = v.nanos) != null ? _i : v.nanoseconds) != null ? _j : v._nanoseconds) != null ? _k : 0);
        return Math.floor(sec * 1e3 + (Number.isFinite(nanos) ? nanos / 1e6 : 0));
      }
      // MongoDB extended-JSON {$date} wrapper; recurse on the inner value.
      if (typeof v.$date === "string" || typeof v.$date === "number") {
        return parseTimestamp(v.$date);
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }
  // Strip all non-alphanumeric characters and lowercase a key (loose, punctuation-insensitive comparison).
  function normalizeKey(key) {
    return String(key || "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, "").toLowerCase();
  }
  // Load the set of the user's own profile ids, normalizing each; returns an empty Set on missing/invalid JSON.
  function loadSelfProfileIds() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadSelfProfileIds", traceArgs(arguments));
    const out = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CHAT_SELF_IDS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return out;
    try {
      const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return out;
      for (const v of arr) {
        const id = normalizeProfileId(v);
        if (id) out.add(id);
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return out;
  }
  // Persist the self-profile-id Set as a JSON array to localStorage.
  function saveSelfProfileIds() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveSelfProfileIds", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(CHAT_SELF_IDS_KEY, JSON.stringify([...selfProfileIds]));
  }
  // Add a normalized id to the self-id set and persist, but only if new (skips the write when already present).
  function rememberSelfProfileId(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ rememberSelfProfileId", traceArgs(arguments));
    const normalized = normalizeProfileId(id);
    if (!normalized || selfProfileIds.has(normalized)) return;
    selfProfileIds.add(normalized);
    saveSelfProfileIds();
  }
  // Load per-profile chat activity into a Map of {myLastTs, theirLastTs, anyLastTs, updatedAt}, accepting short key aliases.
  // Drops entries with no usable timestamp; returns an empty Map on error.
  function loadChatActivity() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadChatActivity", traceArgs(arguments));
    const map = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CHAT_ACTIVITY_KEY);
    if (!raw) return map;
    try {
      const obj = JSON.parse(raw) || {};
      for (const [rawId, val] of Object.entries(obj)) {
        const id = normalizeProfileId(rawId);
        if (!id || !val || typeof val !== "object") continue;
        const myLastTs = parseTimestamp(val.myLastTs || val.my);
        const theirLastTs = parseTimestamp(val.theirLastTs || val.their);
        const anyLastTs = parseTimestamp(val.anyLastTs || val.any || val.lastTs || val.lastMessageTs);
        // Preserve the stored write-time; do NOT synthesize now() on load, or stale entries would be
        // rescued from pruning every reload (pruneChatActivity folds updatedAt into its retention max).
        const updatedAt = parseTimestamp(val.updatedAt || val.updated) || 0;
        if (!myLastTs && !theirLastTs && !anyLastTs) continue;
        map.set(id, {
          myLastTs,
          theirLastTs,
          anyLastTs,
          // Running count of consecutive outbound messages with no reply (see
          // bumpUnansweredOut). Persisted so the "sent N, no reply" hide survives reloads.
          outRun: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(val.outRun) || 0)),
          updatedAt
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return map;
  }
  // Serialize the chatActivity Map to a plain object and persist it, skipping entries with no timestamp.
  // Defaults updatedAt to now() when absent.
  function saveChatActivity() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveChatActivity", traceArgs(arguments));
    const out = {};
    for (const [id, entry] of chatActivity.entries()) {
      if (!entry) continue;
      const myLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry.myLastTs);
      const theirLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry.theirLastTs);
      const anyLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry.anyLastTs);
      if (!myLastTs && !theirLastTs && !anyLastTs) continue;
      out[id] = {
        myLastTs,
        theirLastTs,
        anyLastTs,
        outRun: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(entry.outRun) || 0)),
        updatedAt: parseTimestamp(entry.updatedAt) || 0
      };
    }
    localStorage.setItem(CHAT_ACTIVITY_KEY, JSON.stringify(out));
  }
  // Debounced chat-activity flush: coalesces writes into one save after 250ms.
  // No-op if a save timer is already pending.
  function scheduleChatActivitySave() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleChatActivitySave", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatSaveTimer) return;
    chatSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      chatSaveTimer = 0;
      saveChatActivity();
    }, 250);
  }
  // Drop chat-activity entries whose latest timestamp is older than CHAT_ACTIVITY_TTL_MS (120 days).
  // Saves only if something was removed.
  function pruneChatActivity() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pruneChatActivity", traceArgs(arguments));
    const cutoff = now() - CHAT_ACTIVITY_TTL_MS;
    let changed = false;
    for (const [id, entry] of chatActivity.entries()) {
      const myLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.myLastTs);
      const theirLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.theirLastTs);
      const anyLastTs = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.anyLastTs);
      const latestTs = Math.max(myLastTs || 0, theirLastTs || 0, anyLastTs || 0, parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.updatedAt) || 0);
      if (!latestTs || latestTs < cutoff) {
        chatActivity.delete(id);
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    // Hard entry cap (mirrors the slice caps on bookmarks/appointments): bounds localStorage size and the cost of
    // the full-object re-serialize on each debounced save. Keep the most-recent CHAT_ACTIVITY_MAX_ENTRIES by latest ts.
    const CHAT_ACTIVITY_MAX_ENTRIES = 5e3;
    if (chatActivity.size > CHAT_ACTIVITY_MAX_ENTRIES) {
      const latestOf = (entry) => Math.max(
        parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.myLastTs) || 0,
        parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.theirLastTs) || 0,
        parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.anyLastTs) || 0,
        parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.updatedAt) || 0
      );
      const ranked = [...chatActivity.entries()].sort((a, b) => latestOf(b[1]) - latestOf(a[1]));
      for (let i = CHAT_ACTIVITY_MAX_ENTRIES; i < ranked.length; i += 1) {
        chatActivity.delete(ranked[i][0]);
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (changed) saveChatActivity();
  }
  // Merge new my/their message times into a profile's chat-activity record (max-wins, monotonic).
  // Returns false on no-op (replay-safe); on a real change schedules a save and a chat-badge refresh.
  function upsertChatActivity(profileId, myTs = 0, theirTs = 0, anyTs = 0) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ upsertChatActivity", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const myLastTs = parseTimestamp(myTs);
    const theirLastTs = parseTimestamp(theirTs);
    // anyOnlyTs: a direction-less activity time. It raises anyLastTs (and thus the chat-age badge) WITHOUT
    // touching myLastTs/theirLastTs, so the strict their>mine reply-unhide test stays meaningful.
    const anyOnlyTs = parseTimestamp(anyTs);
    if (!id || !myLastTs && !theirLastTs && !anyOnlyTs) return false;
    const prev = chatActivity.get(id) || { myLastTs: 0, theirLastTs: 0, anyLastTs: 0, updatedAt: 0 };
    const nextMy = Math.max(prev.myLastTs || 0, myLastTs || 0);
    const nextTheir = Math.max(prev.theirLastTs || 0, theirLastTs || 0);
    // anyLastTs is the running max across prior any and both directions (plus any direction-less time), so a replayed older payload can never lower it.
    const nextAny = Math.max(prev.anyLastTs || 0, nextMy || 0, nextTheir || 0, myLastTs || 0, theirLastTs || 0, anyOnlyTs || 0);
    if (nextMy === (prev.myLastTs || 0) && nextTheir === (prev.theirLastTs || 0) && nextAny === (prev.anyLastTs || 0)) return false;
    chatActivity.set(id, {
      // Spread prev first so non-timestamp fields (e.g. outRun) survive the merge.
      ...prev,
      myLastTs: nextMy,
      theirLastTs: nextTheir,
      anyLastTs: nextAny,
      updatedAt: now()
    });
    scheduleChatActivitySave();
    scheduleChatBadgeRefresh();
    return true;
  }
  // Increment a profile's consecutive-unanswered-outbound counter by one. Call ONLY
  // when a genuinely new outbound message was recorded (i.e. upsertChatActivity
  // reported a change), so replayed/re-fetched copies of the same message — which
  // don't advance myLastTs — never inflate the count. Persisted via the chat-activity
  // save so the "sent N, no reply" hide survives reloads.
  function bumpUnansweredOut(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ bumpUnansweredOut", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const entry = id && chatActivity.get(id);
    if (!entry) return;
    entry.outRun = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(entry.outRun) || 0)) + 1;
    scheduleChatActivitySave();
  }
  // Reset a profile's unanswered-outbound counter to zero — called when they reply
  // (an inbound message), which is what un-hides them. No-op (and no save) if already 0.
  function resetUnansweredOut(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ resetUnansweredOut", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const entry = id && chatActivity.get(id);
    if (!entry || !entry.outRun) return;
    entry.outRun = 0;
    scheduleChatActivitySave();
  }
  // Record a profile's last-active time in the in-memory map only if strictly newer than the stored value.
  // Returns true when updated; not persisted to localStorage.
  function recordProfileLastActive(profileId, ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordProfileLastActive", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const at = parseTimestamp(ts) || 0;
    if (!id || !at) return false;
    const prev = profileLastActive.get(id) || 0;
    if (at <= prev) return false;
    profileLastActive.set(id, at);
    return true;
  }
  // Return the in-memory last-active ms timestamp for a profile, or 0 if unknown.
  function getProfileLastActiveTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileLastActiveTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    return profileLastActive.get(id) || 0;
  }
  // Evict last-active entries older than LAST_ACTIVE_TTL_MS (24 hours) from the in-memory map.
  function pruneProfileLastActive() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pruneProfileLastActive", traceArgs(arguments));
    const cutoff = now() - LAST_ACTIVE_TTL_MS;
    for (const [id, ts] of profileLastActive.entries()) {
      if (!ts || ts < cutoff) profileLastActive.delete(id);
    }
  }
  // Load the API rate-limit record {windowStart, count, cooldownUntil}; returns zeroed defaults on missing/invalid JSON.
  function loadRate() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadRate", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(RATE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { windowStart: 0, count: 0, cooldownUntil: 0 };
    try {
      const r = JSON.parse(raw);
      return {
        windowStart: r.windowStart || 0,
        count: r.count || 0,
        cooldownUntil: r.cooldownUntil || 0
      };
    } catch (e) {
      return { windowStart: 0, count: 0, cooldownUntil: 0 };
    }
  }
  // Persist the rate-limit record to localStorage.
  function saveRate(rate) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveRate", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(RATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(rate));
  }
  // Read the rate record, resetting the count when the 1-minute window has elapsed (persists the reset).
  // Returns {rate, cooldownMs, remaining} against MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MIN (6).
  function getRateSnapshot() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getRateSnapshot", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rate = loadRate();
    const t = now();
    // Rolling 1-minute window: once 60s have passed since windowStart, the request count is reset to 0.
    if (t - rate.windowStart > 60 * 1e3) {
      rate.windowStart = t;
      rate.count = 0;
      saveRate(rate);
    }
    // Clamp to at most COOLDOWN_MS: a legitimate cooldown never exceeds the configured window, so a clock skew
    // that wrote a far-future cooldownUntil can't pin the script into a permanent cooldown with no recovery.
    const cooldownMs = Math.min(COOLDOWN_MS, Math.max(0, (rate.cooldownUntil || 0) - t));
    const remaining = Math.max(0, MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MIN - (rate.count || 0));
    return { rate, cooldownMs, remaining };
  }
  // Format a millisecond duration as MM:SS (ceil to whole seconds, clamped at 0).
  function formatTimer(ms) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatTimer", traceArgs(arguments));
    const total = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms / 1e3));
    const mm = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
    const ss = String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${mm}:${ss}`;
  }
  // Refresh the rate-status pill text/'cooldown' class from the current rate snapshot.
  // No-ops if rateStatusEl isn't mounted; shows cooldown timer or remaining req/min budget.
  function updateRateCounter() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateRateCounter", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!rateStatusEl) return;
    const snap = getRateSnapshot();
    if (snap.cooldownMs > 0) {
      // \xB7 is a middot (·) separator in the status pill text.
      rateStatusEl.textContent = `API cooldown ${formatTimer(snap.cooldownMs)} \xB7 ${snap.remaining}/${MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MIN} req left`;
      rateStatusEl.classList.add("cooldown");
    } else {
      rateStatusEl.textContent = `API budget ${snap.remaining}/${MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MIN} req/min`;
      rateStatusEl.classList.remove("cooldown");
    }
  }
  // True when not in cooldown and budget remains; also refreshes the status pill as a side effect.
  function canRequest() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ canRequest", traceArgs(arguments));
    const snap = getRateSnapshot();
    const allowed = snap.cooldownMs <= 0 && snap.remaining > 0;
    updateRateCounter();
    return allowed;
  }
  // Record one API request against the rolling 60s window (resetting the window if expired) and persist.
  // Side effect: writes rate to localStorage and updates the status pill.
  function noteRequest() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ noteRequest", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rate = loadRate();
    const t = now();
    // Rolling 60s budget window: reset count once the window older than 60000ms.
    if (t - rate.windowStart > 60 * 1e3) {
      rate.windowStart = t;
      rate.count = 0;
    }
    rate.count += 1;
    saveRate(rate);
    updateRateCounter();
  }
  // Start a cooldown (now + COOLDOWN_MS) after hitting a server rate limit; persists and refreshes the pill.
  function noteRateLimit() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ noteRateLimit", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rate = loadRate();
    rate.cooldownUntil = now() + COOLDOWN_MS;
    saveRate(rate);
    updateRateCounter();
  }
  // Render an age relative to now as a terse token: now / Nm / Nh / Nd / Nw / Nmo / Ny.
  // Returns '' for unparseable timestamps; months approximated at 30d, years at 365d.
  function formatAgeShort(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatAgeShort", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!t) return "";
    let delta = Math.max(0, now() - t);
    const sec = Math.floor(delta / 1e3);
    if (sec < 60) return "now";
    const min = Math.floor(sec / 60);
    if (min < 60) return `${min}m`;
    const hr = Math.floor(min / 60);
    if (hr < 24) return `${hr}h`;
    const day = Math.floor(hr / 24);
    if (day < 7) return `${day}d`;
    const week = Math.floor(day / 7);
    if (week < 5) return `${week}w`;
    const month = Math.floor(day / 30);
    if (month < 12) return `${month}mo`;
    const year = Math.floor(day / 365);
    return `${year}y`;
  }
  // Build the floatingChatBadges map key '<normalizedId>:<side>'; side coerced to left|right (defaults right).
  function chatBadgeKey(id, side) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ chatBadgeKey", traceArgs(arguments));
    return `${normalizeProfileId(id) || String(id || "unknown")}:${side === "left" ? "left" : "right"}`;
  }
  // Get-or-create the floating chat-age badge div for an id+side, appended to document.body (hidden).
  // Reuses the cached node only if still attached; tracks it in the floatingChatBadges map.
  function ensureFloatingChatBadge(id, side) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ ensureFloatingChatBadge", traceArgs(arguments));
    const key = chatBadgeKey(id, side);
    const existing = floatingChatBadges.get(key);
    if (existing && document.body.contains(existing)) return existing;
    const badge = document.createElement("div");
    badge.className = `sniffies-chat-age-float sniffies-chat-age-float-${side === "left" ? "left" : "right"}`;
    badge.dataset.badgeKey = key;
    badge.style.display = "none";
    document.body.appendChild(badge);
    floatingChatBadges.set(key, badge);
    return badge;
  }
  // Hide (display:none) the floating badge for a given id+side if it exists; does not remove it.
  function hideFloatingChatBadge(id, side) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideFloatingChatBadge", traceArgs(arguments));
    const key = chatBadgeKey(id, side);
    const badge = floatingChatBadges.get(key);
    if (!badge) return;
    badge.style.display = "none";
  }
  // Hide both the left and right floating badges for a profile id.
  function hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId", traceArgs(arguments));
    hideFloatingChatBadge(id, "left");
    hideFloatingChatBadge(id, "right");
  }
  // Drop floatingChatBadges entries whose node is missing or no longer in the DOM.
  function pruneFloatingChatBadges() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pruneFloatingChatBadges", traceArgs(arguments));
    for (const [key, badge] of floatingChatBadges.entries()) {
      if (!badge || !document.body.contains(badge)) {
        floatingChatBadges.delete(key);
      }
    }
  }
  // Pick the smallest sensible on-screen rect to anchor a badge to, from the marker/root and inner avatar/container/profile-link candidates.
  // Ignores rects under 8px or outside the 64-25000 px area band; falls back to root/marker bounding rect.
  function pickBestMarkerAnchorRect(marker, root) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pickBestMarkerAnchorRect", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e, _f, _g, _h, _i, _j;
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const candidates = [];
    // Local helper of pickBestMarkerAnchorRect: add an Element to the candidate list, de-duped via the seen Set.
    const push = (el) => {
      log("→ push");
      if (!el || !(el instanceof Element) || seen.has(el)) return;
      seen.add(el);
      candidates.push(el);
    };
    push(marker);
    push(root);
    push((_a = marker == null ? void 0 : marker.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(marker, ".marker-avatar-image"));
    push((_b = root == null ? void 0 : root.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(root, ".marker-avatar-image"));
    push((_c = marker == null ? void 0 : marker.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _c.call(marker, ".marker-avatar"));
    push((_d = root == null ? void 0 : root.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _d.call(root, ".marker-avatar"));

    }
  }
  // Place a badge at the vertical center of the anchor rect, 6px outside its left/right edge.
  // Returns false (caller hides badge) when no rect, rect too small, or anchor is fully off-screen.
  function positionFloatingChatBadge(badge, marker, root, side) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ positionFloatingChatBadge", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!badge || !root) return false;
    const rect = pickBestMarkerAnchorRect(marker, root);
    if (!rect || rect.width < 4 || rect.height < 4) return false;
    if (rect.bottom < 0 || rect.top > window.innerHeight || rect.right < 0 || rect.left > window.innerWidth) {
      return false;
    }
    const top = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    const left = side === "left" ? rect.left - 6 : rect.right + 6;
    badge.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
    badge.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
    return true;
  }
  // Compute and position the single left-side chat-age badge for one marker from chatActivity (or a howdy/preview fallback).
  // Hides everything when root missing/hidden or showChatAges off; prefers their-last, then my-last, then any-last; always removes any legacy right-side badge.
  function updateMarkerChatBadge(id, marker) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateMarkerChatBadge", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(marker);
    const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id) || String(id || "");
    if (!root) {
      hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(normalizedId);
      return;
    }
    const rootStyle = getComputedStyle(root);
    const rootHidden = ((_a = root.classList) == null ? void 0 : _a.contains(HIDE_CLASS)) || rootStyle.display === "none" || rootStyle.visibility === "hidden";
    if (rootHidden) {
      hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(normalizedId);
      return;
    }
    let entry = chatActivity.get(normalizedId) || null;
    if (!entry) {
      const fallbackTs = Math.max(
        parseTimestamp(howdySentAt[normalizedId]) || 0,
        parseTimestamp((_b = (chatPreviewByProfile.get(normalizedId) || []).slice(-1)[0]) == null ? void 0 : _b.ts) || 0
      );
      if (fallbackTs) {
        entry = { myLastTs: fallbackTs, theirLastTs: 0, anyLastTs: fallbackTs, updatedAt: fallbackTs };
      }
    }
    if (!state.showChatAges || !entry) {
      hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(normalizedId);
      return;
    }
    const theirTs = parseTimestamp(entry.theirLastTs) || 0;
    const myTs = parseTimestamp(entry.myLastTs) || 0;
    const anyTs = parseTimestamp(entry.anyLastTs) || Math.max(theirTs, myTs);
    const left = ensureFloatingChatBadge(normalizedId, "left");
    const rightKey = chatBadgeKey(normalizedId, "right");
    const right = floatingChatBadges.get(rightKey);
    if (right) {
      try {
        right.remove();
      } catch (e) {
      }
      floatingChatBadges.delete(rightKey);
    }
    let label = "";
    let title = "";
    if (theirTs) {
      // \u2190 is the left-arrow glyph; badge label shows '<- age' for the last message received from them.
      label = `\u2190 ${formatAgeShort(theirTs)}`;
      title = `Last message from them: ${new Date(theirTs).toLocaleString()}`;
    } else if (myTs) {
      label = `\u2192 ${formatAgeShort(myTs)}`;
      title = `Last message from you: ${new Date(myTs).toLocaleString()}`;
    } else if (anyTs) {
      label = `~ ${formatAgeShort(anyTs)}`;
      title = `Last chat activity: ${new Date(anyTs).toLocaleString()}`;
    }
    if (label) {
      left.textContent = label;
      left.title = title;
      left.style.display = positionFloatingChatBadge(left, marker, root, "left") ? "block" : "none";
    } else {
      left.style.display = "none";
    }
  }
  // Rebuild all chat-age badges: purge legacy ':right' badges, prune dead entries, re-run updateMarkerChatBadge for every live marker in idToMarker.
  function refreshChatAgeBadges() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ refreshChatAgeBadges", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    for (const [key, badge] of floatingChatBadges.entries()) {
      // Only legacy ':right' badges are purged here; the right side was retired in favor of a single left badge.
      if (!String(key).endsWith(":right")) continue;
      try {
        (_a = badge == null ? void 0 : badge.remove) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(badge);
      } catch (e) {
      }
      floatingChatBadges.delete(key);
    }
    pruneFloatingChatBadges();
    for (const [id, marker] of idToMarker.entries()) {
      if (!marker || !document.body.contains(marker)) continue;
      updateMarkerChatBadge(id, marker);
    }
  }
  // Debounce a chat-age badge rebuild via a 120ms timer; coalesces bursts (guarded by chatBadgeRefreshTimer).
  function scheduleChatBadgeRefresh() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleChatBadgeRefresh", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatBadgeRefreshTimer) return;
    chatBadgeRefreshTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      chatBadgeRefreshTimer = 0;
      refreshChatAgeBadges();
    // 120ms debounce window for coalescing chat-age badge rebuilds.
    }, 120);
  }
  // Collapse whitespace, trim, and cap at 240 chars; returns '' for empty input.
  function sanitizeMessageText(text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sanitizeMessageText", traceArgs(arguments));
    const value = String(text || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!value) return "";
    return value.slice(0, 240);
  }
  // Collapse whitespace, trim, and cap at maxLen (clamped to a 24-char floor; default 240).
  function cleanDisplayText(text, maxLen = 240) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ cleanDisplayText", traceArgs(arguments));
    const value = String(text || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!value) return "";
    return value.slice(0, Math.max(24, maxLen | 0));
  }
  // Normalize an image URL to absolute https: passes http(s) through, prefixes // and root-relative /paths with the sniffies host.
  // Returns '' for anything else (e.g. data: or relative paths).
  function sanitizeImageUrl(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sanitizeImageUrl", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return "";
    if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return text;
    if (/^\/\//.test(text)) return `https:${text}`;
    // Matches a root-relative path (single leading slash, not //) and prefixes the sniffies origin.
    if (/^\/[^/]/.test(text)) return `https://sniffies.com${text}`;
    return "";
  }
  // Turn an array or newline string into a de-duped (case-insensitive) list of cleaned items, max 8, each <=120 chars.
  function normalizePreferenceList(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizePreferenceList", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || "").split(/\n+/);
    const out = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const item of raw) {
      const clean = cleanDisplayText(item, 120);
      if (!clean) continue;
      const key = clean.toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(clean);
      if (out.length >= 8) break;
    }
    return out;
  }
  // Append a sanitized chat-preview row (direction you|them|unknown) to a profile's history, kept sorted and capped at AUTO_MESSAGE_PREVIEW_LIMIT.
  // Skips near-duplicates (same dir/text within 2min); if an outgoing msg matches a canned phrase, marks that phrase used. Returns false when dropped.
  function addChatPreviewMessage(profileId, direction, ts, text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ addChatPreviewMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const safeText = sanitizeMessageText(text);
    const safeTs = parseTimestamp(ts) || now();
    if (!id || !safeText) return false;
    const current = chatPreviewByProfile.get(id) || [];
    const normalizedDirection = direction === "out" ? "you" : direction === "in" ? "them" : "unknown";
    const duplicate = current.some(
      // De-dupe guard: same direction+text within 120000ms (2min) is treated as the same message.
      (row) => row && row.direction === normalizedDirection && row.text === safeText && Math.abs((row.ts || 0) - safeTs) < 12e4
    );
    if (duplicate) return false;
    current.push({ direction: normalizedDirection, ts: safeTs, text: safeText });
    current.sort((a, b) => (a.ts || 0) - (b.ts || 0));
    while (current.length > AUTO_MESSAGE_PREVIEW_LIMIT) current.shift();
    chatPreviewByProfile.set(id, current);
    if (normalizedDirection === "you") {
      const canned = new Set(getCannedPhrases().map((phrase) => String(phrase).toLowerCase()));
      if (canned.has(safeText.toLowerCase())) {
        markPhraseUsedForProfile(id, safeText);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
  // Format a timestamp as a short local time (h:mm); returns '' if unparseable.
  function formatPreviewTimestamp(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatPreviewTimestamp", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!t) return "";
    return new Date(t).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" });
  }
  // Remove the chat-preview hover bubble from the DOM and clear chatPreviewEl.
  function hideChatPreviewBubble() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideChatPreviewBubble", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatPreviewEl) {

    }
  }
  // Build and show the recent-chat hover bubble (newest-first) anchored to a marker; no-op if no stored messages.
  // Positioned to the marker's right, flips left and clamps vertically to stay on-screen; replaces any existing bubble.
  function showChatPreviewBubble(profileId, markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ showChatPreviewBubble", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id || !markerEl) return;
    const messages = (chatPreviewByProfile.get(id) || []).slice(-AUTO_MESSAGE_PREVIEW_LIMIT);
    if (!messages.length) {
      hideChatPreviewBubble();
      return;
    }
    hideChatPreviewBubble();
    const bubble = document.createElement("div");
    bubble.className = "sniffies-chat-preview";
    const head = document.createElement("div");
    head.className = "head";
    head.textContent = `Recent chat (${messages.length})`;
    bubble.appendChild(head);
    for (const row of messages.slice().reverse()) {
      const cls = row.direction === "you" ? "you" : row.direction === "them" ? "them" : "";
      const who = row.direction === "you" ? "You" : row.direction === "them" ? "Them" : "Msg";
      const time = formatPreviewTimestamp(row.ts);
      const msg = document.createElement("div");
      msg.className = `msg ${cls}`.trim();
      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "meta";
      meta.textContent = `${who}${time ? ` \u2022 ${time}` : ""}`;
      const body = document.createElement("div");
      body.textContent = row.text;
      msg.appendChild(meta);
      msg.appendChild(body);
      bubble.appendChild(msg);
    }
    document.body.appendChild(bubble);
    const rect = resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl).getBoundingClientRect();
    const bubbleRect = bubble.getBoundingClientRect();
    const top = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerHeight - bubbleRect.height - 8, rect.top + rect.height / 2 - bubbleRect.height / 2));
    let left = rect.right + 10;
    if (left + bubbleRect.width > window.innerWidth - 8) {
      left = Math.max(8, rect.left - bubbleRect.width - 10);
    }
    bubble.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
    bubble.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
    chatPreviewEl = bubble;
  }
  // Attach passive mouseenter/leave listeners to a marker root to show/hide the chat-preview bubble.
  // Idempotent per root via the markerHoverBindings WeakMap-style map; binds to resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl).
  function ensureMarkerHoverBindings(id, markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ ensureMarkerHoverBindings", traceArgs(arguments));
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl);
    if (!root) return;
    if (markerHoverBindings.has(root)) return;
    // Local mouseenter handler of ensureMarkerHoverBindings: show the chat-preview bubble for this marker.
    const onEnter = () => showChatPreviewBubble(id, root);
    // Local mouseleave handler of ensureMarkerHoverBindings: hide the chat-preview bubble.
    const onLeave = () => hideChatPreviewBubble();
    root.addEventListener("mouseenter", onEnter, { passive: true });
    root.addEventListener("mouseleave", onLeave, { passive: true });
    markerHoverBindings.set(root, { onEnter, onLeave });
  }
  // Detect whether a path is a chat view: /profile/<hex>/chat or /global-chat always true.
  // A bare /profile/<hex> counts only if a chat input element is currently present in the DOM.
  function isChatRoute(pathname = location.pathname) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isChatRoute", traceArgs(arguments));
    const path = String(pathname || "");
    // Profile id is a hex string of >=6 chars; this matches the /profile/<id>/chat sub-route.
    if (/\/profile\/[0-9a-f]{6,}\/chat/i.test(path)) return true;
    if (/\/global-chat/i.test(path)) return true;
    if (/\/profile\/[0-9a-f]{6,}/i.test(path)) return !!findChatInputElement();
    return false;
  }

    }

  }
  // Best-effort extract a phrase from a context/right-click event: live selection, then editable selection, then nearest message/ancestor text.

  function extractPhraseCandidateFromContextEvent(e) {

  // Sanitize and stash the latest quick-phrase candidate (lastQuickPhraseCandidate) and mirror it to window.__sniffiesLastQuickPhraseCandidate with source/ts.
  // Returns '' (and stores nothing) for text under 2 chars.
  function rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(raw, source = "unknown") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ rememberQuickPhraseCandidate", traceArgs(arguments));
    const clean = sanitizeMessageText(raw);
    if (clean.length < 2) return "";
    lastQuickPhraseCandidate = clean;
    exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastQuickPhraseCandidate", {
      text: clean,
      source: String(source || "unknown"),
      ts: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
    });
    return clean;
  }
  // From a menu's text block, pick the longest 2-240 char line that isn't a UI action label (copy/react/reply/delete/etc).
  // Returns '' if nothing qualifies.
  function extractPhraseFromMenuTextBlock(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractPhraseFromMenuTextBlock", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(raw || "");
    if (!text.trim()) return "";
    // Filters out menu/UI action labels so only real message lines (2-240 chars) survive as phrase candidates.
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => sanitizeMessageText(line)).filter(Boolean).filter((line) => line.length >= 2 && line.length <= 240).filter((line) => !/^(copy|copy text|react|reply|delete|report|hide|cancel|block|mute|save selected|new quick phrase|add phrase)/i.test(line));
    if (!lines.length) return "";
    lines.sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
    return lines[0] || "";
  }
  // Walk up from a copy button (its dialog/popover/menu ancestor, then up to 5 parents) and return the first usable phrase from their text blocks.
  function extractPhraseNearCopyButton(el) {

    if (dialog) roots.push(dialog);
    let node = el.parentElement;
    for (let i = 0; node && i < 5; i += 1) {
      roots.push(node);
      node = node.parentElement;
    }

      if (!root || seen.has(root)) continue;
      seen.add(root);
      const guess = extractPhraseFromMenuTextBlock(root.innerText || root.textContent || "");
      if (guess) return guess;
    }
    return "";
  }
  // One-time monkey-patch of navigator.clipboard.writeText and Document.prototype.execCommand to capture copied text as quick-phrase candidates.
  // Wrapped in try/catch (frozen-intrinsics safe); execCommand('copy') snapshots the selection on a 0ms timer; guarded by quickPhraseCaptureHooksInstalled.
  function installQuickPhraseCaptureHooks() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ installQuickPhraseCaptureHooks", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (quickPhraseCaptureHooksInstalled) return;
    quickPhraseCaptureHooksInstalled = true;
    try {
      const clip = navigator.clipboard;
      if (clip && typeof clip.writeText === "function") {
        const originalWriteText = clip.writeText.bind(clip);

        clip.writeText = async function patchedClipboardWriteText(text) {
          rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(text, "clipboard.writeText");
          return originalWriteText(text);
        };
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    try {
      const originalExecCommand = Document.prototype.execCommand;

        Document.prototype.execCommand = function patchedExecCommand(command, ...args) {
          const out = originalExecCommand.call(this, command, ...args);

              rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(((_b = (_a = window.getSelection) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(window)) == null ? void 0 : _b.toString()) || "", "execCommand.copy");
            }, 0);
          }
          return out;
        };
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
  }
  // Async-resolve a quick phrase by precedence: preferred arg, cached candidate, window selection, active-editable selection, then clipboard.readText().
  // Each hit is re-stored via rememberQuickPhraseCandidate; returns '' if all sources fail.
  async function resolveQuickPhraseCandidate(preferred = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ resolveQuickPhraseCandidate", traceArgs(arguments));

    const editableSelected = getSelectedTextFromEditable(document.activeElement);

      try {
        const clip = sanitizeMessageText(await navigator.clipboard.readText());
        if (clip.length >= 2) {
          return rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(clip, "clipboard.readText");
        }
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    return "";
  }

  // Heuristic: does the element's nearest control look like a 'copy text/message' button by aria-label/title/testid/text/class.
  // Excludes this script's own phrase menu; matches the copy-text/copy-message/copy regex.
  function isLikelyCopyTextButton(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isLikelyCopyTextButton", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el || !(el instanceof Element)) return false;
    const ctl = el.closest("button, [role='button'], [aria-label], [title], [data-testid], [class]");
    if (!ctl) return false;
    const label = [
      ctl.getAttribute("aria-label"),
      ctl.getAttribute("title"),
      ctl.getAttribute("data-testid"),
      ctl.getAttribute("data-test"),
      ctl.getAttribute("data-qa"),
      ctl.getAttribute("name"),
      ctl.textContent,
      ctl.className
    ].map((v) => String(v || "").toLowerCase()).join(" ");
    if (!label) return false;
    if (label.includes("sniffies-chat-phrase-menu")) return false;
    // Matches 'copy text', 'copy message', or a standalone 'copy' word in the control's labels.
    return /copy[\s_-]*text|copy[\s_-]*message|copy\b/.test(label);
  }
  // Click handler: when a site 'Copy Text' button is clicked in chat, snapshot the nearby phrase as a quick-phrase candidate.
  // Captures synchronously, then re-resolves 80ms later (clipboard/selection may only settle after the site finishes its copy).
  function handleSniffiesCopyDialogClick(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleSniffiesCopyDialogClick", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isChatRoute()) return;
    const target = (e == null ? void 0 : e.target) instanceof Element ? e.target : null;
    if (!target) return;
    if (!isLikelyCopyTextButton(target)) return;
    const immediate = extractPhraseCandidateFromContextEvent({ target }) || extractPhraseNearCopyButton(target);
    if (immediate.length >= 2) rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(immediate, "copy-dialog-click");
    setTimeout(async () => {
      const resolved = await resolveQuickPhraseCandidate(immediate);
      if (resolved.length >= 2) {
        rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(resolved, "copy-dialog-resolved");
        logInfo("Captured phrase candidate from copy dialog");
      }
    // 80ms delay lets the site's own copy/clipboard write finish before re-resolving the phrase candidate.
    }, 80);
  }
  // Builds and shows the custom 'save phrase' floating menu (save-to-Other/Intro, plus a New-phrase input) at x,y.
  // Pre-remembers the passed phrase, then clamps the menu inside the viewport (8px margin); stores el in chatPhraseContextMenuEl.
  function showChatPhraseContextMenu(x, y, phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ showChatPhraseContextMenu", traceArgs(arguments));
    hideChatPhraseContextMenu();

    newBtn.className = "btn sf-new";
    newBtn.textContent = "New quick phrase...";
    menu.appendChild(newBtn);
    const newWrap = document.createElement("div");

    newWrap.id = "sfQuickPhraseNewWrap";
    const newInput = document.createElement("input");
    newInput.type = "text";
    newInput.className = "new-input";
    newInput.id = "sfQuickPhraseNewInput";
    newInput.maxLength = 240;
    newInput.placeholder = "Type quick phrase";
