    addIntroBtn.type = "button";
    addIntroBtn.className = "btn sf-add-intro";
    addIntroBtn.textContent = "Add to Intro";
    newWrap.appendChild(newInput);
    newWrap.appendChild(addOtherBtn);
    newWrap.appendChild(addIntroBtn);
    menu.appendChild(newWrap);
    let saveInProgress = false;
    // Curried (category) => async(ev) handler for the menu's Save buttons; resolves the live phrase then saves it to that category.
    // If nothing resolves, opens the inline New-phrase input prefilled with the last candidate; saveInProgress guards re-entrancy.
    const runSaveFromSelection = (category) => async (ev) => {
      var _a;
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (saveInProgress) return;
      saveInProgress = true;
      try {
        const resolved = await resolveQuickPhraseCandidate(clean);
        if (!resolved) {
          setStatus("No phrase found: highlight text or use Copy Text first");
          if (newWrap && !newWrap.classList.contains("open")) {
            newWrap.classList.add("open");
            if (newInput) {
              newInput.value = sanitizeMessageText(lastQuickPhraseCandidate || "");
              newInput.focus();
              (_a = newInput.select) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(newInput);
            }
          }
          return;
        }
        saveQuickPhraseFromText(resolved, category);
        hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
      } finally {
        saveInProgress = false;
      }
    };
    const saveOther = runSaveFromSelection("other");
    const saveIntro = runSaveFromSelection("intro");
    saveOtherBtn == null ? void 0 : saveOtherBtn.addEventListener("click", saveOther);
    saveIntroBtn == null ? void 0 : saveIntroBtn.addEventListener("click", saveIntro);
    newBtn == null ? void 0 : newBtn.addEventListener("click", (ev) => {
      var _a;
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (!newWrap) return;
      newWrap.classList.toggle("open");
      if (newWrap.classList.contains("open")) {
        if (newInput) {
          if (!newInput.value) newInput.value = sanitizeMessageText(lastQuickPhraseCandidate || "");
          newInput.focus();
          (_a = newInput.select) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(newInput);
        }
      }
    });
    // Curried (category) => (ev) handler for the New-phrase 'Add' buttons; saves the sanitized input value to that category and closes the menu.
    const addTypedPhrase = (category) => (ev) => {
      if (ev) {
        ev.preventDefault();
        ev.stopPropagation();
      }
      const typed = sanitizeMessageText((newInput == null ? void 0 : newInput.value) || "");
      if (!typed) {
        setStatus("Type a phrase first");
        return;
      }
      saveQuickPhraseFromText(typed, category);
      hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
    };
    const addOther = addTypedPhrase("other");
    const addIntro = addTypedPhrase("intro");
    addOtherBtn == null ? void 0 : addOtherBtn.addEventListener("click", addOther);
    addIntroBtn == null ? void 0 : addIntroBtn.addEventListener("click", addIntro);
    newInput == null ? void 0 : newInput.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "Enter") {
        addOther(ev);
      }
    });
    document.body.appendChild(menu);
    const rect = menu.getBoundingClientRect();
    // Clamp the menu's left edge to keep an 8px gutter and avoid overflowing the right viewport edge.
    const left = Math.max(8, Math.min(Math.round(x), window.innerWidth - rect.width - 8));
    const top = Math.max(8, Math.min(Math.round(y), window.innerHeight - rect.height - 8));
    menu.style.left = `${left}px`;
    menu.style.top = `${top}px`;
    chatPhraseContextMenuEl = menu;
  }
  // Installs one-time global capture listeners (outside-pointerdown, scroll, window blur/resize, Escape) that dismiss the phrase menu.
  // Idempotent via chatPhraseContextMenuCloserBound.
  function bindChatPhraseContextMenuCloser() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ bindChatPhraseContextMenuCloser", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatPhraseContextMenuCloserBound) return;
    chatPhraseContextMenuCloserBound = true;
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
      if (!chatPhraseContextMenuEl) return;
      if (e.target instanceof Element && chatPhraseContextMenuEl.contains(e.target)) return;
      hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
      if (chatPhraseContextMenuEl) hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
    }, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("blur", hideChatPhraseContextMenu, true);
    window.addEventListener("resize", hideChatPhraseContextMenu, true);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
    }, true);
  }
  // contextmenu handler gated on Shift+Alt while in chat; suppresses the native menu (incl. stopImmediatePropagation) and opens the custom one.
  // Offsets the menu by +2,+2 from the cursor.
  function handleChatPhraseContextMenu(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleChatPhraseContextMenu", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isChatRoute()) return;
    // Custom phrase menu only triggers on Shift+Alt+right-click, leaving the plain context menu untouched.
    if (!(e.shiftKey && e.altKey)) return;
    const phrase = extractPhraseCandidateFromContextEvent(e);
    if (phrase.length >= 2) rememberQuickPhraseCandidate(phrase, "shift-contextmenu");
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    showChatPhraseContextMenu(e.clientX + 2, e.clientY + 2, phrase);
  }
  // Heuristically finds the chat pane's rect by walking up to 10 ancestors of the chat input and scoring each by class/id keywords, area ratio, and position.
  // Returns the best-scoring rect (or the input's own rect) to anchor the launcher/panel beside the conversation.
  function getChatPhraseAnchorRect() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getChatPhraseAnchorRect", traceArgs(arguments));
    const input = findChatInputElement();
    if (!input) return null;
    const inputRect = input.getBoundingClientRect();
    const viewportArea = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth * window.innerHeight);
    let bestRect = inputRect;
    let bestScore = -Infinity;
    let node = input;
    let depth = 0;
    while (node && node instanceof Element && depth <= 10) {
      const rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width >= 180 && rect.height >= 60) {
        const cls = String(node.className || "").toLowerCase();
        const id = String(node.id || "").toLowerCase();
        let score = 0;
        const area = rect.width * rect.height;
        const areaRatio = area / viewportArea;
        // Class/id keyword match (+6) is the strongest signal that this ancestor is the chat container.
        if (/(chat|message|conversation|thread|pane)/.test(cls) || /(chat|message|conversation|thread|pane)/.test(id)) {
          score += 6;
        }
        if (areaRatio >= 0.08 && areaRatio <= 0.8) score += 3;
        if (rect.right > window.innerWidth * 0.5) score += 2;
        if (rect.left > window.innerWidth * 0.12) score += 2;
        if (rect.top <= inputRect.top + 16) score += 1;
        if (rect.bottom >= inputRect.bottom - 16) score += 1;
        score -= depth * 0.35;
        // Heavy penalty: a box covering >92% of the viewport is almost certainly a full-page wrapper, not the chat pane.
        if (areaRatio > 0.92) score -= 6;
        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          bestRect = rect;
        }
      }
      node = node.parentElement;
      depth += 1;
    }
    return bestRect;
  }
  // Positions the panel just left of the chat anchor, flipping to the right side if there is no room, then clamps within the viewport.
  // Clears right/bottom so left/top take effect.
  function positionChatPhrasePanel(panel2) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ positionChatPhrasePanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!panel2) return;
    const anchor = getChatPhraseAnchorRect();
    const panelWidth = Math.max(panel2.offsetWidth || panel2.getBoundingClientRect().width || 0, 180);
    const panelHeight = Math.max(panel2.offsetHeight || panel2.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0, 120);
    let left = 10;
    let top = 96;
    if (anchor) {
      left = Math.round(anchor.left - panelWidth - 10);
      if (left < 8) {
        const rightCandidate = Math.round(anchor.right + 10);
        left = rightCandidate + panelWidth <= window.innerWidth - 8 ? rightCandidate : 8;
      }
      top = Math.round(anchor.top);
    }
    left = Math.max(8, Math.min(left, Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - panelWidth - 8)));
    top = Math.max(8, Math.min(top, Math.max(8, window.innerHeight - panelHeight - 8)));
    panel2.style.left = `${left}px`;
    panel2.style.top = `${top}px`;
    panel2.style.right = "auto";
    panel2.style.bottom = "auto";
  }
  // Creates (or returns the cached) 'Quick Phrases' launcher button that opens the panel on click; appends to body and caches in chatPhraseLauncherEl.
  function buildChatPhraseLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildChatPhraseLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatPhraseLauncherEl && document.body.contains(chatPhraseLauncherEl)) return chatPhraseLauncherEl;
    const launcher = document.createElement("button");
    launcher.type = "button";
    launcher.className = "sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher";
    launcher.textContent = "Quick Phrases";
    launcher.title = "Show quick phrases";
    launcher.addEventListener("click", () => setChatPhrasePanelVisible(true));
    document.body.appendChild(launcher);
    chatPhraseLauncherEl = launcher;
    return launcher;
  }
  // Positions the collapsed launcher button beside the chat anchor (left, flipping right if cramped), clamped to the viewport.
  function positionChatPhraseLauncher(launcher) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ positionChatPhraseLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!launcher) return;
    const anchor = getChatPhraseAnchorRect();
    const width = Math.max(launcher.offsetWidth || launcher.getBoundingClientRect().width || 0, 84);
    const height = Math.max(launcher.offsetHeight || launcher.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0, 30);
    let left = 10;
    let top = 96;
    if (anchor) {
      left = Math.round(anchor.left - width - 10);
      if (left < 8) {
        const rightCandidate = Math.round(anchor.right + 10);
        left = rightCandidate + width <= window.innerWidth - 8 ? rightCandidate : 8;
      }
      top = Math.round(anchor.top + 6);
    }
    left = Math.max(8, Math.min(left, Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - width - 8)));
    top = Math.max(8, Math.min(top, Math.max(8, window.innerHeight - height - 8)));
    launcher.style.left = `${left}px`;
    launcher.style.top = `${top}px`;
    launcher.style.right = "auto";
    launcher.style.bottom = "auto";
  }
  // Sets state.showChatPhrasePanel, optionally persists via saveState, then re-renders the panel.
  function setChatPhrasePanelVisible(visible, persist = true) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setChatPhrasePanelVisible", traceArgs(arguments));
    state.showChatPhrasePanel = !!visible;
    if (persist) saveState();
    renderChatPhrasePanel();
  }
  // Creates (or returns cached) the Quick Phrases panel DOM, wiring the sort <select> (updates state.quickPhraseSort + saves) and the Hide button.
  // Caches in chatPhrasePanelEl.
  function buildChatPhrasePanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildChatPhrasePanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    if (chatPhrasePanelEl && document.body.contains(chatPhrasePanelEl)) return chatPhrasePanelEl;
    const panel2 = document.createElement("div");
    panel2.className = "sniffies-chat-phrases-panel";
    panel2.innerHTML = `
      <div class="head">
        <span>Quick Phrases</span>
        <span class="head-actions">
          <select id="sfChatPhraseSort" class="sort-select" title="Sort quick phrases">
            <option value="recent">Most recent</option>
            <option value="used">Most used</option>
            <option value="alpha">A-Z</option>
          </select>
          <button type="button" class="head-btn" id="sfChatPhraseHide">Hide</button>
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="list" id="sfChatPhraseList"></div>
    `;
    (_a = panel2.querySelector("#sfChatPhraseSort")) == null ? void 0 : _a.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
      var _a2;
      const next = normalizedQuickPhraseSort((_a2 = e.target) == null ? void 0 : _a2.value);
      if (state.quickPhraseSort === next) return;
      state.quickPhraseSort = next;
      saveState();
      renderChatPhrasePanel();
    });
    (_b = panel2.querySelector("#sfChatPhraseHide")) == null ? void 0 : _b.addEventListener("click", () => {
      setChatPhrasePanelVisible(false);
    });
    document.body.appendChild(panel2);
    chatPhrasePanelEl = panel2;
    return panel2;
  }
  // Master render for the quick-phrase UI: hides everything off the chat route; otherwise shows either the launcher or the expanded panel per state.
  // Lists Intro then Other phrases; buttons are disabled when no current-chat profile id is resolvable.
  function renderChatPhrasePanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ renderChatPhrasePanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isChatRoute()) {
      hideChatPhraseContextMenu();
      if (chatPhrasePanelEl) chatPhrasePanelEl.style.display = "none";
      if (chatPhraseLauncherEl) chatPhraseLauncherEl.style.display = "none";
      return;
    }
    const profileId = getChatContextProfileId();
    const canSendToCurrentChat = !!profileId;
    const panel2 = buildChatPhrasePanel();
    const launcher = buildChatPhraseLauncher();
    if (!panel2) return;
    if (!state.showChatPhrasePanel) {
      panel2.style.display = "none";
      if (launcher) {
        launcher.style.display = "flex";
        positionChatPhraseLauncher(launcher);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (launcher) launcher.style.display = "none";
    panel2.style.display = "flex";
    positionChatPhrasePanel(panel2);
    const sortSelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfChatPhraseSort");
    if (sortSelect) sortSelect.value = normalizedQuickPhraseSort(state.quickPhraseSort);
    const list = panel2.querySelector("#sfChatPhraseList");
    if (!list) return;
    list.textContent = "";
    const introPhrases = getIntroPhrases();
    const otherPhrases = getOtherPhrases();
    const phrases = [...introPhrases, ...otherPhrases];
    const used = canSendToCurrentChat ? getUsedPhraseSet(profileId) : /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    if (!phrases.length) {
      const empty = document.createElement("div");
      empty.className = "empty";
      empty.textContent = "No phrases configured";
      list.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }
    // Inner closure rendering one titled section of phrase buttons (sorted by sortPhrasesForQuickPanel).
    // Marks/disables phrases already used for this profile; click sends the phrase into the current chat (markPhrase) and re-renders.
    const renderPhraseSection = (title, sectionPhrases) => {
      log("→ renderPhraseSection");
      if (!sectionPhrases.length) return;
      const head = document.createElement("div");
      head.className = "cat-head";
      head.textContent = title;
      list.appendChild(head);
      const sorted = sortPhrasesForQuickPanel(sectionPhrases);
      for (const row of sorted) {
        const phrase = row.phrase;
        const key = row.key;
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.type = "button";
        btn.className = "phrase-btn";
        btn.textContent = phrase;
        const alreadyUsed = used.has(key);
        if (alreadyUsed) {
          btn.classList.add("used");
          btn.title = "Already used for this profile";
        } else {
          const parts = [];
          if (row.count > 0) parts.push(`used ${row.count}x`);
          const seenTs = row.lastUsedAt || row.addedAt;
          if (seenTs) parts.push(`last ${new Date(seenTs).toLocaleString()}`);
          if (parts.length) btn.title = parts.join(" \xB7 ");
        }
        if (!canSendToCurrentChat) {
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.title = "Open a profile chat to send";
        } else {
          btn.disabled = alreadyUsed;
        }
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
          if (!canSendToCurrentChat) {
            setStatus("Open a profile chat to send quick phrases");
            return;
          }
          const ok = sendMessageInCurrentChat(profileId, phrase, { markPhrase: true });
          if (!ok) {
            setStatus("Quick phrase failed: message box not found");
            return;
          }
          setStatus(`Quick phrase sent: ${profileId.slice(0, 8)}`);
          renderChatPhrasePanel();
        });
        list.appendChild(btn);
      }
    };
    renderPhraseSection("Intros", introPhrases);
    renderPhraseSection("Other", otherPhrases);
  }
  // Cheap refresh guard: re-renders the panel only if the launcher or panel is currently visible, or we are on the chat route; otherwise no-op.
  function refreshChatPhrasePanelMaybe() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ refreshChatPhrasePanelMaybe", traceArgs(arguments));
    const launcherVisible = !!(chatPhraseLauncherEl && document.body.contains(chatPhraseLauncherEl) && chatPhraseLauncherEl.style.display !== "none");
    const panelVisible = !!(chatPhrasePanelEl && document.body.contains(chatPhrasePanelEl) && chatPhrasePanelEl.style.display !== "none");
    if (!launcherVisible && !panelVisible && !isChatRoute()) return;
    renderChatPhrasePanel();
  }
  // Pulls a message body from common keys (message/text/body/content/msg/...), unwrapping a nested {text} object; returns sanitized text or ''.
  function extractMessageText(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractMessageText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return "";
    const directKeys = ["message", "text", "body", "content", "msg", "messageText", "chatText"];
    for (const key of directKeys) {
      const value = obj[key];
      if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim()) return sanitizeMessageText(value);
      if (value && typeof value === "object" && typeof value.text === "string" && value.text.trim()) {
        return sanitizeMessageText(value.text);
      }
    }
    return "";
  }
  // Escapes &, <, >, " and ' for safe HTML interpolation.
  function escapeHtml(s) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ escapeHtml", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Order matters: & is escaped first so the &-entities introduced by later replacements are not double-escaped.
    return String(s || "").replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/\"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
  }
  // Normalizes a profile id from a string/number, or from common id fields of an object (profileId/userId/cruiserId/id/_id, plus nested user._id / profile._id).
  function extractIdCandidate(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractIdCandidate", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number") return normalizeProfileId(value);
    if (typeof value !== "object") return null;
    return normalizeProfileId(
      value.profileId || value.userId || value.cruiserId || value.id || value._id || ((_a = value.user) == null ? void 0 : _a._id) || ((_b = value.profile) == null ? void 0 : _b._id)
    );
  }
  // Turns a camelCase/underscore/dash key into a spaced, sentence-cased label (e.g. lookingFor -> 'Looking for').
  function humanizeLabel(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ humanizeLabel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const input = String(raw || "").replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2").replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!input) return "";
    return input.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + input.slice(1);
  }
  // Returns the object's actual key whose normalized form (alphanumerics only, lowercased) matches the requested key, else null.
  function findObjectKeyInsensitive(obj, key) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findObjectKeyInsensitive", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
    const target = normalizeKey(key);
    for (const current of Object.keys(obj)) {
      if (normalizeKey(current) === target) return current;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Walks a nested property path case/punctuation-insensitively (via findObjectKeyInsensitive); returns undefined if any segment is missing.
  function getNestedLoose(obj, path = []) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getNestedLoose", traceArgs(arguments));
    let current = obj;
    for (const part of path) {
      if (!current || typeof current !== "object") return void 0;
      const actualKey = findObjectKeyInsensitive(current, part);
      if (!actualKey) return void 0;
      current = current[actualKey];
    }
    return current;
  }
  // Renders a value to short display text: strings cleaned, finite numbers, booleans -> Yes/No, arrays joined (max 6 items), objects via label/name/title/value.
  // Capped at 120 chars.
  function valueToPreferenceText(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ valueToPreferenceText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (value == null) return "";
    if (typeof value === "string") return cleanDisplayText(value, 120);
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) return String(value);
    if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? "Yes" : "No";
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      const parts = value.map((item) => valueToPreferenceText(item)).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6);
      return cleanDisplayText(parts.join(", "), 120);
    }
    if (typeof value === "object") {
      const named = cleanDisplayText(value.label || value.name || value.title || value.value || "", 120);
      if (named) return named;
    }
    return "";
  }
  // Appends a 'Label: value' line to lines[] if both render non-empty, deduping case-insensitively via the seen set.
  function addPreferenceLine(lines, seen, label, value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ addPreferenceLine", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rendered = valueToPreferenceText(value);
    const cleanLabel = cleanDisplayText(label, 40);
    if (!rendered || !cleanLabel) return;
    const line = `${cleanLabel}: ${rendered}`;
    const key = line.toLowerCase();
    if (seen.has(key)) return;
    seen.add(key);
    lines.push(line);
  }
  // Recursively (depth <= 5) gathers free-text strings (>=12 chars) with a relevance score into out[].
  // Skips id/url/name/location/time-ish keys; boosts bio/about/looking-for keys (+240) and message/content keys (+40).
  function collectCandidateTextStrings(value, path = [], out = [], depth = 0) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ collectCandidateTextStrings", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (value == null || depth > 5) return;
    if (typeof value === "string") {
      const text = cleanDisplayText(value, 420);
      if (!text || text.length < 12) return;
      const key = normalizeKey(path[path.length - 1] || "");
      if (!key) return;
      // Reject obvious non-prose fields (ids, urls, names, locations, times) so only real free-text bios get scored.
      if (/(url|image|img|avatar|photo|thumb|id|slug|username|name|city|state|distance|time|date)/.test(key)) return;
      let score = Math.min(220, text.length);
      if (/(about|bio|description|headline|summary|blurb|tagline|profiletext|lookingfor|looking|into|intro)/.test(key)) score += 240;
      if (/(message|content|body|text)/.test(key)) score += 40;
      out.push({ text, score });
      return;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value.slice(0, 12)) collectCandidateTextStrings(item, path, out, depth + 1);
      return;
    }
    if (typeof value === "object") {
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        collectCandidateTextStrings(child, [...path, key], out, depth + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  // Scans likely roots (data.profile, profile, data, obj) for candidate text strings and returns the highest-scored (longest as tiebreak), else ''.
  function extractProfileTextCandidateFromObject(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractProfileTextCandidateFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const candidates = [];
    const roots = [
      (_a = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data,
      obj
    ].filter(Boolean);
    for (const root of roots) collectCandidateTextStrings(root, [], candidates, 0);
    candidates.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || b.text.length - a.text.length);
    return ((_b = candidates[0]) == null ? void 0 : _b.text) || "";
  }
  // Finds a display label from known paths (displayName/name/headline/title/...) across likely roots; requires length >= 3.
  // Falls back to 'Profile <id-prefix>' or 'Profile'.
  function extractProfileLabelFromObject(obj, profileId = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractProfileLabelFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const roots = [
      (_a = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data,
      obj
    ].filter(Boolean);
    const paths = [
      ["displayName"],
      ["name"],
      ["headline"],
      ["title"],
      ["profileName"],
      ["screenName"],
      ["username"]
    ];
    for (const root of roots) {
      for (const path of paths) {
        const value = cleanDisplayText(getNestedLoose(root, path), 80);
        if (value && value.length >= 3) return value;
      }
    }
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    return id ? `Profile ${id.slice(0, 10)}` : "Profile";
  }
  // Recursively (depth <= 5) finds the first plausible image URL: accepts only image extensions (png/jpg/gif/webp/avif) or the sniffies asset host.
  // Prefers avatar/image/photo-named keys; skips distance/message/time/date/status branches.
  function extractImageUrlFromValue(value, keyHint = "", depth = 0) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractImageUrlFromValue", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (value == null || depth > 5) return "";
    if (typeof value === "string") {
      const text = sanitizeImageUrl(value);
      if (!text) return "";
      // Accept a URL only if it ends in a known image extension (allowing query/hash) or is on the Sniffies profile-asset CDN.
      if (!/\.(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(text) && !/profile\.sniffiesassets\.com/i.test(text)) {
        return "";
      }
      return text;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value.slice(0, 12)) {
        const url = extractImageUrlFromValue(item, keyHint, depth + 1);
        if (url) return url;
      }
      return "";
    }
    if (typeof value === "object") {
      const directKeys = Object.keys(value).filter((key) => /(avatar|image|img|photo|picture|thumbnail|thumb|src)/i.test(key));
      for (const key of directKeys) {
        const url = extractImageUrlFromValue(value[key], key, depth + 1);
        if (url) return url;
      }
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(keyHint || key);
        if (/(distance|message|time|date|status)/.test(normalizedKey)) continue;
        const url = extractImageUrlFromValue(child, key, depth + 1);
        if (url) return url;
      }
    }
    return "";
  }
  // Tries likely roots (data.profile, profile, data, obj) in order, returning the first image URL found via extractImageUrlFromValue.
  function extractImageUrlFromObject(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractImageUrlFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const roots = [
      (_a = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data,
      obj
    ].filter(Boolean);
    for (const root of roots) {
      const url = extractImageUrlFromValue(root);
      if (url) return url;
    }
    return "";
  }
  // Collects up to 8 'Label: value' profile preference lines: first via known label->path pairs across likely roots, then a depth<=4 BFS over preference-ish keys.
  // Returns early once 8 lines are reached.
  function extractPreferenceLinesFromObject(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractPreferenceLinesFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d;
    const lines = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const roots = [
      (_b = (_a = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile) == null ? void 0 : _b.extended,
      (_c = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.profile) == null ? void 0 : _c.extended,
      (_d = obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) == null ? void 0 : _d.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.profile,
      obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data,
      obj
    ].filter(Boolean);
    const candidatePaths = [
      { path: ["sexuality", "attitude"], label: "Attitude" },
      { path: ["attitude"], label: "Attitude" },
      { path: ["sexuality", "position"], label: "Position" },
      { path: ["position"], label: "Position" },
      { path: ["sexuality", "lookingFor"], label: "Looking for" },
      { path: ["lookingFor"], label: "Looking for" },
      { path: ["looking_for"], label: "Looking for" },
      { path: ["pronouns"], label: "Pronouns" },
      { path: ["bodyType"], label: "Body" },
      { path: ["relationshipStatus"], label: "Relationship" },
      { path: ["hosting"], label: "Hosting" },
      { path: ["hostingStatus"], label: "Hosting" },
      { path: ["tribes"], label: "Tribes" },
      { path: ["interests"], label: "Interests" },
      { path: ["into"], label: "Into" },
      { path: ["kinks"], label: "Kinks" },
      { path: ["fetishes"], label: "Fetishes" },
      { path: ["tags"], label: "Tags" },
      { path: ["preferences"], label: "Preferences" }
    ];
    for (const root of roots) {
      for (const candidate of candidatePaths) {
        addPreferenceLine(lines, seen, candidate.label, getNestedLoose(root, candidate.path));
        // Cap preference lines at 8 to keep the profile summary compact and bound the scan.
        if (lines.length >= 8) return lines;
      }
    }
    const queue = roots.map((root) => ({ value: root, depth: 0 }));
    const visited = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakSet();
    while (queue.length && lines.length < 8) {
      const { value, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || visited.has(value) || depth > 4) continue;
      visited.add(value);
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
        if (/(attitude|position|lookingfor|relationship|hosting|pronouns|interest|kink|fetish|tribe|tag|preference|role|into)/.test(normalizedKey)) {
          addPreferenceLine(lines, seen, humanizeLabel(key), child);
          if (lines.length >= 8) break;
        }
        if (child && typeof child === "object") queue.push({ value: child, depth: depth + 1 });
      }
    }
    return lines;
  }
  // Extracts label/profileText/image/preferences/distance from a payload and merges them into profileSummaryCache for the (resolved) profile id.
  // Only overwrites a field when a new non-empty value is found; stamps updatedAt/source. Returns the merged record or null.
  function updateProfileSummaryFromObject(profileId, obj, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateProfileSummaryFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId) || extractIdCandidate(obj);
    if (!id || !obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
    const previous = profileSummaryCache.get(id) || {};
    const next = __spreadProps(__spreadValues({}, previous), {
      profileId: id,
      profileUrl: previous.profileUrl || profileUrlForId(id),
      updatedAt: now(),
      source: String(options.source || previous.source || "partial")
    });
    const label = cleanDisplayText(extractProfileLabelFromObject(obj, id), 80);
    const profileText = cleanDisplayText(extractProfileTextCandidateFromObject(obj), 600);
    const imageUrl = extractImageUrlFromObject(obj);
    const preferences = normalizePreferenceList(extractPreferenceLinesFromObject(obj));
    const distance = options.distance != null ? options.distance : extractDistanceFromValue((obj == null ? void 0 : obj.data) || obj);
    if (label) next.label = label;
    if (profileText) next.profileText = profileText;
    if (preferences.length) next.preferences = preferences;
    if (imageUrl) next.imageUrl = imageUrl;
    if (Number.isFinite(distance)) next.distance = distance;
    profileSummaryCache.set(id, next);
    return next;
  }
  // Returns the first id-valued field whose normalized key contains any of the given substring patterns.
  // With allowSelf=false, skips ids already known to be the viewer (selfProfileIds).
  function findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, patterns, allowSelf = true) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findIdByKeyPatterns", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
      if (!patterns.some((p) => normalizedKey.includes(p))) continue;
      const id = extractIdCandidate(value);
      if (!id) continue;
      if (!allowSelf && selfProfileIds.has(id)) continue;
      return id;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Infers message direction 'out'/'in' from boolean flags (fromMe/isMine/incoming/...) or string direction/type fields; returns null if undecidable.
  function detectDirection(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ detectDirection", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      const k = normalizeKey(key);
      if (value === true) {
        if (["fromme", "ismine", "mine", "sentbyme", "outgoing", "isoutgoing", "mymessage"].includes(k)) return "out";
        if (["incoming", "isincoming", "fromthem", "received", "isreceived", "theirmessage"].includes(k)) return "in";
      }
      // Only trust explicit direction fields and match the value EXACTLY. Generic type/msgtype values such as
      // "shout"/"checkout"/"join"/"typing" would false-match a bare /out/ or /in/ substring and flip my/their,
      // corrupting the strict their>mine reply-unhide test.
      if (typeof value === "string" && ["direction", "messagedirection"].includes(k)) {
        const s = value.toLowerCase().trim();
        if (["out", "outgoing", "sent", "fromme", "mine"].includes(s)) return "out";
        if (["in", "incoming", "received", "fromthem"].includes(s)) return "in";
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Returns a ms timestamp from priority keys (createdAt/sentAt/timestamp/...); if none, returns the max parseable value among any time/date/at/ts-suffixed key.
  function extractEventTimestamp(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractEventTimestamp", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return 0;
    const priority = [
      "createdAt",
      "sentAt",
      "timestamp",
      "time",
      "date",
      "lastMessageAt",
      "lastChatAt",
      "latestMessageAt"
    ];
    for (const key of priority) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
      const ts = parseTimestamp(obj[key]);
      if (ts) return ts;
    }
    let latest = 0;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
      // Only treat keys ending in a real timestamp suffix as a fallback time. The previous bare `at|ts$`
      // matched unrelated words (seat, format, chat, stats, candidate), letting a stray number become the time.
      if (!/(time|date|timestamp)$|(created|sent|received|delivered|read|seen|edited|deleted|removed|retracted|unsent|posted|modified|message|chat|event|activity|expire|expires|expired)(at|ts)$/.test(normalizedKey)) continue;
      const ts = parseTimestamp(value);
      if (ts > latest) latest = ts;
    }
    return latest;
  }
  // Resolves the OTHER party's profile id from peer/partner/recipient/target keys (excluding self), then nested profile/user objects, then fallbackId.
  // If the only id found is the viewer's but a non-self fallback exists, prefers the fallback.
  function findLikelyProfileId(obj, fallbackId = null) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return fallbackId;
    const keyPatterns = [
      "otheruserid",
      "otherprofileid",
      "peerid",
      "partnerid",
      "targetuserid",
      "targetprofileid",
      "recipientid",
      "touserid",
      "profileid",
      "cruiserid",
      "userid"
    ];
    let id = findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, keyPatterns, false);
    if (!id) {
      id = extractIdCandidate(obj.profile || obj.user || obj.otherUser || obj.otherProfile);
      if (id && selfProfileIds.has(id)) id = null;
    }
    if (!id) id = normalizeProfileId(fallbackId);
    // Guard against attributing activity to yourself: if the resolved id is the viewer but the fallback is not, use the fallback.
    if (id && selfProfileIds.has(id) && fallbackId && !selfProfileIds.has(fallbackId)) return normalizeProfileId(fallbackId);
    return id || fallbackId;
  }
  // From a conversation-summary object, parses last-sent/last-received/last-any timestamps by key pattern and upserts them as chat activity for the peer.
  // If only an 'any' time is present, it updates anyLastTs only (not myLastTs/theirLastTs — see the inline
  // comment below). Returns whether activity changed.
  function ingestSummaryTimes(obj, contextProfileId) {
    const profileId = findLikelyProfileId(obj, contextProfileId);
    if (!profileId) return false;
    let myTs = 0;
    let theirTs = 0;
    let anyTs = 0;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj || {})) {
      const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
      const ts = parseTimestamp(value);
      if (!ts) continue;
      if (/(lastsent|lastoutgoing|mylast|youlast|outgoinglast|sentlast)/.test(normalizedKey)) {
        if (ts > myTs) myTs = ts;
      } else if (/(lastreceived|lastincoming|theirlast|fromlast|incominglast|receivedlast|lastreply)/.test(normalizedKey)) {
        if (ts > theirTs) theirTs = ts;
      } else if (/(lastmessage|latestmessage|lastchat|latestchat|lastactivity|recentmessage|messagetime)/.test(normalizedKey)) {
        if (ts > anyTs) anyTs = ts;
      }
    }
    // A direction-less 'last activity' time updates anyLastTs ONLY (the badge falls back to it). It must NOT be
    // written into my/their, or the strict their>mine reply-unhide test (hasUnansweredRecentReply) could never fire.
    if (!myTs && !theirTs && !anyTs) return false;
    return upsertChatActivity(profileId, myTs, theirTs, anyTs);
  }
  // Ingests a single message event: derives direction (flags, then self/context id match), upserts chat activity, and stores a preview snippet.
  // Learns the viewer's own id from out/in inference (rememberSelfProfileId); unresolved direction records
  // activity in the any-only channel (not both-sided — see the inline comment below). Returns whether anything changed.
  function ingestMessageTime(obj, contextProfileId) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return false;
    const ts = extractEventTimestamp(obj);
    if (!ts) return false;
    const messageText = extractMessageText(obj);
    const fromId = findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, ["fromid", "senderid", "authorid", "createdbyid", "ownerid", "sourceuserid"]);
    const toId = findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, ["toid", "recipientid", "receiverid", "targetid", "destuserid"]);
    const contextId = normalizeProfileId(contextProfileId);
    const explicitDirection = detectDirection(obj);
    let direction = explicitDirection;
    if (!direction && fromId && toId) {
      if (selfProfileIds.has(fromId)) direction = "out";
      else if (selfProfileIds.has(toId)) direction = "in";
    }
    if (!direction && contextId) {
      if (fromId && normalizeProfileId(fromId) === contextId) direction = "in";
      else if (toId && normalizeProfileId(toId) === contextId) direction = "out";
    }
    if (direction === "out") {
      // Only learn a self-id from an EXPLICIT direction signal (detectDirection), never from context/positional
      // inference — a single misclassified frame would otherwise permanently shadow a real peer as "self".
      if (fromId && explicitDirection) rememberSelfProfileId(fromId);
      const profileId = normalizeProfileId(toId) || contextId;
      const changed2 = upsertChatActivity(profileId, ts, 0);
      // Count this as a new unanswered outbound only when myLastTs actually advanced
      // (changed2) — replays/history re-fetches of the same message can't inflate it.
      if (changed2) bumpUnansweredOut(profileId);
      if (messageText) addChatPreviewMessage(profileId, "out", ts, messageText);
      return changed2 || !!messageText;
    }
    if (direction === "in") {
      if (toId && explicitDirection) rememberSelfProfileId(toId);
      const profileId = normalizeProfileId(fromId) || contextId;
      const changed2 = upsertChatActivity(profileId, 0, ts);
      // They replied — clear the unanswered-outbound streak (this un-hides them).
      resetUnansweredOut(profileId);
      // Manually-hidden (blocked) profiles auto-unhide when they message you AFTER the hide.
      // The strict ts > blockedAt check keeps a chat-history re-fetch of an OLDER message
      // (from before you hid them) from spuriously undoing the hide.
      if (changed2 && profileId && blocked.has(profileId) && ts > (blockedAt.get(profileId) || 0)) {
        unhideProfileNow(profileId);
      }
      if (messageText) addChatPreviewMessage(profileId, "in", ts, messageText);
      return changed2 || !!messageText;
    }
    const fallbackProfileId = normalizeProfileId(fromId) || normalizeProfileId(toId) || contextId;
    if (!fallbackProfileId) return false;
    // Direction unknown: record the activity time in the any-only channel so the badge updates, but do NOT
    // fabricate a myLastTs/theirLastTs — doing so corrupts the strict their>mine reply-unhide test.
    const changed = upsertChatActivity(fallbackProfileId, 0, 0, ts);
    if (messageText) addChatPreviewMessage(fallbackProfileId, "unknown", ts, messageText);
    return changed || !!messageText;
  }
  // Returns the id of whoever deleted/retracted a message (deletedById/removedById/actorId/...), else null.
  function findDeletionActorId(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findDeletionActorId", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return null;
    return findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, [
      "deletedbyid",
      "removedbyid",
      "actorid",
      "deleterid",
      "retractedbyid",
      "unsentbyid"
    ]);
  }
  // Detects whether an event represents a deleted/removed/retracted/unsent message via *At timestamps, boolean-ish flags, or status/state strings.
  function hasDeletionMarker(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hasDeletionMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return false;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
      if (/(deletedat|removedat|retractedat|unsentat)/.test(normalizedKey) && parseTimestamp(value)) return true;
      if (/(deleted|isdeleted|removed|isremoved|retracted|isretracted|unsent|isunsent|messageDeleted)/.test(normalizedKey)) {
        if (value === true || value === 1 || value === "1") return true;
        if (typeof value === "string" && /^(true|deleted|removed|retracted|unsent)$/i.test(value.trim())) return true;
      }
      if (/(status|state|messagestatus|delivery)/.test(normalizedKey) && typeof value === "string" && /(deleted|removed|retracted|unsent)/i.test(value)) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  // Records a peer-initiated message deletion: ignores self-deletions, dedupes via a fingerprint (profile|actor|msgId|text|ts) in seenChatDeletionEvents.
  // Increments chatDeletionStats[profileId] (count + lastDeletedTs) and schedules a save. Returns true only for a newly counted deletion.
  function ingestDeletedMessageEvent(obj, contextProfileId) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object" || !hasDeletionMarker(obj)) return false;
    const profileId = findLikelyProfileId(obj, contextProfileId);
    if (!profileId) return false;
    const actorId = findDeletionActorId(obj);
    // Skip deletions you performed yourself; only the peer deleting messages should count toward their deletion stats.
    if (actorId && selfProfileIds.has(actorId)) return false;
    const realTs = extractEventTimestamp(obj);
    const ts = realTs || now();
    const messageId = findIdByKeyPatterns(obj, ["messageid", "chatmessageid", "threaditemid", "eventid", "entryid"]) || extractIdCandidate(obj.message) || "";
    const messageText = extractMessageText(obj);
    const fingerprintParts = [
      profileId,
      actorId || "unknown",
      messageId || "no-message-id",
      messageText || "no-text",
      // Stable sentinel when the event had no real timestamp, so a ts-less deletion replayed under a fresh
      // now() doesn't produce a new fingerprint each time and inflate the deletion count.
      realTs ? String(realTs) : "no-ts"
    ];
    // Composite key (profile|actor|msgId|text|ts) dedupes replayed/duplicate deletion events; capped at 800 chars.
    const fingerprint = fingerprintParts.join("|").slice(0, 800);
    if (seenChatDeletionEvents[fingerprint]) return false;
    seenChatDeletionEvents[fingerprint] = ts;
    const current = chatDeletionStats[profileId] || { count: 0, lastDeletedTs: 0, updatedAt: 0 };
    chatDeletionStats[profileId] = {
      count: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(current.count) || 0)) + 1,
      lastDeletedTs: Math.max(parseTimestamp(current.lastDeletedTs) || 0, ts),
      updatedAt: now()
    };
    scheduleChatDeletionSave();
    return true;
  }
  // Scan one object's keys for self/viewer-id fields (selfId, myId, isMe/isSelf flags) and remember any found id as the local user.
  // Side effect: calls rememberSelfProfileId so later captures can exclude your own profile from 'last active' / chat-age tracking.
  function detectSelfIds(obj) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return;
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      const normalizedKey = normalizeKey(key);
      if (["selfid", "myid", "myuserid", "myprofileid", "viewerid", "currentuserid", "currentprofileid", "visitorid"].includes(normalizedKey)) {
        const id = extractIdCandidate(value);
        if (id) rememberSelfProfileId(id);
      }
      if (value && typeof value === "object" && (value.isMe === true || value.isSelf === true || value.me === true)) {
        const id = extractIdCandidate(value);
        if (id) rememberSelfProfileId(id);
      }
    }
    if (obj.isMe === true || obj.isSelf === true || obj.me === true || obj.self === true) {
      const id = extractIdCandidate(obj);
      if (id) rememberSelfProfileId(id);
    }
  }
  // Probe well-known page globals (window.currentUser, SNIFFIES.*, __NUXT__, __INITIAL_STATE__, ...) for the viewer's profile id at startup.
  // Best-effort: each candidate is fed through detectSelfIds plus a direct isMe/self check; absent globals are skipped silently.
  function seedSelfIdsFromWindow() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ seedSelfIdsFromWindow", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c;
    const candidates = [
      window.currentUser,
      window.user,
      window.viewer,
      window.me,
      (_a = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _a.currentUser,
      (_b = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _b.viewer,
      (_c = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _c.user,
      window.__INITIAL_STATE__,
      window.__INITIAL_APP_STATE__,
      window.__NUXT__
    ];
    for (const c of candidates) {
      if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
      detectSelfIds(c);
      const directId = extractIdCandidate(c);
      if (directId && (c.isMe === true || c.me === true || c.self === true || c.isSelf === true)) {
        rememberSelfProfileId(directId);
      }
    }
  }
  // Tally per-URL capture stats (hits/parsed/updates) for the debug panel and push a timestamped entry onto a recent ring.
  // Side effect: mutates chatCaptureStats; recent list is capped at 30 (oldest shifted out).
  function recordChatCapture(url, parsed = false, updates = 0) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordChatCapture", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!url) return;
    const u = String(url);
    const bucket = chatCaptureStats.urls[u] || { hits: 0, parsed: 0, updates: 0 };
    bucket.hits += 1;
    if (parsed) bucket.parsed += 1;
    if (updates) bucket.updates += updates;
    chatCaptureStats.urls[u] = bucket;
    if (updates || parsed) {
      chatCaptureStats.recent.push({
        url: u,
        parsed: !!parsed,
        updates: updates || 0,
        at: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
      });
      if (chatCaptureStats.recent.length > 30) chatCaptureStats.recent.shift();
    }
  }
  // Add a batch of derived chat updates to the running total and trigger a (throttled) badge refresh.
  // No-op when updates is 0.
  function noteChatPayloadUpdates(updates) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ noteChatPayloadUpdates", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!updates) return;
    chatCaptureStats.payloadUpdates += updates;
    scheduleChatBadgeRefresh();
  }
  // Decide if a localStorage/sessionStorage key is worth JSON-parsing for chat data: chat/message/etc. key regex, or a sniffies state/store/cache key.
  function shouldParseStorageKey(key) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldParseStorageKey", traceArgs(arguments));
    const normalized = String(key || "");
    if (!normalized) return false;
    if (CHAT_STORAGE_KEY_RE.test(normalized)) return true;
    if (/sniffies/i.test(normalized) && /(state|store|cache)/i.test(normalized)) return true;
    return false;
  }
  // Cheap pre-filter: true only if the trimmed string starts with '{' or '[', to avoid JSON.parse on non-JSON values.
  function isLikelyJsonString(raw) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isLikelyJsonString", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(raw || "").trim();
    if (!text) return false;
    return text.startsWith("{") || text.startsWith("[");
  }
  // Build a change-detection fingerprint for a stored value: full text if <=128 chars, else length plus first/last 48 chars.
  // Used to skip re-parsing storage entries that have not changed since the last scan.
  function makeStorageDigest(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ makeStorageDigest", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(value || "");
    if (!text) return "0";
    const len = text.length;
    if (len <= 128) return `${len}:${text}`;
    return `${len}:${text.slice(0, 48)}:${text.slice(-48)}`;
  }
  // Walk every key in one Web Storage bucket, parse chat-looking JSON values, and feed them through consumeChatPayload to derive activity times.
  // Skips unchanged (digest-matched) and oversized (>CHAT_STORAGE_MAX_VALUE_BYTES) values; force=true ignores the digest cache. Every storage call is try-wrapped.
  function scanStorageBucketForChat(storageObj, bucketName = "localStorage", reason = "interval", force = false) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scanStorageBucketForChat", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!storageObj) return { scanned: 0, parsed: 0, updates: 0 };
    let scanned = 0;
    let parsed = 0;
    let updates = 0;
    let length = 0;
    try {
      length = Number(storageObj.length) || 0;
    } catch (e) {
      length = 0;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i += 1) {
      let key = "";
      try {
        key = String(storageObj.key(i) || "");
      } catch (e) {
        key = "";
      }
      if (!shouldParseStorageKey(key)) continue;
      scanned += 1;
      let raw = "";
      try {
        raw = storageObj.getItem(key);
      } catch (e) {
        raw = "";
      }
      if (!raw || !isLikelyJsonString(raw)) continue;
      if (raw.length > CHAT_STORAGE_MAX_VALUE_BYTES) continue;
      const digestKey = `${bucketName}:${key}`;
      const digest = makeStorageDigest(raw);
      if (!force && chatStorageDigestByKey.get(digestKey) === digest) continue;
      let payload = null;
      try {
        payload = JSON.parse(raw);
      } catch (e) {
        payload = null;
      }
      chatStorageDigestByKey.set(digestKey, digest);
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") continue;
      parsed += 1;
      detectSelfIds(payload);
      const profileContext = getChatContextProfileId();
      const delta = consumeChatPayload(payload, profileContext);
      if (delta > 0) {
        updates += delta;
        recordChatCapture(`[${bucketName}] ${key}`, true, delta);
      }
    }
    if (updates > 0) {
      logInfo("storage chat scan updates", { bucketName, reason, scanned, parsed, updates });
    }
    return { scanned, parsed, updates };
  }
  // Scan both localStorage and sessionStorage for chat payloads, aggregate the counts, and report updates.
  // Self-throttled to once per 1200ms via chatStorageScanTimer unless force=true; mutates chatCaptureStats and calls noteChatPayloadUpdates.
  function scanStorageForChatPayloads(reason = "interval", force = false) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scanStorageForChatPayloads", traceArgs(arguments));
    const nowTs = now();
    if (!force && chatStorageScanTimer && nowTs < chatStorageScanTimer) return { scanned: 0, parsed: 0, updates: 0 };
    chatStorageScanTimer = nowTs + 1200;
    let local = { scanned: 0, parsed: 0, updates: 0 };
    let session = { scanned: 0, parsed: 0, updates: 0 };
    try {
      local = scanStorageBucketForChat(window.localStorage, "localStorage", reason, force);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    try {
      session = scanStorageBucketForChat(window.sessionStorage, "sessionStorage", reason, force);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    const scanned = local.scanned + session.scanned;
    const parsed = local.parsed + session.parsed;
    const updates = local.updates + session.updates;
    chatCaptureStats.storageScans += 1;
    chatCaptureStats.storageKeysParsed += parsed;
    chatCaptureStats.storagePayloadUpdates += updates;
    noteChatPayloadUpdates(updates);
    return { scanned, parsed, updates };
  }
  // Derive the 'who am I looking at' profile id from the current URL (/profile/<hex>[/chat]).
  // Gives ambiguous payloads (no explicit peer id) a default owner.
  function getChatContextProfileId() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getChatContextProfileId", traceArgs(arguments));
    const href = String(window.location.href || "");
    // Captures a 6+ hex-char profile id from the URL, tolerating an optional trailing /chat segment.
    const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})(?:\/chat)?/i);
    return m ? normalizeProfileId(m[1]) : null;
  }
  // Collapse Sniffies presence fields into one 'last seen online' ms timestamp: max(connectUpdateTime, disconnectTime).
  // Clamped to <= now() so a future/'valid-until' server timestamp cannot read as active in the future; this is the value the 2h-online filter compares.
  function computeLastActiveTs(connectTs, disconnectTs) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ computeLastActiveTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Sniffies presence model (from the /api/user/partials feed): connectUpdateTime
    // advances while a user is connected (heartbeat); disconnectTime is stamped when
    // they drop. The later of the two is the last moment we knew they were online,
    // which is what the profile card renders as "active X ago" -- so it is the value
    // the 2h-online filter compares against. Clamp to <= now so a server-side
    // "valid until" style future timestamp can never read as the future.
    // If a Gate-2 spot-check shows the card's "active X ago" tracks only ONE field,
    // narrow this to that field -- it is the single point of truth for the choice.
    const t = now();
    // Max-wins: whichever of last-heartbeat vs last-disconnect is newer is treated as 'last seen online'.
    const ts = Math.max(parseTimestamp(connectTs) || 0, parseTimestamp(disconnectTs) || 0);
    if (!ts) return 0;
    // Clamp to now() so a future server timestamp can't make a profile look currently active.
    return Math.min(ts, t);
  }
  // Pull presence times from a payload node (either obj.data.* or top-level connectUpdateTime/disconnectTime) and record the profile's last-active moment.
  // Returns false unless an id and a non-zero timestamp resolve; update is monotonic via recordProfileLastActive (never moves backward).
  function ingestPresenceTimes(obj, contextProfileId) {
    if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return false;
    let id = null;
    let connect = 0;
    let disconnect = 0;
    const d = obj.data;
    if (d && typeof d === "object" && (d.connectUpdateTime || d.disconnectTime)) {
      id = normalizeProfileId(obj._id) || normalizeProfileId(contextProfileId);
      connect = parseTimestamp(d.connectUpdateTime) || 0;
      disconnect = parseTimestamp(d.disconnectTime) || 0;
    } else if (obj.connectUpdateTime || obj.disconnectTime) {
      id = normalizeProfileId(obj._id) || normalizeProfileId(contextProfileId);
      connect = parseTimestamp(obj.connectUpdateTime) || 0;
      disconnect = parseTimestamp(obj.disconnectTime) || 0;
    } else {
      return false;
    }
    if (!id) return false;
    const ts = computeLastActiveTs(connect, disconnect);
    if (!ts) return false;
    return recordProfileLastActive(id, ts);
  }
  // Breadth-first walk an arbitrary captured payload, extracting self-ids, chat summary/message/deleted-message times, and presence; returns count of time updates applied.
  // Cycle-safe (WeakSet) and depth-capped at 7; threads a best-guess peer profile id down through children as context.
  function consumeChatPayload(payload, initialContextProfileId = null) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ consumeChatPayload", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return 0;
    let updates = 0;
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakSet();
    const queue = [{ value: payload, contextProfileId: normalizeProfileId(initialContextProfileId), depth: 0 }];
    while (queue.length) {
      const { value, contextProfileId, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") continue;
      if (seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);
      // Depth cap: stop descending past 7 levels to bound work on deeply nested payloads.
      if (depth > 7) continue;
      const currentContext = findLikelyProfileId(value, contextProfileId);
      detectSelfIds(value);
      if (ingestSummaryTimes(value, currentContext)) updates += 1;
      if (ingestMessageTime(value, currentContext)) updates += 1;
      if (ingestDeletedMessageEvent(value, currentContext)) updates += 1;
      ingestPresenceTimes(value, currentContext);
      for (const child of Object.values(value)) {
        if (!child || typeof child !== "object") continue;
        queue.push({ value: child, contextProfileId: currentContext, depth: depth + 1 });
      }
    }
    return updates;
  }
  // Gate for monkey-patched fetch/XHR: only inspect responses whose URL contains both 'sniffies' and '/api/'.
  // Keeps the capture hooks from parsing unrelated third-party traffic.
  function shouldInspectApiUrl(url) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldInspectApiUrl", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!url) return false;
    const s = String(url).toLowerCase();
    if (!s.includes("sniffies")) return false;
    if (!s.includes("/api/")) return false;
    // Skip the profile-snippet endpoints: updatePartials() already parses /api/user/partials and /api/user/full
    // structurally (per row, by _id, incl. presence). Re-running the generic BFS walker over them is redundant
    // and can mis-attribute a nested time to the wrong peer (URL context is null on the map). Chat/inbox/socket
    // endpoints still pass through, so no chat-message capture is lost.
    if (s.includes("/api/user/partials") || s.includes("/api/user/full")) return false;
    return true;
  }
  // Run an already-parsed API JSON body through consumeChatPayload using the URL-derived profile context, then record stats.
  // Returns the number of chat-activity updates derived.
  function captureParsedPayload(url, payload) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ captureParsedPayload", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return 0;
    const updates = consumeChatPayload(payload, getChatContextProfileId());
    noteChatPayloadUpdates(updates);
    if (url) recordChatCapture(url, true, updates);
    return updates;
  }
  // Decode a WebSocket text frame into a chat object: strips the Socket.IO '42' event prefix and unwraps the [event,data] tuple, else parses raw JSON.
  // Returns null for pure-numeric ping/pong frames; double-decodes a JSON string that itself contains JSON.
  function parseWsPayloadText(text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parseWsPayloadText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!text || typeof text !== "string") return null;
    // Cap oversized frames before parsing (mirrors the XHR text path's 1.5MB guard in patchChatCaptureOnWindow) so a
    // pathologically large socket frame can't cause a synchronous JSON.parse jank / memory spike.
    if (text.length > 15e5) return null;
    const trimmed = text.trim();
    // Drops Socket.IO/Engine.IO numeric ping/pong control frames (e.g. '2','3') that carry no chat data.
    if (!trimmed || /^\d+$/.test(trimmed)) return null;
    let parsed = null;
    try {
      // Socket.IO frame prefix: '4'=message, '2'=EVENT; payload after '42' is a JSON [eventName, data] tuple.
      if (trimmed.startsWith("42")) {
        const socketPayload = JSON.parse(trimmed.slice(2));
        if (Array.isArray(socketPayload)) {
          // Take the event's data argument (index 1), falling back to index 0 when there's no event name.
          parsed = socketPayload.length > 1 ? socketPayload[1] : socketPayload[0];
        } else {
          parsed = socketPayload;
        }
      } else if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
        parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    if (typeof parsed === "string" && (parsed.startsWith("{") || parsed.startsWith("["))) {
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(parsed);
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : null;
  }
  // Normalize a WebSocket message's data (string or Blob.text()) into text and feed it to the chat-capture parser.
  // Side effect: increments chatCaptureStats.wsMessages on a parseable frame; all errors swallowed.
  function consumeWsFrameData(data, source = "ws") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ consumeWsFrameData", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Inner helper of consumeWsFrameData: parse one WS text frame and, if it yields an object, count it and capture its payload.
    const handleText = (text) => {
      log("→ handleText");
      const parsed = parseWsPayloadText(text);
      if (!parsed) return;
      chatCaptureStats.wsMessages += 1;
      captureParsedPayload(`[${source}]`, parsed);
    };
    try {
      if (typeof data === "string") {
        handleText(data);
      } else if (data && typeof data.text === "function") {
        data.text().then(handleText).catch(() => {
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
  }
  // Monkey-patch fetch, Response.prototype.json, XMLHttpRequest (open/send), and WebSocket on one realm to siphon chat/presence data from API and socket traffic.
  // Idempotent via per-target/per-prototype flags; __sniffiesChatSeen dedups the fetch vs Response.json paths so a body isn't consumed twice. Returns true if anything was patched.
  function patchChatCaptureOnWindow(targetWindow, label = "window") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ patchChatCaptureOnWindow", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    if (!targetWindow || typeof targetWindow !== "object") return false;
    try {
      if (targetWindow.__sniffiesChatCapturePatched) return false;
      // Per-realm idempotency flag so the fetch/XHR/WS patches are installed only once on this window.
      targetWindow.__sniffiesChatCapturePatched = true;
    } catch (e) {
    }
    let patched = false;
    try {
      const originalFetch = targetWindow.fetch;
      // Idempotent across realms: window and unsafeWindow may share the same fetch under SES/lockdown. Don't wrap
      // an already-wrapped fetch (the wrapper sets __sniffiesChatWrapped), which would add a redundant capture pass.
      if (typeof originalFetch === "function" && !originalFetch.__sniffiesChatWrapped) {
        // Replacement for window.fetch: awaits the real fetch, then (for sniffies /api/ JSON only) clones and parses the response to extract chat activity.
        // Returns the untouched original Response; capture work is best-effort and fully try-wrapped so it never breaks the page's fetch.
        const wrappedFetch = async (...args) => {
          log("→ wrappedFetch");
          var _a2, _b;
          const res = await originalFetch.apply(targetWindow, args);
          try {
            const url = String(((_a2 = args[0]) == null ? void 0 : _a2.url) || args[0] || "");
            if (!shouldInspectApiUrl(url)) return res;
            chatCaptureStats.apiJsonSeen += 1;
            const ct = String(((_b = res.headers) == null ? void 0 : _b.get("content-type")) || "").toLowerCase();
            if (!ct.includes("json")) return res;
            // Clone before reading JSON so the page still consumes the original response body. Mark the CLONE as
            // already-seen so the patched Response.prototype.json (invoked by the clone's .json()) does NOT capture
            // it a second time — capture must run exactly once per response.
            const clone = res.clone();
            try { clone.__sniffiesChatSeen = true; } catch (e) {}
            const data = await clone.json();
            chatCaptureStats.apiJsonParsed += 1;
            const updates = captureParsedPayload(url, data);
            if (updates > 0) {
              try {
                res.__sniffiesChatSeen = true;
              } catch (e) {
              }
            }
          } catch (e) {
          }
          return res;
        };
        wrappedFetch.__sniffiesChatWrapped = true;
        targetWindow.fetch = wrappedFetch;
        patched = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Real failure mode: a hardened/frozen fetch (e.g. under stricter SES lockdown) throwing on
      // reassignment. Logged so a blocked patch is distinguishable from a successful one, instead of
      // silently leaving chat capture non-functional on this realm.
      log(`patchChatCaptureOnWindow(${label}): fetch patch failed`, e);

              try {
                const url = String((this == null ? void 0 : this.url) || "");
                if (!shouldInspectApiUrl(url)) return data;
                // Dedup guard: if the wrapped fetch already captured this Response, the patched .json() skips re-parsing it.
                if (this && this.__sniffiesChatSeen) return data;

            });

      }
    } catch (e) {
      log(`patchChatCaptureOnWindow(${label}): XMLHttpRequest patch failed`, e);
    }
