  // Entry point: patch chat capture on both the userscript sandbox window and (if exposed) unsafeWindow, then do a forced storage scan.
  // Runs once (chatCaptureInstalled guard); deduplicates identical window refs; SES note: it patches only reachable realms and never injects a <script> into the page.
  function installChatCapture() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ installChatCapture", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (chatCaptureInstalled) return;
    let patchedCount = 0;
    const targets = [];
    try {
      targets.push(window);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    try {
      // Also patch the page realm via Tampermonkey's unsafeWindow when present; guarded so a sandboxed/SES build that hides it is fine.
      if (typeof unsafeWindow !== "undefined" && unsafeWindow) targets.push(unsafeWindow);
    } catch (e) {
    }
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const w of targets) {
      if (!w || seen.has(w)) continue;
      seen.add(w);
      if (patchChatCaptureOnWindow(w, w === window ? "sandbox-window" : "page-window")) {
        patchedCount += 1;
      }
    }
    chatCaptureInstalled = patchedCount > 0;
    if (!chatCaptureInstalled) {
      logWarn("Chat capture patch not applied");
    }
    scanStorageForChatPayloads("install", true);
  }
  // Pull a profile id out of a CSS background-image URL pointing at profile.sniffiesassets.com/<hex>/.
  // Returns null if the url doesn't match the avatar-CDN pattern.
  function extractIdFromBg(bg) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractIdFromBg", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!bg) return null;
    // Avatar CDN URLs embed the profile id as a 6+ hex path segment; this recovers the id when no DOM attribute carries it.
    const m = bg.match(/profile\.sniffiesassets\.com\/([0-9a-f]{6,})\//i);
    return m ? m[1] : null;
  }
  // Read an element's background-image, preferring the inline style value and falling back to getComputedStyle.
  // Used to recover a marker's avatar URL (which encodes the profile id).
  function getBg(el) {
    var _a;
    if (!el) return "";
    return ((_a = el.style) == null ? void 0 : _a.backgroundImage) || getComputedStyle(el).backgroundImage || "";
  }
  // Climb from a marker-related element to the actual marker root to toggle hide/show classes on.
  // Tries .maplibregl-marker / .marker-avatar / [data-testid=cv-marker], then parentElement, then the element itself.
  function resolveMarkerRoot(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ resolveMarkerRoot", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return null;
    return el.closest(".maplibregl-marker") || el.closest(".marker-avatar") || el.closest("[data-testid='cv-marker']") || el.parentElement || el;
  }
  // Resolve the profile id for a map-marker element via a fallback chain: profile-link href, then data-* attrs, then avatar background-image, then ancestor bg, then marker-container id.
  // Returns null if no strategy yields an id; tolerant of the Angular/MapLibre DOM where markers carry no stable id attribute.
  function getMarkerIdFromElement(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getMarkerIdFromElement", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return null;
    const a = el.closest("a[href*='/profile/']");
    if (a) {
      const href = a.getAttribute("href") || "";
      const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
      if (m) return m[1];
    }
    const dataEl = el.closest("[data-profile-id],[data-user-id],[data-cruiser-id]");
    if (dataEl) {
      return dataEl.getAttribute("data-profile-id") || dataEl.getAttribute("data-user-id") || dataEl.getAttribute("data-cruiser-id");
    }
    const markerImg = el.closest(".marker-avatar-image");
    if (markerImg) {
      const id = extractIdFromBg(getBg(markerImg));
      if (id) return id;
    }
    let node = el;
    while (node && node !== document.body) {
      const bg = getBg(node);
      const id = extractIdFromBg(bg);
      if (id) return id;
      node = node.parentElement;
    }
    const markerContainer = el.closest(".marker-container");
    if (markerContainer) {
      const id = markerContainer.getAttribute("id");
      if (id) return id;
    }
    const marker = el.closest(".maplibregl-marker");
    if (marker) {
      const container = marker.querySelector(".marker-container");
      if (container) {
        const id = container.getAttribute("id");
        if (id) return id;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Map a free-text attitude/position label to one canonical bucket (e.g. vers-bottom, power-bottom, dom-top-breeder, side, unspecified).
  // Order matters: specific compound matches are tested before the generic bottom/vers/top fallbacks; empty/none/unknown -> 'unspecified'.
  function normalizeAttitude(att) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeAttitude", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (att == null || att === "") return "unspecified";
    const v = String(att).toLowerCase().trim();
    if (v.includes("submissive-bottom") || v.includes("submissive bottom") || v.includes("sub bottom") || v.includes("sub-bottom")) return "submissive-bottom";
    if (v.includes("vers-bottom") || v.includes("verse-bottom") || v.includes("versbottom")) return "vers-bottom";
    if (v.includes("power-bottom") || v.includes("power bottom") || v.includes("powerbottom")) return "power-bottom";
    if (v.includes("dom-top-breeder") || v.includes("dom top breeder") || v.includes("dom top (breeder)") || v.includes("breeder")) return "dom-top-breeder";
    if (v.includes("vers-top") || v.includes("verse-top") || v.includes("verse top") || v.includes("vers top") || v.includes("verstop")) return "vers-top";
    if (v.includes("passive-top") || v.includes("passive top") || v.includes("passivetop")) return "passive-top";
    if (v === "side" || v.includes(" side ") || v.startsWith("side-") || v.endsWith("-side") || v.includes("/side") || v.includes("side/")) return "side";
    if (v.includes("unspecified") || v === "none" || v === "unknown") return "unspecified";
    if (v.includes("bottom")) return "bottom";
    if (v.includes("vers")) return "vers";
    if (v.includes("top")) return "top";
    return v;
  }
  // Given a canonical attitude bucket, return whether the corresponding state.hide* toggle is on.
  // Returns false when filtering is globally disabled (!state.enabled) or attitude is empty.
  function shouldHideAttitude(att) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideAttitude", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.enabled) return false;
    if (!att) return false;
    if (att === "bottom") return state.hideBottom;
    if (att === "vers-bottom") return state.hideVersBottom;
    if (att === "side") return state.hideSide;
    if (att === "submissive-bottom") return state.hideSubmissiveBottom;
    if (att === "power-bottom") return state.hidePowerBottom;
    if (att === "vers") return state.hideVers;
    if (att === "vers-top") return state.hideVersTop;
    if (att === "top") return state.hideTop;
    if (att === "passive-top") return state.hidePassiveTop;
    if (att === "dom-top-breeder") return state.hideDomTopBreeder;
    if (att === "unspecified") return state.hideUnspecified;
    return false;
  }
  // Given a canonical attitude bucket, return whether the corresponding state.highlight* toggle is on.
  // Returns false when filtering is globally disabled or attitude is empty.
  function shouldHighlightAttitude(att) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHighlightAttitude", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.enabled) return false;
    if (!att) return false;
    if (att === "bottom") return !!state.highlightBottom;
    if (att === "vers-bottom") return !!state.highlightVersBottom;
    if (att === "side") return !!state.highlightSide;
    if (att === "submissive-bottom") return !!state.highlightSubmissiveBottom;
    if (att === "power-bottom") return !!state.highlightPowerBottom;
    if (att === "vers") return !!state.highlightVers;
    if (att === "vers-top") return !!state.highlightVersTop;
    if (att === "top") return !!state.highlightTop;
    if (att === "passive-top") return !!state.highlightPassiveTop;
    if (att === "dom-top-breeder") return !!state.highlightDomTopBreeder;
    if (att === "unspecified") return !!state.highlightUnspecified;
    return false;
  }
  // Add the soft-hide CSS class to a marker's resolved root element (CSS hides it; the node stays in the DOM).
  function hideMarker(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return;
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(el);
    if (root) root.classList.add(HIDE_CLASS);
  }
  // Remove the soft-hide CSS class from a marker's resolved root, reversing hideMarker.
  function showMarker(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ showMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return;
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(el);
    if (root) root.classList.remove(HIDE_CLASS);
  }
  // Count how many indexed markers (idToMarker) currently carry the hide class, for the status/debug readout.
  function countHiddenMarkers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ countHiddenMarkers", traceArgs(arguments));
    let count = 0;
    for (const marker of idToMarker.values()) {
      if (!marker) continue;
      const root = resolveMarkerRoot(marker);
      if (root && root.classList.contains(HIDE_CLASS)) count += 1;
    }
    return count;
  }
  // Record a user-assigned attitude override for a profile id, persist it, and prime the attitude cache.
  // Side effects: mutates manualAttitudes, calls saveManualAttitudes (localStorage), and seeds attitudeCache with a fresh timestamp.
  function setManualAttitude(id, att) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setManualAttitude", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!id) return;
    const norm = normalizeAttitude(att);
    if (!norm) return;
    manualAttitudes[id] = norm;
    saveManualAttitudes();
    attitudeCache.set(id, { attitude: norm, ts: now() });
  }
  // Build the canonical sniffies.com profile URL for a given id.
  function profileHref(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ profileHref", traceArgs(arguments));
    return `https://sniffies.com/profile/${id}`;
  }
  // Return the first term in the list that appears as a substring of haystack, else null.
  // Used to find which include/exclude term a profile's text triggered.
  function pickFirstMatchingTerm(haystack, terms) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pickFirstMatchingTerm", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!haystack || !terms.length) return null;
    for (const term of terms) {
      if (haystack.includes(term)) return term;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Lowercase profile text for case-insensitive matching and truncate to PROFILE_TEXT_MAX_CHARS (12k) to bound work.
  // Returns '' for falsy input.
  function sanitizeProfileText(text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sanitizeProfileText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!text) return "";
    let out = String(text).toLowerCase();
    if (out.length > PROFILE_TEXT_MAX_CHARS) {
      out = out.slice(0, PROFILE_TEXT_MAX_CHARS);
    }
    return out;
  }
  // Flatten an arbitrary object to a single lowercased, length-capped search string by JSON-stringifying it.
  // Returns '' if the value can't be stringified (e.g. circular).
  function extractSearchTextFromObject(obj) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractSearchTextFromObject", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Collect only string VALUES (not object keys / field names) so include/exclude terms match the profile's
    // actual text, not metadata. Matching JSON.stringify(obj) let a term like "top" hit keys such as "hideTop"
    // or "createdAt" and produce false-positive hides/highlights unrelated to what the profile wrote.
    try {
      const parts = [];
      const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakSet();
      const walk = (val, depth) => {
        if (val == null || depth > 6) return;
        const t = typeof val;
        if (t === "string") { parts.push(val); return; }
        if (t !== "object") return;
        if (seen.has(val)) return;
        seen.add(val);
        if (Array.isArray(val)) { for (const v of val) walk(v, depth + 1); return; }
        for (const v of Object.values(val)) walk(v, depth + 1);
      };
      walk(obj, 0);
      return sanitizeProfileText(parts.join(" \n "));
    } catch (e) {
      return "";
    }
  }
  // Search-text helper for a partial/profile row: prefer row.data, falling back to the row itself.
  function extractSearchTextFromRow(row) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractSearchTextFromRow", traceArgs(arguments));
    return extractSearchTextFromObject((row == null ? void 0 : row.data) || row);
  }
  // Recursively dig a non-negative distance number out of a value, preferring keys whose name matches /distance/i.
  // Two-pass per object: distance-named numeric keys first, then recurse into nested values; arrays are searched element-wise.
  function extractDistanceFromValue(value, keyHint = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractDistanceFromValue", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && /distance/i.test(keyHint)) {
      return value >= 0 ? value : null;
    }
    if (typeof value !== "object") return null;
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const v of value) {
        const d = extractDistanceFromValue(v, keyHint);
        if (d != null) return d;
      }
      return null;
    }
    const entries = Object.entries(value);
    for (const [k, v] of entries) {
      if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && /distance/i.test(k)) {
        return v >= 0 ? v : null;
      }
    }
    for (const [k, v] of entries) {
      const d = extractDistanceFromValue(v, k);
      if (d != null) return d;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Core text-filter step for one profile: match exclude/include terms against its sanitized text and write the results into the filter/text/include caches.
  // Side effects: updates profileFilterCache, profileTextCache, and adds/removes includeMatches (respecting includeDismissed) for the highlight panel.
  function evaluateProfileFiltersFromText(id, text, distance = null, source = "partial") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ evaluateProfileFiltersFromText", traceArgs(arguments));
    const safeText = sanitizeProfileText(text);
    const matchedExclude = pickFirstMatchingTerm(safeText, profileExcludeTerms);
    const matchedInclude = pickFirstMatchingTerm(safeText, profileIncludeTerms);
    profileFilterCache.set(id, {
      excludeTerm: matchedExclude,
      includeTerm: matchedInclude,
      distance,
      ts: now()
    });
    if (matchedInclude && !includeDismissed.has(id)) {
      includeMatches.set(id, {
        id,
        href: profileHref(id),
        includeTerm: matchedInclude,
        distance,
        ts: now()
      });
    } else if (!matchedInclude) {
      includeMatches.delete(id);
    }
    profileTextCache.set(id, {
      text: safeText,
      distance,
      ts: now(),
      source
    });
  }
  // Re-run include/exclude term filters for an id from a partials row; also updates the profile summary.
  // A 'partial' update will NOT clobber a longer text already cached from a 'full' fetch (keeps the richer source).
  function evaluateProfileFiltersFromRow(id, row) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ evaluateProfileFiltersFromRow", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const incomingText = extractSearchTextFromRow(row);
    const incomingDistance = extractDistanceFromValue((row == null ? void 0 : row.data) || row);
    updateProfileSummaryFromObject(id, row, { source: "partial", distance: incomingDistance });
    const previous = profileTextCache.get(id);
    let text = incomingText;
    let source = "partial";
    // Keep the richer 'full' text: a later partial row must not shorten text already hydrated from a full fetch.
    if ((previous == null ? void 0 : previous.source) === "full" && previous.text && previous.text.length > (incomingText || "").length) {
      text = previous.text;
      source = "full";
    }
    const distance = (_a = incomingDistance != null ? incomingDistance : previous == null ? void 0 : previous.distance) != null ? _a : null;
    evaluateProfileFiltersFromText(id, text, distance, source);
  }
  // Clears includeMatches and re-applies term filters to every cached profile from stored text.
  // Used after the include/exclude term lists change so existing markers re-evaluate without refetching.
  function reevaluateProfileFiltersFromCache() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ reevaluateProfileFiltersFromCache", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    includeMatches.clear();
    for (const [id, entry] of profileTextCache.entries()) {
      const text = (entry == null ? void 0 : entry.text) || "";
      const distance = (_a = entry == null ? void 0 : entry.distance) != null ? _a : null;
      evaluateProfileFiltersFromText(id, text, distance, (entry == null ? void 0 : entry.source) || "partial");
    }
  }
  // True when exclude-terms filter is on (default-on: !== false) and this id matched an exclude term.
  function shouldHideByProfileTerms(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByProfileTerms", traceArgs(arguments));
    const f = profileFilterCache.get(id);
    return state.excludeProfileTermsEnabled !== false && !!(f == null ? void 0 : f.excludeTerm);
  }
  // True when include-terms highlight is on (default-on) and id matched an include term and wasn't dismissed.
  function shouldHighlightByProfileTerms(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHighlightByProfileTerms", traceArgs(arguments));
    const f = profileFilterCache.get(id);
    return state.includeProfileTermsEnabled !== false && !!(f == null ? void 0 : f.includeTerm) && !includeDismissed.has(id);
  }
  // Adds a highlight CSS class to the marker root; 'attitude' mode uses a separate class from text mode.
  function highlightMarker(el, mode = "text") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ highlightMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return;

    if (!root) return;
    if (mode === "attitude") root.classList.add("sniffies-soft-highlight-attitude");
    else root.classList.add("sniffies-soft-highlight");
  }
  // Removes both highlight classes (text and attitude) from the marker root.
  function unhighlightMarker(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ unhighlightMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return;
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(el);
    if (!root) return;
    root.classList.remove("sniffies-soft-highlight");
    root.classList.remove("sniffies-soft-highlight-attitude");
  }
  // Locates the live MapLibre map instance: returns the cached one, else probes known window globals.
  // Last resort brute-forces every window property for an object with matching getCanvas/queryRenderedFeatures; caches the hit.
  function findMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findMap", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e, _f;
    if (cachedMap && cachedMap.getCanvas) return cachedMap;
    const canvas = document.querySelector(".maplibregl-canvas");
    if (!canvas) return null;
    const candidates = [
      window.map,
      window._map,
      window.__map,
      (_a = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _a.map,
      (_b = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _b.mapInstance,
      (_d = (_c = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _c.mapService) == null ? void 0 : _d.map,
      (_f = (_e = window.SNIFFIES) == null ? void 0 : _e.mapService) == null ? void 0 : _f.mapInstance
    ].filter(Boolean);
    for (const c of candidates) {
      try {
        if (c.getCanvas && c.queryRenderedFeatures && c.getCanvas() === canvas) {
          cachedMap = c;
          return c;
        }
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    try {
      for (const k in window) {
        const v = window[k];
        // Brute-force duck-typing: scan every window prop for a MapLibre-like object when the named globals miss.
        if (v && typeof v === "object" && typeof v.getCanvas === "function" && typeof v.queryRenderedFeatures === "function") {
          try {
            if (v.getCanvas() === canvas) {
              cachedMap = v;
              return v;
            }
          } catch (e) {
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Mines a rendered map feature for a profile id from feature.id, known id keys, then all property values.
  // Picks the longest 6+ hex-char match found across all candidates.
  function extractIdFromFeature(feature) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractIdFromFeature", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!feature) return null;
    const candidates = [];
    if (feature.id) candidates.push(feature.id);
    const p = feature.properties || {};
    for (const k of ["_id", "id", "userId", "user_id", "profileId", "profile_id", "cruiserId", "cruiser_id"]) {
      if (p[k]) candidates.push(p[k]);
    }
    for (const v of Object.values(p)) candidates.push(v);
    let best = null;
    for (const c of candidates) {
      if (c == null) continue;
      const s = String(c);
      // Profile ids are 6+ hex chars; longest match across all feature properties wins (see best-length check below).
      const m = s.match(/[0-9a-f]{6,}/i);
      if (m) {
        if (!best || m[0].length > best.length) best = m[0];
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  // Converts viewport client coords to canvas-relative coords and queries rendered features for a profile id.
  // Returns null if the point is outside the canvas; tries each feature until one yields an id.
  function getIdFromMapAtPoint(clientX, clientY) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getIdFromMapAtPoint", traceArgs(arguments));
    const map = findMap();
    if (!map) return null;
    const canvas = map.getCanvas();
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = clientX - rect.left;
    const y = clientY - rect.top;
    if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x > rect.width || y > rect.height) return null;
    let feats = [];
    try {
      feats = map.queryRenderedFeatures([x, y]) || [];
    } catch (e) {
    }
    for (const f of feats) {
      const id = extractIdFromFeature(f);
      if (id) return id;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Fallback: resolve a marker's profile id by hit-testing the map at the marker's center point.
  // Per-marker throttle (markerAttemptTs / MAP_MARKER_RETRY_MS) avoids repeated costly map queries.
  function tryIdFromMapForMarker(markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ tryIdFromMapForMarker", traceArgs(arguments));
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl);
    if (!root) return null;
    const last = markerAttemptTs.get(root) || 0;
    // Per-marker cooldown: skip re-querying the map for this marker until MAP_MARKER_RETRY_MS has elapsed.
    if (now() - last < MAP_MARKER_RETRY_MS) return null;
    markerAttemptTs.set(root, now());
    const rect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
    return getIdFromMapAtPoint(rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + rect.height / 2);
  }
  // Strips surrounding CSS content quotes; returns '' for missing or 'none' content.
  function normalizePseudoContent(content) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizePseudoContent", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!content || content === "none") return "";
    // Strip the leading/trailing quote that getComputedStyle wraps around CSS content values.
    return content.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  }
  // Joins an element's labeling attributes (aria-label, title, alt, data-icon/testid/emoji) into one string.
  function getAttrText(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getAttrText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el || !el.getAttribute) return "";
    return [
      el.getAttribute("aria-label"),
      el.getAttribute("title"),
      el.getAttribute("alt"),
      el.getAttribute("data-icon"),
      el.getAttribute("data-testid"),
      el.getAttribute("data-emoji")
    ].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  }
  // Reads a ::before/::after pseudo-element's content text, background-image and mask-image.
  // Returns null if the pseudo carries no text, background, or mask (nothing useful to match).
  function getPseudoInfo(el, pseudo) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getPseudoInfo", traceArgs(arguments));
    try {
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el, pseudo);
      if (!cs) return null;
      const text = normalizePseudoContent(cs.content || "");
      const bg = cs.backgroundImage || "";
      const mask = cs.maskImage || cs.webkitMaskImage || "";
      if (!text && (!bg || bg === "none") && !mask) return null;
      return { text, bg, mask, className: el.className && String(el.className) || "" };
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  // Gathers icon-like descendants of a marker (i/span/div/svg/img) plus their ::before/::after pseudos.
  // Each candidate records class, text+attrs, background-image and mask-image for attitude matching.
  function collectIconCandidates(markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ collectIconCandidates", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!markerEl) return [];
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl);
    const icons = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll("i, span, div, svg, img")).filter((n) => {
      var _a, _b;
      const cls = n.className && String(n.className) || "";
      const bg = getBg(n);
      const mask = ((_a = n.style) == null ? void 0 : _a.maskImage) || ((_b = n.style) == null ? void 0 : _b.webkitMaskImage) || "";
      const txt = (n.textContent || "").trim();
      const attrs = getAttrText(n);
      return cls || bg !== "none" || mask || txt || attrs;
    }).map((n) => {
      var _a, _b;
      return {
        el: n,
        className: n.className && String(n.className) || "",
        text: [(n.textContent || "").trim(), getAttrText(n)].filter(Boolean).join(" "),
        bg: getBg(n),
        mask: ((_a = n.style) == null ? void 0 : _a.maskImage) || ((_b = n.style) == null ? void 0 : _b.webkitMaskImage) || ""
      };
    });
    const pseudoIcons = [];
    for (const n of icons.map((i) => i.el)) {
      const before = getPseudoInfo(n, "::before");
      if (before) pseudoIcons.push(before);
      const after = getPseudoInfo(n, "::after");
      if (after) pseudoIcons.push(after);
    }
    return icons.concat(pseudoIcons);
  }
  // Infers attitude from a marker's icons; peach => 'bottom', eggplant => 'top'.
  // 4-tier fallback: configured iconRules first, then emoji regex on icon text, then on class/bg/mask, then whole textContent.
  function inferAttitudeFromIcons(markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ inferAttitudeFromIcons", traceArgs(arguments));
    const icons = collectIconCandidates(markerEl);
    for (const rule of iconRules) {
      for (const ic of icons) {
        if (rule.text && ic.text && ic.text.includes(rule.text)) return rule.attitude;
        if (rule.className && ic.className && ic.className.includes(rule.className)) return rule.attitude;
        if (rule.bg && ic.bg && ic.bg.includes(rule.bg)) return rule.attitude;
        if (rule.mask && ic.mask && ic.mask.includes(rule.mask)) return rule.attitude;
      }
    }
    for (const ic of icons) {
      if (!ic.text) continue;
      // Emoji convention: peach => bottom, eggplant => top (fallback when configured iconRules don't match).
      if (PEACH_RE.test(ic.text)) return "bottom";
      if (EGGPLANT_RE.test(ic.text)) return "top";
    }
    for (const ic of icons) {
      const hay = [ic.className, ic.bg, ic.mask].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
      if (PEACH_RE.test(hay)) return "bottom";
      if (EGGPLANT_RE.test(hay)) return "top";
    }
    if (markerEl) {
      const allText = (markerEl.textContent || "").toLowerCase();
      if (PEACH_RE.test(allText)) return "bottom";
      if (EGGPLANT_RE.test(allText)) return "top";
    }
    return null;
  }
  const partialsBase = [
    "https://usw.api.sniffies.com/api/user/partials",
    "https://uswapi2.sniffies.com/api/user/partials"
  ];
  // Returns the origin of a URL, or '' if it doesn't parse.
  function getOriginFromUrl(url) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getOriginFromUrl", traceArgs(arguments));
    try {
      return new URL(url).origin;
    } catch (e) {
      return "";
    }
  }
  // Builds the ordered, de-duplicated list of API origins to try for /api/user/full.
  // Prefers the last-known-good full base and the preferred partials base before hardcoded fallbacks.
  function getFullUserBases() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getFullUserBases", traceArgs(arguments));
    const fromPartials = partialsBase.map(getOriginFromUrl).filter(Boolean);
    const fromPreferred = getOriginFromUrl(preferredBase);
    const bases = [
      preferredFullBase,
      fromPreferred,
      ...fromPartials,
      "https://usw.api.sniffies.com",
      "https://uswapi2.sniffies.com",
      "https://uswapi.sniffies.com"
    ].filter(Boolean);
    return [...new Set(bases)];
  }
  // Network timeout (ms) for the script's own outbound requests; a stalled endpoint otherwise hangs the await
  // forever, silently consuming rate-limit slots (profile fetches) or wedging the Drive sync UI on "Syncing...".
  const FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 15000;
  // fetch() wrapper that aborts after FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS via AbortController. Honors a caller-supplied signal if
  // present; otherwise an aborted request rejects, so the existing try/catch paths handle the timeout normally.
  async function fetchWithTimeout(input, init) {
    const opts = init || {};
    if (opts.signal) return fetch(input, opts);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(function () {
      try { controller.abort(); } catch (e) {}
    }, FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS);
    try {
      return await fetch(input, Object.assign({}, opts, { signal: controller.signal }));
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }
  // POSTs to /api/user/full across candidate bases until one returns an object; sends cookies (credentials include).
  // On success persists the winning base to localStorage (FULL_BASE_KEY); a 429 calls noteRateLimit and bails. Respects the rate limiter.
  async function fetchFullUser(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ fetchFullUser", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!id) return null;
    if (!canRequest()) return null;
    const bases = getFullUserBases();
    for (const base of bases) {
      if (!canRequest()) return null;
      noteRequest();
      try {
        const res = await fetchWithTimeout(`${base}/api/user/full`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ userId: id }),
          credentials: "include"
        });
        // HTTP 429 -> record the rate-limit hit and stop probing further bases/payloads (also handled in fetchPartials).
        if (res.status === 429) {
          noteRateLimit();
          return null;
        }
        if (!res.ok) continue;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data && typeof data === "object") {
          preferredFullBase = base;
          // Remember the working full-user origin so future fetches try the last-known-good base first.
          localStorage.setItem(FULL_BASE_KEY, base);
          return data;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (shouldLog("verbose")) logWarn("fetchFullUser failed", base, e && e.message);
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Fetches partial profile rows, probing base URLs x payload shapes (userIds/profileIds/ids/raw array) until an array returns.
  // Persists the winning base + payload mode to localStorage and reuses them next time; 429 -> noteRateLimit + bail. Respects the rate limiter.
  async function fetchPartials(ids) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ fetchPartials", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!ids.length) return [];
    if (!canRequest()) return [];
    const allPayloads = ["userIds", "profileIds", "ids", "array"];
    // Try the last-successful payload shape first, then the rest; the server's accepted key varies by endpoint.
    const payloads = [preferredMode, ...allPayloads.filter((k) => k !== preferredMode)];
    const bodies = {
      userIds: (ids2) => ({ userIds: ids2 }),
      profileIds: (ids2) => ({ profileIds: ids2 }),
      ids: (ids2) => ({ ids: ids2 }),
      array: (ids2) => ids2
    };
    const bases = [preferredBase, ...partialsBase.filter((b) => b !== preferredBase)];
    for (const base of bases) {
      for (const key of payloads) {
        if (!canRequest()) return [];
        noteRequest();
        try {
          const res = await fetchWithTimeout(base, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(bodies[key](ids)),
            credentials: "include"
          });
          if (res.status === 429) {
            noteRateLimit();
            return [];
          }
          if (!res.ok) continue;
          const data = await res.json();
          if (Array.isArray(data)) {
            preferredMode = key;
            preferredBase = base;
            localStorage.setItem(MODE_KEY, preferredMode);
            localStorage.setItem(BASE_KEY, preferredBase);
            const fullBase = getOriginFromUrl(base);
            if (fullBase) {
              preferredFullBase = fullBase;
              localStorage.setItem(FULL_BASE_KEY, fullBase);
            }
            return data;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          if (shouldLog("verbose")) logWarn("fetchPartials failed", base, e && e.message);
        }
      }
    }
    return [];
  }
  // Pulls data.profile.extended.sexuality.attitude from a partial row and normalizes it.
  // Returns null when the attitude key is absent (hasOwnProperty), distinguishing 'unset' from a falsy value.
  function extractAttitudeFromPartial(p) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractAttitudeFromPartial", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c;
    const sexuality = (_c = (_b = (_a = p == null ? void 0 : p.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile) == null ? void 0 : _b.extended) == null ? void 0 : _c.sexuality;
    // hasOwnProperty check: an absent attitude key means 'unknown' (null), not a profile with a falsy attitude.
    if (!sexuality || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sexuality, "attitude")) return null;
    const att = sexuality.attitude;
    return normalizeAttitude(att);
  }
  // Decides if an attitude cache entry is stale. Dual TTL: unknown attitude expires sooner (UNKNOWN_TTL_MS) than known (CACHE_TTL_MS).
  function needsRefresh(entry) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ needsRefresh", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!entry) return true;
    const age = now() - entry.ts;
    if (!entry.attitude) return age > UNKNOWN_TTL_MS;
    return age > CACHE_TTL_MS;
  }
  // Whether a profile's cached text is stale for term filtering; short-circuits false when no include/exclude terms are set.
  // 'full'-sourced text gets a longer TTL than partial-sourced text.
  function needsProfileTextRefresh(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ needsProfileTextRefresh", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!profileExcludeTerms.length && !profileIncludeTerms.length) return false;
    const entry = profileTextCache.get(id);
    if (!entry) return true;
    const age = now() - (entry.ts || 0);
    if (entry.source === "full") return age > PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_TTL_MS;
    return age > PROFILE_TEXT_TTL_MS;
  }
  // Gate for fetching the full profile text for an id: skips blocked, in-flight, and backed-off (profileTextRetryAt) ids.
  // Once past guards, fetches only when text is missing/short/stale OR the cached text does not already match any term (return !hasMatch).
  function shouldFetchFullProfileText(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldFetchFullProfileText", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!profileExcludeTerms.length && !profileIncludeTerms.length) return false;
    if (blocked.has(id)) return false;
    if (fullProfileFetchInFlight.has(id)) return false;
    if ((profileTextRetryAt.get(id) || 0) > now()) return false;
    const entry = profileTextCache.get(id);
    if (!entry) return true;
    if (entry.source === "full") {
      return now() - (entry.ts || 0) > PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_TTL_MS;
    }
    if ((entry.text || "").length < PROFILE_TEXT_MIN_CHARS) return true;
    if (now() - (entry.ts || 0) > PROFILE_TEXT_TTL_MS) return true;
    // Optimization: skip the costly full fetch if the cheap partial text already matches a term (return !hasMatch).
    const hasMatch = !!pickFirstMatchingTerm(entry.text, profileExcludeTerms) || !!pickFirstMatchingTerm(entry.text, profileIncludeTerms);
    return !hasMatch;
  }
  // Fetches full text for ONE candidate id per call, extracts search text, then re-evaluates filters for it.
  // On empty/short result sets a retry backoff (PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_RETRY_MS); updates both candidateId and the returned fullUser._id.
  async function refreshFullProfileText(reason = "interval") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ refreshFullProfileText", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c;
    if (!profileExcludeTerms.length && !profileIncludeTerms.length) return 0;
    if (!canRequest()) return 0;
    // Only ONE candidate per invocation: full-text fetches are spread across calls to respect rate limits.
    const candidateId = [...idToMarker.keys()].find(shouldFetchFullProfileText);
    if (!candidateId) return 0;
    fullProfileFetchInFlight.add(candidateId);
    try {
      const fullUser = await fetchFullUser(candidateId);
      exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastFullUserSample", fullUser || null);
      if (!fullUser || typeof fullUser !== "object") {
        profileTextRetryAt.set(candidateId, now() + PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_RETRY_MS);
        return 0;
      }
      const text = extractSearchTextFromObject(
        ((_a = fullUser == null ? void 0 : fullUser.data) == null ? void 0 : _a.profile) || (fullUser == null ? void 0 : fullUser.data) || fullUser
      );
      if (!text || text.length < PROFILE_TEXT_MIN_CHARS) {
        profileTextRetryAt.set(candidateId, now() + PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_RETRY_MS);
        return 0;
      }
      const distance = (_c = (_b = profileTextCache.get(candidateId)) == null ? void 0 : _b.distance) != null ? _c : extractDistanceFromValue((fullUser == null ? void 0 : fullUser.data) || fullUser);
      const ids = new Set([candidateId, fullUser == null ? void 0 : fullUser._id].filter(Boolean));
      for (const id of ids) {
        updateProfileSummaryFromObject(id, fullUser, { source: "full", distance });
        evaluateProfileFiltersFromText(id, text, distance, "full");
        profileTextRetryAt.delete(id);
      }
      log("full profile text updated:", { id: candidateId, reason });
      return 1;
    } catch (e) {
      profileTextRetryAt.set(candidateId, now() + PROFILE_TEXT_FULL_RETRY_MS);
      return 0;
    } finally {
      fullProfileFetchInFlight.delete(candidateId);
    }
  }
  // Lists currently-tracked marker ids that need a refresh (attitude stale or profile-text stale), excluding blocked ids.
  function getPendingIdsFromCurrentMarkers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getPendingIdsFromCurrentMarkers", traceArgs(arguments));
    const allIds = [...idToMarker.keys()];
    return allIds.filter((id) => {
      if (blocked.has(id)) return false;
      return needsRefresh(attitudeCache.get(id)) || needsProfileTextRefresh(id);
    });
  }
  // Indexes on-screen markers into idToMarker by extracting profile ids from avatar bg, marker containers, and profile links.
  // Falls back to map hit-testing when no id is found; collects ids needing attitude refresh and marks bookmarks seen.
  function scanMarkers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scanMarkers", traceArgs(arguments));
    const markerImages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".marker-avatar-image"));
    const markerContainers = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".maplibregl-marker"));
    const profileLinks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("a[href*='/profile/']"));
    const allMarkers = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const ids = [];
    for (const m of markerImages) {
      let id = extractIdFromBg(getBg(m));
      if (!id) id = tryIdFromMapForMarker(m);
      if (!id) continue;
      const root = resolveMarkerRoot(m);
      if (root) {
        allMarkers.set(id, root);
        const entry = attitudeCache.get(id);
        if (needsRefresh(entry)) ids.push(id);
      }
    }
    for (const container of markerContainers) {
      let id = getMarkerIdFromElement(container);
      if (id && allMarkers.has(id)) continue;
      if (!id) {
        const link = container.querySelector("a[href*='/profile/']");
        if (link) {
          const href = link.getAttribute("href") || "";
          const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
          if (m) id = m[1];
        }
      }
      if (!id) {
        // Throttled fallback (per-marker cooldown via markerAttemptTs) — matches the markerImages loop
        // above, so a container that can't resolve via link/data-attr doesn't re-run the costly
        // map.queryRenderedFeatures hit-test on every scan cycle.
        id = tryIdFromMapForMarker(container);
      }
      if (id) {
        allMarkers.set(id, container);
        const entry = attitudeCache.get(id);
        if (needsRefresh(entry)) ids.push(id);
      }
    }
    for (const link of profileLinks) {
      const href = link.getAttribute("href") || "";
      const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
      if (!m) continue;
      const id = m[1];
      if (allMarkers.has(id)) continue;
      const markerParent = link.closest(".maplibregl-marker") || link.closest("[class*='marker']") || link.parentElement;
      if (markerParent) {
        allMarkers.set(id, markerParent);
        const entry = attitudeCache.get(id);
        if (needsRefresh(entry)) ids.push(id);
      }
    }
    idToMarker.clear();
    for (const [id, marker] of allMarkers) {
      idToMarker.set(id, marker);
      markBookmarkSeen(id);
    }
    return { markerCount: markerImages.length + markerContainers.length, ids };
  }
  // Core decision engine: rescans markers, then for each runs hide/show/highlight in a FIXED order (do not reorder).
  // Hide gates in sequence: blocked -> profile-terms -> recent-chats-24h -> recent-chats-2h -> any-chats
  // -> missing-chat-history -> offline-2h -> repeated-delete -> unanswered-out -> attitude.
  // Otherwise shows the marker, applies text/attitude highlights, updates its chat badge; refreshes match panel and filter launchers.
  function applyHiding(alreadyScanned) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applyHiding", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Callers that just ran scanMarkers() synchronously (e.g. hideNow) pass true to skip the redundant re-scan.
    // The async paths (updatePartials batch loop, scheduleMapRefresh) keep the default re-scan: the DOM can change
    // across their await/timer boundaries, so re-scanning there is intentional, not redundant.
    if (!alreadyScanned) scanMarkers();
    // Master switch: when disabled the engine hides nothing. Re-show every marker and clear highlights, then
    // bail — otherwise exclude-text and the chat/online filters keep hiding while "Enabled" is off. Global chat
    // already short-circuits on !state.enabled, so this keeps map and global-chat behavior consistent.
    if (!state.enabled) {
      for (const [id, marker] of idToMarker.entries()) {
        if (!marker) continue;
        unhighlightMarker(marker);
        showMarker(marker);
      }
      // renderMatchPanel() already calls updateTextFilterMatchCounts() internally.
      renderMatchPanel();
      updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
      applyGlobalChatHiding();
      ensureGlobalChatHideObserver();
      return;
    }
    // Defensive de-stick: clear HIDE_CLASS from every element currently carrying it, then re-apply it below in the
    // SAME synchronous pass (no repaint between, so no flicker). Prevents a marker getting permanently stuck hidden
    // when scanMarkers remaps an id to a different element while the old node still carries the class.
    for (const staleHidden of document.querySelectorAll("." + HIDE_CLASS)) staleHidden.classList.remove(HIDE_CLASS);
    // Markers that stay visible; their chat-badge layout reads run in a second pass below (see note) to avoid thrash.
    const pendingBadgeUpdates = [];
    for (const [id, marker] of idToMarker.entries()) {
      if (!marker || !document.body.contains(marker)) {
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      unhighlightMarker(marker);
      if (blocked.has(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByProfileTerms(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByRecentChats(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByRecentChats2h(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByAnyChats(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByMissingChatHistory(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByNotOnlineWindow(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByRepeatedDelete(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      if (shouldHideByUnansweredOut(id)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
        continue;
      }
      const manual = manualAttitudes[id];
      const cache = attitudeCache.get(id);
      let att = manual || (cache == null ? void 0 : cache.attitude) || null;
      if (!att) att = inferAttitudeFromIcons(marker);
      if (shouldHideAttitude(att)) {
        hideMarker(marker);
        hideAllFloatingChatBadgesForId(id);
      } else {
        showMarker(marker);
        if (shouldHighlightByProfileTerms(id)) highlightMarker(marker, "text");
        if (shouldHighlightAttitude(att)) highlightMarker(marker, "attitude");
        pendingBadgeUpdates.push([id, marker]);
      }
      ensureMarkerHoverBindings(id, marker);
    }
    // Second pass: badge positioning reads layout (getComputedStyle/getBoundingClientRect); running it after all
    // hide/show class writes above prevents per-marker read-write layout thrash on this hot path (~2s interval).
    for (const [id, marker] of pendingBadgeUpdates) updateMarkerChatBadge(id, marker);
    // renderMatchPanel() already calls updateTextFilterMatchCounts() internally.
    renderMatchPanel();
    updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
    updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
    updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
    updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
    updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
    applyGlobalChatHiding();
    ensureGlobalChatHideObserver();
  }
  // Boot-registered timers and top-level observers, tracked so teardownSniffies() can stop all background work
  // (e.g. on a runtime hand-off). Deliberately NOT auto-wired to pagehide — that would break bfcache restores;
  // call it manually via the __sniffiesTeardown debug global if a newer runtime takes over the tab.
  const bootTimers = [];
  const bootObservers = [];
  function teardownSniffies() {
    const timers = bootTimers.splice(0), observers = bootObservers.splice(0);
    for (const t of timers) { try { clearInterval(t); } catch (e) {} }
    for (const o of observers) { try { o.disconnect(); } catch (e) {} }
    try { if (globalChatHideObserver) globalChatHideObserver.disconnect(); } catch (e) {}
    try { if (profileContainerObserver) profileContainerObserver.disconnect(); } catch (e) {}
    try { window.removeEventListener("scroll", scheduleChatBadgeRefresh, { capture: true }); } catch (e) {}
    try { window.removeEventListener("resize", scheduleChatBadgeRefresh); } catch (e) {}
    return { timersCleared: timers.length, observersDisconnected: observers.length };
  }
  let applyHidingScheduled = false;
  // Coalesces bursts of applyHiding() into a single pass per microtask, for high-frequency incremental callers
  // (e.g. the per-batch partials loop). Direct callers that need the DOM updated synchronously stay on applyHiding().
  function scheduleApplyHiding() {
    if (applyHidingScheduled) return;
    applyHidingScheduled = true;
    queueMicrotask(() => { applyHidingScheduled = false; applyHiding(); });
  }
  // Attitude/position buckets normalizeAttitude recognizes as real (vs sexuality/body/raw tokens).
  const GLOBAL_CHAT_ATTITUDES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
    "bottom", "vers-bottom", "vers", "vers-top", "top", "side",
    "submissive-bottom", "power-bottom", "passive-top", "dom-top-breeder"
  ]);
  // Parse the attitude/position out of a global-chat message header (the userStats line, e.g.
  // "26m, 5'5\", average, vers bottom, clean cut/ftm"). Split on commas, hyphenate spaces so a
  // display value like "vers bottom" normalizes to "vers-bottom", and return the first token that
  // maps to a real attitude bucket. Lets attitude filtering work for authors not on the map.
  function parseGlobalChatAttitude(statsText) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parseGlobalChatAttitude", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!statsText) return null;
    for (const raw of String(statsText).split(",")) {
      const tok = raw.trim();
      if (!tok) continue;
      const att = normalizeAttitude(tok.replace(/\s+/g, "-"));
      if (GLOBAL_CHAT_ATTITUDES.has(att)) return att;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // True when a global-chat message's author would be hidden by the active map filters. The message
  // element's `id` attribute IS the author's profile id. Filters checked by id work for any author;
  // attitude comes from the cache when the author is on the map, else parsed from the message header.
  // (Profile-text exclude/include only applies to authors already loaded on the map.)
  function globalChatMessageHidden(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ globalChatMessageHidden", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el || !state.enabled) return false;
    // Master toggle: when the global-chat feature is switched off, hide nothing here regardless of the map
    // filters. '=== false' (not falsy) so a legacy saved state missing the key keeps the default-on behavior.
    if (state.hideGlobalChat === false) return false;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(el.id || (el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute("id")));
    if (!id) return false;
    if (blocked.has(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByProfileTerms(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByRecentChats(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByRecentChats2h(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByAnyChats(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByMissingChatHistory(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByNotOnlineWindow(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByRepeatedDelete(id)) return true;
    if (shouldHideByUnansweredOut(id)) return true;
    let att = manualAttitudes[id] || (attitudeCache.get(id) || {}).attitude || null;
    if (!att) {
      const stats = el.querySelector && el.querySelector('[data-testid="userStats"]');
      att = parseGlobalChatAttitude(stats ? stats.textContent : "");
    }
    if (att && shouldHideAttitude(normalizeAttitude(att))) return true;
    return false;
  }
  // Resolve the author profile id from a Global Chat message element, or a click target inside one.
  // The message element's `id` attribute IS the author's profile id (see globalChatMessageHidden
  // above). Returns null when target isn't inside a globalChat-message element.
  function getGlobalChatMessageIdFromElement(target) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getGlobalChatMessageIdFromElement", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!target || !(target instanceof Element)) return null;
    const msgEl = target.closest('[data-testid="globalChat-message"]');
    if (!msgEl) return null;
    return normalizeProfileId(msgEl.id || (msgEl.getAttribute && msgEl.getAttribute("id")));
  }
  // Toggle the hide class by author for exactly the given message elements. Shared by the full sweep
  // (applyGlobalChatHiding) and the mutation-observer's targeted per-added-node sweep below.
  function applyGlobalChatHidingToMessages(msgs) {
    for (const el of msgs) {
      const target = (el.closest && el.closest('[data-testid="global-chat-message-container"]')) || el;
      target.classList.toggle("sniffies-gc-hide", globalChatMessageHidden(el));
    }
  }
  // Sweep every global-chat message and toggle the hide class by author. Idempotent: re-shows a
  // message when its author is no longer filtered. No-op when no global-chat messages are present.
  // This is the O(n) correctness backstop, called from the 2s applyHiding() loop (catches an author's
  // filter state changing with no new message arriving) — NOT from the mutation observer, which does
  // its own targeted sweep instead (see ensureGlobalChatHideObserver).
  function applyGlobalChatHiding() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ applyGlobalChatHiding", traceArgs(arguments));
    const msgs = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="globalChat-message"]');
    if (!msgs.length) return;
    applyGlobalChatHidingToMessages(msgs);
  }
  // Lazily attach a MutationObserver to the global-chat list so streamed messages hide instantly.
  // Re-attaches if the list element is replaced on navigation; disconnects when the list is gone.
  function ensureGlobalChatHideObserver() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ ensureGlobalChatHideObserver", traceArgs(arguments));
    const list = document.querySelector('[data-testid="global-chat-list-container"]');
    if (!list) {
      if (globalChatHideObserver) { try { globalChatHideObserver.disconnect(); } catch (e) {} globalChatHideObserver = null; }
      return;
    }
    if (globalChatHideObserver && globalChatHideObserver.__target === list && document.body.contains(list)) return;
    if (globalChatHideObserver) { try { globalChatHideObserver.disconnect(); } catch (e) {} }
    globalChatHideObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
      // Sweep only the newly-added message nodes instead of re-querying/re-evaluating the whole list —
      // the list only grows monotonically as the room streams messages, and the 2s applyHiding() loop
      // already provides the full-list correctness backstop (e.g. a filter changing with no new
      // message), so there is nothing left for a debounced full re-sweep here to catch.
      const added = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      for (const mutation of mutations) {
        for (const node of mutation.addedNodes) {
          if (!(node instanceof Element)) continue;
          if (node.matches && node.matches('[data-testid="globalChat-message"]')) added.add(node);
          if (node.querySelectorAll) {
            for (const el of node.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="globalChat-message"]')) added.add(el);
          }
        }
      }
      if (added.size) applyGlobalChatHidingToMessages(added);
    });
    globalChatHideObserver.observe(list, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    globalChatHideObserver.__target = list;
    applyGlobalChatHiding();
  }
  // Scans markers, fetches partials for pending ids in batches of 50, caches attitude, re-evaluates filters, re-applies hiding.
  // Reentrancy-coalesced via busy/queued flags: a call during a run sets queued and re-runs once via queueMicrotask.
  // When nothing is pending it still tries one refreshFullProfileText pass. Respects the rate limiter (bails if !canRequest).
  async function updatePartials(reason = "interval") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updatePartials", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (updatePartialsBusy) {
      updatePartialsQueued = true;
      return;
    }
    updatePartialsBusy = true;
    try {
      const { markerCount } = scanMarkers();
      const pending = getPendingIdsFromCurrentMarkers();
      log("scan markers:", { markerCount, markerWithId: idToMarker.size, pending: pending.length, reason });
      if (!pending.length) {
        const fullTextUpdated2 = await refreshFullProfileText(reason);
        if (fullTextUpdated2) applyHiding();
        return;
      }
      if (!canRequest()) return;
      // Partials are fetched 50 ids at a time; applyHiding runs after each batch for incremental UI updates.
      const batchSize = 50;
      for (let i = 0; i < pending.length; i += batchSize) {
        const batch = pending.slice(i, i + batchSize);
        const rows = await fetchPartials(batch);
        if (rows.length) {
          for (const p of rows) {
            const id = p == null ? void 0 : p._id;
            if (!id) continue;
            ingestPresenceTimes(p, id);
            const att = extractAttitudeFromPartial(p);
            attitudeCache.set(id, { attitude: att, ts: now() });
            evaluateProfileFiltersFromRow(id, p);
            exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastPartialsSample", p);
          }
        } else {
          for (const id of batch) {
            if (!attitudeCache.has(id)) attitudeCache.set(id, { attitude: null, ts: now() });
          }
        }
        scheduleApplyHiding();
      }
      const fullTextUpdated = await refreshFullProfileText(reason);
      if (fullTextUpdated) applyHiding();
    } finally {
      updatePartialsBusy = false;
      if (updatePartialsQueued) {
        updatePartialsQueued = false;
        // Reentrancy coalescing: a call that arrived mid-run is replayed exactly once after the current run finishes.
        queueMicrotask(() => updatePartials("queued"));
      }
    }
  }
  // Debounces a re-apply: 350ms after the first call (single pending timer) runs applyHiding then updatePartials.
  // Re-entrant calls while a timer is pending are ignored.
  function scheduleMapRefresh(reason = "mutation") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleMapRefresh", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (pendingRefreshTimer) return;
    pendingRefreshTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      pendingRefreshTimer = 0;
      applyHiding();
      // Fire-and-forget: catch rejections so a transient fetch/DOM error doesn't surface as an unhandled
      // promise rejection (browsers and Node 15+ treat these as errors).
      updatePartials(reason).catch((e) => logWarn("updatePartials failed", e));
    }, 350);
  }
  // Manual 'Hide Now': scans + applies hiding immediately, fetches partials, then re-applies after 300ms and reports counts in the status line.
  // If rate-limited it skips the fetch and reports the post-scan counts only.
  async function hideNow() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideNow", traceArgs(arguments));
    setStatus("Hide Now: scanning...");
    const { markerCount } = scanMarkers();
    const pending = getPendingIdsFromCurrentMarkers();
    applyHiding(true);
    const hiddenAfterScan = countHiddenMarkers();
    const includeAfterScan = includeMatches.size;
    if (!canRequest()) {
      setStatus(`Hide Now: markers ${markerCount}, pending ${pending.length}, hidden ${hiddenAfterScan}, includes ${includeAfterScan} (rate limited)`);
      return;
    }
    await updatePartials();
    setTimeout(() => {
      applyHiding();
      const hiddenFinal = countHiddenMarkers();
      const includeFinal = includeMatches.size;
      setStatus(`Hide Now: markers ${markerCount}, pending ${pending.length}, hidden ${hiddenFinal}, includes ${includeFinal}`);
    }, 300);
  }
  // Appends a {id, ts} entry to hideHistory (for undo) and persists it; caps history at the most recent 500 entries.
  function recordHideAction(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordHideAction", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return;
    hideHistory.push({ id, ts: now() });
    // Cap undo history at the most recent 500 hides to bound memory.
    if (hideHistory.length > 500) hideHistory = hideHistory.slice(-500);
    saveHideHistory();
  }
  // Blocks a profile id now: adds to blocked set, records for undo, persists, and re-applies hiding.
  // Returns false if id is empty or already blocked; optionally tracks the supplied marker element.
  function hideProfileNow(profileId, markerEl = null) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hideProfileNow", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    if (blocked.has(id)) return false;
    blocked.add(id);
    blockedAt.set(id, now());
    recordHideAction(id);
    if (markerEl) idToMarker.set(id, resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl));
    saveBlockedSet();
    saveBlockedAtMap();
    applyHiding();
    setStatus(`Hidden profile: ${id}`);
    return true;
  }
  // Unblocks a profile id, persists the blocked set, and re-applies hiding. Returns false if id wasn't blocked.
  function unhideProfileNow(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ unhideProfileNow", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    if (!blocked.has(id)) return false;
    blocked.delete(id);
    blockedAt.delete(id);
    saveBlockedSet();
    saveBlockedAtMap();
    applyHiding();
    setStatus(`Unhid profile: ${id}`);
    return true;
  }
  // Pops hideHistory until it finds a still-blocked id, unblocks it, persists, and re-applies hiding.
  // Skips entries already unblocked; returns false (and a status message) when there's nothing left to undo.
  function undoLastHide() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ undoLastHide", traceArgs(arguments));
    while (hideHistory.length) {
      const row = hideHistory.pop();
      const id = normalizeProfileId(row == null ? void 0 : row.id);
      if (!id || !blocked.has(id)) continue;
      blocked.delete(id);
      blockedAt.delete(id);
      saveHideHistory();
      saveBlockedSet();
      saveBlockedAtMap();
      applyHiding();
      setStatus(`Undo hide: ${id}`);
      return true;
    }
    saveHideHistory();
    setStatus("Undo hide: nothing to undo");
    return false;
  }
  // Toggles a profile id's blocked state (used by shift-block); records for undo and exposes a debug global on block.
  // Tracks the marker element if given (else a null placeholder), persists the blocked set, and re-applies hiding.
  function toggleBlock(id, markerEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleBlock", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!id) return;
    if (blocked.has(id)) {
      blocked.delete(id);
      blockedAt.delete(id);
      setStatus(`Unhid profile: ${id}`);
    } else {
      blocked.add(id);
      blockedAt.set(id, now());
      recordHideAction(id);
      exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastShiftBlock", { id, ts: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString() });
      setStatus(`Shift-blocked: ${id}`);
    }
    if (markerEl) {
      const trackedMarker = resolveMarkerRoot(markerEl);
      idToMarker.set(id, trackedMarker);
    } else if (!idToMarker.has(id)) {
      idToMarker.set(id, null);
    }
    saveBlockedSet();
    saveBlockedAtMap();
    applyHiding();
  }
  // Shift+left-click handler: resolves the profile id from the target, map hit-test, or a /profile/ link, then toggles its block.
  // No-ops unless shiftKey and (button 0 or non-mouse); on success fully suppresses the event (incl. stopImmediatePropagation).
  function handleShiftBlock(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleShiftBlock", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!e.shiftKey) return;
    if (typeof e.button === "number" && e.button !== 0) return;
    let id = getMarkerIdFromElement(e.target);
    let markerEl = resolveMarkerRoot(e.target);
    if (!id) {
      id = getIdFromMapAtPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    }
    if (!id) {
      const profileLink = e.target.closest("a[href*='/profile/']");
      if (profileLink) {
        const href = profileLink.getAttribute("href") || "";
        const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
        if (m) id = m[1];
      }
    }
    if (!id) return;
    if (!markerEl || markerEl === document.body) {
      markerEl = e.target.closest(".maplibregl-marker") || e.target.closest(".marker-avatar") || e.target.closest("[data-profile-id], [data-user-id], [data-cruiser-id]") || resolveMarkerRoot(e.target);
    }
    toggleBlock(id, markerEl);
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  // On a chat route, returns true if the target sits inside the active chat message list / input pane.
  // Excludes this script's own panels/launchers; walks up <=8 ancestors from the chat input and message container collecting matching roots.
  // Used so a middle-click in a chat window triggers an auto-intro instead of hiding a marker.
  function isMiddleClickChatWindowTarget(target) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isMiddleClickChatWindowTarget", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isChatRoute()) return false;
    if (!target || !(target instanceof Element)) return false;
    if (target.closest(".sniffies-soft-panel, .sniffies-soft-panel-launcher, .sniffies-chat-phrases-panel, .sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher, .sniffies-match-panel, .sniffies-bookmarks-panel, .sniffies-appointments-panel, .sniffies-notes-modal")) {
      return false;
    }
    const chatWindowRoots = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const scopeSelector = [
      "[data-testid*='message-list']",
      "[data-testid*='messages']",
      "[class*='message-list']",
      "[class*='messages-list']",
      "[class*='chat-messages']",
      "[class*='conversation-messages']",
      "[class*='thread-messages']",
      "form",
      "[class*='chat']",
      "[class*='conversation']",
      "[class*='thread']",
      "[class*='pane']"
    ].join(", ");
    // Nested helper of isMiddleClickChatWindowTarget: walks up <=8 ancestors from a start node collecting chat-scope roots.
    // Mutates the enclosing closure's chatWindowRoots and seen set; always includes the start node itself.
    const collectRoots = (start) => {
      log("→ collectRoots");
      var _a;
      if (!start || !(start instanceof Element)) return;
      let node = start;
      let depth = 0;
      while (node && node instanceof Element && depth <= 8) {
        // Collect each ancestor that is the start node or matches a chat-scope selector; 'seen' dedupes across two walks.
        if (!seen.has(node) && (node === start || ((_a = node.matches) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(node, scopeSelector)))) {
          seen.add(node);
          chatWindowRoots.push(node);
        }
        node = node.parentElement;
        depth += 1;
      }
    };
    collectRoots(findChatInputElement());
    collectRoots(pickChatMessageContainer());
    return chatWindowRoots.some((root) => root === target || root.contains(target));
  }
  // Middle-mousedown handler: inside a chat window it fires an auto-intro for the conversation's profile; elsewhere it hides the marker.
  // Resolves the id via marker element / map hit-test; suppresses the event fully and reports 'Already hidden' when no change.
  function handleMiddleMark(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleMiddleMark", traceArgs(arguments));
    // button === 1 is the middle mouse button; ignore all other buttons/events.
    if (!e || e.type !== "mousedown" || e.button !== 1) return;
    const target = e.target instanceof Element ? e.target : null;
    if (!target) return;
    // Global Chat: middle-click a message hides/blocks its author, same as a map-marker middle-click,
    // instead of falling into the chat-window auto-intro branch below. /global-chat satisfies
    // isChatRoute() too, so without this check first, a middle-click on a message here would be
    // mistaken for a DM composer/window and would send that person an intro instead of hiding them.
    const globalChatMsgId = isChatRoute() ? getGlobalChatMessageIdFromElement(target) : null;
    if (globalChatMsgId) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const changed = hideProfileNow(globalChatMsgId);
      if (!changed) setStatus(`Already hidden: ${globalChatMsgId}`);
      return;
    }
    if (isMiddleClickChatWindowTarget(target)) {
      const id2 = getProfileIdForAutoMessageEvent(e);
      if (!id2) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      triggerShiftRightIntroForProfile(id2);
      return;
    }
    const markerTarget = target.closest(".maplibregl-marker, .marker-avatar, [data-testid='cv-marker'], .marker-avatar-image, .marker-container");
    const markerEl = markerTarget ? resolveMarkerRoot(markerTarget) : null;
    let id = markerTarget ? getMarkerIdFromElement(markerTarget) : null;
    if (!id) {
      id = getIdFromMapAtPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    }
    if (!id) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const changed = hideProfileNow(id, markerEl);
    if (!changed) {
      const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
      if (normalizedId && blocked.has(normalizedId)) {
        setStatus(`Already hidden: ${normalizedId}`);
      }
    }
  }
  // True if an auto-message went to this profile within AUTO_MESSAGE_COOLDOWN_MS (2 min).
  // Reads the howdySentAt timestamp map; used to throttle repeat auto-sends.
  function recentAutoMessageSent(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recentAutoMessageSent", traceArgs(arguments));
    const ts = parseTimestamp(howdySentAt[id]);
    // Cooldown window is 2 minutes (AUTO_MESSAGE_COOLDOWN_MS); blocks rapid repeat auto-messages to the same profile.
    return !!ts && now() - ts < AUTO_MESSAGE_COOLDOWN_MS;
  }
  // Stamp the current time as this profile's last auto-message and persist howdySentAt.
  // No-op when id is falsy.
  function recordAutoMessageSent(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ recordAutoMessageSent", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!id) return;
    howdySentAt[id] = now();
    saveHowdySentMap();
  }
  // Record a phrase as used for a profile (lowercased) and bump global phrase usage stats.
  // Dedupes and caps the per-profile used list to the most recent 300; persists phraseHistory.
  function markPhraseUsedForProfile(profileId, phrase) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ markPhraseUsedForProfile", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const cleanPhrase = String(phrase || "").trim();
    if (!id || !cleanPhrase) return;
    recordPhraseUsage(cleanPhrase);
    const key = cleanPhrase.toLowerCase();
    const row = phraseHistory[id] && typeof phraseHistory[id] === "object" ? phraseHistory[id] : {};
    const used = Array.isArray(row.used) ? row.used.map((v) => String(v || "").toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (!used.includes(key)) used.push(key);
    phraseHistory[id] = {
      // Caps each profile's used-phrase history to the most recent 300 entries.
      used: used.slice(-300),
      updatedAt: now()
    };
    savePhraseHistory();
  }
  // Return the set of phrases (lowercased) already used for this profile from phraseHistory.
  // Empty set when id is invalid or no history exists.
  function getUsedPhraseSet(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getUsedPhraseSet", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const row = phraseHistory[id];
    const used = Array.isArray(row == null ? void 0 : row.used) ? row.used : [];
    return new Set(used.map((v) => String(v || "").toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean));
  }
  // Set of lowercased outgoing chat-preview texts (direction 'you') for this profile.
  // Used so phrases visibly already sent in the thread are treated as used.
  function getRecentOutgoingPhraseSet(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getRecentOutgoingPhraseSet", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const rows = chatPreviewByProfile.get(id) || [];
    const values = rows.filter((row) => (row == null ? void 0 : row.direction) === "you").map((row) => String((row == null ? void 0 : row.text) || "").trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);
    return new Set(values);
  }
  // Pick a random unused phrase for a profile from the chosen category (intro/other/all).
  // Treats both history and recent-outgoing texts as used; when the pool is exhausted it resets the profile's history and reuses the full list.
  function pickRandomPhraseForProfile(profileId, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pickRandomPhraseForProfile", traceArgs(arguments));
    const category = String((options == null ? void 0 : options.category) || "all").trim().toLowerCase();
    const phrases = category === "intro" ? getIntroPhrases() : category === "other" ? getOtherPhrases() : getCannedPhrases();
    if (!phrases.length) return null;
    const used = getUsedPhraseSet(profileId);
    const recentOutgoing = getRecentOutgoingPhraseSet(profileId);
    for (const phrase of recentOutgoing) used.add(phrase);
    let available = phrases.filter((phrase) => !used.has(String(phrase).toLowerCase()));
    if (!available.length) {
      available = phrases.slice();
      const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
      if (id) {
        phraseHistory[id] = { used: [], updatedAt: now() };
        savePhraseHistory();
      }
    }
    if (!available.length) return null;
    const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * available.length);
    return String(available[idx] || "").trim() || null;
  }
  // Extract a normalized profile id from a /profile/<hex> URL path (6+ hex chars).
  // Returns null when the path has no profile segment.
  function getProfileIdFromPath(pathname = location.pathname) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileIdFromPath", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Matches a profile id of 6+ hex chars in a /profile/<id> path (case-insensitive).
    const m = String(pathname || "").match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
    return m ? normalizeProfileId(m[1]) : null;
  }
  // Heuristic visibility check: in-DOM, not disabled, not display:none/hidden/~transparent, and >1x1px.
  // Used to filter candidate inputs/buttons before clicking or scoring them.
  function isElementVisible(el) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isElementVisible", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el || !(el instanceof Element)) return false;
    if (!document.body.contains(el)) return false;
    if (el.hasAttribute("disabled")) return false;
    const style2 = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (style2.display === "none" || style2.visibility === "hidden" || parseFloat(style2.opacity || "1") < 0.01) return false;
    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return rect.width > 1 && rect.height > 1;
  }
  // Find the page's chat composer by scoring visible textareas/text inputs/contenteditables.
  // Excludes this script's own panels; scores on message/chat placeholder+aria, element type, and being in the lower part of the viewport.
  function findChatInputElement() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findChatInputElement", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const candidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("textarea, input[type='text'], [contenteditable='true']")).filter(isElementVisible).filter((el) => !el.closest(".sniffies-soft-panel, .sniffies-soft-panel-launcher, .sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher, .sniffies-match-panel, .sniffies-bookmarks-panel, .sniffies-appointments-panel, .sniffies-notes-modal"));
    if (!candidates.length) return null;
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;
    for (const el of candidates) {
      const ph = String(((_a = el.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(el, "placeholder")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const aria = String(((_b = el.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(el, "aria-label")) || "").toLowerCase();
      let score = 0;
      if (ph.includes("message") || ph.includes("chat")) score += 4;
      if (aria.includes("message") || aria.includes("chat")) score += 4;
      if (el.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || el.isContentEditable) score += 2;
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.bottom > window.innerHeight * 0.45) score += 1;
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = el;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  // Set text into a chat input (contenteditable or value-based) and dispatch input/change events.
  // Returns whether it filled; falls back to a plain Event('input') if InputEvent throws.
  function fillChatInput(el, text) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ fillChatInput", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!el) return false;
    const value = String(text || "");
    try {
      if (el.isContentEditable) {
        el.focus();
        el.textContent = value;
        try {
          el.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent("input", { bubbles: true, data: value, inputType: "insertText" }));
        } catch (e) {
          el.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
        }
        return true;
      }
      if ("value" in el) {
        el.focus();
        el.value = value;
        el.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
        el.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
        return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return false;
  }
  // Find and click a visible Send button near the input (scoped to its chat/message/form ancestor).
  // Matches button text 'send' or send in aria-label/title; skips this script's panels; returns success.
  function clickChatSendButton(inputEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ clickChatSendButton", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const scope = (inputEl == null ? void 0 : inputEl.closest("form, [class*='chat'], [class*='message']")) || document;
    const buttons = Array.from(scope.querySelectorAll("button, [role='button']")).filter(isElementVisible).filter((btn) => !btn.closest(".sniffies-soft-panel, .sniffies-soft-panel-launcher, .sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher, .sniffies-match-panel, .sniffies-bookmarks-panel, .sniffies-appointments-panel, .sniffies-notes-modal"));
    for (const btn of buttons) {
      const text = String(btn.textContent || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const aria = String(((_a = btn.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(btn, "aria-label")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const title = String(((_b = btn.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(btn, "title")) || "").toLowerCase();
      if (text === "send" || aria.includes("send") || title.includes("send")) {
        btn.click();
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  // Simulate Enter (keydown+keypress+keyup) on the input to submit a chat message.
  // Fallback used when no clickable Send button is found.
  function pressEnterToSend(inputEl) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pressEnterToSend", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!inputEl) return false;
    try {
      inputEl.focus();
      inputEl.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Enter", code: "Enter", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
      inputEl.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keypress", { key: "Enter", code: "Enter", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
      inputEl.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keyup", { key: "Enter", code: "Enter", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  // Build a unique token 'id:ts:nonce' identifying one auto-message send.
