  // Nonce is 6 base-36 random chars; used to correlate the opened tab with its close/signal.
  function makeAutoMessageToken(profileId, ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ makeAutoMessageToken", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId) || "unknown";
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts) || now();
    // 6-char base-36 random nonce making each auto-message token unique.
    const nonce = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
    return `${id}:${t}:${nonce}`;
  }
  // Track an opened auto-message tab handle (with profile id and openedAt) keyed by token.
  // Ignored unless the handle exposes a close() method; lets the opener later close that tab.
  function registerAutoMessageTabHandle(token, profileId, handle) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ registerAutoMessageTabHandle", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!token || !handle || typeof handle.close !== "function") return;
    autoMessageTabHandles.set(String(token), {
      id: normalizeProfileId(profileId),
      handle,
      openedAt: now()
    });
  }
  // Close a tracked auto-message tab by token, or failing that the first one matching profileId.
  // Calls the stored handle.close() (swallowing errors) and removes it from the map; returns whether one closed.
  function closeAutoMessageTabHandle(token = "", profileId = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ closeAutoMessageTabHandle", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d;
    const normalizedToken = String(token || "").trim();
    if (normalizedToken) {
      const row = autoMessageTabHandles.get(normalizedToken);
      if (row) {
        try {
          (_b = (_a = row.handle) == null ? void 0 : _a.close) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(_a);
        } catch (e) {
        }
        autoMessageTabHandles.delete(normalizedToken);
        return true;
      }
    }
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    for (const [key, row] of autoMessageTabHandles.entries()) {
      if ((row == null ? void 0 : row.id) !== id) continue;
      try {
        (_d = (_c = row.handle) == null ? void 0 : _c.close) == null ? void 0 : _d.call(_c);
      } catch (e) {
      }
      autoMessageTabHandles.delete(key);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  // Drop tracked auto-message tab handles older than 45 minutes (or missing openedAt).
  // Bounds growth of autoMessageTabHandles.
  function pruneAutoMessageTabHandles() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pruneAutoMessageTabHandles", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Prunes tab handles older than 45 minutes.
    const cutoff = now() - 45 * 60 * 1e3;
    for (const [key, row] of autoMessageTabHandles.entries()) {
      if (!row || (parseTimestamp(row.openedAt) || 0) < cutoff) {
        autoMessageTabHandles.delete(key);
      }
    }
  }
  // Broadcast an auto-message event to other tabs by writing it to localStorage (AUTO_MESSAGE_SIGNAL_KEY).
  // Adds a random nonce so each write differs and fires a 'storage' event in listening tabs.
  function emitAutoMessageSignal(payload = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ emitAutoMessageSignal", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(payload.profileId || payload.id);
    const event = {
      type: String(payload.type || "sent"),
      profileId: id,
      phrase: String(payload.phrase || "").trim(),
      ts: parseTimestamp(payload.ts) || now(),
      token: String(payload.token || "").trim(),
      source: String(payload.source || "tab"),
      nonce: Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)
    };
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(AUTO_MESSAGE_SIGNAL_KEY, JSON.stringify(event));
    } catch (e) {
    }
  }
  // Find the main chat message-list element by scoring visible candidates by data-testid/class.
  // Score combines area (min 20000px), capped text length, and child count; returns the highest.
  function pickChatMessageContainer() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ pickChatMessageContainer", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const candidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(
      "[data-testid*='message-list'], [data-testid*='messages'], [class*='message-list'], [class*='messages-list'], [class*='chat-messages'], [class*='conversation-messages'], [class*='thread-messages']"
    )).filter(isElementVisible);
    if (!candidates.length) return null;
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;
    for (const el of candidates) {
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const area = rect.width * rect.height;
      // Skip containers smaller than 20000px^2 when choosing the chat message list.
      if (area < 2e4) continue;
      const textLen = (el.innerText || "").length;
      const childCount = ((_a = el.children) == null ? void 0 : _a.length) || 0;
      const score = area + Math.min(6e3, textLen * 2) + Math.min(3e3, childCount * 24);
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = el;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  // Append a locally-rendered 'You' bubble into the open chat for a just-sent message, then scroll to bottom.
  // Only when the route profile matches; dedupes via a data attribute so the same send isn't injected twice.
  function injectLocalOutgoingMessageIntoChat(profileId, text, ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ injectLocalOutgoingMessageIntoChat", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const routeId = getChatContextProfileId();
    const msg = String(text || "").trim();
    const at = parseTimestamp(ts) || now();
    if (!id || !routeId || id !== routeId || !msg) return false;
    const list = pickChatMessageContainer();
    if (!list) return false;
    // Dedupe key (id + timestamp + first 64 chars) prevents injecting the same outgoing bubble twice.
    const dedupe = `${id}:${at}:${msg.slice(0, 64).toLowerCase()}`;
    const existingLocal = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll(".sniffies-local-outgoing-message")).find((el) => {
      var _a;
      return String(((_a = el == null ? void 0 : el.dataset) == null ? void 0 : _a.dedupe) || "") === dedupe;
    });
    if (existingLocal) return false;
    const bubble = document.createElement("div");
    bubble.className = "sniffies-local-outgoing-message";
    bubble.dataset.dedupe = dedupe;
    const body = document.createElement("div");
    body.textContent = msg;
    const meta = document.createElement("div");
    meta.className = "meta";
    meta.textContent = `You \xB7 ${new Date(at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" })}`;
    bubble.appendChild(body);
    bubble.appendChild(meta);
    list.appendChild(bubble);
    try {
      // +9999 overshoot forces scroll fully to the bottom of the message list.
      list.scrollTop = list.scrollHeight + 9999;
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return true;
  }
  // Nudge the site to fetch new messages for the current chat: clicks a refresh/reload/sync button and re-focuses the composer.
  // Only acts when on that profile's chat route; also dispatches a window focus event. Returns whether anything fired.
  function forceRefreshCurrentChatView(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ forceRefreshCurrentChatView", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId || getChatContextProfileId());
    const routeId = getChatContextProfileId();
    if (!id || !routeId || id !== routeId) return false;
    let refreshed = false;
    const refreshButtons = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button, [role='button']")).filter(isElementVisible).filter((btn) => {
      var _a, _b;
      const text = String(btn.textContent || "").toLowerCase();
      const aria = String(((_a = btn.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(btn, "aria-label")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const title = String(((_b = btn.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(btn, "title")) || "").toLowerCase();
      // Identifies a refresh/reload/sync control by its text, aria-label, or title.
      return /(refresh|reload|new messages|check updates|sync)/.test(`${text} ${aria} ${title}`);
    });
    if (refreshButtons.length) {
      try {
        refreshButtons[0].click();
        refreshed = true;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    try {
      const input = findChatInputElement();
      if (input) {
        input.dispatchEvent(new Event("focus", { bubbles: true }));
        input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
        refreshed = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    try {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event("focus"));
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return refreshed;
  }
  // After sending, update all local state for one outgoing message: cooldown, chat activity, preview, phrase-used, injected bubble.
  // Optionally marks the phrase used and refreshes the live chat; then refreshes badge/phrase/bookmark UI.
  function syncOutgoingMessageState(profileId, text, ts, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ syncOutgoingMessageState", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const message = String(text || "").trim();
    const at = parseTimestamp(ts) || now();
    if (!id) return false;
    recordAutoMessageSent(id);
    upsertChatActivity(id, at, 0);
    if (message) {
      addChatPreviewMessage(id, "out", at, message);
      if (options.markPhrase !== false) markPhraseUsedForProfile(id, message);
      injectLocalOutgoingMessageIntoChat(id, message, at);
    }
    if (options.refreshCurrentChat !== false) {
      forceRefreshCurrentChatView(id);
    }
    scheduleChatBadgeRefresh();
    renderChatPhrasePanel();
    refreshBookmarkPanelMaybe();
    return true;
  }
  // Apply a cross-tab 'sent' auto-message signal: sync local outgoing state and close the matching opener tab.
  // Ignores non-'sent' types and payloads without a valid profile id.
  function handleAutoMessageSignalPayload(payload) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleAutoMessageSignalPayload", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return false;
    const type = String(payload.type || "").toLowerCase();
    if (type !== "sent") return false;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(payload.profileId || payload.id);
    if (!id) return false;
    const phrase = String(payload.phrase || "").trim();
    const ts = parseTimestamp(payload.ts) || now();
    const token = String(payload.token || "").trim();
    syncOutgoingMessageState(id, phrase, ts, { markPhrase: true, refreshCurrentChat: true });
    closeAutoMessageTabHandle(token, id);
    return true;
  }
  // Storage-event listener: on a new AUTO_MESSAGE_SIGNAL_KEY value, parse it and dispatch to handleAutoMessageSignalPayload.
  // Ignores other keys and unparseable JSON.
  function handleAutoMessageStorageEvent(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleAutoMessageStorageEvent", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!e || e.key !== AUTO_MESSAGE_SIGNAL_KEY || !e.newValue) return;
    try {
      const payload = JSON.parse(e.newValue);
      handleAutoMessageSignalPayload(payload);
    } catch (e2) {
    }
  }
  // Queue an auto-message and open the profile's chat in a background tab to send it (GM_openInTab, else window.open).
  // Honors cooldown unless bypassed; builds a URL with the automsg/token/autoclose params; falls back to same-tab navigation (autoclose off) if opening a tab fails. Returns an {ok,...} result.
  function openAutoMessageChat(id, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ openAutoMessageChat", traceArgs(arguments));
    const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
    if (!normalizedId) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid-id" };
    const customPhrase = String((options == null ? void 0 : options.phrase) || "").trim();
    const phraseCategory = String((options == null ? void 0 : options.phraseCategory) || "all").trim().toLowerCase();
    const bypassCooldown = !!(options == null ? void 0 : options.bypassCooldown);
    const suppressStatus = !!(options == null ? void 0 : options.suppressStatus);
    const statusPrefix = String((options == null ? void 0 : options.statusPrefix) || "Message queued");
    if (!bypassCooldown && recentAutoMessageSent(normalizedId)) {
      setStatus(`Message cooldown: ${normalizedId.slice(0, 8)}`);
      return { ok: false, reason: "cooldown" };
    }
    const phrase = customPhrase || pickRandomPhraseForProfile(normalizedId, { category: phraseCategory });
    if (!phrase) {
      const hint = phraseCategory === "intro" ? "No intro messages configured" : "No quick phrases configured";
      setStatus(hint);
      return { ok: false, reason: "no-phrase" };
    }
    const pending = queuePendingAutoMessage(normalizedId, phrase);
    if (!pending) return { ok: false, reason: "queue-failed" };
    const token = makeAutoMessageToken(normalizedId, pending.ts);
    const url = new URL(`https://sniffies.com/profile/${normalizedId}/chat`);
    url.searchParams.set(AUTO_MESSAGE_URL_PARAM, "1");
    url.searchParams.set("sf_msg_ts", String(pending.ts));
    url.searchParams.set(AUTO_MESSAGE_TOKEN_PARAM, token);
    url.searchParams.set(AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOCLOSE_PARAM, "1");
    let opened = false;
    let tabHandle = null;
    try {
      if (typeof GM_openInTab === "function") {
        try {
          tabHandle = GM_openInTab(url.toString(), {
            active: false,
            insert: true,
            setParent: true
          });
        } catch (e) {
          tabHandle = GM_openInTab(url.toString(), false);
        }
        opened = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
    if (!opened) {
      try {
        const w = window.open(url.toString(), "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
        tabHandle = w;
        opened = !!w;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    if (opened) {
      registerAutoMessageTabHandle(token, normalizedId, tabHandle);
    }
    if (!opened) {
      url.searchParams.set(AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOCLOSE_PARAM, "0");
      window.location.href = url.toString();
    }
    if (!suppressStatus) setStatus(`${statusPrefix}: ${normalizedId.slice(0, 8)}`);
    return { ok: true, id: normalizedId, phrase, phraseCategory, token };
  }
  // Try to close the open profile chat by clicking a visible close/dismiss/collapse/exit/back control.
  // Skips this script's own panels; if none found and on a /profile chat route, falls back to history.back().
  function closeCurrentProfileChatWindow(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ closeCurrentProfileChatWindow", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId || getChatContextProfileId());
    const closeCandidates = Array.from(
      document.querySelectorAll("button, [role='button'], a")
    ).filter(isElementVisible);
    for (const el of closeCandidates) {
      if (el.closest(".sniffies-soft-panel, .sniffies-soft-panel-launcher, .sniffies-chat-phrases-panel, .sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher, .sniffies-match-panel, .sniffies-bookmarks-panel, .sniffies-appointments-panel, .sniffies-notes-modal")) {
        continue;
      }
      const text = String(el.textContent || "").toLowerCase();
      const aria = String(((_a = el.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(el, "aria-label")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const title = String(((_b = el.getAttribute) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(el, "title")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const cls = String(el.className || "").toLowerCase();
      const label = `${text} ${aria} ${title} ${cls}`;
      if (!/(close|dismiss|collapse|exit|back)/.test(label)) continue;
      try {
        el.click();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    if (id && /\/profile\/[0-9a-f]{6,}(?:\/chat)?/i.test(location.pathname) && history.length > 1) {
      try {
        history.back();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  // Resolve which profile an auto-message mouse event targets, trying several sources in order.
  // Marker element id, then map point under cursor, then nearest /profile/ link href, then the route chat id, then current profile.
  function getProfileIdForAutoMessageEvent(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileIdForAutoMessageEvent", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    if (!e) return null;
    const routeChatProfileId = getChatContextProfileId();
    let id = getMarkerIdFromElement(e.target);
    if (!id && typeof e.clientX === "number" && typeof e.clientY === "number") {
      id = getIdFromMapAtPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    }
    if (!id) {
      const profileLink = (_b = (_a = e.target) == null ? void 0 : _a.closest) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(_a, "a[href*='/profile/']");
      if (profileLink) {
        const href = profileLink.getAttribute("href") || "";
        const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
        if (m) id = m[1];
      }
    }
    if (!id && routeChatProfileId) id = routeChatProfileId;
    if (!id) id = getCurrentProfileId();
    return normalizeProfileId(id);
  }
  // Send a random 'intro' phrase to a profile via shift-right-click.
  // If already on that profile's chat, sends inline then auto-closes the chat after 220ms; otherwise queues+opens a background tab (openAutoMessageChat).
  function triggerShiftRightIntroForProfile(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ triggerShiftRightIntroForProfile", traceArgs(arguments));
    const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
    if (!normalizedId) return false;
    const routeChatProfileId = getChatContextProfileId();
    const inProfileChat = !!routeChatProfileId && /\/profile\/[0-9a-f]{6,}\/chat/i.test(location.pathname);
    const onCurrentProfileChat = inProfileChat && routeChatProfileId === normalizedId;
    if (onCurrentProfileChat) {
      const phrase = pickRandomPhraseForProfile(normalizedId, { category: "intro" });
      if (!phrase) {
        setStatus("No intro messages configured");
        return false;
      }
      const sent = sendMessageInCurrentChat(normalizedId, phrase, { markPhrase: true });
      if (!sent) {
        setStatus(`Intro send failed: ${normalizedId.slice(0, 8)}`);
        return false;
      }
      syncOutgoingMessageState(normalizedId, phrase, now(), { markPhrase: false, refreshCurrentChat: true });
      exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastAutoMessage", { id: normalizedId, text: phrase, ts: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), source: "chat-shift-right" });
      setStatus(`Intro sent: ${normalizedId.slice(0, 8)}`);
      setTimeout(() => {
        closeCurrentProfileChatWindow(normalizedId);
      }, 220);
      return true;
    }
    const queued = openAutoMessageChat(normalizedId, {
      phraseCategory: "intro",
      statusPrefix: "Intro queued"
    });
    return !!(queued == null ? void 0 : queued.ok);
  }
  // mousedown handler: on Shift+right-button (no alt/ctrl/meta) over a resolvable profile, fire the shift-right intro send.
  // Prevents default and stops propagation (incl. stopImmediatePropagation) so the page's right-click is suppressed.
  function handleShiftRightAutoMessageDown(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleShiftRightAutoMessageDown", traceArgs(arguments));
    // Only Shift + right mouse button (button 2), with no alt/ctrl/meta, triggers the intro auto-message.
    if (!e || e.button !== 2 || !e.shiftKey || e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
    const id = getProfileIdForAutoMessageEvent(e);
    if (!id) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    triggerShiftRightIntroForProfile(id);
  }
  // contextmenu handler: when Shift is held over a resolvable profile, suppress the native context menu.
  // Only blocks the menu (preventDefault/stopPropagation); the actual send is done by the mousedown handler.
  function handleDoubleRightMessage(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleDoubleRightMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!e || !e.shiftKey || e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
    const id = getProfileIdForAutoMessageEvent(e);
    if (!id) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (typeof e.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  // Close this auto-send tab after AUTO_MESSAGE_TAB_CLOSE_DELAY_MS (900ms).
  // Retries 250ms later via about:blank + close() if the first window.close() didn't take.
  function maybeCloseAutoMessageTab() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ maybeCloseAutoMessageTab", traceArgs(arguments));
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        window.close();
      } catch (e) {
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          if (!window.closed) {
            window.open("about:blank", "_self");
            window.close();
          }
        } catch (e) {
        }
      }, 250);
    // Auto-send tab self-closes after 900ms (AUTO_MESSAGE_TAB_CLOSE_DELAY_MS).
    }, AUTO_MESSAGE_TAB_CLOSE_DELAY_MS);
  }
  // On a chat page opened with the sf_automsg param, auto-send the queued pending phrase for that profile.
  // Bails (clearing URL params) if no pending, pending too old (>10 min), already in cooldown, or empty phrase.
  // Delete the auto-message queuing/tracking URL params and replace the current history entry.
  // Shared by every exit path of maybeAutoSendPendingMessageFromUrl so they can't drift out of sync.
  function clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url) {
    url.searchParams.delete(AUTO_MESSAGE_URL_PARAM);
    url.searchParams.delete("sf_msg_ts");
    url.searchParams.delete(AUTO_MESSAGE_TOKEN_PARAM);
    url.searchParams.delete(AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOCLOSE_PARAM);
    history.replaceState({}, "", url.toString());
  }
  function maybeAutoSendPendingMessageFromUrl() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ maybeAutoSendPendingMessageFromUrl", traceArgs(arguments));
    let url;
    try {
      url = new URL(location.href);
    } catch (e) {
      return;
    }
    if (url.searchParams.get(AUTO_MESSAGE_URL_PARAM) !== "1") return;
    const messageToken = String(url.searchParams.get(AUTO_MESSAGE_TOKEN_PARAM) || "").trim();
    const shouldAutoClose = url.searchParams.get(AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOCLOSE_PARAM) === "1";
    const profileId = getProfileIdFromPath(url.pathname);
    if (!profileId) return;
    const pending = readPendingAutoMessage(profileId);
    if (!pending) {
      clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url);
      return;
    }
    // Discards a queued auto-message older than 10 minutes instead of sending it.
    if (now() - (parseTimestamp(pending.ts) || 0) > AUTO_MESSAGE_PENDING_MAX_AGE_MS) {
      consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
      clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url);
      return;
    }
    if (recentAutoMessageSent(profileId)) {
      consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
      clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url);
      return;
    }
    const phrase = String(pending.phrase || "").trim();
    if (!phrase) {
      consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
      clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url);
      return;
    }
    let attempts = 0;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      attempts += 1;
      // The SPA can navigate to a different chat while this poller is waiting for the composer to
      // mount (up to ~12s) — re-verify we're still on the target profile's chat before each attempt so
      // a late-arriving composer never receives a message queued for someone else's conversation.
      if (getChatContextProfileId() !== profileId) {
        if (attempts >= AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOSEND_ATTEMPTS) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
          setStatus(`Auto-send failed: navigated away (${profileId.slice(0, 8)})`);
        }
        return;
      }
      const input = findChatInputElement();
      if (!input) {
        if (attempts >= AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOSEND_ATTEMPTS) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
          setStatus(`Auto-send failed: composer not found (${profileId.slice(0, 8)})`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (!fillChatInput(input, phrase)) {
        if (attempts >= AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOSEND_ATTEMPTS) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
          setStatus(`Auto-send failed: compose blocked (${profileId.slice(0, 8)})`);
        }
        return;
      }
      const clicked = clickChatSendButton(input);
      if (!clicked) pressEnterToSend(input);
      const sentTs = now();
      syncOutgoingMessageState(profileId, phrase, sentTs, { markPhrase: true, refreshCurrentChat: true });
      consumePendingAutoMessage(profileId);
      exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastAutoMessage", { id: profileId, text: phrase, ts: new Date(sentTs).toISOString() });
      exposeGlobal("__sniffiesLastHowdy", { id: profileId, text: phrase, ts: new Date(sentTs).toISOString() });
      emitAutoMessageSignal({
        type: "sent",
        profileId,
        phrase,
        ts: sentTs,
        token: messageToken,
        source: "auto-tab"
      });
      setStatus(`Message sent: ${profileId.slice(0, 8)}`);
      clearInterval(timer);
      clearAutoMessageUrlParams(url);
      if (shouldAutoClose) maybeCloseAutoMessageTab();
    // Polls for the composer every 500ms, up to 24 attempts (~12s) before giving up.
    }, AUTO_MESSAGE_AUTOSEND_INTERVAL_MS);
  }
  const NAV_PANEL_SKIP_SELECTOR = ".sniffies-soft-panel, .sniffies-soft-panel-launcher, .sniffies-chat-phrases-panel, .sniffies-chat-phrases-launcher, .sniffies-match-panel, .sniffies-bookmarks-panel, .sniffies-appointments-panel, .sniffies-notes-modal";
  // True if the event target is a text-entry control (text-like input, textarea, select, or contenteditable).
  // Excludes non-text input types (checkbox/radio/button/etc.); used to suppress hotkeys while typing.
  function isTypingTarget(target) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isTypingTarget", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!(target instanceof Element)) return false;
    if (target instanceof HTMLInputElement) {
      const t = String(target.type || "").toLowerCase();
      if (t === "checkbox" || t === "radio" || t === "button" || t === "submit" || t === "reset" || t === "file" || t === "range" || t === "color") return false;
      return true;
    }
    if (target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return true;
    if (target instanceof HTMLSelectElement) return true;
    if (target.isContentEditable) return true;
    return false;
  }
  // Return the first visible /global-chat link not inside this script's panels.
  // Null when none present.
  function findGlobalChatLink() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findGlobalChatLink", traceArgs(arguments));
    const links = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("a[href*='/global-chat'], a[href$='/global-chat/']")).filter(isElementVisible);
    for (const link of links) {
      if (link.closest(NAV_PANEL_SKIP_SELECTOR)) continue;
      return link;
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Open Global Chat: no-op if already there, else click a found link, else hard-navigate to /global-chat.
  // Sets a status message; returns whether it acted.
  function openGlobalChatViaHotkey() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ openGlobalChatViaHotkey", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (/\/global-chat/i.test(location.pathname)) {
      setStatus("Already in Global Chat");
      return true;
    }
    const link = findGlobalChatLink();
    if (link) {
      try { link.click(); setStatus("Opened Global Chat"); return true; } catch (e) {}
    }
    try {
      window.location.assign("https://sniffies.com/global-chat");
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      setStatus("Global Chat navigation failed");
      return false;
    }
  }
  // Score visible links/buttons to find the Chats/Messages/Inbox list toggle.
  // Weights exact 'chats|messages|inbox' text/aria/title, related class/aria phrases, and matching href; returns the best only if its score >= 6.
  function findChatsListToggle() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findChatsListToggle", traceArgs(arguments));
    const candidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("a, button, [role='button'], [role='link']")).filter(isElementVisible);
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;
    for (const el of candidates) {
      if (el.closest(NAV_PANEL_SKIP_SELECTOR)) continue;
      const text = String(el.textContent || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const aria = String((el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute("aria-label")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const title = String((el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute("title")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const cls = String(el.className || "").toLowerCase();
      const dataTest = String((el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute("data-testid")) || "").toLowerCase();
      const href = el.tagName === "A" ? String(el.getAttribute("href") || "") : "";
      const blob = `${aria} ${title} ${dataTest}`;
      let score = 0;
      if (/^(chats|messages|inbox)$/.test(text)) score += 8;
      if (/^(chats|messages|inbox)$/.test(aria) || /^(chats|messages|inbox)$/.test(title)) score += 10;
      if (/(chat[- ]?list|open chats|show chats|toggle chats|message[- ]?list|conversation[- ]?list|inbox)/.test(blob)) score += 6;
      if (/(chat[- ]?list|chats[- ]?toggle|chats[- ]?icon|chats[- ]?button)/.test(cls)) score += 5;
      if (/\/(chats|messages|inbox)\/?$/i.test(href)) score += 12;
      if (text.length > 24) score -= 4;
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = el;
      }
    }
    return bestScore >= 6 ? best : null;
  }
  // Click the detected Chats-list toggle and set status; returns whether it found and clicked one.
  function openChatsListViaHotkey() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ openChatsListViaHotkey", traceArgs(arguments));
    const toggle = findChatsListToggle();
    if (toggle) {
      try { toggle.click(); setStatus("Toggled Chats list"); return true; } catch (e) {}
    }
    setStatus("Chats list toggle not found");
    return false;
  }
  // Collect clickable elements flagged unread via a list of unread/new-message badge selectors.
  // Skips this script's panels, dedupes, climbs to the nearest clickable ancestor, and returns them in document order.
  function gatherUnreadChatTargets() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ gatherUnreadChatTargets", traceArgs(arguments));
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const out = [];
    const badgeSelectors = [
      "[class*='unread' i]",
      "[class*='new-message' i]",
      "[class*='hasUnread' i]",
      "[class*='has-unread' i]",
      "[aria-label*='unread' i]",
      "[data-testid*='unread' i]",
      "[data-unread]"
    ];
    for (const sel of badgeSelectors) {
      let nodes;
      try { nodes = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(sel)); } catch (e) { continue; }
      for (const node of nodes) {
        if (!isElementVisible(node)) continue;
        if (node.closest(NAV_PANEL_SKIP_SELECTOR)) continue;
        const clickable = node.closest("a[href*='/profile/'], a[href*='/chat'], li, [role='button'], [role='link'], [class*='chat-item' i], [class*='conversation' i], [class*='thread' i], [class*='message-item' i]") || node;
        const target = isElementVisible(clickable) ? clickable : node;
        if (!target || seen.has(target)) continue;
        seen.add(target);
        out.push(target);
      }
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => {
      const cmp = a.compareDocumentPosition(b);
      if (cmp & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) return -1;
      if (cmp & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_PRECEDING) return 1;
      return 0;
    });
    return out;
  }
  let lastUnreadNavTarget = null;
  // Cycle to the next/prev unread chat and click it, remembering the last target to advance from.
  // Wraps around the list; scrolls the target into view; sets status with the pending count.
  function navigateUnreadChat(direction) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ navigateUnreadChat", traceArgs(arguments));
    const items = gatherUnreadChatTargets();
    if (!items.length) {
      setStatus("No unread chats found");
      return false;
    }
    let idx = -1;
    if (lastUnreadNavTarget && document.body.contains(lastUnreadNavTarget)) {
      idx = items.indexOf(lastUnreadNavTarget);
    }
    let next;
    if (direction === "next") {
      next = idx >= 0 ? items[(idx + 1) % items.length] : items[0];
    } else {
      next = idx >= 0 ? items[(idx - 1 + items.length) % items.length] : items[items.length - 1];
    }
    if (!next) return false;
    lastUnreadNavTarget = next;
    try {
      if (typeof next.scrollIntoView === "function") next.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
      next.click();
      setStatus(`Unread ${direction === "next" ? "next" : "prev"} (${items.length} pending)`);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      setStatus("Unread click failed");
      return false;
    }
  }
  // keydown handler for single-key nav: g=Global Chat, c=Chats list, n/b=next/prev unread.
  // Ignored while typing, with modifiers, or on key repeat; g/c only fire off a chat route, n/b only on one. Prevents default when it acts.
  function handleNavigationHotkeys(e) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ handleNavigationHotkeys", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!e || e.defaultPrevented) return;
    if (e.repeat) return;
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey || e.shiftKey) return;
    if (!e.key || e.key.length !== 1) return;
    if (isTypingTarget(e.target)) return;
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
    // Nav hotkeys: g=Global Chat, c=Chats list, n=next unread, b=prev unread.
    if (key !== "g" && key !== "c" && key !== "n" && key !== "b") return;
    const inChat = isChatRoute();
    if ((key === "g" || key === "c") && inChat) return;
    if ((key === "n" || key === "b") && !inChat) return;
    let acted = false;
    if (key === "g") acted = openGlobalChatViaHotkey();
    else if (key === "c") acted = openChatsListViaHotkey();
    else if (key === "n") acted = navigateUnreadChat("next");
    else if (key === "b") acted = navigateUnreadChat("prev");
    if (acted) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    }
  }

  // Capture-phase listener (true) so the script intercepts the shift-right-click before the page.
  document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleShiftRightAutoMessageDown, true);

  document.addEventListener("copy", handleChatCopyCapture, true);
  document.addEventListener("click", handleSniffiesCopyDialogClick, true);
  document.addEventListener("keydown", handleNavigationHotkeys, true);

  // Copy the normalized blocked-ID list to the clipboard as pretty JSON; falls back to a prompt() if the Clipboard API is unavailable.
  function exportBlocked() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ exportBlocked", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const text = JSON.stringify(normalizeIdList([...blocked]), null, 2);
    if ((_a = navigator.clipboard) == null ? void 0 : _a.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => alert("Blocked list copied to clipboard."));
    } else {
      prompt("Copy blocked list:", text);
    }
  }
  // Prompt for a blocked list (JSON array or comma/space/newline-separated IDs), normalize, MERGE into the blocked set, then save and re-apply hiding.
  // On JSON parse failure, falls back to splitting on whitespace/commas.
  function importBlocked() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ importBlocked", traceArgs(arguments));
    const input = prompt("Paste blocked list (JSON array or IDs separated by commas/spaces/newlines):");
    if (!input) return;
    let ids = [];
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(input);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) ids = parsed;
      else if (parsed) ids = [parsed];
    } catch (e) {
      ids = input.split(/[\s,]+/).filter(Boolean);
    }
    const normalized = normalizeIdList(ids);
    const importedAt = now();
    for (const id of normalized) {
      blocked.add(id);
      // Backfill blockedAt (see loadNotesFromGDrive) so a stale pre-existing reply can't immediately
      // auto-unhide a profile blocked via this import.
      if (!blockedAt.has(id)) blockedAt.set(id, importedAt);
    }
    saveBlockedSet();
    saveBlockedAtMap();
    applyHiding();
    alert(`Imported ${normalized.length} IDs (merged).`);
  }
  // Serialize the full local dataset (blocked, notes, ratings, bookmarks, stats, last-500 hide history, phrase data) to JSON and copy it.
  // Optionally AES-GCM/PBKDF2-encrypts with a passphrase; blank passphrase exports plain JSON; null prompt cancels.
  async function exportData() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ exportData", traceArgs(arguments));
    const data = {
      blocked: [...blocked],
      notes,
      ratings: profileRatings,
      bookmarks: [...bookmarks.values()],
      chatDeletionStats,
      seenChatDeletionEvents,
      hideHistory: hideHistory.slice(-500),
      phraseHistory,
      phraseStats
    };
    const plainText = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
    let outputText = plainText;
    let exportKind = "plain";
    const passphrasePrompt = prompt("Optional: enter a passphrase to encrypt this export. Leave blank for plain JSON:");
    if (passphrasePrompt === null) return;
    const passphrase = String(passphrasePrompt || "").trim();
    if (passphrase) {
      try {
        const encrypted = await encryptStringWithPassphrase(plainText, passphrase);
        outputText = JSON.stringify(encrypted, null, 2);
        exportKind = "encrypted";
      } catch (e) {
        alert(`Failed to encrypt export: ${(e == null ? void 0 : e.message) || e}`);
        return;
      }
    }
    const copied = await copyTextWithFallback(outputText, "Copy exported data:");
    if (copied) {
      alert(exportKind === "encrypted" ? "Encrypted export copied to clipboard." : "Data export copied to clipboard.");
    }
  }
  // Parse pasted export and merge each present section into local state (blocked/notes/ratings/bookmarks/stats/history/phrases), saving each.
  // Auto-decrypts passphrase-encrypted exports first; merges (does not replace), then re-applies hiding and re-renders bookmarks.
  async function importData() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ importData", traceArgs(arguments));
    const input = prompt("Paste exported data (JSON format):");
    if (!input) return;
    try {
      let data = JSON.parse(input);
      if ((data == null ? void 0 : data.encrypted) === true) {
        const passphrase = prompt("This export is encrypted. Enter passphrase:");
        if (passphrase == null) return;
        try {
          const plain = await decryptStringWithPassphrase(data, passphrase);
          data = JSON.parse(plain);
        } catch (e) {
          alert("Unable to decrypt data. Check passphrase and try again.");
          return;
        }
      }
      if (data.blocked && Array.isArray(data.blocked)) {
        // Normalize like importBlocked()/loadNotesFromGDrive() do — otherwise a malformed/non-string
        // entry from a pasted export could land in `blocked`, which every other call site assumes
        // contains only normalized hex-id strings.
        const importedAt = now();
        for (const id of normalizeIdList(data.blocked)) {
          blocked.add(id);
          // Backfill blockedAt (see loadNotesFromGDrive) so a stale pre-existing reply can't
          // immediately auto-unhide a profile blocked via this import.
          if (!blockedAt.has(id)) blockedAt.set(id, importedAt);
        }
        saveBlockedSet();
        saveBlockedAtMap();
      }
      if (data.notes && typeof data.notes === "object") {
        // Object.entries (own enumerable keys only) avoids importing inherited/prototype-polluted keys; normalize
        // the id and coerce the value to a string before persisting.
        for (const [rawId, val] of Object.entries(data.notes)) {
          const id = normalizeProfileId(rawId);
          if (!id) continue;
          notes[id] = String(val == null ? "" : val);
        }
        saveNotes();
      }
      if (data.ratings && typeof data.ratings === "object") {
        // Object.entries (own enumerable keys only) avoids importing inherited/prototype-polluted keys, matching
        // the notes path above; normalizeProfileId already filters keys, so this is defense-in-depth consistency.
        for (const [id, ratingRaw] of Object.entries(data.ratings)) {
          const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
          const rating = normalizeProfileRating(ratingRaw);
          if (!normalizedId || !rating) continue;
          profileRatings[normalizedId] = rating;
        }
        saveProfileRatings();
      }
      if (data.bookmarks) {
        const imported = Array.isArray(data.bookmarks) ? data.bookmarks : Object.values(data.bookmarks || {});
        for (const row of imported) {
          const id = normalizeProfileId((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileId) || (row == null ? void 0 : row.id));
          if (!id) continue;
          bookmarks.set(id, {
            profileId: id,
            profileUrl: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileUrl) || profileUrlForId(id)),
            profileLabel: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileLabel) || id.slice(0, 10)),
            alias: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.alias) || "").trim().slice(0, 80),
            categories: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.categories),
            tags: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.tags),
            lastSeenAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastSeenAt) || 0,
            createdAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.createdAt) || now(),
            updatedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt) || now()
          });
        }
        saveBookmarks();
      }
      if (data.chatDeletionStats && typeof data.chatDeletionStats === "object") {
        for (const [rawId, value] of Object.entries(data.chatDeletionStats)) {
          const id = normalizeProfileId(rawId);
          const count = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number((value == null ? void 0 : value.count) || value || 0)));
          if (!id || !count) continue;
          chatDeletionStats[id] = {
            count,
            lastDeletedTs: parseTimestamp(value == null ? void 0 : value.lastDeletedTs) || 0,
            updatedAt: parseTimestamp(value == null ? void 0 : value.updatedAt) || now()
          };
        }
        saveChatDeletionStats();
      }
      if (data.seenChatDeletionEvents && typeof data.seenChatDeletionEvents === "object") {
        for (const [fingerprint, tsValue] of Object.entries(data.seenChatDeletionEvents)) {
          const ts = parseTimestamp(tsValue);
          if (!fingerprint || !ts) continue;
          seenChatDeletionEvents[fingerprint] = ts;
        }
        saveSeenChatDeletionEvents();
      }
      if (Array.isArray(data.hideHistory)) {
        hideHistory = data.hideHistory.map((row) => ({ id: normalizeProfileId(row == null ? void 0 : row.id), ts: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.ts) || now() })).filter((row) => !!row.id).slice(-500);
        saveHideHistory();
      }
      if (data.phraseHistory && typeof data.phraseHistory === "object") {
        // Merge per-key (don't replace wholesale) so importing doesn't wipe existing local phrase history; skip junk.
        for (const [rawId, row] of Object.entries(data.phraseHistory)) {
          const id = normalizeProfileId(rawId);
          if (!id || !row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
          phraseHistory[id] = row;
        }
        savePhraseHistory();
      }
      if (data.phraseStats && typeof data.phraseStats === "object") {
        // Merge per-key (don't replace wholesale); skip non-object rows so a malformed export can't poison the map.
        // Unlike phraseHistory/notes/ratings above, this key is arbitrary phrase text, not a profile id run
        // through normalizeProfileId — so it isn't otherwise filtered, and phraseStats[key] = row is a bracket
        // assignment ([[Set]] semantics): a key literally named "__proto__" would reassign phraseStats' own
        // prototype to the attacker-controlled row object. Reject the dangerous key names explicitly.
        for (const [key, row] of Object.entries(data.phraseStats)) {
          if (!key || key === "__proto__" || key === "constructor" || key === "prototype") continue;
          if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
          phraseStats[key] = row;
        }
        savePhraseStats();
      }
      applyHiding();
      renderBookmarkPanel();
      alert("Imported blocked list, notes, and bookmarks.");
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Invalid format: " + e.message);
    }
  }
  // True only when both GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID and GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI are non-empty (Drive sync is off by default in public builds).
  function isGDriveOAuthConfigured() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isGDriveOAuthConfigured", traceArgs(arguments));
    return !!String(GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID || "").trim() && !!String(GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI || "").trim();
  }
  // Coerce a token-meta object to {savedAt, expiresAt, source}, defaulting savedAt to now.
  // If expiresAt is missing but a token exists, synthesize one at savedAt + OAUTH_DEFAULT_TOKEN_TTL_MS.
  function normalizeGDriveTokenMeta(meta, token = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ normalizeGDriveTokenMeta", traceArgs(arguments));
    const savedAt = Number(meta == null ? void 0 : meta.savedAt) || now();
    const expiresAtRaw = Number(meta == null ? void 0 : meta.expiresAt) || 0;
    const expiresAt = expiresAtRaw > 0 ? expiresAtRaw : token ? savedAt + OAUTH_DEFAULT_TOKEN_TTL_MS : 0;
    return {
      savedAt,
      expiresAt,
      source: String((meta == null ? void 0 : meta.source) || "oauth-pkce")
    };
  }
  // Treat the token as expired 5s before its actual expiry (clock-skew guard).
  // Returns false when expiresAt is 0/unknown (unknown expiry is treated as non-expiring).
  function isGDriveTokenExpired(meta = gdriveTokenMetaCache, at = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isGDriveTokenExpired", traceArgs(arguments));
    const expiresAt = Number(meta == null ? void 0 : meta.expiresAt) || 0;
    if (!expiresAt) return false;
    // 5-second skew margin: a token within 5s of expiry is treated as already expired.
    return at >= expiresAt - 5e3;
  }
  // Return the cached Drive access token, or empty string if none.
  // Side effect: if expired, fires an async clearGDriveToken() and returns empty (a getter with a clearing side effect).
  function getGDriveToken() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getGDriveToken", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!gdriveTokenCache) return "";
    if (isGDriveTokenExpired()) {
      void clearGDriveToken();
      return "";
    }
    return gdriveTokenCache;
  }
  // Return a shallow copy of the in-memory token meta cache.
  function getGDriveTokenMeta() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getGDriveTokenMeta", traceArgs(arguments));
    return __spreadValues({}, gdriveTokenMetaCache);
  }
  // Compute expiry (explicit expiresAt > expiresInSec > default TTL), update caches, and persist token+meta to Tampermonkey GM storage.
  // Empty token clears instead. Deliberately removes any localStorage copy so the token lives only in non-page-readable GM storage.
  async function saveGDriveToken(token, options = {}) {
    log("→ saveGDriveToken");
    const value = String(token || "").trim();
    if (!value) {
      await clearGDriveToken();
      return;
    }
    const tokenIssuedAt = now();
    const expiresInSec = Number(options == null ? void 0 : options.expiresInSec) || 0;
    const expiresAtOption = Number(options == null ? void 0 : options.expiresAt) || 0;
    const expiresAt = expiresAtOption > 0 ? expiresAtOption : expiresInSec > 0 ? tokenIssuedAt + expiresInSec * 1e3 : tokenIssuedAt + OAUTH_DEFAULT_TOKEN_TTL_MS;
    gdriveTokenCache = value;
    gdriveTokenMetaCache = normalizeGDriveTokenMeta({
      savedAt: tokenIssuedAt,
      expiresAt,
      source: (options == null ? void 0 : options.source) || "oauth-pkce"
    }, value);
    gdriveTokenCacheInitialized = true;
    await gmSetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY, gdriveTokenCache);
    await gmSetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY, gdriveTokenMetaCache);
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY);
  }
  // Wipe the token caches and delete token+meta from GM storage AND from localStorage (purging any legacy page-readable copy).
  async function clearGDriveToken() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ clearGDriveToken", traceArgs(arguments));
    gdriveTokenCache = "";
    gdriveTokenMetaCache = normalizeGDriveTokenMeta({}, "");
    gdriveTokenCacheInitialized = true;
    await gmDeleteValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY);
    await gmDeleteValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY);
  }
  // One-time load of the Drive token/meta from GM storage into caches (guarded by gdriveTokenCacheInitialized).
  // Migrates a legacy localStorage token into GM storage then removes the localStorage copies; clears the token if already expired.
  async function initializeGDriveTokenCache() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ initializeGDriveTokenCache", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (gdriveTokenCacheInitialized) return;
    let token = String(await gmGetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY, "")) || "";
    let meta = await gmGetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY, null);
    if (!token) {
      const legacyToken = String(localStorage.getItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY) || "").trim();
      if (legacyToken) {
        token = legacyToken;
        const legacyMetaRaw = localStorage.getItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY);
        if (legacyMetaRaw) {
          try {
            meta = JSON.parse(legacyMetaRaw);
          } catch (e) {
          }
        }
        await gmSetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY, token);
        if (meta) await gmSetValueSafe(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY, meta);
      }
    }
    gdriveTokenCache = token;
    gdriveTokenMetaCache = normalizeGDriveTokenMeta(meta || {}, token);
    gdriveTokenCacheInitialized = true;
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_TOKEN_META_KEY);
    if (gdriveTokenCache && isGDriveTokenExpired()) {
      await clearGDriveToken();
    }
  }
  // Read the synced Drive file id from localStorage (file id is non-secret, so not in GM storage).
  function getGDriveFileId() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getGDriveFileId", traceArgs(arguments));
    return localStorage.getItem(GDRIVE_FILE_ID_KEY);
  }
  // Persist the Drive file id to localStorage.
  function saveGDriveFileId(fileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ saveGDriveFileId", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(GDRIVE_FILE_ID_KEY, fileId);
  }
  // Record the current time as the last Drive sync timestamp in localStorage.
  function setGDriveSyncTime() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setGDriveSyncTime", traceArgs(arguments));
    localStorage.setItem(GDRIVE_SYNC_TIME_KEY, now().toString());
  }
  // Set the module-level sync status and update the .sniffies-sync-status badge text/class.
  // For 'synced' it shows the last-sync time and, if known, a 'token expires in Nm' hint.
  function updateSyncStatus(status) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateSyncStatus", traceArgs(arguments));
    lastGDriveSyncStatus = status;
    const statusEl2 = document.querySelector(".sniffies-sync-status");
    if (statusEl2) {
      statusEl2.className = "sniffies-sync-status " + status;
      if (status === "synced") {
        const lastSync = localStorage.getItem(GDRIVE_SYNC_TIME_KEY);
        const date = lastSync ? new Date(parseInt(lastSync, 10)).toLocaleTimeString() : "recently";
        const meta = getGDriveTokenMeta();
        let expiryHint = "";
        if (meta == null ? void 0 : meta.expiresAt) {
          const remainingMs = Math.max(0, Number(meta.expiresAt) - now());
          const remainingMin = Math.ceil(remainingMs / 6e4);
          if (remainingMin > 0) expiryHint = ` (token expires in ${remainingMin}m)`;
        }
        statusEl2.textContent = `\u2713 Synced at ${date}${expiryHint}`;
      } else if (status === "syncing") {
        statusEl2.textContent = "\u27F2 Syncing...";
      } else if (status === "error") {
        statusEl2.textContent = "\u2717 Sync failed";
      } else {
        statusEl2.textContent = "\u25EF Not connected";
      }
    }
  }
  let gdriveAuthInProgress = false;
  let gdriveAuthPromise = null;
  // Clear the gdriveAuthInProgress single-flight flag (called when an auth attempt ends or is cancelled).
  function finishGDriveAuthFlow() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ finishGDriveAuthFlow", traceArgs(arguments));
    gdriveAuthInProgress = false;
  }
  // Create a PKCE session: random 48-char verifier, its SHA-256 base64url challenge, a 'sf_gdrive_'-prefixed state nonce, and createdAt.
  async function createPkceSession() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ createPkceSession", traceArgs(arguments));
    const verifier = randomToken(48);
    const challenge = await sha256Base64Url(verifier);
    const state2 = `sf_gdrive_${randomToken(12)}`;
    return { verifier, challenge, state: state2, createdAt: now() };
  }
  // Build the Google OAuth2 authorization URL for the PKCE code flow from a session.
  // Uses code_challenge_method=S256, access_type=online, prompt=consent; all params URL-encoded.
  function buildGooglePkceAuthUrl(session) {
    log("→ buildGooglePkceAuthUrl");
    const redirectUri = encodeURIComponent(GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI);
    const scope = encodeURIComponent(GOOGLE_SCOPES);
    const responseType = "code";
    const state2 = encodeURIComponent(session.state);
    const codeChallenge = encodeURIComponent(session.challenge);
    // PKCE auth URL: code_challenge_method=S256, access_type=online (no refresh token), prompt=consent forces re-consent.
    return `https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth?client_id=${encodeURIComponent(GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID)}&redirect_uri=${redirectUri}&response_type=${responseType}&scope=${scope}&state=${state2}&code_challenge=${codeChallenge}&code_challenge_method=S256&access_type=online&prompt=consent`;
  }
  // Parse a pasted OAuth callback into {code,state,accessToken,error,errorDescription}.
  // A raw 'ya29.' string is treated as a legacy access token; full URLs read query then fall back to the URL hash fragment; bare input is treated as a code.
  function parseOAuthCallbackInput(raw) {
    log("→ parseOAuthCallbackInput");
    var _a;
    const input = String(raw || "").trim();
    if (!input) return { code: "", state: "", accessToken: "", error: "", errorDescription: "" };
    // A raw Google access token starts with 'ya29.'; such input bypasses URL parsing as a legacy direct-token paste.
    if (/^ya29\./i.test(input)) {
      return { code: "", state: "", accessToken: input, error: "", errorDescription: "" };
    }
    let params = null;
    try {
      if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(input)) {
        const u = new URL(input);
        params = u.searchParams;
        if (!params.get("code") && !params.get("access_token") && ((_a = u.hash) == null ? void 0 : _a.length) > 1) {
          params = new URLSearchParams(u.hash.slice(1));
        }
      } else {
        const normalized = input.startsWith("?") ? input.slice(1) : input;
        params = new URLSearchParams(normalized);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      params = null;
    }
    if (params) {
      return {
        code: params.get("code") || "",
        state: params.get("state") || "",
        accessToken: params.get("access_token") || "",
        error: params.get("error") || "",
        errorDescription: params.get("error_description") || ""
      };
    }
    return { code: input, state: "", accessToken: "", error: "", errorDescription: "" };
  }
  // POST the authorization code + PKCE code_verifier to Google's token endpoint and return the token payload.
  // Throws with the server's error_description/error (or HTTP status) if the response lacks access_token.
  async function exchangeGoogleAuthCodeForToken(code, session) {
    log("→ exchangeGoogleAuthCodeForToken");
    const body = new URLSearchParams();
    body.set("client_id", GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID);
    body.set("grant_type", "authorization_code");
    body.set("code", code);
    body.set("redirect_uri", GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI);
    body.set("code_verifier", session.verifier);
    const response = await fetchWithTimeout("https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
      body: body.toString()
    });
    const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!response.ok || !(payload == null ? void 0 : payload.access_token)) {
      const reason = (payload == null ? void 0 : payload.error_description) || (payload == null ? void 0 : payload.error) || `HTTP ${response.status}`;
      throw new Error(`Token exchange failed: ${reason}`);
    }
    return payload;
  }
  // Build the 'Connect Google Drive' modal and resolve to an access token (or null on cancel); returns Promise.resolve(null) if OAuth is unconfigured.
  // Open-Auth button starts a PKCE session in pendingOAuthPkce and opens the Google consent popup; Save handles either legacy token paste or PKCE code exchange.
  function openTokenModal() {
    log("→ openTokenModal");
    if (!isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      finishGDriveAuthFlow();
      alert("Google Drive sync is disabled. Add your own GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID to enable OAuth.");
      return Promise.resolve(null);
    }
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const modal = document.createElement("div");
      modal.className = "sniffies-notes-modal";
      modal.style.zIndex = "100001";
      const content = document.createElement("div");
      content.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-content";
      const title = document.createElement("div");
      title.style.marginBottom = "12px";
      title.style.fontSize = "16px";
      title.style.fontWeight = "bold";
      title.textContent = "Connect Google Drive (OAuth PKCE)";
      const instructions = document.createElement("div");
      instructions.style.marginBottom = "12px";
      instructions.style.fontSize = "12px";
      instructions.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)";
      instructions.textContent = "Click Open Google Auth, approve access, then paste the full callback URL (or code query) here. Legacy access-token paste still works.";
      const helper = document.createElement("div");
      helper.style.marginBottom = "12px";
      helper.style.fontSize = "11px";
      helper.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.66)";
      helper.textContent = `Redirect URI: ${GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI}`;
      const buttonRow = document.createElement("div");
      buttonRow.style.marginBottom = "12px";
      buttonRow.style.display = "flex";
      buttonRow.style.gap = "8px";
      const authBtn = document.createElement("button");
      authBtn.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-buttons";
      authBtn.style.padding = "6px 12px";
      authBtn.style.borderRadius = "6px";
      authBtn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2)";
      authBtn.style.background = "rgba(100, 150, 255, 0.2)";
      authBtn.style.color = "#fff";
      authBtn.style.cursor = "pointer";
      authBtn.style.fontSize = "12px";
      authBtn.textContent = "Open Google Auth";
      authBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        try {
          pendingOAuthPkce = await createPkceSession();
          const authUrl = buildGooglePkceAuthUrl(pendingOAuthPkce);
          window.open(authUrl, "gdrive_auth", "width=620,height=760");
          helper.textContent = `Auth opened. Paste callback URL here. State expires in ${Math.round(OAUTH_STATE_MAX_AGE_MS / 6e4)} min.`;
        } catch (e) {
          helper.textContent = `Failed to open auth: ${(e == null ? void 0 : e.message) || e}`;
        }
      });
      buttonRow.appendChild(authBtn);
      const textarea = document.createElement("textarea");
      textarea.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-textarea";
      textarea.placeholder = "Paste callback URL / ?code=...&state=... or legacy access token";
      textarea.style.marginBottom = "12px";
      const buttons = document.createElement("div");
      buttons.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-buttons";
      // Modal teardown closure: clear gdriveAuthInProgress and pendingOAuthPkce, remove the modal, and resolve the open promise with the given token (or null).
      const closeWith = (tokenValue = null) => {
        log("→ closeWith");
        finishGDriveAuthFlow();
        pendingOAuthPkce = null;
        modal.remove();
        resolve(tokenValue);
      };
      const saveBtn = document.createElement("button");
      saveBtn.textContent = "Save";
      saveBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
        const raw = textarea.value.trim();
        if (!raw) {
          alert("Paste callback URL/code or access token.");
          return;
        }
        const parsed = parseOAuthCallbackInput(raw);
        if (parsed.error) {
          alert(`Google OAuth error: ${parsed.errorDescription || parsed.error}`);
          return;
        }
        try {
          if (parsed.accessToken) {
            const proceedLegacy = confirm("Use legacy direct token import? PKCE code exchange is recommended.");
            if (!proceedLegacy) return;
            await saveGDriveToken(parsed.accessToken, { source: "manual-token" });
            logInfo("Saved manual Google access token");
            updateSyncStatus("synced");
            closeWith(parsed.accessToken);
            return;
          }
          if (!parsed.code) {
            alert("No authorization code found.");
            return;
          }
          if (!pendingOAuthPkce) {
            alert("No active auth session. Click Open Google Auth first.");
            return;
          }
          if (now() - pendingOAuthPkce.createdAt > OAUTH_STATE_MAX_AGE_MS) {
            alert("Auth session expired. Click Open Google Auth again.");
            pendingOAuthPkce = null;
            return;
          }
          // CSRF check: returned OAuth state must exactly match the session's state nonce or the exchange is refused.
          if (!parsed.state || parsed.state !== pendingOAuthPkce.state) {
            alert("OAuth state mismatch. Start again with Open Google Auth.");
            return;
          }
          const tokenPayload = await exchangeGoogleAuthCodeForToken(parsed.code, pendingOAuthPkce);
          await saveGDriveToken(tokenPayload.access_token, {
            expiresInSec: Number(tokenPayload.expires_in) || 0,
            source: "oauth-pkce"
          });
          logInfo("Saved Google token via OAuth PKCE exchange");
          updateSyncStatus("synced");
          closeWith(tokenPayload.access_token);
        } catch (e) {
          alert((e == null ? void 0 : e.message) || String(e));
        }
      });
      const cancelBtn = document.createElement("button");
      cancelBtn.textContent = "Cancel";
      cancelBtn.addEventListener("click", () => closeWith(null));
      buttons.appendChild(saveBtn);
      buttons.appendChild(cancelBtn);
      content.appendChild(title);
      content.appendChild(instructions);
      content.appendChild(helper);
      content.appendChild(buttonRow);
      content.appendChild(textarea);
      content.appendChild(buttons);
      modal.appendChild(content);
      modal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        if (e.target === modal) closeWith(null);
      });
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
      textarea.focus();
    });
  }
  // Return an existing valid Drive token, otherwise open the token modal to obtain one; returns null if OAuth is unconfigured.
  // Single-flights concurrent callers via gdriveAuthInProgress/gdriveAuthPromise so only one modal/auth runs at a time.
  async function authenticateGDrive() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ authenticateGDrive", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      logWarn("Google Drive OAuth is not configured");
      return null;
    }
    await initializeGDriveTokenCache();
    const token = getGDriveToken();
    if (token) {
      logInfo("Using existing Google Drive token");
      return token;
    }
    if (gdriveAuthInProgress) {
      logInfo("Auth already in progress, waiting");
      return gdriveAuthPromise || null;
    }
    gdriveAuthInProgress = true;
    logInfo("Starting Google Drive authentication");
    gdriveAuthPromise = Promise.resolve(openTokenModal()).finally(() => {
      gdriveAuthPromise = null;
    });
    return gdriveAuthPromise;
  }
  // Upload notes/ratings/blocked/bookmarks to Drive, authenticating first if needed; PATCHes the existing fileId or multipart-POSTs a new file (saving its id).
  // 401 clears the token and marks disconnected; other failures mark error. Updates the sync-status badge throughout.
  // Reentrancy-coalesced via busy/queued flags (mirrors updatePartials' pattern): a call that arrives
  // while a sync is in flight is replayed exactly once after the current run finishes, instead of
  // running concurrently. Without this, the debounced auto-sync timer and the manual "Sync" button can
  // both reach the "no fileId yet, create a new file" branch at once; the second response to resolve
  // silently overwrites the stored file id, orphaning whichever Drive file isn't referenced afterward.
  async function syncNotesToGDrive() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ syncNotesToGDrive", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (gdriveSyncBusy) {
      gdriveSyncQueued = true;
      return;
    }
    gdriveSyncBusy = true;
    try {
      await syncNotesToGDriveNow();
    } finally {
      gdriveSyncBusy = false;
      if (gdriveSyncQueued) {
        gdriveSyncQueued = false;
        queueMicrotask(() => syncNotesToGDrive());
      }
    }
  }
  async function syncNotesToGDriveNow() {
    if (!isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
      alert("Google Drive sync is disabled. Configure GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID first.");
      return;
    }
    await initializeGDriveTokenCache();
    let token = getGDriveToken();
    if (!token) {
      logInfo("No token found, attempting authentication");
      updateSyncStatus("syncing");
      token = await authenticateGDrive();
      if (!token) {
        logWarn("Authentication failed or cancelled");
        updateSyncStatus("error");
        alert("Google Drive authentication failed. Please try again.");
        return;
      }
    }
    updateSyncStatus("syncing");
    const fileId = getGDriveFileId();
    const fileContent = {
      notes,
      ratings: profileRatings,
      blocked: [...blocked],
      bookmarks: [...bookmarks.values()],
      syncedAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
      version: "2.0"
    };
    try {
      let response;
      if (fileId) {
        logInfo("Uploading data to Google Drive");
        response = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files/${fileId}?uploadType=media`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(fileContent)
        });
      } else {
        logInfo("Creating new Google Drive file");
        // Random multipart/related boundary for the create-file upload (metadata part + JSON content part).
        const boundary = "===============" + Math.random().toString().substr(2) + "==";
        const metadata = {
          name: "sniffies_data.json",
          mimeType: "application/json"
        };
        const body = `--${boundary}\r
Content-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8\r
\r
${JSON.stringify(metadata)}\r
--${boundary}\r
Content-Type: application/json\r
\r
${JSON.stringify(fileContent)}\r
--${boundary}--`;
        response = await fetchWithTimeout("https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=multipart", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`,
            "Content-Type": `multipart/related; boundary="${boundary}"`
          },
          body
        });
        if (response.ok) {
          const data = await response.json();
          saveGDriveFileId(data.id);
          logInfo("Created Google Drive file:", data.id);
        }
      }
      if (response.ok || response.status === 200) {
        setGDriveSyncTime();
        updateSyncStatus("synced");
        logInfo("Successfully synced data to Google Drive");
      } else if (response.status === 401) {
        await clearGDriveToken();
        updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
        alert("Google token expired/invalid. Reconnect Google Drive.");
      } else {
        logWarn("Failed to sync to Google Drive:", response.status);
        updateSyncStatus("error");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      logError("Error syncing to Google Drive:", e);
      updateSyncStatus("error");
    }
  }
  // Download the Drive file and OVERWRITE local notes/ratings/blocked/bookmarks with its contents, then location.reload().
  // Requires an existing token and saved fileId; 401 clears the token + disconnects; replaces (not merges) each section.
  async function loadNotesFromGDrive() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ loadNotesFromGDrive", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
      alert("Google Drive sync is disabled. Configure GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID first.");
      return;
    }
    await initializeGDriveTokenCache();
    const token = getGDriveToken();
    if (!token) {
      updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
      alert("Please authenticate with Google Drive first");
      return;
    }
    const fileId = getGDriveFileId();
    if (!fileId) {
      updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
      alert("No data file found on Google Drive");
      return;
    }
    updateSyncStatus("syncing");
    try {
      const response = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/files/${fileId}?alt=media`, {
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`
        }
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (data.notes) {
          notes = data.notes;
          saveNotes();
          logInfo("Loaded notes from Google Drive");
        }
        if (data.ratings && typeof data.ratings === "object") {
          profileRatings = {};
          for (const [id, ratingRaw] of Object.entries(data.ratings)) {
            const normalizedId = normalizeProfileId(id);
            const rating = normalizeProfileRating(ratingRaw);
            if (!normalizedId || !rating) continue;
            profileRatings[normalizedId] = rating;
          }
          saveProfileRatings();
          logInfo("Loaded ratings from Google Drive");
        }
        if (data.blocked && Array.isArray(data.blocked)) {
          blocked = new Set(normalizeIdList(data.blocked));
          // Backfill blockedAt for any id newly present via this full-overwrite Drive sync that wasn't
          // already tracked — loadBlockedAtMap()'s documented anti-race protection (a stale pre-existing
          // reply can't immediately auto-unhide a profile) only works if every blocked id has a timestamp.
          const syncedAt = now();
          for (const id of blocked) {
            if (!blockedAt.has(id)) blockedAt.set(id, syncedAt);
          }
          saveBlockedSet();
          saveBlockedAtMap();
          logInfo("Loaded blocked list from Google Drive");
        }
        if (data.bookmarks) {
          const imported = Array.isArray(data.bookmarks) ? data.bookmarks : Object.values(data.bookmarks || {});
          bookmarks = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
          for (const row of imported) {
            const id = normalizeProfileId((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileId) || (row == null ? void 0 : row.id));
            if (!id) continue;
            bookmarks.set(id, {
              profileId: id,
              profileUrl: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileUrl) || profileUrlForId(id)),
              profileLabel: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileLabel) || id.slice(0, 10)),
              alias: String((row == null ? void 0 : row.alias) || "").trim().slice(0, 80),
              categories: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.categories),
              tags: normalizeTagValues(row == null ? void 0 : row.tags),
              lastSeenAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastSeenAt) || 0,
              createdAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.createdAt) || now(),
              updatedAt: parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt) || now()
            });
          }
          saveBookmarks();
          renderBookmarkPanel();
          logInfo("Loaded bookmarks from Google Drive");
        }
        setGDriveSyncTime();
        updateSyncStatus("synced");
        alert("Data loaded from Google Drive!");
        location.reload();
      } else if (response.status === 401) {
        await clearGDriveToken();
        updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
        alert("Google token expired/invalid. Reconnect Google Drive.");
      } else {
        updateSyncStatus("error");
        alert("Failed to load data from Google Drive");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      logError("Error loading from Google Drive:", e);
      updateSyncStatus("error");
      alert("Error loading from Google Drive: " + e.message);
    }
  }
  // After confirm, clear the Drive token, remove the stored fileId and sync-time, end any auth flow, and mark disconnected (local data is kept).
  async function disconnectGDrive() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ disconnectGDrive", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (confirm("Disconnect Google Drive? Notes will still be stored locally.")) {
      await clearGDriveToken();
      localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_FILE_ID_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(GDRIVE_SYNC_TIME_KEY);
      finishGDriveAuthFlow();
      updateSyncStatus("disconnected");
      logInfo("Disconnected from Google Drive");
      alert("Google Drive disconnected");
    }
  }
  // Extract the current profile id from the page URL (/profile/<hex>), else from the first /profile/ anchor's href; null if none.
  function getCurrentProfileId() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getCurrentProfileId", traceArgs(arguments));
    const href = window.location.href;
    // Profile ids are hex, at least 6 chars; captured from the /profile/ path segment.
    const m = href.match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
    if (m) return m[1];
    const profileLink = document.querySelector("a[href*='/profile/']");
    if (profileLink) {
      const m2 = profileLink.getAttribute("href").match(/\/profile\/([0-9a-f]{6,})/i);
      if (m2) return m2[1];
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Multi-strategy heuristic to locate the visible open-profile container: .his-profile content/child, then #sniffies-infowindow, then visible dialog/modal/panel elements containing profile-info text.
  // Falls back all the way to document.body when nothing matches.
  function findProfileContainer() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ findProfileContainer", traceArgs(arguments));
    const hisProfile = document.querySelector(".his-profile");
    if (hisProfile) {
      const computed = window.getComputedStyle(hisProfile);
      if (computed.visibility !== "hidden" && computed.display !== "none") {
        const contentDivs = hisProfile.querySelectorAll('div[style*="overflow"], [class*="content"], [class*="scroll"]');
        for (const div of contentDivs) {
          const rect = div.getBoundingClientRect();
          const divComputed = window.getComputedStyle(div);
          if (rect.height > 100 && div.querySelectorAll("*").length > 3 && divComputed.visibility !== "hidden" && divComputed.display !== "none") {
            return div;
          }
        }
        const children = Array.from(hisProfile.children);
        for (const child of children) {
          const rect = child.getBoundingClientRect();
          const childComputed = window.getComputedStyle(child);
          if (rect.height > 100 && childComputed.visibility !== "hidden" && childComputed.display !== "none") {
            return child;
          }
        }
        return hisProfile;
      }
    }
    const infoWindow = document.querySelector('[id="sniffies-infowindow"]');
    if (infoWindow) {
      const computed = window.getComputedStyle(infoWindow);
      if (computed.visibility !== "hidden" && computed.display !== "none") {
        log("Found sniffies-infowindow");
        return infoWindow;
      }
    }
    const dialogs = document.querySelectorAll('[role="dialog"], .modal, [class*="panel"][class*="left"], [class*="panel"][class*="bottom"]');
    for (const el of dialogs) {
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const computed = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      const isVisible = rect.width > 100 && rect.height > 200 && computed.visibility !== "hidden" && computed.display !== "none";
      if (isVisible) {
        // Heuristic to recognize a profile card by its body text (height/weight like '180, 75' or the word 'looking').
        const hasProfileInfo = el.textContent.includes("m,") || el.textContent.match(/\d+,\s*\d+["\']/) || el.innerText.includes("looking");
        if (hasProfileInfo) {
          log("Found visible dialog with profile info");
          return el;
        }
      }
    }
    // No real profile container found: return null (NOT document.body). All callers null-check and bail, which
    // avoids injecting notes/reminder widgets into <body> and attaching a body-wide childList observer.
    // getCurrentProfileId can resolve a marker id on the map with no profile open — exactly when this is hit.
    return null;
  }
  // Format an appointment timestamp as a short locale date-time (e.g. 'Jun 14, 3:05 PM'); returns 'invalid date' if the timestamp won't parse.
  function formatApptDate(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatApptDate", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!t) return "invalid date";
    return new Date(t).toLocaleString([], {
      month: "short",
      day: "numeric",
      hour: "numeric",
      minute: "2-digit"
    });
  }
  // Return the configured appointment 'approaching' window in minutes, clamped to 1-1440; falls back to DEFAULT_APPOINTMENT_APPROACH_MINUTES when not finite.
  function getApproachMinutes() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getApproachMinutes", traceArgs(arguments));
    const raw = Number(state.appointmentApproachMinutes);
    if (!Number.isFinite(raw)) return DEFAULT_APPOINTMENT_APPROACH_MINUTES;
    return Math.min(1440, Math.max(1, Math.round(raw)));
  }
  // The approach window from getApproachMinutes() converted to milliseconds.
  function getApproachMs() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getApproachMs", traceArgs(arguments));
    return getApproachMinutes() * 60 * 1e3;
  }
  // Format a timestamp as a local 'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm' string for an <input type=datetime-local> value; empty string if it won't parse.
  // Uses local date parts (not UTC), zero-padded.
  function formatDateTimeLocalValue(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatDateTimeLocalValue", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!t) return "";
    const d = new Date(t);
    const yyyy = d.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    const hh = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, "0");
    const mi = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}T${hh}:${mi}`;
  }
  // Parse a datetime-local input value to epoch ms; returns 0 for empty or unparseable input.
  function parseDateTimeLocalValue(value) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ parseDateTimeLocalValue", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!value) return 0;
    const t = new Date(value).getTime();
    return Number.isFinite(t) ? t : 0;
  }
