  // Render a relative countdown for an appointment: future as 'in <1m'/'in Nm'/'in Hh Mm', past as 'now'/'Nm ago'/'Hh Mm ago'; empty if unparseable.
  function formatApptCountdown(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatApptCountdown", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!t) return "";
    const diff = t - now();
    const abs = Math.abs(diff);
    const min = Math.round(abs / 6e4);
    if (diff > 0) {
      if (min < 1) return "in <1m";
      if (min < 60) return `in ${min}m`;
      const hr2 = Math.floor(min / 60);
      const rem2 = min % 60;
      return rem2 ? `in ${hr2}h ${rem2}m` : `in ${hr2}h`;
    }
    if (min < 1) return "now";
    if (min < 60) return `${min}m ago`;
    const hr = Math.floor(min / 60);
    const rem = min % 60;
    return rem ? `${hr}h ${rem}m ago` : `${hr}h ago`;
  }
  // Canonical https://sniffies.com/profile/<id> URL for a normalized id; returns the site root if the id is invalid.
  function profileUrlForId(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ profileUrlForId", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    return id ? `https://sniffies.com/profile/${id}` : "https://sniffies.com/";
  }
  // Find the actual profile href from a /profile/<id> anchor in the container or document, absolutizing relative hrefs; falls back to profileUrlForId.
  function getProfileUrlFromContainer(profileId, container) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileUrlFromContainer", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return profileUrlForId(profileId);
    const selector = `a[href*="/profile/${id}"]`;
    const fromContainer = container == null ? void 0 : container.querySelector(selector);
    const globalLink = document.querySelector(selector);
    const href = (fromContainer == null ? void 0 : fromContainer.getAttribute("href")) || (globalLink == null ? void 0 : globalLink.getAttribute("href")) || "";
    if (!href) return profileUrlForId(profileId);
    if (href.startsWith("http")) return href;
    if (href.startsWith("/")) return `https://sniffies.com${href}`;
    return profileUrlForId(profileId);
  }
  // Build a unique appointment id from the profile id, target timestamp, and a random base36 suffix.
  function createAppointmentId(profileId, atTs) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ createAppointmentId", traceArgs(arguments));
    return `${normalizeProfileId(profileId) || "profile"}-${atTs}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
  }
  // Classify an appointment by time-to-due: 'due' (<=0), 'approaching' (within getApproachMs()), else 'upcoming'.
  function appointmentStatus(appt, t = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ appointmentStatus", traceArgs(arguments));
    const dueIn = (parseTimestamp(appt == null ? void 0 : appt.atTs) || 0) - t;
    if (dueIn <= 0) return "due";
    if (dueIn <= getApproachMs()) return "approaching";
    return "upcoming";
  }
  // Return all appointments as an array sorted ascending by due time (atTs).
  // Snapshots the Map values; missing atTs sorts as 0 (earliest).
  function sortedAppointments() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sortedAppointments", traceArgs(arguments));
    return [...appointments.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.atTs || 0) - (b.atTs || 0));
  }
  // Find the soonest still-relevant appointment for a profile, or null.
  // Skips entries older than APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS (14 days); returns a copy with a re-parsed atTs.
  function getNextAppointmentForProfile(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getNextAppointmentForProfile", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const t = now();
    let next = null;
    for (const appt of appointments.values()) {
      if (normalizeProfileId(appt.profileId) !== id) continue;
      const atTs = parseTimestamp(appt.atTs);
      // Drops appointments older than 14 days (APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS) so stale reminders never resurface.
      if (!atTs || atTs < t - APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS) continue;
      if (!next || atTs < next.atTs) {
        next = __spreadProps(__spreadValues({}, appt), { atTs });
      }
    }
    return next;
  }
  // True if the normalized profile id has a bookmark entry.
  function isBookmarked(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ isBookmarked", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    return !!id && bookmarks.has(id);
  }
  // Best-known distance (mi) for a profile, or null.
  // Prefers include-match distance, then profileFilterCache, then profileTextCache; first finite wins.
  function getBookmarkDistance(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getBookmarkDistance", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const includeDistance = (_a = includeMatches.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _a.distance;
    if (Number.isFinite(includeDistance)) return includeDistance;
    const profileDistance = (_b = profileFilterCache.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _b.distance;
    if (Number.isFinite(profileDistance)) return profileDistance;
    const textDistance = (_c = profileTextCache.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _c.distance;
    if (Number.isFinite(textDistance)) return textDistance;
    return null;
  }
  // Latest interaction time for a profile: max of all chat-activity timestamps and the howdy-sent time.
  // Body is identical to getLastChatInteractionTs.
  function getBookmarkLastInteractionTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getBookmarkLastInteractionTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    const row = chatActivity.get(id);
    // updatedAt is bookkeeping (the row's last write time), NOT a message time — exclude it so interaction
    // recency reflects real messages and stays aligned with the chat-age badge.
    const chatTs = row ? Math.max(
      parseTimestamp(row.myLastTs) || 0,
      parseTimestamp(row.theirLastTs) || 0,
      parseTimestamp(row.anyLastTs) || 0
    ) : 0;
    const sentTs = parseTimestamp(howdySentAt[id]) || 0;
    return Math.max(chatTs, sentTs);
  }
  // Latest chat-activity time for a profile: max of my/their/any/updatedAt row timestamps and howdy-sent time.
  // Ignores chat-preview rows (see getLastAnyChatInteractionTs).
  function getLastChatInteractionTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getLastChatInteractionTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    const row = chatActivity.get(id);
    // updatedAt is bookkeeping (the row's last write time), NOT a message time — exclude it so interaction
    // recency reflects real messages and stays aligned with the chat-age badge.
    const chatTs = row ? Math.max(
      parseTimestamp(row.myLastTs) || 0,
      parseTimestamp(row.theirLastTs) || 0,
      parseTimestamp(row.anyLastTs) || 0
    ) : 0;
    const sentTs = parseTimestamp(howdySentAt[id]) || 0;
    return Math.max(chatTs, sentTs);
  }
  // Newest timestamp across the profile's captured chat-preview rows, else 0.
  function getLastPreviewInteractionTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getLastPreviewInteractionTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    const rows = chatPreviewByProfile.get(id);
    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return 0;
    let latest = 0;
    for (const row of rows) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.ts);
      if (ts > latest) latest = ts;
    }
    return latest;
  }
  // True if any chat/preview interaction happened within windowMs (default RECENT_CHAT_HIDE_MS, 24h) of t.
  function hasRecentChatInteraction(profileId, t = now(), windowMs = RECENT_CHAT_HIDE_MS) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hasRecentChatInteraction", traceArgs(arguments));
    const ts = getLastAnyChatInteractionTs(profileId);
    return !!ts && t - ts <= windowMs;
  }
  // Max of chat-activity time and chat-preview time for a profile (union of both sources).
  function getLastAnyChatInteractionTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getLastAnyChatInteractionTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    return Math.max(
      getLastChatInteractionTs(profileId),
      getLastPreviewInteractionTs(profileId)
    );
  }
  // True if the profile has any recorded chat or preview interaction at all.
  function hasAnyChatInteraction(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hasAnyChatInteraction", traceArgs(arguments));
    return !!getLastAnyChatInteractionTs(profileId);
  }
  // Human-readable label for an attitude, or "" if it normalizes to empty.
  function formatAttitudeLabel(attitude) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatAttitudeLabel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const normalized = normalizeAttitude(attitude);
    if (!normalized) return "";
    return humanizeLabel(normalized);
  }
  // Count of deleted messages tracked for a profile, floored at >= 0.
  // Reads chatDeletionStats[id].count; coerces and floors the stored value.
  function getChatDeletionCount(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getChatDeletionCount", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number((chatDeletionStats[id] == null ? void 0 : chatDeletionStats[id].count) || 0)));
  }
  // Timestamp of the most recent deleted message for a profile, or 0.
  function getLastDeletedMessageTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getLastDeletedMessageTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    return parseTimestamp(chatDeletionStats[id] == null ? void 0 : chatDeletionStats[id].lastDeletedTs) || 0;
  }
  // Pull the URL out of a CSS background-image value and sanitize it.
  // Returns "" for empty or "none"; regex backreference matches the same quote style.
  function extractImageUrlFromBackground(bgValue) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ extractImageUrlFromBackground", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!bgValue || bgValue === "none") return "";
    // Backreference \1 forces the closing quote to match the opening one; group 2 is the URL (handles "", '', or no quotes).
    const match = String(bgValue).match(/url\((['"]?)(.*?)\1\)/i);
    return match ? sanitizeImageUrl(match[2]) : "";
  }
  // Resolve a profile marker's avatar image URL by probing its DOM root.
  // Tries .marker-avatar-image, any background-image element, then the root; first non-empty url wins, else "".
  function getMarkerImageUrl(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getMarkerImageUrl", traceArgs(arguments));
    const marker = idToMarker.get(normalizeProfileId(profileId));
    const root = resolveMarkerRoot(marker);
    if (!root) return "";
    const candidates = [
      root.querySelector(".marker-avatar-image"),
      root.querySelector("[style*='background-image']"),
      root
    ].filter(Boolean);
    for (const el of candidates) {
      const url = extractImageUrlFromBackground(getBg(el));
      if (url) return url;
    }
    return "";
  }
  // Compute a profile's effective attitude plus the single highest-priority reason it would be hidden (a 0-or-1 element array).
  // else-if chain checks in priority order (blocked > text > recent-chat-24h > recent-chat-2h > any-chat >
  // missing-history > not-online > repeated-delete > unanswered-out > attitude); visibleOnMap is true only when none apply.
  // Falls back to inferring attitude from marker icons when no manual/cached value exists.
  function getProfileVisibilityState(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileVisibilityState", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const marker = idToMarker.get(id);
    const manual = manualAttitudes[id];
    const cache = attitudeCache.get(id);
    let attitude = manual || (cache == null ? void 0 : cache.attitude) || null;
    if (!attitude) attitude = inferAttitudeFromIcons(marker);
    const hiddenReasons = [];
    if (blocked.has(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Blocked");
    else if (shouldHideByProfileTerms(id)) hiddenReasons.push(`Excluded by profile text${((_a = profileFilterCache.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _a.excludeTerm) ? `: ${profileFilterCache.get(id).excludeTerm}` : ""}`);
    else if (shouldHideByRecentChats(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Chatted in last 24 hours");
    else if (shouldHideByRecentChats2h(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Chatted in last 2 hours");
    else if (shouldHideByAnyChats(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Has chat history");
    else if (shouldHideByMissingChatHistory(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Missing chat history");
    else if (shouldHideByNotOnlineWindow(id)) hiddenReasons.push(`Not online in last ${formatNotOnlineWindowLong()}`);
    else if (shouldHideByRepeatedDelete(id)) hiddenReasons.push("Deleted their messages 2+ times");
    else if (shouldHideByUnansweredOut(id)) hiddenReasons.push(`Sent ${UNANSWERED_OUT_HIDE_THRESHOLD}+ messages with no reply`);
    else if (shouldHideAttitude(attitude)) hiddenReasons.push(`Hidden attitude: ${formatAttitudeLabel(attitude)}`);
    return {
      profileId: id,
      attitude: attitude || "unspecified",
      hiddenReasons,
      visibleOnMap: hiddenReasons.length === 0
    };
  }
  // Rescan markers and build one descriptor row per on-map profile for the debug profile table.
  // Merges summary cache, visibility state, distance, chat/deletion stats; sorts by distance (unknown last) then id.
  function buildProfileTableRows() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildProfileTableRows", traceArgs(arguments));
    scanMarkers();
    const rows = [];
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      const summary = profileSummaryCache.get(id) || {};
      const visibility = getProfileVisibilityState(id);
      const distance = Number.isFinite(getBookmarkDistance(id)) ? getBookmarkDistance(id) : Number.isFinite(summary.distance) ? summary.distance : null;
      rows.push({
        profileId: id,
        profileUrl: summary.profileUrl || profileUrlForId(id),
        label: summary.label || `Profile ${id.slice(0, 10)}`,
        imageUrl: summary.imageUrl || getMarkerImageUrl(id),
        distance,
        distanceLabel: formatDistance(distance).replace(/^distance:\s*/i, ""),
        profileText: cleanDisplayText(summary.profileText || "", 600),
        preferences: normalizePreferenceList(summary.preferences || []),
        notes: String(notes[id] || ""),
        lastChatTs: getLastAnyChatInteractionTs(id),
        deletedMessageCount: getChatDeletionCount(id),
        lastDeletedTs: getLastDeletedMessageTs(id),
        attitude: visibility.attitude,
        attitudeLabel: formatAttitudeLabel(visibility.attitude),
        visibleOnMap: visibility.visibleOnMap,
        hiddenReasons: visibility.hiddenReasons
      });
    }
    rows.sort((a, b) => {
      const ad = Number.isFinite(a.distance) ? a.distance : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      const bd = Number.isFinite(b.distance) ? b.distance : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      if (ad !== bd) return ad - bd;
      return a.profileId.localeCompare(b.profileId);
    });
    return rows;
  }
  // Public/debug API: return the profile table with totals and only the map-visible rows.
  // When options.hydrate, awaits updatePartials first; otherwise just scans markers. Includes current filter settings.
  async function sniffiesGetProfileTableData(options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sniffiesGetProfileTableData", traceArgs(arguments));
    const hydrate = !!(options && options.hydrate);
    if (hydrate) {
      await updatePartials("profile-table");
    } else {
      scanMarkers();
    }
    const allRows = buildProfileTableRows();
    const visibleRows = allRows.filter((row) => row.visibleOnMap);
    return {
      generatedAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
      totalCount: allRows.length,
      visibleCount: visibleRows.length,
      filteredOutCount: allRows.length - visibleRows.length,
      rows: visibleRows,
      filters: sniffiesGetSettings()
    };
  }
  // True if their last message is newer than mine AND arrived within RECENT_CHAT_HIDE_MS (24h).
  // Used to keep profiles visible despite hide rules when they replied and you haven't answered.
  function hasUnansweredRecentReply(profileId, t = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ hasUnansweredRecentReply", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    const entry = chatActivity.get(id);
    if (!entry) return false;
    const their = parseTimestamp(entry.theirLastTs) || 0;
    const mine = parseTimestamp(entry.myLastTs) || 0;
    // "They replied and I haven't answered, within 24h" — used as an override to keep such profiles visible.
    return their > mine && t - their <= RECENT_CHAT_HIDE_MS;
  }
  // Hide a profile chatted within 24h, unless the recent-chat filter is off or they have an unanswered recent reply.
  function shouldHideByRecentChats(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByRecentChats", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.hideRecentChats24h) return false;
    if (hasUnansweredRecentReply(profileId)) return false;
    return hasRecentChatInteraction(profileId);
  }
  // Hide a profile chatted within 2h, unless the 2h chat filter is off or they have an unanswered recent reply.
  function shouldHideByRecentChats2h(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByRecentChats2h", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.hideRecentChats2h) return false;
    if (hasUnansweredRecentReply(profileId)) return false;
    return hasRecentChatInteraction(profileId, now(), RECENT_CHAT_HIDE_2H_MS);
  }
  // Active "not online" window in minutes. Configurable via state.notOnlineWindowMinutes (the panel's window
  // selector: 10m / 2h / custom). Clamped to 1..1440 — presence data (profileLastActive) is only retained for
  // LAST_ACTIVE_TTL_MS (24h), so a window longer than that can't work. Defaults to 2h (the historical value).
  function notOnlineWindowMinutesValue() {
    const m = Math.round(Number(state.notOnlineWindowMinutes));
    if (!Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return Math.round(ONLINE_RECENT_HIDE_MS / 6e4);
    return Math.min(Math.max(m, 1), 24 * 60);
  }
  // The active "not online" window in milliseconds.
  function notOnlineWindowMs() {
    return notOnlineWindowMinutesValue() * 60 * 1e3;
  }
  // Short window label for compact UI (launcher/status), e.g. "10m", "2h", "90m": whole hours render as "Nh".
  function formatNotOnlineWindowShort() {
    const m = notOnlineWindowMinutesValue();
    return m % 60 === 0 ? `${m / 60}h` : `${m}m`;
  }
  // Long window label for prose (visibility reason), e.g. "10 minutes", "2 hours", "90 minutes".
  function formatNotOnlineWindowLong() {
    const m = notOnlineWindowMinutesValue();
    if (m % 60 === 0) {
      const h = m / 60;
      return `${h} hour${h === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    }
    return `${m} minute${m === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  }
  // Hide a profile last active more than the configured "not online" window ago (default 2h).
  // Gated by state.hideNotOnline2h; spared if they have an unanswered recent reply or no known last-active time.
  // (Name keeps the historical "2h" suffix; the window is now configurable via notOnlineWindowMs.)
  function shouldHideByNotOnlineWindow(profileId, t = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByNotOnlineWindow", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.hideNotOnline2h) return false;
    if (hasUnansweredRecentReply(profileId, t)) return false;
    const ts = getProfileLastActiveTs(profileId);
    if (!ts) return false;
    // Profiles inactive longer than the configured window count as offline.
    return t - ts > notOnlineWindowMs();
  }
  // Hide any profile with chat history when the hideAnyChats filter is on.
  function shouldHideByAnyChats(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByAnyChats", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.hideAnyChats) return false;
    // Honor the same "never hide someone with an unanswered recent reply" override the recent-chat and
    // offline-2h filters use, so a profile actively messaging you isn't hidden by "hide chatted ever".
    if (hasUnansweredRecentReply(profileId)) return false;
    return hasAnyChatInteraction(profileId);
  }
  // Inverse filter: when showOnlyChats is on, hide profiles that have no chat history.
  function shouldHideByMissingChatHistory(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByMissingChatHistory", traceArgs(arguments));
    return !!state.showOnlyChats && !hasAnyChatInteraction(profileId);
  }
  // Auto-hide a profile once you've sent UNANSWERED_OUT_HIDE_THRESHOLD (4) messages in
  // a row with no reply (chatActivity.outRun). The myLastTs > theirLastTs check makes
  // it un-hide automatically the instant they reply (their last message passes yours),
  // independent of the counter reset. Gated by state.hideUnansweredOut (default on).
  function shouldHideByUnansweredOut(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByUnansweredOut", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (state.hideUnansweredOut === false) return false;
    const entry = chatActivity.get(normalizeProfileId(profileId));
    if (!entry) return false;
    return (entry.outRun || 0) >= UNANSWERED_OUT_HIDE_THRESHOLD && (entry.myLastTs || 0) > (entry.theirLastTs || 0);
  }
  // Auto-hide a profile that has deleted REPEATED_DELETE_HIDE_THRESHOLD (2)+ of their
  // own messages (chatDeletionStats[id].count). Gated by state.hideRepeatedDeleters
  // (default on).
  function shouldHideByRepeatedDelete(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ shouldHideByRepeatedDelete", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (state.hideRepeatedDeleters === false) return false;
    const stats = chatDeletionStats[normalizeProfileId(profileId)];
    return !!stats && (Number(stats.count) || 0) >= REPEATED_DELETE_HIDE_THRESHOLD;
  }
  // Count on-map profiles with a chat interaction in the last 24h (shared timestamp for the loop).
  function countRecentChatProfilesOnMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ countRecentChatProfilesOnMap", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = now();
    let count = 0;
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      if (hasRecentChatInteraction(id, t)) count += 1;
    }
    return count;
  }
  // Count on-map profiles that have any chat history.
  function countAnyChatProfilesOnMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ countAnyChatProfilesOnMap", traceArgs(arguments));
    let count = 0;
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      if (hasAnyChatInteraction(id)) count += 1;
    }
    return count;
  }
  // Count on-map profiles last active more than the configured window ago, excluding unanswered recent replies.
  function countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = now();
    const windowMs = notOnlineWindowMs();
    let count = 0;
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      if (hasUnansweredRecentReply(id, t)) continue;
      const ts = getProfileLastActiveTs(id);
      if (ts && t - ts > windowMs) count += 1;
    }
    return count;
  }
  // Stored 0-5 star rating for a profile (normalized), or 0 if unset.
  function getProfileRating(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileRating", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    return normalizeProfileRating(profileRatings[id]);
  }
  // Set or clear a profile's rating; deletes the entry when the normalized rating is 0.
  // Persists via saveProfileRatings (localStorage write + panel re-render + Drive sync); returns the applied rating.
  function setProfileRating(profileId, rating) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setProfileRating", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    const nextRating = normalizeProfileRating(rating);
    if (nextRating) profileRatings[id] = nextRating;
    else delete profileRatings[id];
    saveProfileRatings();
    return nextRating;
  }
  // Parsed lastSeenAt timestamp from a profile's bookmark row, or 0.
  function getBookmarkLastSeenTs(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getBookmarkLastSeenTs", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return 0;
    const row = bookmarks.get(id);
    return parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastSeenAt) || 0;
  }
  // Classify a bookmarked profile as "online" / "recent" / "offline".
  // "online" if currently on the map; "recent" if last seen within BOOKMARK_ONLINE_RECENT_MS (2h); else "offline".
  function getBookmarkOnlineState(profileId, t = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getBookmarkOnlineState", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return "offline";
    if (idToMarker.has(id)) return "online";
    const lastSeen = getBookmarkLastSeenTs(id);
    // BOOKMARK_ONLINE_RECENT_MS is 2h; bookmarks seen within this window show as "recent" rather than offline.
    if (lastSeen && t - lastSeen <= BOOKMARK_ONLINE_RECENT_MS) return "recent";
    return "offline";
  }
  // Human status string for a bookmark's online state (e.g. "status: online now", "status: online 5m ago", "status: offline").
  function formatBookmarkOnline(profileId, t = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatBookmarkOnline", traceArgs(arguments));
    const stateKey = getBookmarkOnlineState(profileId, t);
    if (stateKey === "online") return "status: online now";
    if (stateKey === "recent") {
      const seen = getBookmarkLastSeenTs(profileId);
      const age = seen ? `${formatAgeShort(seen)} ago` : "recently";
      return `status: online ${age}`;
    }
    return "status: offline";
  }
  // Stamp a bookmark's lastSeenAt/updatedAt and schedule a debounced save.
  // No-op (returns false) if no bookmark or if within 45s of the previous seen time (dedup guard).
  function markBookmarkSeen(profileId, ts = now()) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ markBookmarkSeen", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    const row = bookmarks.get(id);
    if (!row) return false;
    const prevSeen = parseTimestamp(row.lastSeenAt) || 0;
    // 45e3 = 45s dedup window; suppresses repeated lastSeen writes (and the debounced save) for rapid re-sightings.
    if (prevSeen && Math.abs(ts - prevSeen) < 45e3) return false;
    row.lastSeenAt = ts;
    row.updatedAt = Math.max(parseTimestamp(row.updatedAt) || 0, ts);
    bookmarks.set(id, row);
    scheduleSaveBookmarks(700);
    return true;
  }
  // Format a distance as "distance: N.N mi", or "distance: n/a" when not finite.
  function formatBookmarkDistance(distance) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatBookmarkDistance", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!Number.isFinite(distance)) return "distance: n/a";
    return `distance: ${distance.toFixed(1)} mi`;
  }
  // Format a last-interaction timestamp as "last interaction: <age> ago", or "...: never" when missing.
  function formatBookmarkInteraction(ts) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatBookmarkInteraction", traceArgs(arguments));
    const parsed = parseTimestamp(ts);
    if (!parsed) return "last interaction: never";
    const age = formatAgeShort(parsed);
    return `last interaction: ${age} ago`;
  }
  // Pick a bookmark's display name: alias, else profileLabel, else "Profile <id-prefix>".
  // Falls back to plain "Profile" when no usable id.
  function bookmarkDisplayName(row) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ bookmarkDisplayName", traceArgs(arguments));
    const alias = String((row == null ? void 0 : row.alias) || "").trim();
    if (alias) return alias;
    const label = String((row == null ? void 0 : row.profileLabel) || "").trim();
    if (label) return label;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(row == null ? void 0 : row.profileId);
    return id ? `Profile ${id.slice(0, 10)}` : "Profile";
  }
  // Create or merge a bookmark, carrying forward prior fields and clamping alias to 80 chars.
  // Persists synchronously (saveBookmarks) and re-renders the bookmark panel; preserves original createdAt, bumps updatedAt.
  function addOrUpdateBookmark(profileId, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ addOrUpdateBookmark", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e, _f, _g;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const prev = bookmarks.get(id);
    const next = {
      profileId: id,
      profileUrl: String(options.profileUrl || (prev == null ? void 0 : prev.profileUrl) || profileUrlForId(id)),
      profileLabel: String(options.profileLabel || (prev == null ? void 0 : prev.profileLabel) || `Profile ${id.slice(0, 10)}`),
      // Alias is trimmed and hard-capped at 80 characters.
      alias: String((_b = (_a = options.alias) != null ? _a : prev == null ? void 0 : prev.alias) != null ? _b : "").trim().slice(0, 80),
      categories: normalizeTagValues((_d = (_c = options.categories) != null ? _c : prev == null ? void 0 : prev.categories) != null ? _d : []),
      tags: normalizeTagValues((_f = (_e = options.tags) != null ? _e : prev == null ? void 0 : prev.tags) != null ? _f : []),
      lastSeenAt: parseTimestamp((_g = options.lastSeenAt) != null ? _g : prev == null ? void 0 : prev.lastSeenAt) || 0,
      createdAt: parseTimestamp(prev == null ? void 0 : prev.createdAt) || now(),
      updatedAt: now()
    };
    bookmarks.set(id, next);
    saveBookmarks();
    renderBookmarkPanel();
    return next;
  }
  // Patch an existing bookmark's editable fields, leaving unset fields at their prior value.
  // Returns null if no bookmark exists; delegates the actual write to addOrUpdateBookmark.
  function updateBookmark(profileId, patch = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateBookmark", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return null;
    const prev = bookmarks.get(id);
    if (!prev) return null;
    return addOrUpdateBookmark(id, {
      profileUrl: (_a = patch.profileUrl) != null ? _a : prev.profileUrl,
      profileLabel: (_b = patch.profileLabel) != null ? _b : prev.profileLabel,
      alias: (_c = patch.alias) != null ? _c : prev.alias,
      categories: (_d = patch.categories) != null ? _d : prev.categories,
      tags: (_e = patch.tags) != null ? _e : prev.tags
    });
  }
  // Delete a bookmark, then save and re-render the panel; false if it didn't exist.
  function removeBookmark(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ removeBookmark", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!id) return false;
    if (!bookmarks.has(id)) return false;
    bookmarks.delete(id);
    saveBookmarks();
    renderBookmarkPanel();
    return true;
  }
  // Return bookmarks sorted per state.bookmarkSortBy: "distance", "last-interaction", or default newest-by-createdAt.
  // Distance/interaction ties break by display name; unknown distances sort last.
  function sortedBookmarks() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sortedBookmarks", traceArgs(arguments));
    const rows = [...bookmarks.values()];
    const mode = String(state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest");
    if (mode === "distance") {
      rows.sort((a, b) => {
        const ad = getBookmarkDistance(a.profileId);
        const bd = getBookmarkDistance(b.profileId);
        const av = Number.isFinite(ad) ? ad : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
        const bv = Number.isFinite(bd) ? bd : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
        if (av !== bv) return av - bv;
        return (bookmarkDisplayName(a) || "").localeCompare(bookmarkDisplayName(b) || "");
      });
      return rows;
    }
    if (mode === "last-interaction") {
      rows.sort((a, b) => {
        const at = getBookmarkLastInteractionTs(a.profileId);
        const bt = getBookmarkLastInteractionTs(b.profileId);
        if (at !== bt) return bt - at;
        return (bookmarkDisplayName(a) || "").localeCompare(bookmarkDisplayName(b) || "");
      });
      return rows;
    }
    rows.sort((a, b) => (parseTimestamp(b.createdAt) || 0) - (parseTimestamp(a.createdAt) || 0));
    return rows;
  }
  // Test a bookmark row against the panel's category/tag/online filters.
  // Tag/category match is substring (needle.includes), case-insensitive; online uses live getBookmarkOnlineState.
  function bookmarkMatchesFilters(row) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ bookmarkMatchesFilters", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!row) return false;
    const categoryFilters = parseTagFilter(state.bookmarkCategoryFilter);
    const tagFilters = parseTagFilter(state.bookmarkTagFilter);
    const onlineFilter = String(state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all").toLowerCase();
    const categories = normalizeTagValues(row.categories).map((v) => v.toLowerCase());
    const tags = normalizeTagValues(row.tags).map((v) => v.toLowerCase());
    const hasCategory = !categoryFilters.length || categoryFilters.some(
      (needle) => categories.some((value) => value.includes(needle))
    );
    const hasTag = !tagFilters.length || tagFilters.some(
      (needle) => tags.some((value) => value.includes(needle))
    );
    if (!hasCategory || !hasTag) return false;
    if (onlineFilter === "all") return true;
    const onlineState = getBookmarkOnlineState(row.profileId);
    if (onlineFilter === "online") return onlineState === "online";
    if (onlineFilter === "recent") return onlineState === "recent";
    if (onlineFilter === "offline") return onlineState === "offline";
    return true;
  }
  // Create/overwrite a reminder for a profile at a parsed timestamp; note clamped to 180 chars.
  // Persists, re-renders the panel; returns null on bad id/time. notifiedApproach/Due start false.
  function addAppointment(profileId, atTs, profileUrl, profileLabel, note = "") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ addAppointment", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    const when = parseTimestamp(atTs);
    if (!id || !when) return null;
    const row = {
      id: createAppointmentId(id, when),
      profileId: id,
      profileUrl: profileUrl || profileUrlForId(id),
      profileLabel: profileLabel || id.slice(0, 10),
      // Reminder note is hard-capped at 180 characters.
      note: String(note || "").slice(0, 180),
      atTs: when,
      createdAt: now(),
      notifiedApproach: false,
      notifiedDue: false
    };
    appointments.set(row.id, row);
    saveAppointments();
    renderAppointmentPanel();
    return row;
  }
  // Delete one appointment by id, persist, and re-render the panel.
  function removeAppointment(id) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ removeAppointment", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!id) return;
    appointments.delete(id);
    saveAppointments();
    renderAppointmentPanel();
  }
  // Drop appointments older than APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS (or with an unparseable time).
  // Saves only if any removed; always re-renders; returns the count removed.
  function clearPastAppointments() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ clearPastAppointments", traceArgs(arguments));
    const cutoff = now() - APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS;
    let removed = 0;
    for (const [id, appt] of appointments.entries()) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(appt == null ? void 0 : appt.atTs);
      if (!ts || ts < cutoff) {
        appointments.delete(id);
        removed += 1;
      }
    }
    if (removed) saveAppointments();
    renderAppointmentPanel();
    return removed;
  }
  // Show a single transient alert toast (removes any prior popup first).
  // Auto-dismisses after 10s (1e4 ms) via a tracked timer; role=alert / aria-live assertive.
  function showReminderPopup(title, body) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ showReminderPopup", traceArgs(arguments));
    const existing = document.querySelector(".sniffies-reminder-popup");
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    if (reminderPopupTimer) {
      clearTimeout(reminderPopupTimer);
      reminderPopupTimer = 0;
    }
    const popup = document.createElement("div");
    popup.className = "sniffies-reminder-popup";
    popup.setAttribute("role", "alert");
    popup.setAttribute("aria-live", "assertive");
    const closeBtn = document.createElement("button");
    closeBtn.className = "sniffies-reminder-popup-close";
    closeBtn.title = "Close";
    closeBtn.textContent = "\xD7";
    closeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => popup.remove());
    const titleEl = document.createElement("div");
    titleEl.className = "sniffies-reminder-popup-title";
    titleEl.textContent = String(title || "");
    const bodyEl = document.createElement("div");
    bodyEl.textContent = String(body || "");
    popup.appendChild(closeBtn);
    popup.appendChild(titleEl);
    popup.appendChild(bodyEl);
    document.body.appendChild(popup);
    reminderPopupTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      reminderPopupTimer = 0;
      popup.remove();
    // 1e4 = 10000ms: the reminder popup auto-dismisses after 10 seconds.
    }, 1e4);
  }
  // Open a modal textarea to edit/save/delete the free-text note for one profile.
  // Empty value deletes the note; Save/Delete persist via saveNotes() then refresh the inline display.
  function showNotesModal(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ showNotesModal", traceArgs(arguments));
    const currentNote = notes[profileId] || "";
    const modal = document.createElement("div");
    modal.className = "sniffies-notes-modal";
    const content = document.createElement("div");
    content.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-content";
    const title = document.createElement("div");
    title.style.marginBottom = "12px";
    title.style.fontSize = "16px";
    title.style.fontWeight = "bold";
    title.textContent = `Notes for profile ${profileId}`;
    const textarea = document.createElement("textarea");
    textarea.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-textarea";
    textarea.placeholder = "Add notes about this profile...";
    textarea.value = currentNote;
    const buttons = document.createElement("div");
    buttons.className = "sniffies-notes-modal-buttons";
    const saveBtn = document.createElement("button");
    saveBtn.textContent = "Save";
    saveBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (textarea.value.trim()) {
        notes[profileId] = textarea.value;
      } else {
        delete notes[profileId];
      }
      saveNotes();
      modal.remove();
      updateProfileNotesDisplay(profileId);
    });
    const deleteBtn = document.createElement("button");
    deleteBtn.className = "btn-delete";
    deleteBtn.textContent = "Delete";
    deleteBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (confirm("Delete notes for this profile?")) {
        delete notes[profileId];
        saveNotes();
        modal.remove();
        updateProfileNotesDisplay(profileId);
      }
    });
    const cancelBtn = document.createElement("button");
    cancelBtn.textContent = "Cancel";
    cancelBtn.addEventListener("click", () => modal.remove());
    buttons.appendChild(saveBtn);
    buttons.appendChild(deleteBtn);
    buttons.appendChild(cancelBtn);
    content.appendChild(title);
    content.appendChild(textarea);
    content.appendChild(buttons);
    modal.appendChild(content);
    modal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) modal.remove();
    });
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
    textarea.focus();
  }
  // Debounced (default 220ms) call to updateProfileNotesDisplay for a profile.
  // Cancels any pending refresh first via the shared profileRefreshTimer.
  function scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, delayMs = 220) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!profileId) return;
    if (profileRefreshTimer) {
      clearTimeout(profileRefreshTimer);
      profileRefreshTimer = 0;
    }
    profileRefreshTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      profileRefreshTimer = 0;
      updateProfileNotesDisplay(profileId);
    }, delayMs);
  }
  // Best-effort human label for a profile from its container's heading (h1/h2/h3/[name]).
  // Rejects labels over 48 chars; falls back to 'Profile <id-prefix>'.
  function getProfileLabelFromContainer(profileId, container) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ getProfileLabelFromContainer", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId) || profileId;
    const heading = (_a = container == null ? void 0 : container.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(container, "h1, h2, h3, [class*='name']");
    const label = ((heading == null ? void 0 : heading.textContent) || "").trim();
    // Reject overly long headings (>48 chars) as profile labels; fall back to an id-based name.
    if (label && label.length <= 48) return label;
    return id ? `Profile ${id.slice(0, 10)}` : "Profile";
  }
  // Build the inline 'Remind Me' widget shown on a profile (summary + collapsible date/note form).
  // Save validates a future time, calls addAppointment, opens the appt panel, and re-renders. compactEmpty shrinks empty state.
  function buildProfileReminderDisplay(profileId, container, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildProfileReminderDisplay", traceArgs(arguments));
    const compactEmpty = !!options.compactEmpty;
    const reminderDisplay = document.createElement("div");
    reminderDisplay.className = "sniffies-profile-reminder";
    if (compactEmpty) reminderDisplay.classList.add("compact-empty");
    const head = document.createElement("div");
    head.className = "sniffies-profile-reminder-head";
    const summary = document.createElement("div");
    summary.className = "sniffies-profile-reminder-summary";
    const next = getNextAppointmentForProfile(profileId);
    summary.textContent = next ? `Next reminder: ${formatApptDate(next.atTs)} (${formatApptCountdown(next.atTs)})` : compactEmpty ? "No reminders" : "No reminders set";
    const toggleBtn = document.createElement("button");
    toggleBtn.className = "btn sniffies-profile-reminder-btn";
    toggleBtn.textContent = compactEmpty ? "Remind" : "Remind Me";
    head.appendChild(summary);
    head.appendChild(toggleBtn);
    const form = document.createElement("div");
    form.className = "sniffies-profile-reminder-form";
    const dtInput = document.createElement("input");
    dtInput.type = "datetime-local";
    // Default reminder time is 30 minutes from now (30*60*1e3 ms) when none exists.
    dtInput.value = formatDateTimeLocalValue((next == null ? void 0 : next.atTs) || now() + 30 * 60 * 1e3);
    const noteInput = document.createElement("input");
    noteInput.type = "text";
    noteInput.placeholder = "optional note";
    noteInput.style.flex = "1";
    noteInput.style.minWidth = "120px";
    noteInput.style.fontSize = "12px";
    noteInput.style.borderRadius = "6px";
    noteInput.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2)";
    noteInput.style.background = "rgba(255,255,255,.1)";
    noteInput.style.color = "#fff";
    noteInput.style.padding = "5px 6px";
    if (next == null ? void 0 : next.note) noteInput.value = next.note;
    const saveBtn = document.createElement("button");
    saveBtn.className = "btn sniffies-profile-reminder-btn";
    saveBtn.textContent = "Save";
    saveBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const atTs = parseDateTimeLocalValue(dtInput.value);
      if (!atTs) {
        alert("Invalid date/time");
        return;
      }
      if (atTs < now() - 60 * 1e3) {
        alert("Choose a future date/time");
        return;
      }
      const url = getProfileUrlFromContainer(profileId, container);
      const label = getProfileLabelFromContainer(profileId, container);
      const row = addAppointment(profileId, atTs, url, label, noteInput.value || "");
      if (!row) return;
      state.showAppointmentPanel = true;
      saveState();
      processAppointmentAlerts();
      setStatus(`Reminder set: ${formatApptDate(row.atTs)} for ${label}`);
      form.classList.remove("open");
      scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, 120);
    });
    const cancelBtn = document.createElement("button");
    cancelBtn.className = "btn sniffies-profile-reminder-btn";
    cancelBtn.textContent = "Cancel";
    cancelBtn.addEventListener("click", () => form.classList.remove("open"));
    form.appendChild(dtInput);
    form.appendChild(noteInput);
    form.appendChild(saveBtn);
    form.appendChild(cancelBtn);
    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      form.classList.toggle("open");
      if (form.classList.contains("open")) dtInput.focus();
    });
    reminderDisplay.appendChild(head);
    reminderDisplay.appendChild(form);
    return reminderDisplay;
  }
  // Insert a widget into a profile container, preferring after a given anchor else as first child.
  // Wrapped in try/catch with a prepend() fallback; returns whether insertion succeeded.
  function injectProfileWidget(container, widget, fallbackAfter = null) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ injectProfileWidget", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!container || !widget) return false;
    try {
      if (fallbackAfter && fallbackAfter.parentNode === container) {
        fallbackAfter.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", widget);
      } else {
        const firstChild = container.firstChild;
        if (firstChild) container.insertBefore(widget, firstChild);
        else container.appendChild(widget);
      }
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      try {
        container.prepend(widget);
        return true;
      } catch (e2) {
        return false;
      }
    }
  }
  // Rebuild the injected notes panel for a profile: note text, 5-star rating, bookmark/hide buttons, edit pencil.
  // Removes stale panels first, then injects notes + reminder widgets and starts the container observer.
  function updateProfileNotesDisplay(profileId) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateProfileNotesDisplay", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    const normalizedProfileId = normalizeProfileId(profileId);
    if (!normalizedProfileId) return;
    const container = findProfileContainer();
    if (!container) return;
    (_a = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-notes")) == null ? void 0 : _a.remove();
    (_b = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-reminder")) == null ? void 0 : _b.remove();
    const noteValue = String(notes[normalizedProfileId] || "");
    const hasNote = !!noteValue.trim();
    const hasReminder = !!getNextAppointmentForProfile(normalizedProfileId);
    const compactEmpty = !hasNote && !hasReminder;
    const notesDisplay = document.createElement("div");
    notesDisplay.className = "sniffies-profile-notes";
    if (compactEmpty) notesDisplay.classList.add("compact-empty");
    const text = document.createElement("div");
    text.className = "sniffies-profile-notes-text";
    text.textContent = hasNote ? noteValue : compactEmpty ? "No notes" : "No notes yet - click the pencil to add notes";
    if (!hasNote) {
      text.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5)";
      text.style.fontStyle = "italic";
    }
    const icon = document.createElement("div");
    icon.className = "sniffies-profile-notes-icon";
    icon.textContent = "\u270F\uFE0F";
    icon.title = "Edit notes";
    icon.addEventListener("click", () => showNotesModal(normalizedProfileId));
    const hideBtn = document.createElement("button");
    hideBtn.className = "sniffies-profile-hide-btn";
    hideBtn.textContent = blocked.has(normalizedProfileId) ? "Profile hidden" : "Hide this profile";
    if (blocked.has(normalizedProfileId)) hideBtn.disabled = true;
    hideBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const changed = hideProfileNow(normalizedProfileId);
      if (changed) {
        hideBtn.textContent = "Profile hidden";
        hideBtn.disabled = true;
      }
    });
    const bookmarkBtn = document.createElement("button");
    bookmarkBtn.className = "sniffies-profile-bookmark-btn";
    bookmarkBtn.textContent = isBookmarked(normalizedProfileId) ? "Bookmarked" : "Bookmark";
    bookmarkBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (isBookmarked(normalizedProfileId)) {
        state.showBookmarkPanel = true;
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
        setStatus(`Already bookmarked: ${normalizedProfileId}`);
        return;
      }
      const url = getProfileUrlFromContainer(normalizedProfileId, container);
      const label = getProfileLabelFromContainer(normalizedProfileId, container);
      addOrUpdateBookmark(normalizedProfileId, { profileUrl: url, profileLabel: label });
      state.showBookmarkPanel = true;
      saveState();
      renderBookmarkPanel();
      bookmarkBtn.textContent = "Bookmarked";
      setStatus(`Bookmarked: ${normalizedProfileId}`);
    });
    const ratingWrap = document.createElement("div");
    ratingWrap.className = "sniffies-profile-rating";
    const currentRating = getProfileRating(normalizedProfileId);
    for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i += 1) {
      const starBtn = document.createElement("button");
      starBtn.type = "button";
      starBtn.className = "sniffies-profile-rating-star";
      if (i <= currentRating) starBtn.classList.add("active");
      starBtn.textContent = "\u2605";
      starBtn.title = currentRating === i ? `Clear ${i}-star rating` : `Set ${i}-star rating`;
      starBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const nextRating = setProfileRating(normalizedProfileId, currentRating === i ? 0 : i);
        updateProfileNotesDisplay(normalizedProfileId);
        setStatus(
          nextRating ? `Rated ${normalizedProfileId}: ${nextRating}/5` : `Cleared rating: ${normalizedProfileId}`
        );
      });
      ratingWrap.appendChild(starBtn);
    }
    notesDisplay.appendChild(text);
    notesDisplay.appendChild(ratingWrap);
    notesDisplay.appendChild(bookmarkBtn);
    notesDisplay.appendChild(hideBtn);
    notesDisplay.appendChild(icon);
    notesDisplay.style.display = "flex";
    notesDisplay.style.backgroundColor = "rgba(100, 150, 255, 0.1)";
    notesDisplay.style.borderLeft = "3px solid rgba(100, 150, 255, 0.5)";
    notesDisplay.style.padding = "10px";
    notesDisplay.style.marginBottom = "12px";
    notesDisplay.style.alignItems = "center";
    notesDisplay.style.gap = "8px";
    notesDisplay.style.borderRadius = "6px";
    notesDisplay.style.position = "relative";
    notesDisplay.style.zIndex = "1000";
    const reminderDisplay = buildProfileReminderDisplay(normalizedProfileId, container, { compactEmpty });
    injectProfileWidget(container, notesDisplay);
    injectProfileWidget(container, reminderDisplay, notesDisplay);
    observeContainerForChanges(normalizedProfileId, container);
  }
  // Watch a profile container and re-inject widgets if the notes/reminder nodes get removed.
  // Uses a single-child MutationObserver plus a 1500ms interval; resets any prior observer/timer.
  function observeContainerForChanges(profileId, container) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ observeContainerForChanges", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!container) return;
    if (profileContainerObserver) {
      profileContainerObserver.disconnect();
      profileContainerObserver = null;
    }
    if (profileContainerCheckTimer) {
      clearInterval(profileContainerCheckTimer);
      profileContainerCheckTimer = 0;
    }
    profileContainerObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (!document.body.contains(container)) return;
      const notesStillThere = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-notes");
      const reminderStillThere = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-reminder");
      if (!notesStillThere || !reminderStillThere) {
        scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, 120);
      }
    });
    profileContainerObserver.observe(container, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: false
    });
    profileContainerCheckTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.body.contains(container)) return;
      const notesStillThere = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-notes");
      const reminderStillThere = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-reminder");
      if (!notesStillThere || !reminderStillThere) {
        scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, 120);
      }
    }, 1500);
  }
  // Global observer: detect profile/container changes on the SPA and schedule a display refresh.
  // Body MutationObserver (href/data-testid only — see attributeFilter below) + 1500ms poll; also kicks an initial refresh.
  function observeProfileChanges() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ observeProfileChanges", traceArgs(arguments));
    let lastProfileId = null;
    let lastContainer = null;
    logInfo("Starting profile observer");
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      const profileId = normalizeProfileId(getCurrentProfileId());
      if (!profileId) return;
      const container = findProfileContainer();
      if (!container) return;
      if (profileId !== lastProfileId || container !== lastContainer) {
        lastProfileId = profileId;
        lastContainer = container;
        scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, 220);
      }
    });
    observer.observe(document.body, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
      attributes: true,
      // Watch only href/data-testid — NOT class. The engine mutates marker/chat `class` constantly (hide and
      // highlight toggles in applyHiding/applyGlobalChatHiding); watching class here made the engine retrigger
      // this body-wide observer every pass, each time doing reflow-heavy getCurrentProfileId/findProfileContainer.
      attributeFilter: ["href", "data-testid"]
    });
    bootObservers.push(observer);
    // Registered in bootTimers so teardownSniffies() can stop this route/profile poll
    // too (every other boot interval is tracked; this one was previously orphaned).
    bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
      const profileId = normalizeProfileId(getCurrentProfileId());
      if (!profileId) return;
      const container = findProfileContainer();
      if (!container) return;
      const notesVisible = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-notes");
      const reminderVisible = container.querySelector(".sniffies-profile-reminder");
      if (profileId !== lastProfileId || container !== lastContainer || !notesVisible || !reminderVisible) {
        lastProfileId = profileId;
        lastContainer = container;
        scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(profileId, 140);
      }
    }, 1500));
    const initialId = normalizeProfileId(getCurrentProfileId());
    logInfo("Initial profile ID check:", initialId);
    if (initialId) {
      scheduleProfileDisplayRefresh(initialId, 420);
    }
  }
  // Format a numeric distance as 'distance: N.N mi', or 'distance: n/a' when missing/non-finite.
  function formatDistance(distance) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ formatDistance", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (distance == null || !Number.isFinite(distance)) return "distance: n/a";
    return `distance: ${distance.toFixed(1)} mi`;
  }
  // Return include-match rows sorted by ascending distance (n/a sorts last), tie-broken by id.
  function sortedIncludeMatches() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sortedIncludeMatches", traceArgs(arguments));
    return [...includeMatches.values()].sort((a, b) => {
      const ad = Number.isFinite(a.distance) ? a.distance : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      const bd = Number.isFinite(b.distance) ? b.distance : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      if (ad !== bd) return ad - bd;
      return (a.id || "").localeCompare(b.id || "");
    });
  }
  // Write the live exclude/include counts into the #sfExcludeCount / #sfIncludeCount badges.
  // Exclude count is profiles in profileFilterCache flagged excludeTerm; include is includeMatches.size.
  function updateTextFilterMatchCounts() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateTextFilterMatchCounts", traceArgs(arguments));
    let excludeCount = 0;
    for (const f of profileFilterCache.values()) {
      if (f == null ? void 0 : f.excludeTerm) excludeCount += 1;
    }
    const includeCount = includeMatches.size;
    const excludeEl = document.querySelector("#sfExcludeCount");
    const includeEl = document.querySelector("#sfIncludeCount");
    if (excludeEl) excludeEl.textContent = String(excludeCount);
    if (includeEl) includeEl.textContent = String(includeCount);
  }
  // Dismiss every current include match (adds each id to includeDismissed so it won't re-add), clears the map.
  // Re-renders the match panel and re-applies hiding.
  function clearAllIncludeMatches() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ clearAllIncludeMatches", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!includeMatches.size) return;
    for (const id of includeMatches.keys()) includeDismissed.add(id);
    includeMatches.clear();
    renderMatchPanel();
    applyHiding();
  }
  // Lazily create the draggable 'Include Matches' floating panel (cached in matchPanelEl).
  // Wires Hide and Clear All buttons; restores saved position.
  function buildMatchPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildMatchPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (matchPanelEl) return matchPanelEl;
    const panel2 = document.createElement("div");
    panel2.className = "sniffies-match-panel";
    panel2.innerHTML = `
      <div class="head">
        <strong>Include Matches</strong>
        <div class="head-actions">
          <button class="btn" id="sfMatchClearAll">Clear All</button>
          <button class="btn" id="sfMatchHide">Hide</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="list" id="sfMatchList"></div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(panel2);
    applyMatchPanelPos(panel2);
    const hideBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfMatchHide");
    const clearAllBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfMatchClearAll");
    hideBtn == null ? void 0 : hideBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.showMatchPanel = false;
      saveState();
      renderMatchPanel();
    });
    clearAllBtn == null ? void 0 : clearAllBtn.addEventListener("click", clearAllIncludeMatches);
    const head = panel2.querySelector(".head");
    makePanelDraggable(panel2, head);
    matchPanelEl = panel2;
    return panel2;
  }
  // Render include-match rows; hidden when include terms disabled, none present, or panel toggled off.
  // Each row links the profile, shows distance + matched term, and a Remove (dismiss) button.
  function renderMatchPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ renderMatchPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const panel2 = buildMatchPanel();
    if (!panel2) return;
    updateTextFilterMatchCounts();
    const rows = sortedIncludeMatches();
    const clearAllBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfMatchClearAll");
    if (clearAllBtn) clearAllBtn.disabled = rows.length === 0;
    if (state.includeProfileTermsEnabled === false || !profileIncludeTerms.length || !rows.length) {
      panel2.style.display = "none";
      return;
    }
    if (!state.showMatchPanel) {
      panel2.style.display = "none";
      return;
    }
    panel2.style.display = "flex";
    const list = panel2.querySelector("#sfMatchList");
    if (!list) return;
    list.textContent = "";
    for (const row of rows) {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "item";
      const left = document.createElement("div");
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = row.href;
      link.target = "_blank";
      link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
      link.textContent = row.href;
      left.appendChild(link);
      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "meta";
      meta.textContent = `${formatDistance(row.distance)} | matched: "${row.includeTerm}"`;
      left.appendChild(meta);
      const removeBtn = document.createElement("button");
      removeBtn.className = "btn";
      removeBtn.textContent = "Remove";
      removeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        includeDismissed.add(row.id);
        includeMatches.delete(row.id);
        renderMatchPanel();
        applyHiding();
      });
      item.appendChild(left);
      item.appendChild(removeBtn);
      list.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
  // Type a message into the active chat input and send it (click send button, else press Enter).
  // Records an outbound preview + chat activity; marks the phrase as used unless options.markPhrase===false.
  function sendMessageInCurrentChat(profileId, message, options = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ sendMessageInCurrentChat", traceArgs(arguments));
    const id = normalizeProfileId(profileId || getChatContextProfileId());
    const text = String(message || "").trim();
    if (!id || !text) return false;
    const input = findChatInputElement();
    if (!input) return false;
    if (!fillChatInput(input, text)) return false;
    const clicked = clickChatSendButton(input);
    if (!clicked) pressEnterToSend(input);
    const sentTs = now();
    addChatPreviewMessage(id, "out", sentTs, text);
    upsertChatActivity(id, sentTs, 0);
    if ((options == null ? void 0 : options.markPhrase) !== false) markPhraseUsedForProfile(id, text);
    return true;
  }
  // Halt the broadcast job: clear its interval, null the active job, re-render the bookmark panel.
  // Status text varies by reason (user/complete/other); returns false if nothing was running.
  function stopBroadcastMessage(reason = "stopped") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ stopBroadcastMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (broadcastTimer) {
      clearInterval(broadcastTimer);
      broadcastTimer = 0;
    }
    const wasActive = !!activeBroadcastJob;
    const prev = activeBroadcastJob;
    activeBroadcastJob = null;
    renderBookmarkPanel();
    if (!wasActive) return false;
    if (reason === "user") {
      setStatus(`Broadcast stopped: queued ${prev.sent}/${prev.total} (skipped ${prev.skipped})`);
    } else if (reason === "complete") {
      setStatus(`Broadcast done: sent ${prev.sent}, skipped ${prev.skipped}`);
    } else {
      setStatus(`Broadcast ${reason}: queued ${prev.sent}/${prev.total} (skipped ${prev.skipped})`);
    }
    return true;
  }
  // Queue a message to many bookmarked profiles, dedup+cap at BROADCAST_MAX_RECIPIENTS.
  // Sends first immediately then one per BROADCAST_OPEN_INTERVAL_MS via setInterval; replaces any running job.
  function startBroadcastMessage(message, recipientIds) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ startBroadcastMessage", traceArgs(arguments));
    const text = String(message || "").trim();
    if (!text) {
      setStatus("Broadcast: message is empty");
      return;
    }
    // Dedup recipients via a Set, then cap at BROADCAST_MAX_RECIPIENTS.
    const uniqueIds = [...new Set((recipientIds || []).map(normalizeProfileId).filter(Boolean))].slice(0, BROADCAST_MAX_RECIPIENTS);
    if (!uniqueIds.length) {
      setStatus("Broadcast: no recipients after filters");
      return;
    }
    if (activeBroadcastJob) stopBroadcastMessage("replaced");
    activeBroadcastJob = {
      ids: uniqueIds,
      text,
      sent: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      index: 0,
      total: uniqueIds.length,
      startedAt: now()
    };
    // Broadcast iterator (one tick): open the next recipient's chat with bypassCooldown and queue the text.
    // Tallies sent/skipped, updates status, finishes via stopBroadcastMessage('complete') when index passes total.
    const step = () => {
      log("→ step");
      if (!activeBroadcastJob) return;
      if (activeBroadcastJob.index >= activeBroadcastJob.total) {
        stopBroadcastMessage("complete");
        return;
      }
      const id = activeBroadcastJob.ids[activeBroadcastJob.index];
      activeBroadcastJob.index += 1;
      const queued = openAutoMessageChat(id, {
        phrase: activeBroadcastJob.text,
        bypassCooldown: true,
        suppressStatus: true
      });
      if (queued == null ? void 0 : queued.ok) {
        activeBroadcastJob.sent += 1;
      } else {
        activeBroadcastJob.skipped += 1;
      }
      setStatus(`Broadcast: queued ${activeBroadcastJob.sent}/${activeBroadcastJob.total} (skipped ${activeBroadcastJob.skipped})`);
      renderBookmarkPanel();
    };
    setStatus(`Broadcast started: ${activeBroadcastJob.total} recipient(s)`);
    renderBookmarkPanel();
    step();
    if (activeBroadcastJob && activeBroadcastJob.index < activeBroadcastJob.total) {
      // After the immediate first send, pace remaining sends one per BROADCAST_OPEN_INTERVAL_MS.
      broadcastTimer = setInterval(step, BROADCAST_OPEN_INTERVAL_MS);
    }
  }
  // Lazily build the draggable Bookmarks panel: sort select, category/tag/online filters, broadcast box, list.
  // Wires all controls to mutate state + re-render; Broadcast confirms then calls startBroadcastMessage on filtered rows.
  function buildBookmarkPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildBookmarkPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    if (bookmarkPanelEl) return bookmarkPanelEl;
    const panel2 = document.createElement("div");
    panel2.className = "sniffies-bookmarks-panel";
    panel2.innerHTML = `
      <div class="head">
        <strong>Bookmarks</strong>
        <div class="head-actions">
          <select class="sort" id="sfBookmarkSort" title="Sort bookmarks">
            <option value="newest">Newest</option>
            <option value="distance">Distance</option>
            <option value="last-interaction">Last interaction</option>
          </select>
          <button class="btn" id="sfBookmarkHide">Hide</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="filters">
        <input type="text" id="sfBookmarkCategoryFilter" placeholder="Filter category">
        <input type="text" id="sfBookmarkTagFilter" placeholder="Filter tag">
        <select id="sfBookmarkOnlineFilter" title="Online status filter">
          <option value="all">All statuses</option>
          <option value="online">Online now</option>
          <option value="recent">Online past 2h</option>
          <option value="offline">Offline</option>
        </select>
        <button class="btn" id="sfBookmarkFilterClear">Clear</button>
      </div>
      <div class="broadcast">
        <textarea id="sfBookmarkBroadcastText" placeholder="Broadcast message to filtered bookmarks"></textarea>
        <div class="broadcast-actions">
          <button class="btn" id="sfBookmarkBroadcastSend">Broadcast</button>
          <button class="btn" id="sfBookmarkBroadcastStop">Stop</button>
        </div>
        <div class="hint" id="sfBookmarkBroadcastHint"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="list" id="sfBookmarkList"></div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(panel2);
    applyBookmarkPanelPos(panel2);
    const sortSelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkSort");
    const categoryFilterInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkCategoryFilter");
    const tagFilterInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkTagFilter");
    const onlineFilterSelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkOnlineFilter");
    const clearFilterBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkFilterClear");
    const broadcastInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastText");
    const broadcastBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastSend");
    const broadcastStopBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastStop");
    const broadcastHint = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastHint");
    if (sortSelect) {
      sortSelect.value = state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest";
      sortSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
        state.bookmarkSortBy = sortSelect.value || "newest";
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
      });
    }
    if (categoryFilterInput) {
      categoryFilterInput.value = state.bookmarkCategoryFilter || "";
      categoryFilterInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
        state.bookmarkCategoryFilter = categoryFilterInput.value || "";
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
      });
    }
    if (tagFilterInput) {
      tagFilterInput.value = state.bookmarkTagFilter || "";
      tagFilterInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
        state.bookmarkTagFilter = tagFilterInput.value || "";
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
      });
    }
    if (onlineFilterSelect) {
      onlineFilterSelect.value = state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all";
      onlineFilterSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
        state.bookmarkOnlineFilter = String(onlineFilterSelect.value || "all");
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
      });
    }
    clearFilterBtn == null ? void 0 : clearFilterBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.bookmarkCategoryFilter = "";
      state.bookmarkTagFilter = "";
      state.bookmarkOnlineFilter = "all";
      saveState();
      if (categoryFilterInput) categoryFilterInput.value = "";
      if (tagFilterInput) tagFilterInput.value = "";
      if (onlineFilterSelect) onlineFilterSelect.value = "all";
      renderBookmarkPanel();
    });
    broadcastBtn == null ? void 0 : broadcastBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const msg = String((broadcastInput == null ? void 0 : broadcastInput.value) || "").trim();
      if (!msg) {
        setStatus("Broadcast: enter a message first");
        return;
      }
      const filtered = sortedBookmarks().filter(bookmarkMatchesFilters);
      if (!filtered.length) {
        setStatus("Broadcast: no bookmarks match current filters");
        return;
      }
      const names = filtered.slice(0, 3).map((row) => bookmarkDisplayName(row)).filter(Boolean);
      const preview = names.length ? `
Examples: ${names.join(", ")}${filtered.length > 3 ? ", ..." : ""}` : "";
      const ok = window.confirm(`Broadcast this message to ${filtered.length} bookmarked profile(s)?${preview}

"${msg}"`);
      if (!ok) return;
      startBroadcastMessage(msg, filtered.map((row) => row.profileId));
    });
    broadcastStopBtn == null ? void 0 : broadcastStopBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (!activeBroadcastJob) {
        setStatus("Broadcast: no active job");
        return;
      }
      stopBroadcastMessage("user");
    });
    if (broadcastHint) broadcastHint.textContent = "Uses current filters (category/tag/online). Opens chats in background and queues sends.";
    (_a = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkHide")) == null ? void 0 : _a.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.showBookmarkPanel = false;
      saveState();
      renderBookmarkPanel();
    });
    makePanelDraggable(panel2, panel2.querySelector(".head"), saveBookmarkPanelPos);
    bookmarkPanelEl = panel2;
    return panel2;
  }
  // Render the bookmark list filtered by the active category/tag/online filters and current sort.
  // Per row: link, meta (online/distance/last-interaction/rating), notes, and editable alias/categories/tags fields. Syncs broadcast button enable + hint.
  function renderBookmarkPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ renderBookmarkPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const panel2 = buildBookmarkPanel();
    if (!panel2) return;
    if (!state.showBookmarkPanel) {
      panel2.style.display = "none";
      return;
    }
    panel2.style.display = "flex";
    const sortSelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkSort");
    if (sortSelect && sortSelect.value !== (state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest")) {
      sortSelect.value = state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest";
    }
    const categoryFilterInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkCategoryFilter");
    const tagFilterInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkTagFilter");
    const onlineFilterSelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkOnlineFilter");
    const broadcastBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastSend");
    const broadcastStopBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastStop");
    const broadcastHint = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkBroadcastHint");
    if (categoryFilterInput && categoryFilterInput.value !== (state.bookmarkCategoryFilter || "")) {
      categoryFilterInput.value = state.bookmarkCategoryFilter || "";
    }
    if (tagFilterInput && tagFilterInput.value !== (state.bookmarkTagFilter || "")) {
      tagFilterInput.value = state.bookmarkTagFilter || "";
    }
    if (onlineFilterSelect && onlineFilterSelect.value !== (state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all")) {
      onlineFilterSelect.value = state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all";
    }
    const list = panel2.querySelector("#sfBookmarkList");
    if (!list) return;
    list.textContent = "";
    const allRows = sortedBookmarks();
    const rows = allRows.filter(bookmarkMatchesFilters);
    if (broadcastBtn) broadcastBtn.disabled = !!activeBroadcastJob;
    if (broadcastStopBtn) broadcastStopBtn.disabled = !activeBroadcastJob;
    if (broadcastHint) {
      const jobs = activeBroadcastJob;
      if (jobs) {
        broadcastHint.textContent = `Broadcast running: queued ${jobs.sent}/${jobs.total}, skipped ${jobs.skipped}. Click Stop to interrupt.`;
      } else {
        const total = rows.length;
        broadcastHint.textContent = total ? `Broadcast target count: ${total}` : "No bookmark recipients for current filters";
      }
    }
    const titleEl = panel2.querySelector(".head strong");
    if (titleEl) {
      titleEl.textContent = rows.length === allRows.length ? `Bookmarks (${rows.length})` : `Bookmarks (${rows.length}/${allRows.length})`;
    }
    if (!rows.length) {
      const empty = document.createElement("div");
      empty.className = "empty";
      empty.textContent = allRows.length ? "No bookmarks match current filters" : "No bookmarks yet";
      list.appendChild(empty);
      return;
    }
    for (const row of rows) {
      const id = normalizeProfileId(row.profileId);
      if (!id) continue;
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "item";
      const top = document.createElement("div");
      top.className = "item-top";
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = row.profileUrl || profileUrlForId(id);
      link.target = "_blank";
      link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
      link.textContent = bookmarkDisplayName(row);
      top.appendChild(link);
      const removeBtn = document.createElement("button");
      removeBtn.className = "btn";
      removeBtn.textContent = "Remove";
      removeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        removeBookmark(id);
        setStatus(`Removed bookmark: ${id}`);
      });
      top.appendChild(removeBtn);
      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "meta";
      const categories = (row.categories || []).join(", ") || "none";
      const tags = (row.tags || []).join(", ") || "none";
      meta.textContent = [
        `profile: ${id}`,
        formatBookmarkOnline(id),
        formatBookmarkDistance(getBookmarkDistance(id)),
        formatBookmarkInteraction(getBookmarkLastInteractionTs(id)),
        getProfileRating(id) ? `rating: ${getProfileRating(id)}/5` : "",
        `categories: ${categories}`,
        `tags: ${tags}`
      ].join("\n");
      const notesEl = document.createElement("div");
      notesEl.className = "notes";
      const noteText = String(notes[id] || "").trim();
      notesEl.textContent = noteText ? noteText : "No notes";
      const fields = document.createElement("div");
      fields.className = "fields";
      const aliasField = document.createElement("div");
      aliasField.className = "field";
      aliasField.innerHTML = `<span class="label">Alias</span>`;
      const aliasInput = document.createElement("input");
      aliasInput.type = "text";
      aliasInput.placeholder = "Optional display alias";
      aliasInput.value = String(row.alias || "");
      aliasField.appendChild(aliasInput);
      const categoriesField = document.createElement("div");
      categoriesField.className = "field";
      categoriesField.innerHTML = `<span class="label">Categories</span>`;
      const categoriesInput = document.createElement("input");
      categoriesInput.type = "text";
      categoriesInput.placeholder = "comma-separated";
      categoriesInput.value = (row.categories || []).join(", ");
      categoriesField.appendChild(categoriesInput);
      const tagsField = document.createElement("div");
      tagsField.className = "field";
      tagsField.innerHTML = `<span class="label">Tags</span>`;
      const tagsInput = document.createElement("input");
      tagsInput.type = "text";
      tagsInput.placeholder = "comma-separated";
      tagsInput.value = (row.tags || []).join(", ");
      tagsField.appendChild(tagsInput);
      // On-blur saver for a bookmark row: write alias + normalized categories/tags via updateBookmark.
      const persist = () => {
        log("→ persist");
        updateBookmark(id, {
          alias: aliasInput.value || "",
          categories: normalizeTagValues(categoriesInput.value || ""),
          tags: normalizeTagValues(tagsInput.value || "")
        });
      };
      aliasInput.addEventListener("blur", persist);
      categoriesInput.addEventListener("blur", persist);
      tagsInput.addEventListener("blur", persist);
      for (const input of [aliasInput, categoriesInput, tagsInput]) {
        input.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
          if (e.key !== "Enter") return;
          e.preventDefault();
          input.blur();
        });
      }
      fields.appendChild(aliasField);
      fields.appendChild(categoriesField);
      fields.appendChild(tagsField);
      item.appendChild(top);
      item.appendChild(meta);
      item.appendChild(notesEl);
      item.appendChild(fields);
      list.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
  // Re-render the bookmark panel only if it's shown and there are bookmarks or an existing panel element.
  // Cheap guard to avoid building the panel for nothing.
  function refreshBookmarkPanelMaybe() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ refreshBookmarkPanelMaybe", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!state.showBookmarkPanel) return;
    if (!bookmarks.size && !bookmarkPanelEl) return;
    renderBookmarkPanel();
  }
  // Lazily build the draggable Appointments panel (cached in appointmentPanelEl).
  // Wires Hide and 'Clear Past' (clearPastAppointments) buttons; restores saved position.
  function buildAppointmentPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildAppointmentPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a, _b;
    if (appointmentPanelEl) return appointmentPanelEl;
    const panel2 = document.createElement("div");
    panel2.className = "sniffies-appointments-panel";
    panel2.innerHTML = `
      <div class="head">
        <strong>Appointments</strong>
        <div class="head-actions">
          <button class="btn" id="sfApptClearPast">Clear Past</button>
          <button class="btn" id="sfApptHide">Hide</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="list" id="sfApptList"></div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(panel2);
    applyApptPanelPos(panel2);
    (_a = panel2.querySelector("#sfApptHide")) == null ? void 0 : _a.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.showAppointmentPanel = false;
      saveState();
      renderAppointmentPanel();
    });
    (_b = panel2.querySelector("#sfApptClearPast")) == null ? void 0 : _b.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const removed = clearPastAppointments();
      setStatus(`Cleared ${removed} past appointment${removed === 1 ? "" : "s"}`);
    });
    makePanelDraggable(panel2, panel2.querySelector(".head"), saveApptPanelPos);
    appointmentPanelEl = panel2;
    return panel2;
  }
  // Render sorted appointments; hidden when none or panel toggled off.
  // Each item carries a status class (appointmentStatus), date/countdown, optional note, and a Done (remove) button.
  function renderAppointmentPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ renderAppointmentPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    const panel2 = buildAppointmentPanel();
