    if (!panel2) return;
    const rows = sortedAppointments();
    if (!rows.length || !state.showAppointmentPanel) {
      panel2.style.display = "none";
      return;
    }
    panel2.style.display = "flex";
    const list = panel2.querySelector("#sfApptList");
    if (!list) return;
    list.textContent = "";
    const t = now();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      const status = appointmentStatus(row, t);
      item.className = `item ${status}`;
      const left = document.createElement("div");
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = row.profileUrl || profileUrlForId(row.profileId);
      link.target = "_blank";
      link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
      link.textContent = row.profileLabel || row.profileId;
      left.appendChild(link);
      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "meta";
      const lines = [`${formatApptDate(row.atTs)} (${formatApptCountdown(row.atTs)})`];
      if (row.note) lines.push(`note: ${row.note}`);
      meta.textContent = lines.join("\n");
      left.appendChild(meta);
      const removeBtn = document.createElement("button");
      removeBtn.className = "btn";
      removeBtn.textContent = "Done";
      removeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        removeAppointment(row.id);
      });
      item.appendChild(left);
      item.appendChild(removeBtn);
      list.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
  // Fire one-shot reminder popups: an 'approach' alert within getApproachMs(), then a 'due' alert at/after the time.
  // Sets per-appointment notifiedApproach/notifiedDue flags so each fires once; saves if any changed; re-renders.
  function processAppointmentAlerts() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ processAppointmentAlerts", traceArgs(arguments));
    const t = now();
    const approachMs = getApproachMs();
    let changed = false;
    for (const appt of appointments.values()) {
      const atTs = parseTimestamp(appt.atTs);
      if (!atTs) continue;
      const dueIn = atTs - t;
      if (!appt.notifiedApproach && dueIn > 0 && dueIn <= approachMs) {
        appt.notifiedApproach = true;
        changed = true;
        showReminderPopup("Upcoming Appointment", `${appt.profileLabel} at ${formatApptDate(appt.atTs)} (${formatApptCountdown(appt.atTs)})`);
      }
      if (!appt.notifiedDue && dueIn <= 0) {
        appt.notifiedDue = true;
        changed = true;
        showReminderPopup("Appointment Now", `${appt.profileLabel} scheduled for ${formatApptDate(appt.atTs)}`);
      }
    }
    if (changed) saveAppointments();
    renderAppointmentPanel();
  }
  // Periodic GC: prune stale/orphaned entries across every cache, map, bookmark/appointment, chat-preview, phrase, and tab-handle store.
  // Caps unbounded sets (includeDismissed>1500, capture urls>240, recent>60); persists changed stores and re-renders affected panels.
  // Returns a per-bucket counts report (also exposed for console debugging).
  function runMemoryGc(reason = "interval") {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ runMemoryGc", traceArgs(arguments));
    var _a;
    const t = now();
    let prunedMarkers = 0;
    let prunedAttitudes = 0;
    let prunedProfileText = 0;
    let prunedProfileFilter = 0;
    let prunedProfileSummary = 0;
    let prunedIncludeMatches = 0;
    let prunedRetries = 0;
    let prunedAppointments = 0;
    let prunedHowdy = 0;
    let prunedBookmarks = 0;
    let prunedChatPreview = 0;
    let prunedPhraseHistory = 0;
    let prunedPhraseStats = 0;
    let prunedChatDeletes = 0;
    let prunedChatDeleteEvents = 0;
    let clearedPendingMessage = 0;
    let prunedAutoTabs = 0;
    let prunedStorageDigests = 0;
    for (const [id, marker] of idToMarker.entries()) {
      if (!marker || !document.body.contains(marker)) {
        idToMarker.delete(id);
        prunedMarkers += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, entry] of attitudeCache.entries()) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.ts);
      if (!idToMarker.has(id) && (!ts || t - ts > ATTITUDE_RETENTION_MS)) {
        attitudeCache.delete(id);
        prunedAttitudes += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, entry] of profileTextCache.entries()) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.ts);
      const stale = !ts || t - ts > CACHE_RETENTION_MS;
      if (!idToMarker.has(id) && stale) {
        profileTextCache.delete(id);
        prunedProfileText += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, entry] of profileFilterCache.entries()) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.ts);
      const stale = !ts || t - ts > CACHE_RETENTION_MS;
      if (!idToMarker.has(id) && stale) {
        profileFilterCache.delete(id);
        prunedProfileFilter += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, entry] of profileSummaryCache.entries()) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.updatedAt);
      const stale = !ts || t - ts > CACHE_RETENTION_MS;
      if (!idToMarker.has(id) && stale) {
        profileSummaryCache.delete(id);
        prunedProfileSummary += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, entry] of includeMatches.entries()) {
      const includeTs = parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.ts);
      // Treat a missing/unparseable ts as stale (not as "just now"). The old `|| t` made the delta 0, so a
      // ts-less entry whose marker left the map could never be pruned and leaked for the life of the tab.
      if (!idToMarker.has(id) && (!includeTs || t - includeTs > CACHE_RETENTION_MS)) {
        includeMatches.delete(id);
        prunedIncludeMatches += 1;
      }
    }
    // Cap the dismissed-id set at 1500 by evicting the oldest entries (Set iteration order == insertion
    // order). A dismissed id is REMOVED from includeMatches at dismiss time (see dismissAllIncludeMatches),
    // so rebuilding from includeMatches' current membership would keep only currently-active (i.e. NOT
    // dismissed) ids and wipe almost all real dismissal history — trim by recency instead.
    if (includeDismissed.size > 1500) {
      const excess = includeDismissed.size - 1500;
      const it = includeDismissed.values();
      for (let i = 0; i < excess; i++) {
        const next = it.next();
        if (next.done) break;
        includeDismissed.delete(next.value);
      }
    }
    for (const [id, retryTs] of profileTextRetryAt.entries()) {
      const retry = parseTimestamp(retryTs);
      if (!retry || t - retry > RETRY_RETENTION_MS) {
        profileTextRetryAt.delete(id);
        prunedRetries += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, row] of bookmarks.entries()) {
      const normalized = normalizeProfileId(id);
      if (!normalized || !row || typeof row !== "object") {
        bookmarks.delete(id);
        prunedBookmarks += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedBookmarks) saveBookmarks();
    for (const [id, rows] of chatPreviewByProfile.entries()) {
      const clean = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows.filter((row) => {
        const ts = parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.ts);
        return !!ts && t - ts <= CHAT_PREVIEW_TTL_MS;
      }) : [];
      if (!clean.length) {
        chatPreviewByProfile.delete(id);
        prunedChatPreview += 1;
        continue;
      }
      if (clean.length !== rows.length) {
        chatPreviewByProfile.set(id, clean.slice(-AUTO_MESSAGE_PREVIEW_LIMIT));
        prunedChatPreview += rows.length - clean.length;
      }
    }
    for (const [id, row] of Object.entries(phraseHistory)) {
      const updated = parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt);
      // Phrase history entries expire after 90 days.
      const stale = !updated || t - updated > 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3;
      const invalidId = !normalizeProfileId(id);
      if (stale || invalidId) {
        delete phraseHistory[id];
        prunedPhraseHistory += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedPhraseHistory) savePhraseHistory();
    for (const [key, row] of Object.entries(phraseStats || {})) {
      const normalizedKey = String(key || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const count = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(row == null ? void 0 : row.count) || 0));
      const lastUsedAt = parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastUsedAt) || 0;
      // Phrase usage stats expire by AGE only (180 days). Do NOT delete just because a phrase isn't currently
      // canned — editing or removing a phrase would otherwise wipe its usage history, and re-adding the same
      // phrase would reset its count/recency to zero.
      const stale = !lastUsedAt || t - lastUsedAt > 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3;
      if (!normalizedKey || count <= 0 || stale) {
        delete phraseStats[key];
        prunedPhraseStats += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedPhraseStats) savePhraseStats();
    for (const [id, row] of Object.entries(chatDeletionStats || {})) {
      const updatedAt = parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.updatedAt) || parseTimestamp(row == null ? void 0 : row.lastDeletedTs);
      if (!normalizeProfileId(id) || !updatedAt || t - updatedAt > CHAT_DELETE_RETENTION_MS) {
        delete chatDeletionStats[id];
        prunedChatDeletes += 1;
      }
    }
    for (const [fingerprint, tsValue] of Object.entries(seenChatDeletionEvents || {})) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(tsValue);
      if (!fingerprint || !ts || t - ts > CHAT_DELETE_RETENTION_MS) {
        delete seenChatDeletionEvents[fingerprint];
        prunedChatDeleteEvents += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedChatDeletes) saveChatDeletionStats();
    if (prunedChatDeleteEvents) saveSeenChatDeletionEvents();
    for (const [id, pending] of Object.entries(pendingAutoMessages || {})) {
      if (!normalizeProfileId(id) || !pending || t - (parseTimestamp(pending.ts) || 0) > AUTO_MESSAGE_PENDING_MAX_AGE_MS) {
        delete pendingAutoMessages[id];
        clearedPendingMessage += 1;
      }
    }
    if (clearedPendingMessage) savePendingAutoMessages();
    const autoTabsBefore = autoMessageTabHandles.size;
    pruneAutoMessageTabHandles();
    prunedAutoTabs = Math.max(0, autoTabsBefore - autoMessageTabHandles.size);
    for (const [id, appt] of appointments.entries()) {
      const atTs = parseTimestamp(appt == null ? void 0 : appt.atTs);
      if (!atTs || atTs < t - APPOINTMENT_RETENTION_MS) {
        appointments.delete(id);
        prunedAppointments += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedAppointments) saveAppointments();
    for (const [id, sentTs] of Object.entries(howdySentAt)) {
      const ts = parseTimestamp(sentTs);
      // Howdy/auto-intro 'sent at' records expire after 7 days.
      if (!ts || t - ts > 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1e3) {
        delete howdySentAt[id];
        prunedHowdy += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedHowdy) saveHowdySentMap();
    // Keep only the 60 most-recent chat-capture samples.
    if (chatCaptureStats.recent.length > 60) {
      chatCaptureStats.recent = chatCaptureStats.recent.slice(-60);
    }
    const urlEntries = Object.entries(chatCaptureStats.urls);
    // Cap captured-URL stats at 240 entries, keeping the highest-hit URLs.
    if (urlEntries.length > 240) {
      const keep = urlEntries.sort((a, b) => {
        var _a2, _b;
        return (((_a2 = b[1]) == null ? void 0 : _a2.hits) || 0) - (((_b = a[1]) == null ? void 0 : _b.hits) || 0);
      }).slice(0, 240);
      const next = {};
      for (const [k, v] of keep) next[k] = v;
      chatCaptureStats.urls = next;
    }
    pruneChatActivity();
    pruneProfileLastActive();
    for (const [key, badge] of floatingChatBadges.entries()) {
      const id = String(key || "").split(":")[0];
      if (idToMarker.has(id)) continue;
      try {
        (_a = badge == null ? void 0 : badge.remove) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(badge);
      } catch (e) {
      }
      floatingChatBadges.delete(key);
    }
    pruneFloatingChatBadges();
    // Bound the chat-storage digest cache (an optimization to skip re-parsing unchanged chat buckets). It gains an
    // entry per distinct chat-like storage key seen; SPA per-conversation keys make it grow for the tab's life and
    // nothing else prunes it. Drop oldest entries past the cap; a dropped key simply re-derives its digest once.
    if (chatStorageDigestByKey.size > 1000) {
      let over = chatStorageDigestByKey.size - 1000;
      const dropKeys = [];
      for (const k of chatStorageDigestByKey.keys()) {
        if (over-- <= 0) break;
        dropKeys.push(k);
      }
      for (const k of dropKeys) {
        chatStorageDigestByKey.delete(k);
        prunedStorageDigests += 1;
      }
    }
    if (prunedIncludeMatches || prunedAppointments || prunedBookmarks) {
      renderMatchPanel();
      renderAppointmentPanel();
      renderBookmarkPanel();
    }
    return {
      reason,
      markers: idToMarker.size,
      appointments: appointments.size,
      prunedMarkers,
      prunedAttitudes,
      prunedProfileText,
      prunedProfileFilter,
      prunedProfileSummary,
      prunedIncludeMatches,
      prunedRetries,
      prunedAppointments,
      prunedHowdy,
      prunedBookmarks,
      prunedChatPreview,
      prunedPhraseHistory,
      prunedPhraseStats,
      prunedChatDeletes,
      prunedChatDeleteEvents,
      clearedPendingMessage,
      prunedAutoTabs,
      prunedStorageDigests
    };
  }
  // Body MutationObserver that schedules a map refresh when marker/profile-link nodes are added.
  // Matches .maplibregl-marker / .marker-avatar-image / a[href*='/profile/']; debounced via scheduleMapRefresh.
  function observeMapForNewMarkers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ observeMapForNewMarkers", traceArgs(arguments));
    const observer = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
      var _a, _b;
      let markerChanged = false;
      for (const m of mutations) {
        if (markerChanged) break;
        if (m.type !== "childList") continue;
        for (const n of m.addedNodes) {
          if (!(n instanceof Element)) continue;
          // Treat added nodes as marker changes only if they are/contain a map marker or profile link.
          if (((_a = n.matches) == null ? void 0 : _a.call(n, ".maplibregl-marker, .marker-avatar-image, a[href*='/profile/']")) || ((_b = n.querySelector) == null ? void 0 : _b.call(n, ".maplibregl-marker, .marker-avatar-image, a[href*='/profile/']"))) {
            markerChanged = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (markerChanged) scheduleMapRefresh("map-mutation");
    });
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    bootObservers.push(observer);
  }
  // Top-bar attitude/position quick-filter buttons. Each maps a hide-state key to its button label and the
  // matching panel checkbox id, so the on-map buttons toggle the SAME state.hide* flag the panel checkboxes do
  // and the two stay in sync. Order mirrors the attitude taxonomy in the panel (and shouldHideAttitude).
  const ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS = [
    { key: "hideBottom", label: "Bottom", checkboxId: "sfBottom" },
    { key: "hideVersBottom", label: "Vers Bottom", checkboxId: "sfVersBottom" },
    { key: "hideVers", label: "Vers", checkboxId: "sfVers" },
    { key: "hideVersTop", label: "Vers Top", checkboxId: "sfVersTop" },
    { key: "hideTop", label: "Top", checkboxId: "sfTop" },
    { key: "hideSide", label: "Side", checkboxId: "sfSide" },
    { key: "hideSubmissiveBottom", label: "Sub Bottom", checkboxId: "sfSubmissiveBottom" },
    { key: "hidePowerBottom", label: "Power Bottom", checkboxId: "sfPowerBottom" },
    { key: "hidePassiveTop", label: "Passive Top", checkboxId: "sfPassiveTop" },
    { key: "hideDomTopBreeder", label: "Dom Top Breeder", checkboxId: "sfDomTopBreeder" },
    { key: "hideUnspecified", label: "Unspecified", checkboxId: "sfUnspecified" }
  ];
  // Update the top '24h Chats' launcher button: On/Off label with live count, tooltip, and active class.
  // No-op if the button element isn't present.
  function updateRecentChatFilterLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateRecentChatFilterLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!softPanelRecentFilterEl) return;
    const count = countRecentChatProfilesOnMap();
    const enabled = !!state.hideRecentChats24h;
    softPanelRecentFilterEl.textContent = enabled ? `24h Chats: On (${count})` : `24h Chats: Off (${count})`;
    softPanelRecentFilterEl.title = enabled ? "Showing filter: hide profiles chatted with in last 24 hours. Click to disable." : "Click to hide profiles chatted with in last 24 hours.";
    softPanelRecentFilterEl.classList.toggle("recent-chat-filter-active", enabled);
  }
  // Update the top 'Any Chats' launcher button: On/Off label with live count, tooltip, and active class.
  // No-op if the button element isn't present.
  function updateAnyChatFilterLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateAnyChatFilterLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!softPanelAnyChatFilterEl) return;
    const count = countAnyChatProfilesOnMap();
    const enabled = !!state.hideAnyChats;
    softPanelAnyChatFilterEl.textContent = enabled ? `Any Chats: On (${count})` : `Any Chats: Off (${count})`;
    softPanelAnyChatFilterEl.title = enabled ? "Showing filter: hide profiles with any chat history. Click to disable." : "Click to hide profiles with any chat history.";
    softPanelAnyChatFilterEl.classList.toggle("any-chat-filter-active", enabled);
  }
  // Update the top '2h Online' launcher button (counts profiles NOT online in last 2h), tooltip, active class.
  // No-op if the button element isn't present.
  function updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!softPanelOnline2hFilterEl) return;
    const count = countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap();
    const enabled = !!state.hideNotOnline2h;
    const win = formatNotOnlineWindowShort();
    const winLong = formatNotOnlineWindowLong();
    softPanelOnline2hFilterEl.textContent = enabled ? `${win} Online: On (${count})` : `${win} Online: Off (${count})`;
    softPanelOnline2hFilterEl.title = enabled ? `Showing filter: hide profiles not online in the last ${winLong}. Click to disable.` : `Click to hide profiles not online in the last ${winLong}.`;
    softPanelOnline2hFilterEl.classList.toggle("online-2h-filter-active", enabled);
  }
  // Update the top 'Global Chat' master toggle button: On/Off label, tooltip, and active class. Reflects
  // state.hideGlobalChat via the '!== false' default-on idiom. No-op if the button element isn't present.
  function updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl) return;
    const enabled = state.hideGlobalChat !== false;
    softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl.textContent = enabled ? "Global Chat: On" : "Global Chat: Off";
    softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl.title = enabled ? "Showing filter: hide Global Chat messages whose author is hidden on the map. Click to disable." : "Click to hide Global Chat messages whose author is hidden on the map.";
    softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl.classList.toggle("global-chat-filter-active", enabled);
  }
  // Update every top-bar attitude quick-filter button: label, On/Off tooltip, and active class, each from its
  // state.hide* flag. No-op for any def whose button hasn't been created. Mirrors the chat-filter launchers.
  function updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers", traceArgs(arguments));
    for (const def of ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS) {
      const btn = softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.get(def.key);
      if (!btn) continue;
      const enabled = !!state[def.key];
      btn.textContent = enabled ? `${def.label}: On` : `${def.label}: Off`;
      btn.title = enabled ? `Showing filter: hide ${def.label} profiles on the map. Click to disable.` : `Click to hide ${def.label} profiles on the map.`;
      btn.classList.toggle("attitude-filter-active", enabled);
    }
  }
  // Lay the filter launcher buttons in a row to the right of the main launcher, wrapping to additional rows
  // below when they run out of horizontal room (the chat + global-chat + 11 attitude buttons are too wide for
  // one row on most screens). Reads the main launcher's rect each call; clamps every button into the viewport.
  function positionTopLaunchers() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ positionTopLaunchers", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!softPanelLauncherEl) return;
    const rect = softPanelLauncherEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    const offset = 8;
    const buttons = [
      softPanelRecentFilterEl,
      softPanelAnyChatFilterEl,
      softPanelOnline2hFilterEl,
      softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl,
      ...ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS.map((def) => softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.get(def.key))
    ].filter(Boolean);
    const rowStartLeft = Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.right + offset));
    const wrapStartLeft = Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.left));
    const maxRight = window.innerWidth - 8;
    let left = rowStartLeft;
    let rowTop = Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.top));
    let rowHeight = 0;
    for (const btn of buttons) {
      const width = btn.offsetWidth || 120;
      const height = btn.offsetHeight || 26;
      // Wrap before placing a button that would overflow the right edge — but never on a row's first slot
      // (left === wrapStartLeft), so a button wider than the row still lands instead of looping forever.
      if (left > wrapStartLeft && left + width > maxRight) {
        rowTop += rowHeight + offset;
        left = wrapStartLeft;
        rowHeight = 0;
      }
      const clampedLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(left, maxRight - width));
      btn.style.left = `${clampedLeft}px`;
      btn.style.top = `${rowTop}px`;
      btn.style.right = "auto";
      left = clampedLeft + width + offset;
      if (height > rowHeight) rowHeight = height;
    }
  }
  // Toggle the hide-recent-chats-24h filter: persist, re-apply hiding, refresh launcher, report count.
  function toggleRecentChatFilter() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleRecentChatFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    state.hideRecentChats24h = !state.hideRecentChats24h;
    saveState();
    applyHiding();
    updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
    const count = countRecentChatProfilesOnMap();
    setStatus(
      state.hideRecentChats24h ? `24h chat filter enabled (${count} profile${count === 1 ? "" : "s"} on map)` : "24h chat filter disabled"
    );
  }
  // Toggle the hide-any-chat-history filter; also force-clears showOnlyChats when enabling (mutually exclusive).
  // Persists, re-applies hiding, refreshes launcher, reports affected count.
  function toggleAnyChatFilter() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleAnyChatFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    state.hideAnyChats = !state.hideAnyChats;
    if (state.hideAnyChats) state.showOnlyChats = false;
    saveState();
    applyHiding();
    updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
    const count = countAnyChatProfilesOnMap();
    setStatus(
      state.hideAnyChats ? `Any-chat filter enabled (${count} profile${count === 1 ? "" : "s"} on map)` : "Any-chat filter disabled"
    );
  }
  // Toggle the hide-not-online-in-2h filter: persist, re-apply hiding, refresh launcher, report count.
  function toggleNotOnlineFilter() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleNotOnlineFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    state.hideNotOnline2h = !state.hideNotOnline2h;
    saveState();
    applyHiding();
    updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
    const count = countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap();
    setStatus(
      state.hideNotOnline2h ? `Not-online ${formatNotOnlineWindowShort()} filter enabled (${count} offline profile${count === 1 ? "" : "s"} hidden)` : "Not-online filter disabled"
    );
  }
  // Toggle the global-chat hiding feature on/off: persist, re-sweep global chat (applyHiding -> applyGlobalChatHiding),
  // refresh the launcher, and report. Flipping off re-shows every previously hidden Global Chat message.
  function toggleGlobalChatFilter() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleGlobalChatFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    state.hideGlobalChat = !(state.hideGlobalChat !== false);
    saveState();
    applyHiding();
    updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
    setStatus(
      state.hideGlobalChat ? "Global Chat filter enabled (messages from map-hidden authors hidden)" : "Global Chat filter disabled (all Global Chat messages shown)"
    );
  }
  // Toggle one attitude/position exclude filter from its top-bar button: flip state[def.key], mirror the change
  // onto the panel checkbox when the panel is open (so both UIs agree), persist, re-apply hiding, refresh launchers.
  function toggleAttitudeFilter(def) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ toggleAttitudeFilter", traceArgs(arguments));
    if (!def || !def.key) return;
    state[def.key] = !state[def.key];
    // Keep the open panel's checkbox in lockstep; harmless no-op when the panel isn't built.
    const checkbox = softPanelEl && def.checkboxId ? softPanelEl.querySelector("#" + def.checkboxId) : null;
    if (checkbox) checkbox.checked = !!state[def.key];
    saveState();
    applyHiding();
    updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
    setStatus(
      state[def.key] ? `${def.label} filter enabled` : `${def.label} filter disabled`
    );
  }
  // Idempotently (re)creates the top launcher buttons (main + recent/any/2h-online/global-chat quick filters and
  // one button per attitude exclude filter), re-appending any the page removed. Restores the saved launcher
  // position, binds a one-time window-resize reposition, and returns the main launcher element.
  function ensureSoftPanelLauncher() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ ensureSoftPanelLauncher", traceArgs(arguments));
    const hasMain = softPanelLauncherEl && document.body.contains(softPanelLauncherEl);
    const hasRecent = softPanelRecentFilterEl && document.body.contains(softPanelRecentFilterEl);
    const hasAny = softPanelAnyChatFilterEl && document.body.contains(softPanelAnyChatFilterEl);
    const hasOnline2h = softPanelOnline2hFilterEl && document.body.contains(softPanelOnline2hFilterEl);
    const hasGlobalChat = softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl && document.body.contains(softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl);
    const hasAllAttitudes = ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS.every((def) => {
      const btn = softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.get(def.key);
      return btn && document.body.contains(btn);
    });
    if (hasMain && hasRecent && hasAny && hasOnline2h && hasGlobalChat && hasAllAttitudes) {
      updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
      positionTopLaunchers();
      return softPanelLauncherEl;
    }
    const launcher = hasMain ? softPanelLauncherEl : document.createElement("button");
    if (!hasMain) {
      launcher.type = "button";
      launcher.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
      launcher.textContent = "Show Filter";
      launcher.title = "Show soft filter panel";
      launcher.addEventListener("click", () => setSoftPanelVisible(true));
      document.body.appendChild(launcher);
      softPanelLauncherEl = launcher;
    }
    const recentBtn = hasRecent ? softPanelRecentFilterEl : document.createElement("button");
    if (!hasRecent) {
      recentBtn.type = "button";
      recentBtn.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
      recentBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleRecentChatFilter);
      document.body.appendChild(recentBtn);
      softPanelRecentFilterEl = recentBtn;
    }
    const anyBtn = hasAny ? softPanelAnyChatFilterEl : document.createElement("button");
    if (!hasAny) {
      anyBtn.type = "button";
      anyBtn.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
      anyBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleAnyChatFilter);
      document.body.appendChild(anyBtn);
      softPanelAnyChatFilterEl = anyBtn;
    }
    const online2hBtn = hasOnline2h ? softPanelOnline2hFilterEl : document.createElement("button");
    if (!hasOnline2h) {
      online2hBtn.type = "button";
      online2hBtn.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
      online2hBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleNotOnlineFilter);
      document.body.appendChild(online2hBtn);
      softPanelOnline2hFilterEl = online2hBtn;
    }
    const globalChatBtn = hasGlobalChat ? softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl : document.createElement("button");
    if (!hasGlobalChat) {
      globalChatBtn.type = "button";
      globalChatBtn.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
      globalChatBtn.addEventListener("click", toggleGlobalChatFilter);
      document.body.appendChild(globalChatBtn);
      softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl = globalChatBtn;
    }
    // One quick-toggle button per attitude/position exclude filter, created once (cached in
    // softPanelAttitudeFilterEls) and re-appended if the page removed it. Each closes over its def so the
    // click flips the matching state.hide* flag and mirrors the panel checkbox.
    for (const def of ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS) {
      let attBtn = softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.get(def.key);
      if (attBtn && document.body.contains(attBtn)) continue;
      if (!attBtn) {
        attBtn = document.createElement("button");
        attBtn.type = "button";
        attBtn.className = "sniffies-soft-panel-launcher";
        attBtn.addEventListener("click", () => toggleAttitudeFilter(def));
        softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.set(def.key, attBtn);
      }
      document.body.appendChild(attBtn);
    }
    const pos = loadSoftPanelPos();
    if (pos && typeof pos.left === "number" && typeof pos.top === "number") {
      launcher.style.left = `${Math.max(0, pos.left)}px`;
      launcher.style.top = `${Math.max(0, pos.top)}px`;
      launcher.style.right = "auto";
    }
    updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
    updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
    updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
    updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
    updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
    positionTopLaunchers();
    if (!launcherResizeBound) {
      window.addEventListener("resize", positionTopLaunchers, { passive: true });
      launcherResizeBound = true;
    }
    return launcher;
  }
  // Shows/hides the soft-filter panel and the launcher buttons; when hiding, snapshots the panel's screen position onto the launcher first.
  // Launchers stay hidden while controls are hosted in the extension popup. persist=true mirrors the state to state.showPanel and saves.
  function setSoftPanelVisible(visible, persist = true) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ setSoftPanelVisible", traceArgs(arguments));
    const show = !!visible;
    const launcher = ensureSoftPanelLauncher();
    // Every top-bar quick-filter button (recent/any/2h-online/global-chat + the attitude buttons) shares the
    // same show/hide rule, so collect them once and toggle display uniformly.
    const quickFilterBtns = [
      softPanelRecentFilterEl,
      softPanelAnyChatFilterEl,
      softPanelOnline2hFilterEl,
      softPanelGlobalChatFilterEl,
      ...ATTITUDE_FILTER_DEFS.map((def) => softPanelAttitudeFilterEls.get(def.key))
    ].filter(Boolean);
    if (!show && launcher && softPanelEl) {
      const rect = softPanelEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      launcher.style.left = `${Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.left))}px`;
      launcher.style.top = `${Math.max(0, Math.round(rect.top))}px`;
      launcher.style.right = "auto";
      positionTopLaunchers();
    }
    if (softPanelEl) softPanelEl.style.display = show ? "block" : "none";
    if (show) updateSoftPanelViewportBounds();
    // When controls live in the extension popup, the in-page launcher is always hidden regardless of 'show'.
    const launcherDisplay = show || extensionControlsHostedInPopup ? "none" : "flex";
    if (launcher) launcher.style.display = launcherDisplay;
    for (const btn of quickFilterBtns) btn.style.display = launcherDisplay;
    if (!show) {
      updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      updateGlobalChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAttitudeFilterLaunchers();
      positionTopLaunchers();
    }
    if (persist) {
      state.showPanel = show;
      saveState();
    }
  }
  // Builds the entire draggable soft-filter panel: injects the HTML, queries every control, seeds each from state, and wires change/click/blur handlers.
  // Most handlers write state, saveState(), then applyHiding(); caches statusEl/rateStatusEl and starts the per-second rate-counter timer.
  // Stores the node in softPanelEl and applies initial visibility (forced hidden when hosted in the extension popup).
  function buildPanel() {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ buildPanel", traceArgs(arguments));
    logInfo(`buildPanel() called, showPanel=${state.showPanel}`);
    const panel2 = document.createElement("div");
    panel2.className = "sniffies-soft-panel";

      </div>
      <div class="row">
        <span>Console logging</span>
        <select class="select-input" id="sfLogVerbosity">
          <option value="quiet">Quiet</option>
          <option value="normal">Normal</option>
          <option value="verbose">Verbose</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="row">

      </div>

          <button class="btn" id="sfImport">Import Blocked</button>
        </div>
        <div class="row">
          <button class="btn" id="sfExportData">Export All</button>
          <button class="btn" id="sfImportData">Import All</button>

          <button class="btn" id="sfGDriveLoad">Load GDrive</button>
          <button class="btn" id="sfGDriveDisconnect">Disconnect</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="row support-links">
        <strong>Support this script</strong>
        <div class="links">
          <a href="https://cash.app/$mitchphudson" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cash App</a>
          <a href="https://account.venmo.com/u/Mitchphudson" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Venmo</a>
        </div>
        <div class="alt">Chime: $Mitchell-Hudson-1 \xB7 Zelle: mitchphudson@gmail.com</div>
      </div>
      <div class="sniffies-sync-status">\u25EF Not connected</div>
      <div class="status" id="sfStatus">Status: ready</div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(panel2);
    applySoftPanelPos(panel2);
    updateSoftPanelViewportBounds(panel2);
    const dragHandle = panel2.querySelector(".row");
    makePanelDraggable(panel2, dragHandle, (left, top2) => {
      saveSoftPanelPos(left, top2);
      updateSoftPanelViewportBounds(panel2);
    });
    if (!softPanelViewportResizeBound) {
      window.addEventListener("resize", () => updateSoftPanelViewportBounds(panel2), { passive: true });
      softPanelViewportResizeBound = true;
    }
    const enabled = panel2.querySelector("#sfEnabled");
    const chatAges = panel2.querySelector("#sfChatAges");
    const recentChats24h = panel2.querySelector("#sfRecentChats24h");
    const recentChats2h = panel2.querySelector("#sfRecentChats2h");
    const hideNotOnline2h = panel2.querySelector("#sfHideNotOnline2h");
    const notOnlineWindow = panel2.querySelector("#sfNotOnlineWindow");
    const notOnlineCustomMin = panel2.querySelector("#sfNotOnlineCustomMin");
    const notOnlineCustomUnit = panel2.querySelector("#sfNotOnlineCustomUnit");
    const hideAnyChats = panel2.querySelector("#sfHideAnyChats");
    const showOnlyChats = panel2.querySelector("#sfShowOnlyChats");
    const side = panel2.querySelector("#sfSide");
    const submissiveBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfSubmissiveBottom");
    const bottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfBottom");
    const versBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfVersBottom");
    const powerBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfPowerBottom");
    const vers = panel2.querySelector("#sfVers");
    const versTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfVersTop");
    const top = panel2.querySelector("#sfTop");
    const passiveTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfPassiveTop");
    const domTopBreeder = panel2.querySelector("#sfDomTopBreeder");
    const unspecified = panel2.querySelector("#sfUnspecified");
    const hlSide = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlSide");
    const hlSubmissiveBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlSubmissiveBottom");
    const hlBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlBottom");
    const hlPowerBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlPowerBottom");
    const hlVersBottom = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlVersBottom");
    const hlVers = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlVers");
    const hlVersTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlVersTop");
    const hlPassiveTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlPassiveTop");
    const hlTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlTop");
    const hlDomTopBreeder = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlDomTopBreeder");
    const hlUnspecified = panel2.querySelector("#sfHlUnspecified");
    const introMessagesInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfIntroMessages");
    const otherMessagesInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfOtherMessages");
    const saveIntroMessagesBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfSaveIntroMessages");
    const saveOtherMessagesBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfSaveOtherMessages");
    const undoHideBtnTop = panel2.querySelector("#sfUndoHideTop");
    const undoHideBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfUndoHide");
    const clear = panel2.querySelector("#sfClear");
    const hideNowBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfHideNow");
    const showMatchesBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfShowMatches");
    const showBookmarksBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfShowBookmarks");
    const showAppointmentsBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfShowAppointments");
    const approachMinutesInput = panel2.querySelector("#sfApproachMinutes");
    const logVerbositySelect = panel2.querySelector("#sfLogVerbosity");
    const optionsToggleBtn = panel2.querySelector("#sfOptionsToggle");
    const advancedOptions = panel2.querySelector("#sfAdvancedOptions");
    const excludeTerms = panel2.querySelector("#sfExcludeTerms");
    const includeTerms = panel2.querySelector("#sfIncludeTerms");
    const excludeTermsEnabled = panel2.querySelector("#sfExcludeTermsEnabled");
    const includeTermsEnabled = panel2.querySelector("#sfIncludeTermsEnabled");
    const hide = panel2.querySelector("#sfHide");
    const exp = panel2.querySelector("#sfExport");
    const imp = panel2.querySelector("#sfImport");
    const expData = panel2.querySelector("#sfExportData");
    const impData = panel2.querySelector("#sfImportData");
    const enterToken = panel2.querySelector("#sfEnterToken");
    const gdriveSync = panel2.querySelector("#sfGDriveSync");
    const gdriveLoad = panel2.querySelector("#sfGDriveLoad");
    const gdriveDisconnect = panel2.querySelector("#sfGDriveDisconnect");
    statusEl = panel2.querySelector("#sfStatus");
    rateStatusEl = panel2.querySelector("#sfRateCounter");
    statusEl == null ? void 0 : statusEl.setAttribute("role", "status");
    statusEl == null ? void 0 : statusEl.setAttribute("aria-live", "polite");
    updateRateCounter();
    if (!rateCountdownTimer) {
      // Starts the once-per-second API-budget countdown; guarded so only one timer is ever created.
      rateCountdownTimer = setInterval(updateRateCounter, 1e3);
    }
    enabled.checked = state.enabled;
    // '!== false' default-on idiom: an undefined/missing flag is treated as enabled.
    chatAges.checked = state.showChatAges !== false;
    if (recentChats24h) recentChats24h.checked = !!state.hideRecentChats24h;
    if (recentChats2h) recentChats2h.checked = !!state.hideRecentChats2h;
    if (hideNotOnline2h) hideNotOnline2h.checked = !!state.hideNotOnline2h;
    // Sync the window selector UI from state: a stored 10 or 120 shows the matching preset; anything else is
    // treated as "Custom" with the minutes box revealed and populated.
    const syncNotOnlineWindowUi = () => {
      if (!notOnlineWindow) return;
      const mins = notOnlineWindowMinutesValue();
      const isPreset = mins === 10 || mins === 120;
      notOnlineWindow.value = isPreset ? String(mins) : "custom";
      if (notOnlineCustomMin) {
        notOnlineCustomMin.value = String(mins);
        notOnlineCustomMin.style.display = isPreset ? "none" : "";
      }
      if (notOnlineCustomUnit) notOnlineCustomUnit.style.display = isPreset ? "none" : "";
    };
    syncNotOnlineWindowUi();
    if (hideAnyChats) hideAnyChats.checked = !!state.hideAnyChats;
    if (showOnlyChats) showOnlyChats.checked = !!state.showOnlyChats;
    side.checked = state.hideSide;
    submissiveBottom.checked = state.hideSubmissiveBottom;
    bottom.checked = state.hideBottom;
    versBottom.checked = state.hideVersBottom;
    powerBottom.checked = state.hidePowerBottom;
    vers.checked = state.hideVers;
    versTop.checked = state.hideVersTop;
    top.checked = state.hideTop;
    passiveTop.checked = state.hidePassiveTop;
    domTopBreeder.checked = state.hideDomTopBreeder;
    unspecified.checked = state.hideUnspecified;
    if (hlSide) hlSide.checked = !!state.highlightSide;
    if (hlSubmissiveBottom) hlSubmissiveBottom.checked = !!state.highlightSubmissiveBottom;
    if (hlBottom) hlBottom.checked = !!state.highlightBottom;
    if (hlPowerBottom) hlPowerBottom.checked = !!state.highlightPowerBottom;
    if (hlVersBottom) hlVersBottom.checked = !!state.highlightVersBottom;
    if (hlVers) hlVers.checked = !!state.highlightVers;
    if (hlVersTop) hlVersTop.checked = !!state.highlightVersTop;
    if (hlPassiveTop) hlPassiveTop.checked = !!state.highlightPassiveTop;
    if (hlTop) hlTop.checked = !!state.highlightTop;
    if (hlDomTopBreeder) hlDomTopBreeder.checked = !!state.highlightDomTopBreeder;
    if (hlUnspecified) hlUnspecified.checked = !!state.highlightUnspecified;
    if (approachMinutesInput) {
      approachMinutesInput.value = String(getApproachMinutes());
    }
    if (logVerbositySelect) {
      const selected = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS, state.logVerbosity) ? state.logVerbosity : "quiet";
      logVerbositySelect.value = selected;
      state.logVerbosity = selected;
    }
    if (excludeTerms) excludeTerms.value = state.excludeProfileTerms || "";
    if (includeTerms) includeTerms.value = state.includeProfileTerms || "";
    if (excludeTermsEnabled) excludeTermsEnabled.checked = state.excludeProfileTermsEnabled !== false;
    if (includeTermsEnabled) includeTermsEnabled.checked = state.includeProfileTermsEnabled !== false;
    if (introMessagesInput) introMessagesInput.value = state.introMessages || DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages;
    if (otherMessagesInput) otherMessagesInput.value = state.otherMessages || "";
    if (!isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      for (const btn of [enterToken, gdriveSync, gdriveLoad, gdriveDisconnect]) {
        if (!btn) continue;
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.title = "Set GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID in the script to enable Google Drive sync.";
      }
    }
    const textEditFields = [excludeTerms, includeTerms, introMessagesInput, otherMessagesInput].filter(Boolean);
    for (const field of textEditFields) {
      for (const evtName of ["pointerdown", "mousedown", "click", "keydown", "keypress", "keyup"]) {
        field.addEventListener(evtName, (evt) => {
          evt.stopPropagation();
          if (typeof evt.stopImmediatePropagation === "function") evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
        });
      }
    }
    enabled.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.enabled = enabled.checked;
      saveState();
      applyHiding();
    });
    chatAges.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.showChatAges = chatAges.checked;
      saveState();
      refreshChatAgeBadges();
      applyHiding();
    });
    recentChats24h == null ? void 0 : recentChats24h.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.hideRecentChats24h = !!recentChats24h.checked;
      saveState();
      applyHiding();
      updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
    });
    recentChats2h == null ? void 0 : recentChats2h.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.hideRecentChats2h = !!recentChats2h.checked;
      saveState();
      applyHiding();
    });
    hideNotOnline2h == null ? void 0 : hideNotOnline2h.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.hideNotOnline2h = !!hideNotOnline2h.checked;
      saveState();
      applyHiding();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      const count = countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap();
      setStatus(
        state.hideNotOnline2h ? `Not-online ${formatNotOnlineWindowShort()} filter enabled (${count} offline profile${count === 1 ? "" : "s"} hidden)` : "Not-online filter disabled"
      );
    });
    // Window selector: a preset (10/120) sets the minutes directly; "Custom" reveals the minutes box and uses it.
    const applyNotOnlineWindowChange = () => {
      let mins;
      if (notOnlineWindow && notOnlineWindow.value === "custom") {
        const raw = Math.round(Number(notOnlineCustomMin && notOnlineCustomMin.value));
        mins = Number.isFinite(raw) && raw > 0 ? Math.min(Math.max(raw, 1), 24 * 60) : notOnlineWindowMinutesValue();
        if (notOnlineCustomMin) notOnlineCustomMin.style.display = "";
        if (notOnlineCustomUnit) notOnlineCustomUnit.style.display = "";
      } else {
        mins = Math.round(Number(notOnlineWindow && notOnlineWindow.value)) || 120;
        if (notOnlineCustomMin) notOnlineCustomMin.style.display = "none";
        if (notOnlineCustomUnit) notOnlineCustomUnit.style.display = "none";
      }
      state.notOnlineWindowMinutes = mins;
      saveState();
      applyHiding();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      const count = countNotOnlineProfilesOnMap();
      if (state.hideNotOnline2h) setStatus(`Not-online window: ${formatNotOnlineWindowLong()} (${count} offline profile${count === 1 ? "" : "s"} hidden)`);
    };
    if (notOnlineWindow) notOnlineWindow.addEventListener("change", applyNotOnlineWindowChange);
    if (notOnlineCustomMin) notOnlineCustomMin.addEventListener("change", applyNotOnlineWindowChange);
    hideAnyChats == null ? void 0 : hideAnyChats.addEventListener("change", () => {
      state.hideAnyChats = !!hideAnyChats.checked;
      // hideAnyChats and showOnlyChats are mutually exclusive; enabling one force-clears the other (panel + state).

    // Empty intro input falls back to DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages and reports a 'reset to defaults' status.
    const saveIntroMessages = () => {
      log("→ saveIntroMessages");

      }
    };

    const saveOtherMessages = () => {
      log("→ saveOtherMessages");

      const minutes = Math.min(1440, Math.max(1, parseInt(approachMinutesInput.value || "", 10) || DEFAULT_APPOINTMENT_APPROACH_MINUTES));
      state.appointmentApproachMinutes = minutes;

    });
    logVerbositySelect == null ? void 0 : logVerbositySelect.addEventListener("change", () => {

      state.logVerbosity = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS, next) ? next : "quiet";
      saveState();

    });
    optionsToggleBtn == null ? void 0 : optionsToggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      if (!advancedOptions) return;

    });
    hide.addEventListener("click", () => setSoftPanelVisible(false));
    exp.addEventListener("click", exportBlocked);
    imp.addEventListener("click", importBlocked);
    expData.addEventListener("click", exportData);
    impData.addEventListener("click", importData);
    if (enterToken && isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {

    if (gdriveSync && isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      gdriveSync.addEventListener("click", syncNotesToGDrive);

      logWarn("GDrive Sync button not found");
