    if (gdriveLoad && isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      gdriveLoad.addEventListener("click", loadNotesFromGDrive);
      logInfo("GDrive Load button connected");
    } else if (isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      logWarn("GDrive Load button not found");
    }
    if (gdriveDisconnect && isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      gdriveDisconnect.addEventListener("click", disconnectGDrive);
      logInfo("GDrive Disconnect button connected");
    } else if (isGDriveOAuthConfigured()) {
      logWarn("GDrive Disconnect button not found");
    }
    softPanelEl = panel2;
    // Initial panel visibility: hidden when hosted in the popup, else restores saved showPanel (default visible); persist=false avoids re-saving on boot.
    setSoftPanelVisible(extensionControlsHostedInPopup ? false : state.showPanel !== false, false);
    return panel2;
  }
  const style = document.createElement("style");
  style.textContent = BADGE_STYLE;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
  const panel = buildPanel();
  if (panel) {
    logInfo("Panel created successfully");
    const statusEl2 = panel.querySelector(".sniffies-sync-status");
    if (statusEl2) {
      initializeGDriveTokenCache().then(() => {
        const token = getGDriveToken();
        updateSyncStatus(token ? "synced" : "disconnected");
        logInfo("Sync status initialized");
      }).catch((e) => {
        logWarn("Token cache init failed:", (e == null ? void 0 : e.message) || e);
        updateSyncStatus("error");
      });
    } else {
      logWarn("Status element not found in panel");
    }
  } else {
    logError("buildPanel() returned null");
  }
  renderMatchPanel();
  renderBookmarkPanel();
  renderAppointmentPanel();
  renderChatPhrasePanel();
  clearPastAppointments();
  processAppointmentAlerts();
  maybeAutoSendPendingMessageFromUrl();
  pruneChatActivity();
  seedSelfIdsFromWindow();
  runMemoryGc("boot");
  installChatCapture();
  installQuickPhraseCaptureHooks();
  observeMapForNewMarkers();
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    updatePartials().catch((e) => logWarn("updatePartials failed", e));
  }, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    applyHiding();
  }, APPLY_INTERVAL_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    refreshChatAgeBadges();
  }, CHAT_LABEL_REFRESH_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    scanStorageForChatPayloads("interval");
  }, CHAT_STORAGE_SCAN_INTERVAL_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    seedSelfIdsFromWindow();
    scheduleChatBadgeRefresh();
  }, 15e3));
  window.addEventListener("scroll", scheduleChatBadgeRefresh, { passive: true, capture: true });
  window.addEventListener("resize", scheduleChatBadgeRefresh, { passive: true });
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    refreshBookmarkPanelMaybe();
  }, 15e3));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    refreshChatPhrasePanelMaybe();
  }, CHAT_PHRASE_PANEL_REFRESH_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    processAppointmentAlerts();
  }, APPOINTMENT_CHECK_MS));
  bootTimers.push(setInterval(() => {
    if (isUiWorkSuspended()) return;
    runMemoryGc("interval");
  }, MEMORY_GC_INTERVAL_MS));
  observeProfileChanges();
  // Debug/popup helper: flags state.showMatchPanel on, saves, and renders the include-match panel.
  const sniffiesShowMatchPanel = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesShowMatchPanel");
    state.showMatchPanel = true;
    saveState();
    renderMatchPanel();
  };
  // Debug/popup helper: shows the soft-filter panel via setSoftPanelVisible(true).
  const sniffiesShowPanel = () => setSoftPanelVisible(true);
  // Debug/popup helper: hides the soft-filter panel via setSoftPanelVisible(false).
  const sniffiesHidePanel = () => setSoftPanelVisible(false);
  // Builds the canonical settings snapshot the extension popup consumes: every toggle/highlight/text-filter plus live counts (blocked, include matches, recent/any chat).
  // Booleans are normalized (several default true via '!== false'); also reports pageControlsHostedInPopup.
  const sniffiesGetSettings = () => ({
    enabled: !!state.enabled,
    showChatAges: state.showChatAges !== false,
    hideRecentChats24h: !!state.hideRecentChats24h,
    hideRecentChats2h: !!state.hideRecentChats2h,
    hideAnyChats: !!state.hideAnyChats,
    showOnlyChats: !!state.showOnlyChats,
    hideNotOnline2h: !!state.hideNotOnline2h,
    hideSide: !!state.hideSide,
    hideSubmissiveBottom: !!state.hideSubmissiveBottom,
    hideBottom: !!state.hideBottom,
    hidePowerBottom: !!state.hidePowerBottom,
    hideVersBottom: !!state.hideVersBottom,
    hideVers: !!state.hideVers,
    hideVersTop: !!state.hideVersTop,
    hidePassiveTop: !!state.hidePassiveTop,
    hideTop: !!state.hideTop,
    hideDomTopBreeder: !!state.hideDomTopBreeder,
    hideUnspecified: !!state.hideUnspecified,
    highlightSide: !!state.highlightSide,
    highlightSubmissiveBottom: !!state.highlightSubmissiveBottom,
    highlightBottom: !!state.highlightBottom,
    highlightPowerBottom: !!state.highlightPowerBottom,
    highlightVersBottom: !!state.highlightVersBottom,
    highlightVers: !!state.highlightVers,
    highlightVersTop: !!state.highlightVersTop,
    highlightPassiveTop: !!state.highlightPassiveTop,
    highlightTop: !!state.highlightTop,
    highlightDomTopBreeder: !!state.highlightDomTopBreeder,
    highlightUnspecified: !!state.highlightUnspecified,
    excludeProfileTermsEnabled: state.excludeProfileTermsEnabled !== false,
    includeProfileTermsEnabled: state.includeProfileTermsEnabled !== false,
    excludeProfileTerms: String(state.excludeProfileTerms || ""),
    includeProfileTerms: String(state.includeProfileTerms || ""),
    introMessages: String(state.introMessages || ""),
    otherMessages: String(state.otherMessages || ""),
    quickPhraseSort: normalizedQuickPhraseSort(state.quickPhraseSort),
    appointmentApproachMinutes: getApproachMinutes(),
    notOnlineWindowMinutes: notOnlineWindowMinutesValue(),
    bookmarkSortBy: String(state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest"),
    bookmarkOnlineFilter: String(state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all"),
    logVerbosity: currentLogVerbosity(),
    blockedCount: blocked.size,
    includeMatchCount: includeMatches.size,
    recentChatCount: countRecentChatProfilesOnMap(),
    anyChatCount: countAnyChatProfilesOnMap(),
    pageControlsHostedInPopup: extensionControlsHostedInPopup
  });
  // Applies a partial settings patch from the popup: diffs each known key, updates state, and tracks which UI subsystems changed.
  // Enforces mutual exclusion of hideAnyChats vs showOnlyChats; saves only if something changed, then refreshes just the affected panels/launchers and applyHiding().
  // Returns the fresh sniffiesGetSettings() snapshot.
  function updateSettingsFromPopup(patch = {}) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ updateSettingsFromPopup", traceArgs(arguments));
    const next = patch && typeof patch === "object" ? patch : {};
    // updateSettingsFromPopup-local: true when the incoming patch object has its own property 'key' (guards against inherited props).
    const hasOwn = (key) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next, key);
    const booleanKeys = [
      "enabled",
      "showChatAges",
      "hideRecentChats24h",
      "hideRecentChats2h",
      "hideAnyChats",
      "showOnlyChats",
      "hideNotOnline2h",
      "hideSide",
      "hideSubmissiveBottom",
      "hideBottom",
      "hidePowerBottom",
      "hideVersBottom",
      "hideVers",
      "hideVersTop",
      "hidePassiveTop",
      "hideTop",
      "hideDomTopBreeder",
      "hideUnspecified",
      "highlightSide",
      "highlightSubmissiveBottom",
      "highlightBottom",
      "highlightPowerBottom",
      "highlightVersBottom",
      "highlightVers",
      "highlightVersTop",
      "highlightPassiveTop",
      "highlightTop",
      "highlightDomTopBreeder",
      "highlightUnspecified",
      "excludeProfileTermsEnabled",
      "includeProfileTermsEnabled",
      "showMatchPanel",
      "showBookmarkPanel",
      "showAppointmentPanel",
      "showChatPhrasePanel"
    ];
    let changed = false;
    let textFiltersChanged = false;
    let chatUiChanged = false;
    let bookmarkUiChanged = false;
    let appointmentUiChanged = false;
    let phraseUiChanged = false;
    let matchUiChanged = false;
    for (const key of booleanKeys) {
      if (!hasOwn(key)) continue;
      const nextValue = !!next[key];
      if (state[key] === nextValue) continue;
      state[key] = nextValue;
      changed = true;
      if (key === "showChatAges" || key === "hideRecentChats24h" || key === "hideRecentChats2h" || key === "hideAnyChats" || key === "showOnlyChats" || key === "hideNotOnline2h") {
        chatUiChanged = true;
      }
      if (key === "showBookmarkPanel") bookmarkUiChanged = true;
      if (key === "showAppointmentPanel") appointmentUiChanged = true;
      if (key === "showChatPhrasePanel") phraseUiChanged = true;
      if (key === "showMatchPanel") matchUiChanged = true;
      if (key === "excludeProfileTermsEnabled" || key === "includeProfileTermsEnabled") {
        textFiltersChanged = true;
        if (key === "includeProfileTermsEnabled") matchUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("hideAnyChats") && state.hideAnyChats) {
      if (state.showOnlyChats) {
        state.showOnlyChats = false;
        changed = true;
        chatUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("showOnlyChats") && state.showOnlyChats) {
      if (state.hideAnyChats) {
        state.hideAnyChats = false;
        changed = true;
        chatUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("excludeProfileTerms")) {
      const value = String(next.excludeProfileTerms || "");
      if (state.excludeProfileTerms !== value) {
        state.excludeProfileTerms = value;
        changed = true;
        textFiltersChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("includeProfileTerms")) {
      const value = String(next.includeProfileTerms || "");
      if (state.includeProfileTerms !== value) {
        state.includeProfileTerms = value;
        changed = true;
        textFiltersChanged = true;
        matchUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("logVerbosity")) {
      const nextVerbosity = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOG_VERBOSITY_LEVELS, String(next.logVerbosity || "").toLowerCase()) ? String(next.logVerbosity || "").toLowerCase() : "quiet";
      if (state.logVerbosity !== nextVerbosity) {
        state.logVerbosity = nextVerbosity;
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("bookmarkSortBy")) {
      const nextSort = String(next.bookmarkSortBy || "newest");
      if (state.bookmarkSortBy !== nextSort) {
        state.bookmarkSortBy = nextSort;
        changed = true;
        bookmarkUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("bookmarkOnlineFilter")) {
      const nextFilter = String(next.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all");
      if (state.bookmarkOnlineFilter !== nextFilter) {
        state.bookmarkOnlineFilter = nextFilter;
        changed = true;
        bookmarkUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("quickPhraseSort")) {
      const nextSort = normalizedQuickPhraseSort(next.quickPhraseSort);
      if (state.quickPhraseSort !== nextSort) {
        state.quickPhraseSort = nextSort;
        changed = true;
        phraseUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("appointmentApproachMinutes")) {
      // Clamps the approach-alert minutes to 1..1440 (one day), falling back to the default on a non-numeric value.
      const nextMinutes = Math.min(1440, Math.max(1, parseInt(next.appointmentApproachMinutes, 10) || DEFAULT_APPOINTMENT_APPROACH_MINUTES));
      if (getApproachMinutes() !== nextMinutes) {
        state.appointmentApproachMinutes = nextMinutes;
        changed = true;
        appointmentUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("notOnlineWindowMinutes")) {
      // Clamps the not-online window to 1..1440 minutes (last-online data is only kept ~24h), falling back to the
      // current value on a non-numeric input. chatUiChanged then refreshes the not-online launcher and re-applies hiding.
      const rawWin = Math.round(Number(next.notOnlineWindowMinutes));
      const nextWin = Number.isFinite(rawWin) && rawWin > 0 ? Math.min(Math.max(rawWin, 1), 24 * 60) : notOnlineWindowMinutesValue();
      if (notOnlineWindowMinutesValue() !== nextWin) {
        state.notOnlineWindowMinutes = nextWin;
        changed = true;
        chatUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (hasOwn("introMessages") || hasOwn("otherMessages")) {
      const intro = hasOwn("introMessages") ? parsePhraseLines(next.introMessages || "") : getIntroPhrases();
      const other = hasOwn("otherMessages") ? parsePhraseLines(next.otherMessages || "") : getOtherPhrases();
      const introText = (intro.length ? intro : parsePhraseLines(DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages)).join("\n");
      const otherText = other.join("\n");
      if (state.introMessages !== introText || state.otherMessages !== otherText) {
        setQuickPhraseLists(intro.length ? intro : parsePhraseLines(DEFAULT_STATE.introMessages), other, { save: false });
        changed = true;
        phraseUiChanged = true;
      }
    }
    if (!changed) return sniffiesGetSettings();
    saveState();
    if (textFiltersChanged) {
      refreshProfileTerms();
      includeDismissed.clear();
      profileTextRetryAt.clear();
      reevaluateProfileFiltersFromCache();
    }
    if (phraseUiChanged) {
      syncQuickPhraseTextareas();
      renderChatPhrasePanel();
    }
    if (matchUiChanged) renderMatchPanel();
    if (bookmarkUiChanged) renderBookmarkPanel();
    if (appointmentUiChanged) {
      processAppointmentAlerts();
      renderAppointmentPanel();
    }
    if (chatUiChanged) {
      updateRecentChatFilterLauncher();
      updateAnyChatFilterLauncher();
      updateNotOnlineFilterLauncher();
      refreshChatAgeBadges();
    }
    applyHiding();
    if (extensionControlsHostedInPopup) setSoftPanelVisible(false, false);
    return sniffiesGetSettings();
  }
  // Dispatches a named popup command (hideNow, undoLastHide, clearBlocked, show panels, export/import, Drive sync/load/disconnect) to its handler.
  // Awaits async actions; throws on an unknown action name; returns the post-action settings snapshot.
  async function runPopupAction(action) {
    if (shouldLog("verbose")) log("→ runPopupAction", traceArgs(arguments));
    const name = typeof action === "string" ? action : String(action && action.type || "");
    switch (name) {
      case "hideNow":
        await hideNow();
        break;
      case "undoLastHide":
        undoLastHide();
        break;
      case "clearBlocked":
        blocked.clear();
        saveBlockedSet();
        applyHiding();
        setStatus("Cleared blocked profiles");
        break;
      case "showMatches":
        state.showMatchPanel = true;
        saveState();
        renderMatchPanel();
        break;
      case "showBookmarks":
        state.showBookmarkPanel = true;
        saveState();
        renderBookmarkPanel();
        break;
      case "showAppointments":
        state.showAppointmentPanel = true;
        saveState();
        renderAppointmentPanel();
        break;
      case "clearPastAppointments":
        clearPastAppointments();
        break;
      case "exportBlocked":
        exportBlocked();
        break;
      case "importBlocked":
        importBlocked();
        break;
      case "exportData":
        await exportData();
        break;
      case "importData":
        await importData();
        break;
      case "syncDrive":
        await syncNotesToGDrive();
        break;
      case "loadDrive":
        await loadNotesFromGDrive();
        break;
      case "disconnectDrive":
        await disconnectGDrive();
        break;
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unknown popup action: ${name}`);
    }
    return sniffiesGetSettings();
  }
  // Debug helper: counts cached profile texts by source (full vs partial) and how many matched the exclude/include term filters.
  const sniffiesProfileTextStats = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesProfileTextStats");
    let full = 0;
    let partial = 0;
    let matchedExclude = 0;
    let matchedInclude = 0;
    for (const [id, entry] of profileTextCache.entries()) {
      if ((entry == null ? void 0 : entry.source) === "full") full += 1;
      else partial += 1;
      const filters = profileFilterCache.get(id);
      if (filters == null ? void 0 : filters.excludeTerm) matchedExclude += 1;
      if (filters == null ? void 0 : filters.includeTerm) matchedInclude += 1;
    }
    return {
      trackedProfiles: profileTextCache.size,
      full,
      partial,
      matchedExclude,
      matchedInclude,
      includeMatches: includeMatches.size,
      blocked: blocked.size,
      excludeProfileTermsEnabled: state.excludeProfileTermsEnabled !== false,
      includeProfileTermsEnabled: state.includeProfileTermsEnabled !== false
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: forces a full profile-text refresh; re-applies hiding if anything updated.
  // Returns the updated flag, text stats, and the last full-user sample captured on globalThis.
  const sniffiesForceFullTextFetch = async () => {
    log("→ sniffiesForceFullTextFetch");
    const updated = await refreshFullProfileText("manual");
    if (updated) applyHiding();
    return {
      updated,
      stats: sniffiesProfileTextStats(),
      lastFullSample: (typeof globalThis !== "undefined" ? globalThis.__sniffiesLastFullUserSample : null) || null
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: snapshots the filter engine — marker counts, attitude breakdown (bottom/vers-bottom/other/unknown), hidden count, and cache sizes.
  // Reads attitude from manualAttitudes first, then attitudeCache.
  const sniffiesSoftFilterStats = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesSoftFilterStats");
    var _a;
    let knownAttitude = 0;
    let bottom = 0;
    let versBottom = 0;
    let other = 0;
    let unknown = 0;
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      const att = manualAttitudes[id] || ((_a = attitudeCache.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _a.attitude) || null;
      if (!att) {
        unknown += 1;
        continue;
      }
      knownAttitude += 1;
      if (att === "bottom") bottom += 1;
      else if (att === "vers-bottom") versBottom += 1;
      else other += 1;
    }
    return {
      markerCount: document.querySelectorAll(".maplibregl-marker").length,
      markerWithId: idToMarker.size,
      knownAttitude,
      bottom,
      versBottom,
      other,
      unknown,
      hiddenMarkers: countHiddenMarkers(),
      blockedCount: blocked.size,
      chatTrackedProfiles: chatActivity.size,
      knownSelfIds: selfProfileIds.size,
      appointments: appointments.size,
      bookmarks: bookmarks.size,
      bookmarkSortBy: state.bookmarkSortBy || "newest",
      bookmarkOnlineFilter: state.bookmarkOnlineFilter || "all",
      hideRecentChats24h: !!state.hideRecentChats24h,
      hideRecentChats2h: !!state.hideRecentChats2h,
      hideAnyChats: !!state.hideAnyChats,
      showOnlyChats: !!state.showOnlyChats,
      excludeProfileTermsEnabled: state.excludeProfileTermsEnabled !== false,
      includeProfileTermsEnabled: state.includeProfileTermsEnabled !== false,
      recentChatProfilesOnMap: countRecentChatProfilesOnMap(),
      anyChatProfilesOnMap: countAnyChatProfilesOnMap(),
      approachMinutes: getApproachMinutes(),
      profileTextCache: profileTextCache.size,
      profileFilterCache: profileFilterCache.size,
      attitudeCache: attitudeCache.size
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: forces a partial-attitude fetch pass, then returns the soft-filter stats snapshot.
  const sniffiesForcePartialsFetch = async () => {
    log("→ sniffiesForcePartialsFetch");
    await updatePartials("manual");
    return sniffiesSoftFilterStats();
  };
  // Debug helper: thin wrapper exposing extractAttitudeFromPartial(sample) for testing attitude parsing.
  const sniffiesExtractAttitude = (sample) => extractAttitudeFromPartial(sample);
  // Debug helper: counts tracked chat profiles having a valid outgoing/incoming/any last-message timestamp; also lists known self IDs.
  const sniffiesChatAgeStats = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesChatAgeStats");
    let withOutgoing = 0;
    let withIncoming = 0;
    let withAny = 0;
    for (const entry of chatActivity.values()) {
      if (parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.myLastTs)) withOutgoing += 1;
      if (parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.theirLastTs)) withIncoming += 1;
      if (parseTimestamp(entry == null ? void 0 : entry.anyLastTs)) withAny += 1;
    }
    return {
      trackedProfiles: chatActivity.size,
      withOutgoing,
      withIncoming,
      withAny,
      selfIds: [...selfProfileIds]
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: registers a profile ID as 'self' (so own-sent messages are attributed correctly); returns ok plus the self-ID list.
  const sniffiesSetSelfId = (id) => {
    log("→ sniffiesSetSelfId");
    const normalized = normalizeProfileId(id);
    if (!normalized) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid id" };
    rememberSelfProfileId(normalized);
    return { ok: true, selfIds: [...selfProfileIds] };
  };
  // Debug helper: forces a re-parse of the currently-open chat view for the given (or in-view) profile.
  // Returns ok:false when no profile chat is in view.
  const sniffiesForceChatRefresh = (id = null) => {
    log("→ sniffiesForceChatRefresh");
    const normalized = normalizeProfileId(id || getChatContextProfileId());
    if (!normalized) return { ok: false, reason: "no profile chat in view" };
    const refreshed = forceRefreshCurrentChatView(normalized);
    return { ok: refreshed, id: normalized };
  };
  // Debug helper: rescans localStorage for chat payloads (force=true ignores throttle), schedules a badge refresh, and merges in chat-age stats.
  const sniffiesRescanChatAges = (force = true) => {
    log("→ sniffiesRescanChatAges");
    const storage = scanStorageForChatPayloads("manual", !!force);
    scheduleChatBadgeRefresh();
    // esbuild object-spread helpers: shallow-merge chat-age stats with the extra storage/capture fields.
    return __spreadProps(__spreadValues({}, sniffiesChatAgeStats()), {
      storage,
      captureInstalled: !!chatCaptureInstalled
    });
  };
  // Debug helper: dumps the fetch/XHR/WebSocket capture instrumentation — JSON/WS counters and the top-20 source URLs ranked by updates, then parsed, then hits.
  // Includes recent-event list and chat-age stats.
  const sniffiesChatCaptureDebug = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesChatCaptureDebug");
    const recent = [...chatCaptureStats.recent];
    const topUrls = Object.entries(chatCaptureStats.urls).sort((a, b) => {
      var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e, _f;
      return (((_a = b[1]) == null ? void 0 : _a.updates) || 0) - (((_b = a[1]) == null ? void 0 : _b.updates) || 0) || (((_c = b[1]) == null ? void 0 : _c.parsed) || 0) - (((_d = a[1]) == null ? void 0 : _d.parsed) || 0) || (((_e = b[1]) == null ? void 0 : _e.hits) || 0) - (((_f = a[1]) == null ? void 0 : _f.hits) || 0);
    }).slice(0, 20).map(([url, data]) => __spreadValues({ url }, data || {}));
    return __spreadProps(__spreadValues({}, sniffiesChatAgeStats()), {
      captureInstalled: !!chatCaptureInstalled,
      apiJsonSeen: chatCaptureStats.apiJsonSeen,
      apiJsonParsed: chatCaptureStats.apiJsonParsed,
      responseJsonSeen: chatCaptureStats.responseJsonSeen,
      responseJsonParsed: chatCaptureStats.responseJsonParsed,
      wsMessages: chatCaptureStats.wsMessages,
      payloadUpdates: chatCaptureStats.payloadUpdates,
      storageScans: chatCaptureStats.storageScans,
      storageKeysParsed: chatCaptureStats.storageKeysParsed,
      storagePayloadUpdates: chatCaptureStats.storagePayloadUpdates,
      topUrls,
      recent
    });
  };
  // Debug helper: explains chat-badge visibility per marker — which markers have chat data and why each is shown/hidden (recent, any-chat, or show-only-missing filters).
  // Also counts live floating chat-badge DOM nodes.
  const sniffiesChatBadgeDebug = () => {
    log("→ sniffiesChatBadgeDebug");
    var _a, _b;
    let markerWithChatData = 0;
    let visibleMarkersWithChatData = 0;
    let hiddenMarkersWithChatData = 0;
    let suppressedByRecentFilterWithChatData = 0;
    let suppressedByAnyChatFilterWithChatData = 0;
    let hiddenByShowOnlyMissingChatHistory = 0;
    for (const id of idToMarker.keys()) {
      const hasChatData = chatActivity.has(id) || !!parseTimestamp(howdySentAt[id]) || !!((_a = chatPreviewByProfile.get(id)) == null ? void 0 : _a.length);
      if (hasChatData) {
        markerWithChatData += 1;
        if (shouldHideByRecentChats(id)) suppressedByRecentFilterWithChatData += 1;
        if (shouldHideByAnyChats(id)) suppressedByAnyChatFilterWithChatData += 1;
      } else if (shouldHideByMissingChatHistory(id)) {
        hiddenByShowOnlyMissingChatHistory += 1;
      }
      if (!hasChatData) continue;
      const marker = idToMarker.get(id);
      const root = resolveMarkerRoot(marker);
      // A marker counts as hidden if it carries HIDE_CLASS or is display:none / visibility:hidden via computed style.
      const hidden = !!root && (((_b = root.classList) == null ? void 0 : _b.contains(HIDE_CLASS)) || getComputedStyle(root).display === "none" || getComputedStyle(root).visibility === "hidden");
      if (hidden) hiddenMarkersWithChatData += 1;
      else visibleMarkersWithChatData += 1;
    }
    let visibleFloatingBadges = 0;
    for (const badge of floatingChatBadges.values()) {
      if (!badge || !document.body.contains(badge)) continue;
      if (badge.style.display !== "none") {
        visibleFloatingBadges += 1;
      }
    }
    return {
      showChatAges: !!state.showChatAges,
      hideRecentChats24h: !!state.hideRecentChats24h,
      hideRecentChats2h: !!state.hideRecentChats2h,
      hideAnyChats: !!state.hideAnyChats,
      showOnlyChats: !!state.showOnlyChats,
      markerWithId: idToMarker.size,
      markerWithChatData,
      visibleMarkersWithChatData,
      hiddenMarkersWithChatData,
      suppressedByRecentFilterWithChatData,
      suppressedByAnyChatFilterWithChatData,
      hiddenByShowOnlyMissingChatHistory,
      floatingBadgeNodes: floatingChatBadges.size,
      visibleFloatingBadges
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: returns the sorted appointments list.
  const sniffiesAppointments = () => sortedAppointments();
  // Debug helper: returns the sorted bookmarks list.
  const sniffiesBookmarks = () => sortedBookmarks();
  // Debug helper: starts a broadcast of 'message' to all bookmarks passing the current bookmark filters.
  // Returns the requested recipient count and a copy of the active broadcast job.
  const sniffiesBroadcastToBookmarks = (message) => {
    log("→ sniffiesBroadcastToBookmarks");
    const recipients = sortedBookmarks().filter(bookmarkMatchesFilters).map((row) => row.profileId);
    startBroadcastMessage(message, recipients);
    return {
      requested: recipients.length,
      active: activeBroadcastJob ? __spreadValues({}, activeBroadcastJob) : null
    };
  };
  // Debug helper: aborts any in-progress bookmark broadcast (reason 'user').
  const sniffiesStopBroadcast = () => stopBroadcastMessage("user");
  // Debug helper: reports current sizes of every long-lived cache/map (markers, attitude/text/filter/summary caches, chat activity, bookmarks, etc.) for memory inspection.
  const sniffiesMemoryStats = () => ({
    markers: idToMarker.size,
    attitudeCache: attitudeCache.size,
    profileTextCache: profileTextCache.size,
    profileFilterCache: profileFilterCache.size,
    profileSummaryCache: profileSummaryCache.size,
    includeMatches: includeMatches.size,
    includeDismissed: includeDismissed.size,
    retryQueue: profileTextRetryAt.size,
    appointments: appointments.size,
    bookmarks: bookmarks.size,
    pendingAutoMessages: Object.keys(pendingAutoMessages || {}).length,
    chatActivity: chatActivity.size,
    chatDeletionStats: Object.keys(chatDeletionStats || {}).length,
    phraseStats: Object.keys(phraseStats || {}).length,
    chatRecent: chatCaptureStats.recent.length,
    chatUrls: Object.keys(chatCaptureStats.urls).length
  });
  // Debug helper: triggers an immediate memory GC pass via runMemoryGc('manual').
  const sniffiesRunMemoryGc = () => runMemoryGc("manual");
  // Debug helper: returns the quick-phrase state — counts and full intro/other/combined lists, current sort, plus last capture candidate and last save from globalThis.
  const sniffiesQuickPhrases = () => ({
    count: getCannedPhrases().length,
    introCount: getIntroPhrases().length,
    otherCount: getOtherPhrases().length,
    sort: normalizedQuickPhraseSort(state.quickPhraseSort),
    intros: getIntroPhrases(),
    others: getOtherPhrases(),
    phrases: getCannedPhrases(),
    lastCandidate: (typeof globalThis !== "undefined" ? globalThis.__sniffiesLastQuickPhraseCandidate : null) || null,
    lastSave: (typeof globalThis !== "undefined" ? globalThis.__sniffiesLastQuickPhraseSave : null) || null
  });
  // Debug helper: saves 'text' as a quick phrase (default category 'other') via saveQuickPhraseFromText.
  const sniffiesAddQuickPhrase = (text, category = "other") => saveQuickPhraseFromText(text, category);
  // exposeGlobal writes each helper onto window, globalThis, and (unless sandboxOnly:true, see its
  // own doc comment) Tampermonkey's unsafeWindow so it is callable from the page console. Anything
  // that mutates state, triggers network calls, sends messages, or exposes bookmark/appointment
  // content is sandboxOnly — reachable only from the userscript's own sandbox realm, not the page's.
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesShowMatchPanel", sniffiesShowMatchPanel);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesShowPanel", sniffiesShowPanel);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesHidePanel", sniffiesHidePanel);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesGetSettings", sniffiesGetSettings);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesUpdateSettings", updateSettingsFromPopup, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesRunAction", runPopupAction, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesProfileTextStats", sniffiesProfileTextStats);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesForceFullTextFetch", sniffiesForceFullTextFetch, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesSoftFilterStats", sniffiesSoftFilterStats);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesSoftFilterDebug", sniffiesSoftFilterStats);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesForcePartialsFetch", sniffiesForcePartialsFetch, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesExtractAttitude", sniffiesExtractAttitude);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesChatAgeStats", sniffiesChatAgeStats);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesSetSelfId", sniffiesSetSelfId, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesForceChatRefresh", sniffiesForceChatRefresh, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesRescanChatAges", sniffiesRescanChatAges, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesChatCaptureDebug", sniffiesChatCaptureDebug);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesChatBadgeDebug", sniffiesChatBadgeDebug);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesAppointments", sniffiesAppointments, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesBookmarks", sniffiesBookmarks, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesBroadcastToBookmarks", sniffiesBroadcastToBookmarks, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesStopBroadcast", sniffiesStopBroadcast, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesMemoryStats", sniffiesMemoryStats);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesRunMemoryGc", sniffiesRunMemoryGc, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesTeardown", teardownSniffies, { sandboxOnly: true });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesGetProfileTableData", sniffiesGetProfileTableData);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesProfileLastActive", () => {
    const t = now();
    const out = {};
    for (const [id, ts] of profileLastActive.entries()) {
      // 6e4 = 60000 ms/min; converts the last-active delta into whole minutes ago.
      out[id] = { lastActiveTs: ts, minutesAgo: Math.round((t - ts) / 6e4), onMap: idToMarker.has(id) };
    }
    return out;
  });
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesQuickPhrases", sniffiesQuickPhrases);
  exposeGlobal("__sniffiesAddQuickPhrase", sniffiesAddQuickPhrase, { sandboxOnly: true });
  // Final boot log; version string is one of the 4 places to update when bumping the version.
  logInfo("Sniffies soft filter loaded (v0.11.2)");
})();
